# Changelog (canonical change log)

This file is the single source of truth for repository changes. Topology and
deployment rules live in `docs/SSOT.md` and `AGENTS.md`; this file records only
what changed and when, by merge commit and PR.

## 2026-11-27
- chore(nexus): the System status row "TradingView alerts" removed (A4 of the TradingView cleanup; owner 2026-11-27 "Lanjutkan pembersihan ketergantungan tradingview"). xauusd S3 retires the TradingView alert webhook (410), so the row (`ui/src/lib/health.ts`, key `webhook`; `pages/Status.tsx` STATUS_ROWS) would only ever read stale. UI only; `/api/status` is unchanged and its `webhook` field, if still sent, is ignored.
- fix(bridge): the feed bridge keeps its terminal ini's owner (F2-fix; found by the VPS install of 2472e48). The feed daemon (root) rewrote `/opt/mt5-oanda/login.ini` (600 mt5:mt5) as root:root on its first sync, so `mt5-oanda` and `run.sh` (user mt5) could no longer read it; it now writes through the executor's `writeTerminalIniPreservingOwner` (stat, chown to the original uid and gid, mode 600). Test FD-7. Runbook: the binary is `MetaEditor64.exe`.
- feat(nexus): Data feeds page in Live (F4; owner 2026-11-27 "Setuju", placement "tempatkan di nexus > live"). New `#/feeds` (Live › Data feeds, after System status): one card over the Omega overview's `broker.feeds` (F1) with, per feed bridge, fresh or stale and the sync age, the terminal, server and symbol, the login status and version, and a warning when the stored login is sealed to an old key; "Data feed (no trading)" on each; "No feed bridge has connected yet" when there is none. The owner (`viewer.can_control`, the broker connection form's gate) gets a login form per feed, sealed with the Omega page's own `buildEnvelope` (now exported, otherwise unchanged) to that feed's `public_key` and `kid`, then `POST /omega/api/feed-connection`; uncontrolled inputs cleared after every submit; a disconnect with a required reason (`ConfirmDialog` gains an optional reason field), `DELETE /omega/api/feed-connection`. Every refusal is shown inline (`role="alert"`). Fixtures `test/fixtures/nexus-f4/` from the real Omega routes and AI Worker (`test/support/nexus-f4-seed.mjs`); tests `test/nexus-feeds-page.test.mjs`; render-check views F4*. The Omega page and the executor's connection form are unchanged.
- feat(bridge): the data-only feed role and its exporter EA (F2, F3; owner 2026-11-27 "Setuju"). Bridge 1.8.0: `OMEGA_ROLE=feed` runs `src/feed-daemon.mjs`, a separate loop (the executor's daemon unchanged but for its version string): it reads its own terminal's `state.json` and `corpus.json`, syncs to `/api/bridge/feed-sync` with `OMEGA_FEED_ID`, applies its own sealed login with `applyFeedCredentials` (algo trading off, `Expert=OmegaFeed`), restarts only its own `TERMINAL_SERVICE`, empties `Password=` once the terminal is logged in on that server, forces `AllowLiveTrading=0` on every sync (`ensureFeedSettings`) and never writes intents or a reference; it refuses to start on the `__OWNER_FILLS__` secret placeholder or without its own paths. `omega-bridge@.service` and `feed.env.example`. New `ea/mt5/OmegaFeed.mq5` 1.00: state (connected, server, the gold symbol's quote, contract size and profit currency; no account amount, no login) and the gold M1 corpus in OmegaExecutor's formats, from `FeedSymbol` or the chart symbol (`XAUUSD.sml`); no trade code, pinned by test/omega-feed-ea.test.mjs. `feed_on_executor_server` now compares brokers (`sameBroker`), not server strings. Runbook docs/RUNBOOK_OF1_FEED.md (F6). Tests FD-1..FD-5, OF-1..OF-3, FB-3.
- chore(omega): `OMEGA_FEED_CLIENT_ID` set to the feed bridge's Access service token client id (F5; owner 2026-11-27 "Setuju"; token `cryptophys-agent-omega-feed`, created by the owner). Only that token reaches `POST /api/bridge/feed-sync` (F1); the executor's token and route are unchanged. Effective once the token is admitted by the Access policy.
- feat(omega): data-only feed bridges on the server (F1, OF1; owner 2026-11-27 "gunakan vps existing dikerjakan sekarang sebagai prioritas utama"; orchestrator OK on the OF1 brief). New `src/feed-bridge.mjs`: `feedSync` (D1 `omega_feed_bridges`, `role = 'feed'`; a stripped state, never an account, position or deal; its own sealed login back; no `intents` key ever), `setFeedConnection`/`clearFeedConnection` (D1 `omega_feed_connections`, per-bridge kid) and `feedOverview` (`broker.feeds` on the Omega overview). Omega Worker: `POST /api/bridge/feed-sync` behind `OMEGA_FEED_CLIENT_ID` (unset: every feed refused) and `POST`/`DELETE /api/feed-connection` (a person). `ingestFeedCorpus` stores a feed's gold M1 bars as their own source marked in `corpus_feeds`; `readCorpusBars({ preferFeed })` gives the MT5 fallback a fresh feed first (3 minutes), every other reader the executor's own source only; the pull adapter's fallback asks with `prefer_feed`. Also: the market-data-stopped alert says when the fallback's broker bars stopped too. Tests FB-1..FB-5, F1 (Omega Worker), health-alerts. Inert without a feed bridge; no EA, bridge, cron or executor-path change.
- feat(omega): the MT5 fallback on another broker's bars is a gold reference (MX1; owner 2026-11-27 "monex ubah aturan dulu agar bisa eksekusi"). `referenceFrom` withheld the reference and every fallback 4h close whenever the feed ran on the fallback, so a Monex account found no gold while the fallback ran on FxPro's bars ("no reference gold price yet"). It now withholds only when the fallback's corpus source and the account's server are one broker (`sameBroker`, conservative: platform/account-type/number words ignored, unsure is the same), or when either is unknown. `fallbackSourceOf` reads the source the pull adapter already records; the overview's `bridge.symbol_check.reference` gives its origin and line. Tests MX1 (gold-symbol, omega-broker). Tolerance, contract checks, money and entry gates unchanged; no EA, bridge or cron change.
- feat(parity): nothing is compared any more (parity removal S2; owner 2026-11-26 "hapus parity dulu saja tapi tetap pertahankan arsitektur sekarang"; orchestrator PR1 rulings 1-2). `reconcileLocalComputation` writes the trusted `localcalc:` copy straight from the frozen table and reads no webhook; no `recordComparison`, promotion or revocation (the registry rows stay, read-only); HUD and group identities never get a copy. The sweep no longer runs the approved_at repair, the input-only reclassification or the intrabar recheck. The input-bar check goes with the comparisons (a provisional bar's record enters the lane; the republished final bar replaces it; input-mismatch candles 2026-11-03..27: 5m 12/3,401, 15m 10/1,196, 1H 14/345, 4H 2/72). `barsVerified` = the candle's trusted copy on native bars (15m: 10 of 1,196 candles read differently). `/ready` reports `provenance_mode: "frozen_identity_registry"` and `trusted_identities`; deploy-worker.yml and the preview e2e check both. The queue tasks still reconcile, so the H1 copy is immediate. No engine, hash, cron, EA or Omega change.
- fix(news): public `/health` serves no upstream free text, the stored and logged upstream diagnostic is redacted, and the gateway keeps its Workers Logs (RD; orchestrator 2026-11-27, after the QB1 heal check found `tv_quota_refusal` unreadable). `redactDiagnosticText` (fundamental-news-ingestion/src/index.mjs) had doubled escapes (a backslash before s), so a Bearer token or `access_token=...` in an upstream error survived into the last-pull summary (public `/health` `last_scheduled_ingest.failure_diagnostic`) and the ingest log; it now uses the new `sanitizeText` (the O5 refusal rules plus credential key=value pairs), shared with `sanitizeRefusalText`, the scheduled catch logs a sanitized `error.message`, and the `/pull` and RPC error bodies' `detail` is sanitized. `observability` added to fundamental-news-ingestion/wrangler.jsonc (head sampling 1, logs persisted, invocation logs off: they record request URLs, and `/oauth/callback?code=...` carries the OAuth code). `/health` `last_scheduled_ingest` drops `failure_diagnostic` and gains its `failure_code` and `upstream_error_code` (`publicLastPull`; Nexus reads only status and checked_at). A read-only scan of what `/health` served found no token-like text. Tests RD-1..RD-4. No cron, route or behaviour change.
- feat(parity): trust from a frozen identity table (parity removal S1; owner 2026-11-26: "Kita akan remove parity karena efeknya kecil dan sudah terbukti", "langkah pertama hapus parity dulu saja tapi tetap pertahankan arsitektur sekarang kita akan review lagi nanti"; orchestrator PR1 rulings). New `src/trusted-identities.mjs`: `TRUSTED_IDENTITIES`, the eight approved identities at their current hashes, and `UNTRUSTED_IDENTITIES` (Mizan through its pair, `*_d1`, `gmi_hud`, `prophet_adopt`). `isTrusted` and `m5ParityApproved` read the table; the registry is still written and kept as `registryApproved`, read for trust by nothing. Byte-identical for every identity the producers emit (16 of 16 on the 2026-11-26 snapshot). Guard: a hashed engine change fails TI-1 until the table moves in the same PR. Tests TI-1..TI-3; parity, 5m and B7 tests now state trust through the table. AGENTS.md rule 6 and SSOT §3 reworded. No engine, hash, cron, EA or Omega change.

## 2026-11-26
- revert(market-data): silver is pulled from TradingView again (QB1-R; orchestrator 2026-11-26). QB1 O3 (#622) built `OANDA:XAGUSD` 4h from the broker's silver, but the bridge sends no macro symbol on the active broker (its corpus holds gold only), so the series froze at its last TradingView bar. The SERIES entry loses `broker`, XAGUSD is back in `MACRO_TARGETS`, and `BROKER_TARGETS` is empty (the builder stays, inert; `rebuildFromBroker` exported for its test). 6 calls a weekday back on the quota. O5 (the quota reset read) is unchanged. The engines' bundle is byte-identical (QB-3). Tests QB-1, QB-2 rewritten; index and F7 back to their pre-QB1 form.
- fix(projection): the `recaps` RPC's row limit covers its window (RC1; orchestrator 2026-11-26, from the CTA session's finding). `PublicProjection.recaps` passed only `days`, so the default LIMIT 50 applied: with `PUBLIC_DELAY_HOURS` 0 (RT1, #590) that is about 12.5 hours of 15m decisions, and the CTA recap window found none on 11-25 and 11-26. It now passes `recapsLimit` (the caller's `limit`, else `days` x 96 + 1, both ends of the window, at most 200). The projection itself and `/api/public-projection` (LIMIT 50) are unchanged; the only callers are CTA's two `recaps({ days: 3 })`. Test RC1 at delay 0 and 24.
- fix(market-data): silver from the broker and the provider's quota reset read (QB1 O3 + O5; orchestrator ruling 2026-11-26 on the TradingView quota brief). O3: `OANDA:XAGUSD` 4h (context only, the Nexus correlations panel) is built from the broker's silver M1 bars at all times (`broker: "XAGUSD"`, `applyBrokerSeries`, the broker asked only once a new bucket can have closed), never pulled, never marked for healing; it left the fallback's macro targets. 6 calls a weekday back to the quota; the engines' bundle is byte-identical (QB-3). O5: the gateway reads the provider's reset from its refusal ("until HH:MM UTC|GMT|Z", ISO, "in N minutes|hours", Retry-After; at most 48 hours ahead), else one hour as before, and logs `tv_quota_refusal` with the form and the refusal's text without secrets. Tests QB-1..QB-5.
- feat(adopt): the Prophet adoption record (Track B, B11a; owner 2026-11-19 "adopsi semuanya", 2026-11-22 (a)-(c)). The pull adapter publishes `prophet_adopt` for the latest closed 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D bar (`publishAdoptRecords`, switch `PROPHET_ADOPT_PUBLISH`): the v2 alert's 62 fields from the Prophet core's own run, reused from the publishers' setup state of the same tick (no core run of its own), plus the alert's latches. The AI Worker ingests it for group parity only (`ADOPT_ENGINES`, switch `ADOPT_INGEST`): group identities only, never the analysis path, never read by the HUD store, an agent, a council, Key Levels, the Position Manager or Omega. The sweep prunes adoption comparison rows after 30 days (at most 2,000 a run). Hashes and V14 unchanged; new identity `prophet_adopt`.
- feat(nexus): the Liquidity page's level moves table (LM1; owner 2026-11-26: "Langsung build", no mockup, in the page's style). UI and tests only: no API, engine, D1 or Worker change; the rows are B19's `effects.level_moves` ({ at_ms, side, mode, action, outcome }, #617), its fixtures unchanged. In "What it changed in Omega", the Level moves stay grouped by action (`ladder_lock`, `key_level_lock` in words, the count, "past a lens B level", the outcome counts), and each move now has its row (`LevelMovesTable` in pages/Liquidity.tsx): the time in New York, then UTC and GMT+7 (`nyDayTime`), the position's side (▲ long, ▼ short in the direction colours), the mode, and the outcome (hit, miss, neutral, else pending) in a neutral chip, never green or red; no price. One ⓘ (`LEVEL_MOVES_HINT`) carries the scoring's own numbers (src/liquidity-lens.mjs `LEVEL_HOLD_BARS_15M` 8, `LEVEL_HOLD_ATR` 0.1) and no order word. Render check: `LiquidityViewLevelMoves` (every outcome, no side or mode, a move older than 7 days left out, each path nulled), `LiquidityViewNoLevelMoves`; `LiquidityViewQuorumNotMet` runs without level moves (a long move also reads "▲ long"); `LiquidityViewPreB19` unchanged. Tests: test/nexus-liquidity-page.test.mjs LQ5c.
- feat(lens): lens B's effects recorded and scored (Track B, B19; owner 2026-11-22: "tidak perlu shadow, langsung integrasikan ... position dan lainnya"). Two new `lens_readings` kinds, reported only and kept out of the floor (`FLOOR_KINDS`: sweep, block, maker). `skip`: the limit council's skips on `liquidity_sweep_against`, scored as blocks from the v4 result. `hold`: the stops Omega moved to a lens B level (SL+ and the ladder's lock), one per ticket and stop price with a hashed id, scored over eight closed 15m broker bars (0.1 × ATR15). The ladder's lock now carries `level_source: "liquidity"` on an exact, untied lens B rung (the only engine change; byte for byte without one). Switch `LENS_LEVEL_READINGS` (default on). The Nexus liquidity view shows skip outcomes and `level_moves`. `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` b4077f53… → 20e78488…; V14 unchanged. No EA, cron, entry or sizing change.
- feat(key-levels): lens B members as management candidates and the room_liquidity guard (Track B, B18; owner 2026-11-22: "tidak perlu shadow, langsung integrasikan ... key level, position dan lainnya"). The ledger step writes the feed basis and `levels_feed` into the lens state; `loadStrategyInputs` passes them as `brokerLiquidity` only under rule 3's gates (`brokerLiquidityInputs`). The Key Level Agent's family `broker_liquidity` (`withBrokerLiquidity`, weight 1) adds `key_levels.management`, the zones a member would promote (one per side, a separate list); zones, candidates, plans and entries are unchanged. The new guardrail check `room_liquidity` fails only when the management candidates make the squeeze (inside SQUEEZE_GUARD, not lifted by RM1b, ATR-only detail), so it can only add a WAIT; `decision.squeeze` and `room` are unchanged. The decision's `management_levels` reach the signal's flat `management_levels`, read only by Omega's management mode. The public recap and the model's features leave `room_liquidity` out (errata E4). Off: `KEY_LEVEL_FAMILIES_OFF=broker_liquidity` (via modes). Replay (read-only, 79 hourly captures with lens B levels, 2026-11-20 to 11-26): management on 3 bars (resistance), room_liquidity would have failed 0 times (0 entries blocked), A8 exposure 2 positions (a level beyond the stop), council prompt +1.3% when present. The RFC also records the weekly COT study (§3.6). `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 9766e732… → b4077f53…; V14 unchanged. Tests: test/broker-liquidity.test.mjs KL18-1..10; PL27; PL21's state assertions.
- feat(nexus): the Opening range page in Intelligence (Track C4), as the owner-approved mockup (artifact Y8D411jrAUUCjRw9fqrpd5, version 1795534115-bb10, its project files unchanged in the live 1795534210-ac9b; owner 2026-11-26: "setuju mockup C4", with "ya, ubah jadi ▼ merah"). UI, tests, fixtures, docs and a deploy smoke step only: no API, engine, D1 or Worker change; the AI Worker's `GET /api/orb` is backend B14 (#613), whose fixture is the contract, used byte for byte.
  - **Route.** `/#/orb` (key `orb`, icon Sunrise), in NAV_GROUPS Intelligence directly after Strategy agents (Chart patterns, Liquidity and Zones follow), and in App.tsx PAGES.
  - **Reads.** GET only, from the page's allow-list (`lib/intel.ts` `ORB_READS`, `intelGet`), one poll each: `/api/orb` 60 s, `/api/strategy-agents` 120 s (orb_breakout's status only), `/api/engine/decision` 60 s (`armed.ids` and `selection.candidates` only), `/api/ohlcv?timeframe=M15&limit=200` as a chart read (60 s). Everything `/api/orb` carries is read from it, the decision's `session_range` included.
  - **Panels** (`pages/Orb.tsx`, the pure null-safe `OrbView`): the heading (the agent's status, armed / not armed / no strategy armed, ORB levels on or off); Sessions (a card per session: range and window end in New York, UTC and GMT+7, the phase in words, the status chip in words with `status_detail` in the ⓘ, high, low, mid on the feed scale, the width in ATR (1H) on its band gauge, the breakout and its state, a plan per side or why there is none; the overlap's holder; "No ORB setup yet"; "Market closed" with the next range's set time); Ranges on the chart; Records per session; Recent sessions; Where it counted (the selection's candidates, the edges in key zones, `session_range`).
  - **Chart.** `components/IntelChart.tsx` gains `boxes` (new `lib/boxes-primitive.ts`): each formed range a box over its bars, its edges dashed to the window's last bar or the last candle (the label carries the window's end time), the broken edge in its breakout colour (grey once invalidated), the midpoint dotted, its label above the candles; the breakout bar a circle marker; one plan's Entry, Stop, TP1 and TP2 with their tags through the opt-in `plan` (the picked plan, else the overlap holder's), dimmed and suffixed by `planMark` when stale; no plan line without a plan. The Liquidity and Chart patterns pages pass no boxes and are unchanged.
  - **Code over mockup.** The contract has one R:R per plan (at TP1), the edge zones' `candidate` flag only (no "qualified"), no stop rate per session, no width or session status per recent row (the rows are the agent's setups, not every session), no prices in `session_range` and nulls for an unformed range's prices: the page shows what the contract carries. R is neutral (the mockup coloured it). A plan's role is "holds the side" in the overlap, "picked" by the decision, else its status; a side without a plan says invalidated, stands aside, no breakout on that side or not traded with its reason.
  - **Fixtures.** New `test/support/nexus-orb-seed.mjs` (`--write`): the real routes on seeded environments: `orb-candles.json`, `orb-decision.json` and `orb-agents.json` beside B14's `orb.json` (the seed checks it equals the contract scenario); `orb-overlap*.json` (Mon 23 Nov 15:00 UTC over the chart patterns fixture's 15m bars: both windows live, New York holding the long side with a limit at its high, London's long side invalidated, armed, the selection from the real `pickStrategy`, ten setups with every exit); `orb-mismatch.json` (16 March 2026: London's clock mismatch, New York formed with no breakout).
  - **Render check.** The registry renders `OrbView` over the contract, the overlap and the mismatch, and the forming, market-closed, mode-off, both-invalidated, no-breakout, range-event, too-narrow, short, not-armed, auto, stale and intel-views-off variants, the agents, decision and candles bodies varied, and `OrbUnread` with the 500 and 404 errors.
  - **Tests.** New test/nexus-orb-page.test.mjs OR1-OR12; CP1, CP13, LQ1, ZN1 and FSU1 follow the new NAV order and SSOT line; IP3's spawn limit is 5 minutes (the check took 115 s of its 120 s on main in a full local run; C4 adds about 3,500 variants); CA2 checks the page's chart credit; the new files join DIRECTION_FILES, REVIEW_FILES and PS10.
  - **Deploy.** deploy-nexus-dashboard.yml "Smoke test the Opening range view": the Access redirect without the token; with it 200 JSON, no-store, `cryptophys.orb-view.v1` and `scale.prices` "feed", or the 404 of `INTEL_VIEWS` off as a pass.
- feat(position): lens B in one management level set (Track B, B17; owner 2026-11-22: "tidak perlu shadow, langsung integrasikan ... position dan lainnya"). `brokerKeyLevels(signal, { spread })` (src/omega-engine.mjs) is the management mode: lens B's levels join only while `liquidity_on` and `feed_basis_known` hold, and one within two spreads (`managementSpread`: the hour's spread median × the symbol's point; none when unknown) of a Key Level zone merges into it. `manageIntents`, the ladder's TP2 wait, `runPositionManager` and v4's Position Manager step read it; a move only lens B's levels set carries `level_source: "liquidity"`. The Position Manager keeps `brokerLevels` for the council-visible fields and passes the set as `positionFacts({ managementLevels })`: its `management` { tp2, ladder_hold, loosen_levels } is read only by `positionAction` (TP2, the ladder's hold, A8), `PM_LINES_OFF=liquidity` reads it absent, and `lines_json.cause` is `liquidity@1` when lens B changed the action. The council sees lens B only as `liquidity_levels` [{ kind, side, distance_atr }] (5m ATR, never a price); without a lens B level its messages are byte for byte as before. The Nexus liquidity view shows the management mode. - feat(key-levels): the opening-range edges as key levels (Track A PR 8, orchestrator queue A8). While a London or New York opening range is formed and its window is live, `rawLevels` (src/key-level-agent.mjs) adds its high and low: kind `open` (the session family), tf "15", reader `session`, weight 0.5 (`ORB_LEVEL_WEIGHT`), not unique, labels in words only. They can add the session family to a zone and widen it, never make a candidate on their own. No engine hash moves (the Key Level Agent is not in `ENGINE_CODE`). - feat(strategy-agents): the opening range breakout agent, live (Track A PR 7, orchestrator queue A7; rebuilds #507). New `src/opening-range.mjs` (`openingRanges`, `orbMode`, `OPENING_RANGE_SESSIONS`): the London and New York ranges (first three closed, contiguous 15m bars from 03:00 and 08:00 New York time; windows to 11:45 and 16:45 New York time, `sessionWindow`), with the statuses `not_formed`, `window_over`, `market_paused`, `range_incomplete`, `range_event` (a high-impact USD release inside the range) and `session_clock_mismatch` (London while 03:00 New York is not 08:00 in London). - fix(bridge): lost acks from the results-file race (bridge 1.7.1). Owner 2026-11-26, on fixing it in the bridge only: "Ya, bridge saja". No EA change; the case where the EA's FileOpen fails and it writes nothing stays with CA1 (`src/close-reconcile.mjs`).
  - **Cause.** OmegaExecutor's `AppendResult` opens `omega/results.txt` (creating it), seeks to the end, writes, closes. Up to 1.7.0 the bridge woke on the file's creation (or its 5 s period) and at once renamed it to `results.<ms>.done`, then read it (`consumeResults`). Wine's locks do not stop a native rename, so the EA's still-open handle followed the file and its line landed in a `.done` file already read: the ack was lost (2026-11-18 to 2026-11-25: 10 of 28 close intents expired without a result, docs/SSOT.md CA1).
  - **Bridge** (`bridge/omega-bridge/src/files.mjs` `collectResults`, `resultsSettleLeftMs`, `splitCompleteLines`; `src/daemon.mjs`). Debounce: a result-file watcher event wakes a sync after 400 ms without another (`RESULTS_DEBOUNCE_MS`), and `results.txt` is renamed only once its mtime is 400 ms old (a tick waits for it, at most 1 s). Re-read: every pass reads the `.done` files modified in the last 60 s again (`RESULTS_REREAD_MS`); a line found only there is logged `result_recovered`. Partial line: only newline-terminated lines are read; a `.done` file with an incomplete last line is read again and never pruned for up to 10 minutes (`RESULTS_PARTIAL_MAX_MS`), then `result_partial_line_dropped` once. Dedupe: by intent id, the bridge's existing pending and reported ids, now a memory bounded at 30 minutes and 5000 ids (`createResultIdMemory`). `BRIDGE_VERSION` 1.7.0 → 1.7.1; `daemon_started` adds `results_debounce_ms` and `results_reread_ms`.
  - **Restart.** The reported ids persist in the bridge's state dir (`$OMEGA_STATE_DIR/reported-results.json`, default `/var/lib/omega-bridge`; intent ids and times only, written by tmp + rename, mode 0600, the same 30-minute and 5000-id bounds; `readReportedIds`, `writeReportedIds`). A restarted bridge re-sends nothing; a missing file starts empty, a corrupt one starts empty with one `reported_ids_unreadable` warning.
  - **Worker** (review of this fix: a line re-sent after a restart repeated the "EA answered" fault-clear, so it could lift a no-answer fault raised after the original ack). `bridgeSync` (`src/omega-broker.mjs`) calls `clearNoAnswerFault` only for a result whose intent was still pending, sent or expired at receipt (`RESULT_OPEN_STATUSES`); a late line for a done or failed intent, or an unknown id, clears nothing. The result's own handling is unchanged (it lands only on pending, sent or expired). `bridgeSync` is not in ENGINE_CODE and `clearNoAnswerFault` (hashed) is unchanged: ENGINE_CODE_HASH 70930517… and ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH ecfe3f90… unchanged. No minimum bridge version is checked.
  - **Tests.** New `bridge/omega-bridge/test/results-race.test.mjs` RR0 to RR7b (the debounce at the file, tick and watcher level; seen twice, sent once; a late line after the rename; a partial line completed, and one past the 60 s window kept and unpruned, dropped after 10 minutes; the dedupe bound; a restart with the persisted ids re-sends nothing, a corrupt file starts empty); new `test/fault-clear-results.test.mjs` FC1 to FC3 (an old line for a done or failed intent leaves a later fault; a result for a pending, sent or expired intent clears it); `omega-bridge.test.mjs` pins 1.7.1 and backdates its results.txt past the debounce.
  - **Docs.** docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md "Results-file race", docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE_PROTOCOL.md (how `results` are collected), docs/SSOT.md (beside CA1). VPS: docs/VPS_UPGRADE_BRIDGE_ACK.md, the bridge only (`omega-bridge.service`); never the terminal or the EA.

## 2026-11-25
- feat(shadow): news_compare on Beam behind SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER (SB1). Owner 2026-11-25, on routing shadow calls to Beam: "semua shadow pakai beam"; the read-only survey then found that only one source can route safely, and the owner chose: "news_compare saja". docs/SSOT.md ("Shadow model router", FS1, the organs' DNA), docs/RFC_INDICATOR_ADOPTION.md (Cost). The platform router merged at 982971c (AIR1: the ADR-0007 amendment, `XAUUSD_CEILING` grants `xauusd-model-router` `model_router.status` and `model_router.report_paid`); the switch stays off, and until the kernel deploy and the agent's registration land a refused or unknown status reads as not "on" and every call falls back.
  - **The router.** New `src/model-router.mjs` (+ `.d.mts`), a small copy of CTA's router (cryptophys-cta `src/agent/router.mjs`, b2a30f3) with its defaults: Chat Completions at `MODEL_BASE_URL` (default `https://api.reflection.ai/openai/v1`), `MODEL_ID` (default `Beam-1.0-501B-A23B`), the AI Worker secret `REFLECTION_API_KEY` (set by the owner through the platform; no key, no call); `reasoning_effort` "low", `max_completion_tokens` 2,000 a call.
  - **Scope.** Only `runNewsShadowCompare`'s two readings (TradingView's set and the free set, together), selected by call site: it passes its own allow-list `MODEL_ROUTER_ALLOW = ["news_compare"]`; never by the source prefix. `metric_opening`, the note arm, the benched seats, `decision_council`, every other shadow source and every live caller are untouched.
  - **Switch.** FS1 row `SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER` (knowledge group; `off` default, `on`; not in wrangler.jsonc; owner-flipped). Off, the router is never called: the comparison's Workers AI requests, D1 statements, meter rows and KV reads are byte for byte main's (SB1-1; also checked once against main's own modules).
  - **Fallbacks** (each runs the Workers AI call of today, unchanged, with a reason code in a `model_router_fallback` line and D1 `model_router_events`): a 25 s timeout or network error; a breaker of 3 failures in a 15-minute window, open for the rest of it; a 429; Beam's own counters (100k in, 20k out with this call's cap reserved, 20 requests a day, 2 a minute); a 402, a billing or credit error or a cost above 0 (these also call `model_router.report_paid` once and hold routing off, KV `model-router:paid:v1`, until the kernel's status reads "on" again); `model_router.status` not "on" (60 s cache; refused, failed or unknown reads as not "on"); no `reflection-data-retention` header or "contributor"; an invalid answer (`parseModelObject`, `cleanReading`). A paid sign whose `report_paid` call failed keeps routing held even when the kernel reads "on" (the org-wide switch-off is not in force), and each later call reports again until it lands (SB1-4b).
  - **Kernel.** `kernelSyscall` over `AGENTS`, as every kernel capability, as `xauusd-model-router`; `organism.json` declares the agent (tier 1, no seat, the two capabilities) so the census proposes its `register_agent`. xauusd has no capability-id validator of its own; test D2 now checks every declared id against the platform's widened rule `/^[a-z][a-z_]*\.[a-z_]+(\.[a-z_]+)?$/` (new ids and old). The kernel's answers, as on platform 982971c (workers/ai-kernel/src/syscall.ts, control-plane-shared/src/model-router.ts): `model_router.status` returns `{ outcome: "succeeded", result }` with `result` `modelRouterStatus()` (`schema: "model_router.status.v1"`, `state: { state, mode, ... } | null`, `effective`, `health`, `limits`, `today`). Routing needs `result.state.state` "on" **and** `result.state.mode` "free_only"; `on`/`paid_capped` falls back as `kernel_paid_capped`, any `off*` state as `kernel_off`, a null or unreadable state as `kernel_unknown`; `effective` is not read (it also reflects the kernel's own var and key). `model_router.report_paid` takes `{ signal }` only (the kernel refuses any other key): a 402 is `http_402`, a billing or credit error `billing_error`, a cost above 0 `cost_field` (`plan_change` is mapped but not detected here); the model, host and source stay in this side's `model_router_events` row.
  - **429 and paid signs (the platform's order, AIR1 B1).** A 402 is paid; a 429 never is, whatever its text (a daily free-quota answer saying "upgrade your plan" or "credits" would otherwise report paid org-wide and turn Beam off for Cerebrum and CTA too): it is `rate_limited` and backs off to the next 00:00 UTC for a daily quota (day, daily, today), else Retry-After, else 60 s (`rate_limit_hold`, KV `model-router:rate-limited:v1`). Billing text counts only on another 4xx (a 403 with billing text is `billing_error`), never on a 5xx (`http_5xx`); a cost above 0 only on a 2xx. SB1-8c.
  - **For the owner.** news_compare needs its own `REFLECTION_API_KEY` secret on `xauusd-ai-trading-assistant`, set through the platform; without it every reading is `no_key` (Workers AI). The switch `SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER` stays off until the owner flips it.
  - **Counters.** Beam calls are metered in `ai_usage_daily` (`shadow:news_compare`, the Beam model name, tokens, calls, failed, timeouts). `shadowSpendToday` reads those rows as Beam's counters (`beam`) instead of an unpriced pass, `checkShadowBudget` reports them, and the router's day limits are that reading; request counts and the breaker come from `model_router_events` (90 days). No Beam row: the breaker's reading and its SQL as before.
  - **Switch date.** FS1's own audit rows (`omega_events` `feature_switch`, key `SHADOW_MODEL_ROUTER`) mark each flip; `model_router_events` holds each reading's route and day. The 14-day agreement window splits at them; `GET /api/news/shadow-compare` is unchanged.
  - **Memo.** No memo hash covers the news comparison (the room memo hashes room steps only), so none moves at the switch.
  - **Tests.** New test/shadow-model-router.test.mjs SB1-1 to SB1-7 (and SB1-4b; SB1-8a/8b on the kernel's real status and report_paid shapes); test/feature-switches.test.mjs (the accessor), test/organism-dna.test.mjs (the agent, the capability rule); fixtures regenerated (nexus-fs1, nexus-h3f, nexus-zones).
  - **Unchanged.** No UI, EA, Pine Script, TradingView or cron change; no `ERA_SWITCHES` change; no hashed function changes (ENGINE_CODE_HASH 70930517…, V14 ecfe3f90…).
- fix(zones): a supply/demand box types the cluster its own edge made (ZF1). Owner (via the orchestrator): "ZF1 tepi kotak".
  - **Cause.** `boxTypes` (src/zone-types.mjs) needed a strict overlap. A cluster's edges are written to two decimals (key-level-agent.mjs `clusterLevels`), while its members and the boxes keep their own prices. So a box whose own edge is one of the cluster's levels could fall just outside the rounded band and not type its own cluster. Two live misses on 2026-11-25 (Z6 diagnosis): the 15m whale demand bottom 3433.454 against its cluster's top 3433.45, and the 4H whale 3531.643 against 3531.64.
  - **Rule.** A box also types a zone when one of its edges (or a point) is within `BOX_EDGE_TOL` = 0.001 of one of the zone's member prices (+ `.d.mts`). Every other box still needs a strict overlap: a box that only touches a cluster it did not make still does not type it (Z0-4 and Z1b-2 unchanged). A price flag still types only the zone at price.
  - **Effect.** The frozen real capture of 2026-11-20 22:00 is unchanged (ZA-15 `capture_guards`, ZA-18 `capture`). In the synthetic zone fixture, four Z0 zones are retyped by a box whose edge made them (test/zone-box-edge.test.mjs ZF1-4): [3982, 3982.5] supply medium to strong (+whale@1H); [3992.5, 3995] none to demand medium (prophet_best@1H); [4016.92, 4019.6] +whale@15m; [4023, 4025] none to supply medium (prophet_best@1H). - fix(public-analysis): zone metric names keep Fibonacci ratios and whole words (ZN-names). Orchestrator, from three names the public H1 map and the CTA Telegram showed cut short: "1H Fibonacci 0. (OTE)", "1H Fibonacci 1. Extension" and "1H right-angled descending broadening formation: measured pr".
  - **Cause.** `zoneMetrics` (src/public-analysis.mjs) strips price-shaped numbers with `/\$?\b\d{3,5}(\.\d+)?\b/`, whose word boundary after the "." took the decimal run of a ratio ("618" of 0.618, "272" of 1.272); and `.slice(0, 60)` cut a long name mid-word.
  - **Fix.** A Fibonacci ratio (one integer digit and three or more decimals) is written to two decimals before the strip (0.618 → "0.62", 0.705 → "0.71", 1.272 → "1.27"; half-up, so 0.705 is not 0.70 by float). Two decimals never make a run of three or more digits, so the strip still lets no price through. A long name is cut at a word boundary at most 60 characters, without a dangling ":" / "-" / "(" / "," (`cutAtWord`, exported, with its .d.mts).
  - **Tests.** test/public-analysis.test.mjs ZN-names: the three names, 0.705, a short ratio left as it is, `cutAtWord`, and a seeded property over 400 random names with prices, ratios and long words (no run of three or more digits, at most 60 characters, ending on a whole word).
  - **Unchanged.** The public projection's other keys, `analysisLeaks`, and every non-public reader. No hashed function changes (ENGINE_CODE_HASH 70930517…, V14 ecfe3f90…).
- fix(omega): reconcile lost close acks from ea_deals (CA1). Orchestrator brief CA1; docs/SSOT.md (Omega broker engine, Intents), docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE_PROTOCOL.md (intent ids and idempotency). Server side only: the EA and the bridge are unchanged.
  - **The problem.** 2026-11-18 to 2026-11-25: of 28 close intents, 14 done, 4 failed (`:1` retries created about 0.2 s after the deal) and 10 expired with no result although `ea_deals` shows the ticket's deal closed 0.3 to 1.2 s after the intent, reason `expert` (confidence_drop, pm_take_profit_weakening, pm_cut_loss, manual_user_exit). Over 30 days: 20 of 53.
  - **The rule.** New `src/close-reconcile.mjs` (+ `.d.mts`): `planCloseReconcile` (pure) and `reconcileLostCloses`. A close `pending`, `sent` or `expired` with no result becomes `done` only when the deal is the ticket's closing deal (its last exit deal, the position gone from the terminal's report, the exit deals covering the open intent's volume where it records one), its reason is `expert`, its time is in [created_ms, created_ms + 5 s], and the intent is the earliest close, `partial_close` or `close_all` whose window holds the deal (a deal already taken is never used again). A `failed` or `done` intent is never touched.
  - **The marker.** The result: `{ ok: true, ticket, error: null, executed_ms: <deal time>, fill_price: <exit price or null>, slippage_pts: null, latency_ms, reconciled: true, reconciled_from: "ea_deals", deal_ticket }`; the audit row `intent_reconciled` (actor `engine`, `from: "ea_deals"`), already in change-notify's `NOT_A_CHANGE`. A later EA result does not change it.
  - **Live.** `bridgeSync` runs it when a deal lands in the sync or a close is about to expire (the expiry read now lists `close` intents too), before the TTL expiry; a reconciled Position Manager close gets no "not applied" line.
  - **Backfill.** `closeReconcileBackfill` in the 5-minute ledger step (src/index.ts, after `dedupeDeals`): once over 30 days, pages of 500, behind KV `omega:close_reconcile:backfill:v1` (written only once the window is read, with a readable terminal state); idempotent. Estimated from production (SELECT only, 2026-11-25): 20 rows (10 of them in the last 7 days). `position_actions` is not backfilled: its 8 rows tied to these closes already read `applied` (marked when planned), none `pending` or `expired`.
  - **Readers.** B5's breaker credits the close to its own line; the same-side re-entry guard (`lastWeakeningExit`) starts its cooldown after a reconciled weakening or cut-loss close; the sync no longer returns a reconciled close as open. Unchanged: the ledgers (v4 reads close reasons whatever the status), CDG, RE1, the exit notices, the limit council.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 68e6e3f8… and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` ecfe3f90… unchanged (no hashed function changed).
  - **Root cause (report only, not changed).** Best supported: a race on `MQL5/Files/omega/results.txt`. The EA appends a result by open, seek, write, close (`AppendResult`, ea/mt5/OmegaExecutor.mq5) and drops it silently when the open fails; the bridge renames the file and then reads it (`consumeResults`, bridge/omega-bridge/src/files.mjs), woken by the file's own creation (`wakesOn`, daemon.mjs). A rename between the EA's open and its write puts the line in the renamed `results.<ms>.done` after the bridge read it. Opens lose acks too (7 expired and 13 reconciled of 116 in 30 days), masked by `reconcileFilledOpens`; closes had no such path. The newest 20 `.done` files on the host would hold the lost lines.
  - **Tests.** New test/close-ack-reconcile.test.mjs CA1-1 to CA1-13.

- feat(omega): a learned gate on the confidence_drop exit, hold otherwise (CDG). Owner 2026-11-25, verbatim: "Learned gate, hold otherwise (Recommended)"; the ruling (owner and orchestrator, binding): CDG is P0, a confidence_drop close only where the learned gate passes for the agent × side × drop bucket, otherwise nothing happens; the gate by lift; default on, live. `src/cd-gate.mjs` (new); docs/SSOT.md "The confidence-drop gate (CDG)"; docs/RFC_STRATEGY_EXECUTION.md §3.
  - **Why.** The measurement before the ruling: agentConfidence's setup term (0.35 × {1, 0.5, 0}) makes the drops come in steps, 0.175 when the agent's at_level flips and 0.35 when it switches sides, and holding to the plan beat the actual confidence_drop exits.
  - **P0.** `strategyExits` (`src/omega-engine.mjs`): with `cd_gate` "on" and the gate given (`planIntents`' `cdGate`), a would-be confidence_drop close closes only when its cell passes; a failing or missing cell, or a drop below 0.15, is held: no intent at all (no close, no modify_sl, no break-even); the position follows its plan, SLG1 and the Position Manager. The plan's `cd_gate` lists each event (held or closed) only when there is one. `lean_flip` and the extend gate unchanged.
  - **The gate.** Cell `agent|side|bucket`, buckets 0.15-0.25 and ≥ 0.25 (none below 0.15); the drop prev − now on the signal's `agent_conf` / `agent_conf_prev` (what `confidenceTrend` reads). Adverse: the broker 1m close 1 h after the decision against the side (the replay scripts' definition); `base_rate(side)` the share of all decisions of the same 30-day window against that side; pass when n ≥ 30 and adverse_rate − base_rate ≥ 0.10.
  - **The daily job.** `runCdGate` on the 5-minute ledger step beside RM1b's revert, once a UTC day (no cron change): KV `cd-gate:v1` (the cells, window, base rates), KV `cd-gate:history:v1` (the passing set per change, for v4), one `omega_events` row (`cd_gate_cell`) per pass/fail flip and one Telegram line; idempotent.
  - **The held-drop log.** D1 `omega_cd_holds` (CREATE IF NOT EXISTS), one row per (ticket, decision bar): agent, side, drop, bucket, the cell's n, lift and pass, `at_level_flip` (from the two decisions' `strategy_plans`), the price, open price, volume, r_dist and the old rule's exit R (`cf_r`), action held|closed, at_ms. A gated close replaces a hold of its bar, never back.
  - **Switch and auto-revert.** The Omega setting `cd_gate` "on" (default) | "off" (the rule before CDG byte for byte): `OMEGA_DEFAULTS`, `settingsFrom`, the FS1 row (omega-settings, era switch), `ERA_SWITCHES` += `cd_gate`, the change audit's label; Aether's policy and its rollback never write it. `cdGateRevert`: the first 15 outcomes after it went live (a held position's realised R on the volume held, a gated close's counterfactual); a mean below the old rule's on the same positions sets it "off" (actor `agent:cd-gate`, which no longer clears a limit fault in `writeSettings`), one event and one line, once per window.
  - **Eras.** On by default it adds nothing to the switch signature (like `reentry_1m`, `limit_supersede`); unlike them it does not move `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH`: the gate is an input the fixtures do not carry, so the era carries over at the deploy and v4 replays the gate in force at each bar. `cd_gate=off` starts a new era.
  - **v4.** `loadOmega4Context` reads the history while on; `replayOmega4` hands planIntents `gateAt(history, minute)`; before the first entry, off, or no history: today's replay byte for byte.
  - **Read.** `GET /api/strategy-agents` gains `cd_gate` (`cdGateStatus`: the cells, window, base, passing keys, history count, log counts, revert state, rule). No UI change.

Each decision record carries `atr_range_ratio` = ATR(14) of the closed 15m bars (`pineAtr`, `src/prophet-r37-oscillator.mjs`, unedited) ÷ the high − low of the last 16 closed 15m bars, 3 places, on the decision's own 15m feed series (`atrRangeRatio`, `src/decision-engine.mjs`); absent with fewer than 16 bars. Nothing reads it; the signal, Omega and every gate are unchanged. The decision-record pins (AV2, MSH-15, RW-2, ZA-15, ZA-18, Z0-9, Z1a-6, Z1b-6) hold with it stripped (`withoutAtrRangeRatio`, test/support/zone-aim-inert.mjs).

- fix(knowledge): KA2 retries a stuck pending verdict post, bounded (`src/knowledge-verdicts.mjs`; SSOT §3 "Verdicts to the platform", Retries). Production D1 (read-only, 2026-11-25): `k-caution-h1-60-new-york` v1 `collecting`, `window_end` 00:00Z, pending since 00:11:58Z (reason `timeout`, attempts 1), never touched again, because only the current plan's entries were tried and each run makes a `collecting` entry with a new `window_end`.
  - **Orphan rows.** A pending row the current entries no longer cover is retried from its stored `payload_json` after the current entries, oldest first, through the same gates (`per_run`, `strikes_per_hour`, the agent backoff, the payload check plus "is the row's own", not registered, not found, the denial). The platform's dedup answers a repeat with `deduplicated: true`, marked posted.
  - **Bounded.** A timeout now counts an attempt (still no strike). At 5 attempts (`max_attempts`) the row is `failed` with `attempts_exhausted:<last reason>`; a pending row already at the cap is closed without a call. No row stays `pending`.
  - **Superseded** (orchestrator ruling). An orphan `collecting` row is marked `superseded` (final, in the done set) with no call, no attempt and no strike when the same item@version already has a posted `collecting` row with a later `window_end` (from an earlier run, or posted earlier in the same run) or a posted final verdict; the reason is `superseded:<window_end of the newer row>` or `superseded:<final verdict>`. The platform never receives an older `collecting` window after a newer one. It skips the retry gap (no call is made). A final-verdict orphan is never superseded. The run result gains `superseded`.
  - **Backoff.** `KNOWLEDGE_VERDICT_RULES.retry_gap_ms` = 50 minutes on the row's own `updated_at` (runs are hourly at :11), for current and orphan rows alike.
  - **No schema change.** The marker query also reads `reason`, `payload_json`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. The run result gains `orphans` and `exhausted`.
  - **Tests.** test/knowledge-verdicts.test.mjs KV-14 (the production row, with the h1 item no longer in the plan and only an older window posted, as in production: skipped by the old logic, retried from `payload_json` after the gap, timeouts to 5 then final `failed attempts_exhausted:timeout`), KV-15 (deduplicated marks it posted), KV-14a (a later window posted in an earlier run: `superseded:<window_end>`, no call, never retried), KV-14b (the newer window posted earlier in the same run: superseded in that run, the older window never sent), KV-14c (a posted final verdict: `superseded:<verdict>`, inside the gap too; a final-verdict orphan is never superseded), KV-19 (`supersededBy` and the plan: older or unposted rows never supersede; a superseded row is done), KV-16 (a timed-out final verdict, current and orphan), KV-17 (the gates, the payload check, the cap, the switch off, on an orphan of an item with no other row, so nothing supersedes it), KV-18 (the plan's gap and order), KV-9d (timeouts bounded). KV-9b changed: a timeout now counts an attempt (it asserted 0).

A value in force that the panel cannot set (the registry's `config_only`, e.g. wrangler's `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` "record") shows as text beside the settable values, none pressed, and its tooltip says why; the payload carries neither `config_only` nor `resolve` (registry-internal), so the page reads `config_only` only when present and otherwise treats any value outside `values` the same way.

- feat(learning): Z5, zone readings, separate counterfactual tables, the decision-side zone breaker and Omega attribution (`src/zone-readings.mjs`; SSOT §3 Decision Engine "Zone readings, counterfactuals and the decision-side zone breaker (Z5)"; docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md §7).
  - **Readings.** D1 `zone_readings`, words and ATR only: each touched key-level zone held, broke or neither within 8 closed 15m bars (0.5 ATR / 0.1 ATR), MFE/MAE, approach. One a (0.1 ATR bucket, approach) per 2 h. Written in the ledger step after the plan learner, at most 200 decisions a run, behind KV `zone-readings:cursor:v1`.
  - **Counterfactuals, their own tables.** `zone_blocked_setups` (plan_setups' columns): the zone_blocked WAITs, scored with plan learning's newly exported outcome step (`setupOutcome`; also `setupFromRow`, `CONTROL_ONLY`; no query or schema change there). `zone_rule_arms`: Z2b's acted plan against its rebuilt base. plan_setups and its 14 readers are unchanged (tested).

The holds are read into the decision's env copy for the run, recorded as `zone_rules_held`. A hold is `{ by, since, until, rule_version }` and stands 7 days (SV1b's period) whatever the evidence says, then is re-evaluated on its own rule version; an owner rule change releases it at once. One `XAUUSD ZONES` line per change. It never writes an owner switch, `omega_settings` or any Omega/PM state, and never changes a stored `rule_version` (the mode evidence does not restart).

- fix(parity): approval times (`src/parity-registry.mjs`, `src/parity-sweep.mjs`; SSOT §3 "Approval times"). The promotion bind in `recordComparison` stored `approved_at` "24.0" (the threshold) and never cleared `revoked_at` on promotion; it now stores the comparison time and clears `revoked_at`. Status and trust were never affected.
  - **Repair.** `repairApprovedAtOnce` runs once in the parity sweep (KV marker `parity:repair:approved-at:v1`, `SIGNAL_CACHE`, no TTL): a non-ISO `approved_at` becomes the `compared_at` of the 24th `match` after the identity's last `mismatch` (or from the start), an approved row's `revoked_at` becomes NULL; a row without such a match is not written and is listed in the one log line `parity_approved_at_repair`. Never status, counters or `updated_at`.
  - **Reclassification.** `reclassifyInputOnlyMismatches` never restores a row whose `approved_at` is not ISO. `reclassifyOnce` is single-shot: the marker is written before the work, and without a marker store it refuses.
  - **Tests.** New test/parity-approved-at.test.mjs PF-1 to PF-8. test/fixtures/parity-b7-main.json regenerated: in its scenario the old "24.0" let the one-time reclassification restore four gmi identities revoked by real indicator mismatches; they now stay revoked.
  - **KA2.** knowledge.verdict has its own 20 s timeout (`KNOWLEDGE_VERDICT_TIMEOUT_MS`; `PLATFORM_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS` unchanged for the other kernel calls). A timeout is unknown, not failed: the run stops, the row stays pending without an attempt or a strike, and a later `deduplicated: true` reply is marked posted (`src/knowledge-verdicts.mjs`; tests KV-9b, KV-9c).
- feat(parity): field groups (B7, Track B; inert until B11). Parity can now be trusted per group of fields, so one bad field revokes only its group (`src/parity-registry.mjs`, `src/parity-sweep.mjs`, `src/hud-store.mjs`; SSOT §3 "Field groups").
  - **Registry.** `PARITY_GROUPS` declares groups by id (engine, webhook source, `fields` as { local: webhook }, `kind`). `groupIdentityOf` gives a group the engine's hashes under `<group id><timeframe suffix>`, so it has its own 24-candle window, approval and revocation in the same tables. `parityGroupProblems` keeps group ids off `mizan*`, the base engine ids and the timeframe suffixes. `compareWithWebhooks` gains a fourth argument `{ fieldMap, nullEqual, requireFinite }`; `groupCompareOptions` sets `nullEqual` only for a HUD group. Without the options the comparison is exactly main's.
  - **The witness group** `probe_mizan_witness` (MZ1, owner 2026-11-23): engine `probe_mizan`, source `probe_mizan_hud`, fields `net_witness` and `prior_edge`. It matches only when both are finite and equal on both sides; null on both sides is `incomplete` (`reason: "fields_not_finite"`, `null_fields`), never a match; finite on one side is a mismatch. A later PR touching the Probe, `mizan-probe/src`, `gmi/src/mizan.mjs` or `producer/src/macro-aether.mjs` restarts its 24-candle parity.
  - **Sweep.** Each group of an observed record is compared and trusted on its own, with its trusted copy at `localcalc:<candle>:<group id>` (its own fields only). The result gains a `groups` key only when a group ran.
  - **HUD store.** `approvedComparable` merges per source and per group. `validateHudSnapshot` drops an unknown HUD id instead of rejecting the snapshot, and lists it in `dropped_huds` (on the stored snapshot, in the 202 answer and in the readings) plus one log line `hud_snapshot_dropped_huds`.
  - **Inert.** A group is compared only for an ingested engine's observed record, and no ingested engine declares one (`probe_mizan` is not ingested, and nothing emits its fields). `approvedComparable` reads no group key today.
  - **Tests.** New test/parity-groups.test.mjs B7-1 to B7-11. B7-1 runs a fixed 1H and 15m scenario (prophet, gmi, gmi_hud, Mizan; matches, mismatches, incomplete, input mismatch and republish, fallback, the sweep) and compares the sweep output, the registry and comparison rows, the trusted lane and the parity view byte for byte with test/fixtures/parity-b7-main.json, generated on main. B7-2 checks compareWithWebhooks without the options against main's outputs (test/fixtures/parity-b7-compare-main.json). The test/hud-store.test.mjs unknown-HUD case now expects the id dropped. No engine hash, EA, cron or trading-behaviour change.

A copy that fails the reward gate its base passed keeps the base (`zone_rules` `{ rule, held: "rrr" }`); a base the gate refuses is never aimed.
  - **tp_typed** (key-level targets only: `KEY_TARGET_AGENTS` in `src/strategy-agents.mjs`; TP1-KZ on): 'label' (default) keeps TP1's price and adds a decisive opposing TP1's words to `tp1_note`; TP2 goes to the next decisive opposing key zone at least 0.5 ATR beyond TP1 and 1.5R, else today's. 'qualified' (owner-set only, after TP1-KZ becomes tp1_kz@2): a decisive opposing qualified zone at or beyond `ZONE_NEED`, nearer than TP1, becomes TP1.
  - **stop_skip** (the zone_pullback geometry): the stop goes beyond the first zone at least 0.5 ATR beyond the entry that is not a decisive opposing-type zone; today's 0.2 ATR pad, 2.5 ATR cap and 1.5 ATR fallback. Structural stops unchanged.
  - **factor:** `pickStrategy`'s score x `zoneFactorOf` (1.2 own-type fresh/tested entry zone, 0.85 opposite-type, else 1), carried as `zone_factor` on typed candidates; eligibility and WAIT unchanged.

Never in the council's evidence, cards, rooms, the EA signal or public output. `rule_version` adds `+zone_aim@1` (`:qualified`, `:no_<token>`) only while a rule is on, so the mode selector's evidence restarts when the owner turns the first rule on, not at this deploy.

It now uses `timeMs` (from #568), like `shadowReport`. Measurement only: nothing the rooms read, no fetch, cadence or key changes. Test: the comparison with TradingView times as epoch seconds, as a number and as digits.
- feat(knowledge): idle shadow items close as not_tested (KA3, a follow-up to KA1 and KA2). In the hourly `runShadowClosures`, after KA1, `planIdleClosures` (`src/shadow-knowledge.mjs`) closes an item that has been idle for 72 hours of the arm running. It writes a `learned_live` row with status `retired` and reason `shadow:not_tested` through `closeShadowItems`, with the evidence in `verdict_json` (`rule` `untestable.agent_inactive`, rows, scored, window, idle start, counted hours). `promotedKeys` then takes it out of the queue. KA2 (`src/knowledge-verdicts.mjs`) posts it once as `not_tested` with rule `untestable.agent_inactive` (`arm`, `rows`, `scored`, window, `charts`; `idleEvidence`), inside the platform's schema. `untestableReason` moved to `src/shadow-knowledge.mjs` and is still exported from `src/knowledge-verdicts.mjs`. The Telegram closure line gains a not-tested form.
  - **The rule (from the arm's own call log, not market hours).** The item is idle from its newest `shadow_agent_outputs` row (any subject), or from when the pack first carried it. An hour counts only when the arm stored a note row on a bar of a room the item can be tested in, for an item that ranks after it in the queue. That proves the arm was on, the room met, and the slots and the budget reached past it. Arm-off, kill-switch, breaker and closed-market hours store no row and never count, and an earlier-ranked queue-mate never counts. It never closes a version already live or retired, an item KA1 can judge (a final outcome, or 300 answered rows waiting to be scored), an untestable item, or an item not in the pack.
  - **Study (production D1, read-only).** supertrend (1H) and aegis_footprint (15m) stopped when the neuron allocation benched them (2026-11-17 09:11Z and 2026-11-18 03:12Z). Aegis's 1H rows continue, so it is not idle. Supertrend has 56 of the 72 hours. Closing now: `k-role-htf-calibration-talk@1` and `k-role-interstellar-eyes-data-scope@1`, both never tested.
  - **Tests.** New test/shadow-idle.test.mjs KA3-1 to KA3-5. In test/knowledge-verdicts.test.mjs the world's pack items now carry their 1H chart, its untested item is first seen an hour before, and aegis's rows start later; the KV expectations are unchanged. With the arm off, nothing is read or written. No engine hash, EA, cron or trading-behaviour change.
- fix(news-shadow): two measurement bugs in the news Worker's `GET /shadow/report` (report only; `fundamental-news-ingestion/src/news-sources.mjs` `shadowReport`). Found by the 7-day shadow study of 2026-11-25.
  - **TradingView times.** Its `published_at` is stored as it came, epoch seconds, and `Date.parse` read it as NaN. TradingView latency came out empty (`n` 0), the six-hour pairing window never applied, and the lead was wrong. The new `timeMs` reads a bare number below 1e12 (or a string of digits) as seconds, a larger one as milliseconds, and anything else as a date string. `high_impact` now shows `published_at` as ISO.
  - **Re-recording at the UTC day change.** Items are stored per UTC day (`news:shadow:items:<date>`), so a headline still in a feed after 00:00 is stored again with a later first-seen time. Shadow counts were doubled and shadow latency inflated. The report now dedupes across days by item id and keeps the earliest first-seen, so reports over the existing keys are right. A day's `shadow` and `tv` count the headlines first seen that day.
  - **Unchanged:** the write side and keys, `shadowItemsSince` (phase B, read by the AI Worker), the hourly comparison, the fetches, their cadence and rate limits, and the news source production uses (TradingView). Tests: `fundamental-news-ingestion/test/news-sources.test.mjs` (two new).
- fix(hud): the latest HUD snapshot per chart outlives its bar (HT1, orchestrator-approved; found in the Z2 live replay). `src/hud-store.mjs` `HUD_LATEST_TTL_S`: `hud:<tf>:latest` keeps 3 hours for 15m and 1H, 5 hours for 4H (was 3) and 26 hours for 1D (was 3), so the last hour of each 4H bar and most of each day no longer read without a 4H or 1D chart. Per-candle keys keep 14 days. Test: test/hud-store.test.mjs HT1. No engine hash, EA or cron change.
- feat(decision): `ZONE_PRIORITY` "on" in wrangler.jsonc: the Z2 zone_ahead check goes live in 'trend' (orchestrator ruling on Z2: on only after the live-capture replay). Replay on 8 hourly captures (2026-11-24 16:41Z to 2026-11-25 00:41Z) with 1H/4H Prophet boxes: 55 plans, 28 with a decisive opposing zone within need, 15 (27%) fail in 'trend' (all counter-trend longs), 28 (51%) would fail in 'all'; Z1a on vs off changes no plan verdict (typed 75.0% vs 74.0%). `ZONE_NEED` kept at max(1R, 0.75 ATR). `rule_version` becomes `tp1_kz:on+zone_ahead@1` from this deploy, so the mode selector's evidence restarts, as TP1-KZ did. No code change; Z2's off pins stay.

## 2026-11-24
- feat(knowledge): the shadow arm's verdicts posted to the platform as the kernel agent `shadow-knowledge` (KA2; platform PF3, capability `knowledge.verdict`, migration 0088), so Aether's curator learns from them. `src/knowledge-verdicts.mjs` (new), docs/SSOT.md shadow knowledge arm and the DNA.
  - **DNA.** `organism.json` declares `shadow-knowledge` (capabilities `knowledge.verdict` only, tier 1, no seat); `test/organism-dna.test.mjs` D2 adds it to the code set and the per-agent ceiling (`AGENT_EXTRA`) and pins its entry. No hash pins organism.json. The census then proposes `register_agent` (platform side).
  - **Posts.** Hourly after `runShadowClosures` (its plan, handed over as a non-enumerable `plan`; the closure log line and `learned_live` rows are unchanged), one call per item on each verdict change, through the existing `AGENTS` binding (`kernelSyscall`, newly exported from `src/platform-reports.mjs` with its backoff helpers). Order: the final verdicts of `learned_live` (oldest first), then the items still collecting (at most once a UTC day each, with rows and scored), then `not_tested` once per pack text item no shadow call can test (moderator only, Omega's audience, the 4H chart only). The first backlog: `k-role-chronos-matrix-calibration-talk@1` `not_better`, the 10 collecting items, then the untestable pack items.
  - **Mapping.** A KA1 closure posts its token as the verdict (`not_better`, `holdout_failed`, `worse`), with the test in `evidence.rule` (`sign_holm.better`, `holdout.no_worse`, `sign_holm.worse`; for `worse` the reverse test's p in `p_value`/`p_holm`); `retired` means a live note that left; `inconclusive` is unused. The payload is checked against the platform's schema before every call (no other key, no null, no price, no run of 3 digits in `rule`).
  - **Errors and marker.** D1 `knowledge_verdict_posts` and `knowledge_verdict_strikes` (ensureSchema, no migration). `knowledge_proposal_not_found`: marked, never retried. Not yet registered: the run stops, nothing marked, retried next run (one log line, no Telegram). A denial: 55-minute backoff in `platform_backoff`, then one whoami. Other failures: at most 5 attempts. At most 12 calls a run and 3 refusals or failures an hour.

  - **The check.** `guardrailsFor` adds `zone_ahead` after `reward` (`src/zone-types.mjs` `zoneAheadCheck`). It reads the side's plan's `opposing_ahead` (Z0's `zoneAhead`). Room short of `ZONE_NEED` (max(1R, 0.75 ATR(1H))): 'trend' fails only when dir x trend < 0.15 (Z0's `would_block`), a with-trend plan passes with the note in the detail only and nothing in `cautions`; 'all' fails it. MS2's shadow modes run the same check and filter (reason `zone_ahead`), and a replay reproduces it from the record (Z2-14). Room tuning keeps the check as recorded.

Unset `ZONE_PRIORITY` reads "off". `ZONE_AHEAD_MODE` "all" is owner-set only and logs one `zone_ahead_mode_all` warning line per decision; "record" keeps only Z0's `would_block`; "off" or `KEY_LEVEL_ZONE_TYPES` "off": no check. Off, every output is byte-identical (Z2-3, Z2-15; Z0's and Z1a's pins hold unedited).

A6: the lean veto holds only with at least 2 opposing agents that do not also speak for the side (the lean itself unchanged). A7: the arm veto ignores an armed agent that also speaks for the side. On SV1a's 54 blocks: A6 releases exactly its 19, A7 exactly its 11 (test/agent-veto.test.mjs, fixtures from the study).

The owner's force (KV `agent-veto:owner:v1`, owner-only `POST /omega/api/strategy-council` `veto_rule`) wins. Status in `GET /api/strategy-agents` `veto_rule`.

- feat(key-levels): demand/supply zone types, strength and freshness, recorded only, with no behaviour change (Z0; owner 2026-11-23, "Langsung prioritaskan"; orchestrator brief, after MS2). `src/zone-types.mjs` (new, pure, with `.d.mts`); docs/RFC_DEMAND_SUPPLY_ZONES.md (new).
  - **Typing.**
    - `buildKeyLevels` overlays `z.ds = { type, strength, score, freshness, touches, sources }` on each zone near price, after `limitCandidates`.
    - **Sources:** whale zones (0.9 active, 0.7 mitigation), the best Prophet order blocks (0.7), the GABFX RBS/SBR (1.0) and QM (0.9) points, and the 15m GMI flags (0.7, only at price), each times `TF_WEIGHT`.
    - **Rule:** strict overlap, the heaviest box per (source, timeframe), 2x dominance; strong >= 2.0, medium >= 1.0.
    - **Freshness:** touches on the 40 closed 15m bars before the decision bar (10 hours for every zone timeframe), within 0.15 ATR(1H), at least 3 bars apart: fresh, tested, worn.
    - Zones, candidates, scores, labels, members and roles are unchanged.
  - **Recorded.**
    - `decision.key_levels` (`keyLevelsAround`, now `aroundSet`, the same rule) adds `zone_type`, `zone_strength`, `zone_freshness` and `zone_rank` beside low and high.
    - Plans add `zone_types { entry, stop, tp1, tp2 }` (`tradeLevels`, `targetsAhead`, `finishPlan`; `zoneLevels` passes `ds`).
    - Plans also add `opposing_ahead { o_type, o_strength, o_freshness, o_rank, room_atr, room_r, need_r, trend_lean, with_trend, would_block }`, from `zoneAhead`, last, on the final entry and stop. It reads the recorded set only, with `ZONE_NEED` = max(1R, 0.75 ATR) and the trend exemption at 0.15.
    - `slimPlan`, `tradeFor` and MS2's shadow trade copy both fields when present.
    - `plan_setups` adds `entry_zone_type`, `stop_zone_type`, `tp1_zone_type` and `opposing_room_atr` (ALTERs in `ensurePlanLearningSchema`). `decisionPlans` and `setupsFrom` carry them for live and shadow setups.

Off is main 74be83c byte for byte: test/zone-types.test.mjs Z0-9 pins the strategy, guardrails, decision record, council, EA signal, public output and plan_setups rows.
  - **Untouched.** `managementFields`, the council's evidence and messages, the guardrails' checks, `pickStrategy`, Omega, the Position Manager, `publicAnalysis` and `publicRecap` are byte-identical with it on.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` (c4aa5772…) and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` (ecfe3f90…) unchanged, as is ERA_SWITCHES; no era moves. MS2's MSH-15 holds unedited (its fixture's zones are untyped).
  - **Replay** (production D1, SELECT only; 245 hourly captures; the final rule without the pattern levels):
    - 34% of the recorded zones are decisive;
    - decisive zones held 63% fresh, 51% tested and 39% worn (supply 53%, demand 28% in a SELL-heavy fortnight);
    - `would_block` fired on 27% of both-side plans (0 with the trend) and on 14 of 421 live BUY/SELL decisions' sides (3.3%).
  - **Tests.** test/zone-types.test.mjs Z0-1 to Z0-17; test/chart-patterns-mode.test.mjs PM3 and PM6 add `zoneTypes` to the modes.
- feat(mode): shadow per-mode evidence and a probation switch (MS2; orchestrator brief 2026-11-23, after MS1). The daily mode selector only had evidence for the mode that traded; now every mode collects it at every 15m decision.
  - **Shadow pass.** After the live record is stored, `runDecisionEngine` runs `shadowModeDecisions` (`src/decision-engine.mjs`) for each mode not in force: the deterministic part only, with the live witnesses, quorum and market (`assess`'s new `witnesses` option) and the live council's answer; the mode's geometry (scalp, `SCALP_TARGET`, TP1-KZ), `modeThresholds(m, knobs)`, room (`activeRoom(env, m)`), vetoes and agent selection, through `decide()`. The side is the council's BUY or SELL; on its WAIT a side the mode opens that the live gates had closed (a side the live gates had open and the council declined stays WAIT; both open: the quorum's lean); no answer: the live fallback. Range only while the 1H regime ranges and only from `RANGE_GATED_AGENTS`. A mode that would trade writes D1 `mode_shadow_plans` (`src/mode-shadow.mjs`; `migrations/0026_mode_shadow_plans.sql` records it). No model call, no room, no price in a room; the decision record is byte-identical.
  - **Setups.** `plan_setups.source` ('live' | 'shadow') and `plan_setups.rule_version` ("tp1_kz:on" | "tp1_kz:off") by the run-time ALTER pattern (MS2 prepares rule_version: TP1-KZ did not write it). Live rows take the rule in force at the plan learner's run; rows of before MS2 stay NULL (not counted). `runPlanLearning` derives shadow setups per mode with the live rules (`shadowSetupsFrom`, key `shadow:<mode>:…`), replayed in their own queue on the control arm only. `ensurePlanLearningSchema` caches per database.
  - **Readers.** Every `plan_setups` reader filters `source = 'live'` (the learner, referee, agents' records, range cells and counts; grid-adapt; the strategy council's walk-forward; the CFC pack and triggers; the council knob tuning; the recommendation cards). The mode selector alone reads shadow: per mode live ∪ shadow of the rule in force, one per bar and agent with live first (`unionSetups`), range's cells from both (`rangeShadowEvidence`); the gate, margin, streak and council unchanged; `n_live`/`n_shadow` in the outcome and change rows.
  - **Probation.** A selector switch to a non-baseline mode with fewer than 31 live setups (`MODE_PROBATION_RULES`) is stored in `trading:mode:control:v1` (`probation`). Omega's new entries of that mode at 0.5 of the risk (`modeProbationRisk` in the sync, `planIntents` `modeRisk`; the hard cap not scaled, open positions untouched, the smaller scale with the reversal probation; intent `mode_probation`). It fails back to the baseline (`probation_failed`, the existing change path) at 15 live setups since it started with a mean under 0R or a walk-forward fail, and passes at 31 live setups and a walk-forward pass (`mode_probation_passed`, one line). Owner pin and veto clear it; Auto keeps it.
  - **Retention.** `mode_shadow_plans` rows older than 90 days by `bar_close` are dropped in the cron's existing retention line beside `pruneShadowKnowledge` and `pruneModelArena` (`pruneModeShadowPlans`: `DELETE … WHERE rowid IN (SELECT rowid … WHERE bar_close < ? ORDER BY bar_close LIMIT 5000)`; orchestrator ruling 4). `plan_setups` shadow rows are not pruned: no sweep prunes `plan_setups` by age (a follow-up).
  - **Status.** `GET /api/trading-mode` adds `selector` (`modeEvidenceStatus`: per mode and 1H regime, live against shadow, and the probation); the other fields are unchanged; `modeStatus` adds `probation`.
  - **Switches.** `MODE_SHADOW_EVIDENCE` and `MODE_PROBATION` ("on"; wrangler.jsonc vars). Both "off": the decision record, the plan setups and state and the selector's outcome hash the same as on main fdc6c38 (MSH-15, after SS1, B15, B16 and V3L).
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` unchanged (no fixture carries `modeRisk`); `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 7b23836a… → c4aa5772… (planIntents, on top of SS1); not an Omega setting, not in `ERA_SWITCHES`; v4 counts each trade in its own R, so parity is unaffected.
  - **Cost.** The shadow pass measured 3-4 ms per decision in node (about 1 ms per mode; the live `assess` about 1.7 ms), three room reads and one D1 batch.
  - **Tests.** test/mode-shadow.test.mjs MSH-1 to MSH-19 (rows per mode, the range rule, the council's stance, the learner's shadow setups, one reader test each, the rule_version cut, a council switch on shadow evidence, probation fail/pass/scale/veto, range cells, off byte-identical, lens-steps `modeNow`, the CPU measure, the migration, MSH-19 the retention); test/mode-selector.test.mjs and test/mode-carry.test.mjs seed the new columns and expect the split.

- refactor(nexus): less cosmetic text on the Omega chart (owner 2026-11-23: "yang diminta adalah pengurangan kosmetik text di chart bukan mengubah layout sebelumnya"; less cosmetic text on the chart, not a new layout; then, on the before/after: "Bid ask sudah ada di side panel, tidak perlu lagi text di chart. Tidak usah juga pakai kata kata broker karena sumber data omega memang dari broker"). `pages/Omega.tsx` `Mt5ChartCanvas`, `lib/entryZone.ts`. Text only: the page's layout, every line, its colour and style, and the MT5-to-feed conversion are as before; no API, engine, D1 or Worker change.
  - **Labels.** One short label per line (`CHART_LABEL`), its price only on the axis tag: "Limit", "Zone", "Plan", "Plan SL", "Plan TP", and each position's "BUY 0.10", "SL", "TP" (`positionLines`; no ticket). The bid and ask lines carry no text (the side panel's Market Watch shows them): with a known basis converted and without an axis tag, as before (the bid's feed price is the last price's tag); without one at MT5's prices with their axis tags, now without a title. Gone: "bid broker X · feed Y", "LIMIT · broker X (feed Y)", "Entry zone · broker A–B (feed C–D)" and its phone form, "#ticket … · broker X · feed Y", "BID broker", "PLAN BUY", "PLAN TP1" (`quoteTitle`, `limitTitle` and `chartZoneLabel` removed; nothing else used them).

Gone: the two caption lines of prose, the "feed" tag over the price axis, the overlay's "feed" after the OHLC, the bars' tick volume (the histogram shows it) and the overlay's quote line ("Bid X (feed Y) / Ask X (feed Y) · broker", the spread; the side panel has them). The overlay keeps the symbol, timeframe and server and the OHLC; the time axis keeps its "GMT+7".

- feat(ledger): the Signal Ledger's Omega v3 follows the limit entry (V3L; owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "Make v3 follow the limit entry (Rekomendasi)"). `src/ledger-v3-limit.mjs`, `src/signal-ledger.mjs` (`simulateOmega`'s `entry`, `evaluateOmega`, `recomputeV3LimitOnce`, `blocksNewDecision`/`omegaEntryWindowEnd`, `readLedger`'s `parity_v3`), `src/ledger-omega-limit.mjs` (`limitFillPrice` exported, the counterfactual unchanged). The ledger only: no live trading change, `planIntents` untouched, no switch, no migration.
  - **Why.** OG1: v3 matched the live account's trade/no-trade on 4 of today's 15 decisions (the limit counterfactual on 11): v3 still entered at market while live Omega has entered by limit since 2026-11-20.
  - **Which entry.** The Omega setting `entry_order` in force at each decision, from the settings' audited history (`omega_events` `settings`, each save's whole settings; never the actor), the stored settings after the last save, market before any save off market. "market": v3 byte for byte (no version key). "limit" / "council": rows tagged `omega_json.version` `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v3.1-limit` with `entry_order` and `limit` (placement time, wait, distance in R, basis; no price).
  - **Rule.** The live entry council's recorded answer (`limit_council` kind `entry`, as v4 reads the Position Council's): under limit anything but `place_limit` is `council_skip`, no answer places (the mechanical fallback); under council a skip skips, `place_limit` rests, `enter_market` or silence is v3's market entry. Then `limitPlacement` (band, EZ1, EZ1b with the decision's `entry_zone_mode`, `zone_*`, `limit_through_price`, `limit_out_of_band`) at each 1m open while the signal is live and not before the council's answer; resting, the counterfactual's `limitCancelReason` (`pending_expired`, `decision_against`, `quorum_share_drop`) and SS1's `superseded_by_signal`; the fill by `limitFillPrice`; then v3's own management from the fill, R on the risk from the fill to the placed stop; the account gate at the fill. Unfilled rows are skipped with that code.
  - **Only the rules live had** (orchestrator ruling on the first report). One table, `V3L_RULES_FROM`: EZ1 from 2026-11-23 04:05:15Z and EZ1b from 07:57:38Z (the AI Worker's "Deploy Worker" step of #533 3c301ff and #534 8edcd37), each read at the placement attempt's minute; SS1 from its live deploy 15:43:27Z, read where it acts (the bar a newer signal ends the order, the new decision's hold-back); `entry_order` from the settings' history. On the recompute window they change no row (no zone before 04:05, no edge before 07:57, no supersede case before 15:43).
  - **Hold-back.** Under a limit entry every entry window is the mode's `max_pending_life`; with `limit_supersede` on and SS1 live at the decision a row Omega has not filled no longer holds a newer same-side decision back (it is superseded, as live). LH1's rule is unchanged under market.
  - **Recompute, once.** `recomputeV3LimitOnce` from `runSignalLedger` (KV `ledger:v3l:recompute:v1`): the decision rows since the first save off market, 2026-11-20 11:46:03 UTC (`council`; `limit` from 13:40:51, `council` 13:43:53, `limit` from 16:29:22 on), oldest first, at most 400 a run, the D1 1m archive before the KV 1m series; each row's earlier v3 values in `omega_json.prev` (never overwritten, never taken twice); the marker keeps the before/after counts.
  - **Parity.** `GET /api/ledger` `parity_v3`: per UTC day, v3 traded against an Omega entry filled live (`limit_filled`, or a done `open:` intent with a ticket). On today's fixture: 4/15 before, 14/15 after (the one left is the 14:15 BUY live skipped behind the 14:00 BUY's resting limit, the SS1 case).
  - **Unchanged.** PUB-V3 still reads v3 (`signal_ledger` r_omega × omega_size): the same text and `replay_check { status: "not_required", shown: true }`, its numbers from the recomputed rows. v4, the limit counterfactual, the TP policy, the execution report's shape. No EA, cron, Pine or UI change; no `tp2`/`extended_tp` key.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` and `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` unchanged (V13, V14 green): v3 is the ledger; `limitFillPrice` is the counterfactual's own fill moved into a function (its outputs, in the fingerprint's `ss/` group, byte for byte); `src/limit-supersede.mjs` only its header comment.
  - **Tests.** test/ledger-v3-limit.test.mjs V3L-1 to V3L-9 over today's read-only fixture (`test/fixtures/ledger-v3-limit/2026-11-23.json`, every price shifted by a constant; `test/support/ledger-v3-limit-fixture.mjs`): the timeline; 14/15 agreement (≥ 11); market byte for byte against main's code and production's stored v3; the recompute (only since the date, prev kept, once, bounded, idempotent); the unfilled codes and SS1; the fill and R; the hold-back; the parity and its D1 reads; PUB-V3; the rules' dates (V3L-10).
- fix(ledger): the limit counterfactual reads the decision's EZ1b switch (found by V3L, 2026-11-23). v4's `DECISION_SQL` does not extract `entry_zone_mode`, so `eaSignalFromDecision` never set `entry_zone_edge` and the counterfactual never rested a limit at the zone's near edge as the live `limitPlacement` has since EZ1b. `src/ledger-omega-limit.mjs`: `LIMIT_DECISION_SQL` (v4's query plus the column) for its own read, `LIMIT_ZONE_MODE_SQL` joined by decision key (`withZoneModes`) when the steady state reuses v4's decisions. v4's `DECISION_SQL`, its records and both engine hashes unchanged (V13, V14). Rows already in `ledger_omega4` are not replayed again; on today's fixture replayed locally the counterfactual changes on one decision, the 14:45 BUY: `limit_out_of_band` → filled at the zone's top, +0.09R (live's council skipped it). - feat(decision): LIQUIDITY, lens B's council-only card (B15, Track B; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md §5.2). `src/decision-engine.mjs` `liquidityCard`, `withLiquidity`, `liquidityQuorum`, live on merge behind `LIQUIDITY_WITNESS` ("on"; "off": no card, no `decision.liquidity`, the council's messages byte for byte as before).
  - **Council only.** The council reads one more card beside the witnesses, `LIQUIDITY` with `counts: false`, and one prompt sentence (`LIQUIDITY_PROMPT`). `quorumOf` counts every card it gets and `decision.witnesses` is the cards, so the card joins neither: the quorum, the guardrails, the power, `pickStrategy`, the witnesses and the witness weights are unchanged. No key in `src/witness-weighting.mjs` (`WITNESS_KEYS`, `WITNESS_PRIORS`) or `src/witness-tuning.mjs` (`WITNESS_KEYS`); only the council's local `weighed` list gains `LIQUIDITY`. No canon change.
  - **Weight.** `LIQUIDITY_WITNESS` {share 0.1, cap 0.06, lean 0.6}: w = min(0.06, 0.1 × lens B's council weight in force, `liquidity.council_weight_<mode>` from `lensKnobs`; anchors 0.04/0.03/0.02/0.03). No card while lens B is off, floored (`active` false) or its state stale or unavailable (`liquidityEvidence`, 15 minutes).
  - **Lean.** ±0.6 only from a reclaimed sweep at most 15 minutes old at the decision (the facts' `age_min` plus the state's age, `sweepFor`'s rule) when another counting witness leans the same way by at least 0.15; else 0, with `lean_reason`.
