- feat(omega): a newer same-side signal supersedes a resting, unfilled Omega limit (SS1; owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "Ya, sinyal baru menggantikan (Rekomendasi)"; live on merge). `src/limit-supersede.mjs`, behind the Omega setting `limit_supersede` ("on" by default; "off" is the engine before it byte for byte; the owner's switch only, Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; in `ERA_SWITCHES`; labelled in the change audit; no environment variable).
  - **Why.** At 14:10:54Z the 14:00 BUY (breakout_retest) rested a BUY_LIMIT at its zone's edge (intent `open:1795442400000:B`, 30 minutes) that never filled. At 14:17:23Z the 14:15 BUY (zone_pullback, price inside its new zone) was skipped `same_side_unprotected` because the resting limit counted as an unprotected position on the side; price then ran up. v3 and v4 filled the 14:15 BUY.
  - **Rule.** `planIntents`, under `entry_order` limit or council, after the deterministic cancels: an Omega pending limit on the current signal's side whose placing signal is older (`place_limit`'s `signal_issued_ms`, else the order comment `open:<issued>:<B|S>`; unknown: never) is deleted, `delete_order` reason `superseded_by_signal` (`del:<ticket>:<minute>`, with `side`, `signal_issued_ms`, `superseded_by`). It counts in none of the new signal's gates (`same_side_unprotected`, `max_positions`, `platform_cap`, `pending_order_exists`, the budget's pending risk); its risk still counts in the fill guard and A8's budget until gone; a council action on it is dropped. The new signal goes through every gate in the same plan (the entry council, the band, EZ1/EZ1b, `zone_*`, the caps).
  - **Unchanged.** Open positions (a filled limit included) keep `same_side_unprotected`; the other side's resting limit keeps `pending_order_exists`, the same signal's `already_opened`; market entries delete every limit as before; kill, pause, `tick_value_mismatch`, `volume_below_min`, the profile caps and the daily loss limit apply unchanged. No EA change (OmegaExecutor 1.21's `delete_order`), no cron change, no `tp2`/`extended_tp` key.
  - **Refused.** A new signal refused (council skip or silence, out of band, expired, …) still cancels the old limit; the note `superseded_by_signal` names the ticket and both signals and ends with the new signal's refusal code.
  - **Delete first.** No entry while a superseded order is still in the terminal's report (the delete not done, or refused: sent again a minute later) or a `superseded_by_signal` delete of the last 10 minutes is unanswered (`omega_intents` pending or sent, read by `limitInputs` only with the switch on; a failed read holds): note `superseded_delete_waiting`; the next sync places it.
  - **Ledgers.** The limit counterfactual beside v4 (`limitEntryPolicy.atOpen`, now handed the minute's signal) ends an older signal's resting order when the current signal is on its side (missed, `superseded_by_signal`, in `LIMIT_MISSED_REASONS` and the rules text) and places the new one in that minute. v4 is the market replay (no resting order): its records are the same on and off. v3: `blocksNewDecision` (LH1) needed no change: v3's Omega entry ends by the next 15m close, so an older row is skipped (no hold) or an open position (held while unprotected) by a newer decision; only a row not yet simulated keeps LH1's hold inside its window.
  - **SSOT.** The stage 3 limit entries (the new SS1 entry, placement's `pending_order_exists`), stage 1's counting, the plan's same-side guard and the switch signature. RFC: docs/RFC_OMEGA_LIMIT_ENTRY.md §6 and §8.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` 39c10287… → ecfe3f90… (a recorded exception, a new parity era at the deploy: the fingerprint's new group `ss/`, planIntents under limit and council and the limit counterfactual over today's two BUYs, written with the switch on; the market fixtures alone cannot move it); with `limit_supersede` "off" on every input the fixtures give 39c10287… byte for byte (`engineBehaviourSource({ limitSupersede: "off" })`). `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 38aa8e6f… → 7b23836a… (`src/limit-supersede.mjs`, `planIntents`, `settingsFrom`, `OMEGA_DEFAULTS`, `limitInputs`, `replayOmega4`'s hook call).
  - **Tests.** test/limit-supersede.test.mjs SS1-1 to SS1-10 (the switch, validation, era signature and audit label; today's 14:00 and 14:15 BUYs, off as the incident and on deleting then placing in the zone; each refusal; the open position, the other side and the same signal; a refused and an unanswered delete, then the place, under limit and council; the sync's read of the deletes, fail closed, and Aether's policy keeping "off"; the limit counterfactual and v4; v3's `blocksNewDecision`; off byte for byte against main's code over the same cases; the hashes). V14 lists `limit_supersede` and the new records line; the old-hash checks add `limitSupersede: "off"`: reentry R13, pm-fast F10, change-thresholds CH1-12, profit-lock-gate SLG1-15.
- fix(nexus): the War Room chart's clear-decision check reads the Decision Engine's stance (the orchestrator's #547 review, 2026-11-23). UI and tests only.
  - **Bug.** `PriceChart.tsx` read the engine's stance from `global.engine`, which the Global Conclusion never carries (the engine is the body's top-level `engine`, useMarket's `engine`). So an engine BUY or SELL over a strategy WAIT never counted as a clear decision for the plan lines or the "plan hidden" note.
  - **Fix.** `lib/entryZone.ts` `clearDecision(strategy, engine)`: the strategy's BUY or SELL, or the engine's BUY or SELL stance. PriceChart uses it for the plan lines, the Entry band and the plan-hidden note, and the plan effect re-runs when the engine changes.
  - **One source.** `chartPlan(strategy, engine)` gives the plan lines the Entry band's and the Decision card's source (bandSource): the engine's trade (its `rrr_all` per target) when the engine is on, else the strategy plan; none with the engine on and no trade. So an engine BUY over a strategy WAIT draws the engine's levels, never a null, stale or opposite strategy plan.
  - **Tests.** test/nexus-clear-decision.test.mjs CD1 to CD3; PS22's effect-dependency pattern gains `engine`.

The chart's Entry band (zones-primitive, anchored label "Entry zone A–B · feed scale"), dimmed and dropped with the plan lines while the decision is stale; "feed" over the price axis, the time axis and the crosshair in GMT+7, the footer "· feed scale" and the last bar in UTC and GMT+7. The band and the card read the same plan (`lib/entryZone.ts` `bandSource`): the engine's trade when the engine is on, else the strategy plan. With no zone fields (or low ≥ high) nothing new is drawn: no item, no note, today's Entry row.
  - **Omega page (broker scale).** The Signal card labels every price (Entry, Stop, Target, Feed price: feed), adds the entry zone (feed), "Near edge on/off · EZ1b · entry_zone_edge" (a boolean: only `true` is on) and "Basis +0.420 · broker − feed", Issued and Expires in UTC and GMT+7. OmegaWhy has wording for the five codes: `limit_in_zone` and `limit_zone_edge` an amber adjust, `zone_passed`, `zone_tight` and `zone_rr` a red why under the amber NOT ENTERING; formatters for `limit_in_zone`'s "a -> b" and `limit_zone_edge`'s JSON detail, both labelled broker; the refusals keep the code's own text with "· broker"; "since" in UTC and GMT+7. The chart keeps the feed's candles (/api/ohlcv) and draws the broker's zone (the resting limit's intent `entry_zone`, else the signal's), the resting limit and the bid and ask converted to the feed (broker − `signal.feed_basis`, only with `feed_basis_known`), labelled "Entry zone · broker A–B (feed C–D)", "LIMIT · broker X (feed Y)", "bid broker X · feed Y"; the broker bid painted into the last candle is converted too, and not painted without a basis (the bid and ask are then drawn at the broker's prices, marked "broker"). The open positions' entry, SL and TP lines are converted the same way (`positionLines`), titled "#ticket BUY 0.10 · broker X · feed Y" ("#ticket SL …", "#ticket TP …"), and without a basis drawn at the broker's prices titled "… · broker"; every conversion rounds to the symbol's digits. Everything else on that chart is the feed's (the candles, the PLAN lines, the key-level blocks, the last price). The bid/ask box shows the feed prices and "· broker" as a line of the chart's top-left box; a footer names each scale and the basis. The intents' Entry, SL and TP columns say broker, Created shows UTC and GMT+7, the Entry cell how the limit came to rest ("in zone · from …", "near edge · from …") and its zone; a phone shows one card per intent.
  - **Files.** `ui/src/lib/entryZone.ts` (pure helpers), `components/PlanLadder.tsx` (the ladder and tradePlan moved from GlobalDecision.tsx, with the zone pieces and `DecisionPlanView`), `components/OmegaZone.tsx` (`SignalRows`, `IntentEntry`, `IntentCards`, `OmegaSignalView`); GlobalDecision.tsx, PriceChart.tsx, OmegaWhy.tsx, pages/Omega.tsx.
  - **Render check.** `scripts/render-entry.tsx` adds eleven EZ1 views over `test/fixtures/nexus-ez1/conclusion-global.json` and `omega-overview.json` (invented sample prices): the zone with the engine on, stale, the engine off (the plan's band), no zone fields, the Signal card with and without a zone, and each of the five codes; `render-check.mjs` also fails on an entry's `hides` text.

- fix(ledger): the no-stacking hold-back follows Omega (LH1; orchestrator 2026-11-23 after the owner's report that Performance did not update; owner: "Ya, masukkan 6" for the re-ingest). `src/signal-ledger.mjs` `blocksNewDecision`, `omegaEntryWindowEnd`, `reingestHeldOnce`.
  - **Why.** The 04:15 BUY row (pattern_peaks) waited 8 hours for its TP-policy fill (04:17 to 12:17, then cancelled/unfilled) after Omega's side of it had closed at 05:55. The hold-back read the TP-policy status, so it held back every BUY decision meanwhile: 06:15, 07:00, 08:15, 08:45, 09:30 and 12:00. Omega traded the 08:45 BUY live (+16.6 pips), and the ledger, the v3 performance, the public record and the Nexus Performance page had no row for it.
  - **Rule.** A same-side row holds a new decision back only while Omega would, read from its times at the new decision: Omega skipped or closed holds nothing; Omega filled and open holds while unprotected (`omega_json.protected_at`); Omega not filled holds only inside its entry window (an `ENTER_LIMIT` plan the mode's `max_pending_life`, scalp 30 minutes; a market entry until the next close or `max_signal_age`); a TP-policy row already open holds while it has neither TP1 nor SL+; a row not simulated yet, or from before the Omega policy, keeps today's rule. The TP-policy simulation is unchanged. The held reason for a pending row reads "... (inside Omega's entry window)".
  - **Re-ingest, once.** The first ledger run after the deploy re-ingests every decision the old rule held on 2026-11-23 (00:00 to 23:59:59 UTC: the old rule kept holding until this deploy; orchestrator), oldest first, each from its own `engine_decisions.decision_json` through `ingestDecision` (decided_at kept, INSERT OR IGNORE by id), simulating the open rows after each insert so the next one sees the fills and exits as live would have. Re-ingested ones leave `signal_ledger_held`; one the new rule still holds keeps a new trace. The KV marker `ledger:lh1:reingest:2026-11-23:v1` keeps the report (decision, side, re-ingested or why not).
  - **Unchanged.** No Omega engine change (V13, V14 green); no switch; the TP-policy and Omega simulations as before; no cron change.
  - **Tests.** test/ledger-hold-lh1.test.mjs LH1-1 to LH1-6 (today's 04:15 row and the six times; inside and past the 30-minute window; a market entry; an open unprotected row and one whose Omega side closed; the pre-Omega rule; ingestDecision on today's sequence; the one-shot re-ingest, in order, once, with a still-held decision). O8 and the Decision Engine's ledger test pass unchanged.

Read-only; no engine change: `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` and `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` are unchanged.
  - **The contract.** `test/fixtures/nexus-intel/chart-patterns.json` is the Nexus Chart patterns page's (C3) binding contract (the seeded view with `?history=24`). Keys: `mode`, `calibration_mode`, `scale` {`prices` "feed", `symbol`}, `price`, `atr_h1`, `decision` {`decision_key`, `bar_close`, `trade_agent`, `council_strategy`}, `charts` {`15`, `60`, `240`, `D`: `label`, `interval_s`, `ohlcv` (60 bars)}, `patterns` [the `detectPatterns` fields with `far_target`, `target_up`/`target_down`, `line_from`/`line_to`, `upper_from`/`lower_from`, `event`, `held`, `breakout_bars_ago`, `bars_since_breakout`, `horizon_bars`, `bars_left`, `record` {`chart`, `pooled`}, `zone`, `plan` {`agent`, `side`, `status`, `at_level`, `entry`, `entry_low`, `entry_high` (EZ1, null without a zone), `stop`, `tp1`, `tp2`, `rrr`, `far_target`, `reach`}], `far_targets`, `calibration` {`version`, `computed_at`, `base_rate`, `resolved`, `pending`, `patterns`, `by_tf`, `open`}, `seat` {`rooms` {M15, H1, H4, D1}, `judged_on`, `horizon_bars`, `scoring`}, `history`. A10/A12's `held`, `breakout_bars_ago`, `bars_since_breakout`, `horizon_bars`, `bars_left` and `seat.horizon_bars`, and a later PR's `plan.reach`, are null until they merge (explicit null, so the page reads one shape).
  - **TP1-KZ2.** A pattern's measured target is only the far target: `far_target` (`detectPatterns`' `target`, renamed), `target_up`/`target_down`, `far_targets`, and `far_target` on the calibration's open breakouts; no price key is called `target` (the only `target` keys are the calibration's counts). A pattern agent's `plan` keeps the plan's own `tp1` (the key zone) and `tp2`.
  - **Prices, Nexus only.** Behind Cloudflare Access and proxied by Nexus like every `GET /api/*` (no-store; POST 405). Every price is the feed's (TradingView OANDA:XAUUSD closed bars): no broker price, no scale conversion. No account number, login, server or identity. Nothing reaches a room, a decision card, Aether or a public page.
  - **Reads.** The strategy memo (`readStrategyMemo`: `key_levels.patterns` and zones, price, ATR(1H)); without it the patterns are detected on the same bars (no event patterns). KV: the feed's 15m/1h/4h/1D series, `chart-patterns:calibration:v1` (`readPatternCalibration`), `decision-engine:latest`. D1 SELECTs: `engine_decisions` (the decision when KV has none, in try/catch, and `?history` through `json_extract`), `outcome_scores` (the Projection Agent's seat, `agent:projection`, 30 days, this scoring version). Never `readLatestDecision`, `readDecisions`, `readAllocationEvents` or `scorecards` (DDL). `?history=N` is 0 by default, at most 24. A D1 error answers 500 `chart_patterns_unavailable`.
  - **Smoke.** `deploy-nexus-dashboard.yml` checks `/api/chart-patterns` as it checks `/api/liquidity` (the Access redirect without the token; 200 JSON no-store and the schema with it).
  - **Tests.** `test/chart-patterns-view.test.mjs` (CPV1-CPV9: the fixture's keys and types against the seeded view, the measured target only as the far target, `CHART_PATTERNS_MODE` off gives no patterns (and the calibration switches), no DDL on a cold KV and D1 (a D1 refusing CREATE/ALTER/INSERT/UPDATE) with the memo stubbed, no account/login/server/identity key, the seat, `?history`, the pure parts, and the route through the AI Worker and Nexus's proxy: 200 no-store, POST 405, off 404, 500); seed `test/support/chart-patterns-view-seed.mjs`.
- feat(strategy): TP1 at the key zone for the agents with their own targets too (TP1-KZ2; owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "setuju", to its proposal that TP1-KZ applies to the pattern agents). `src/strategy-agents.mjs` `ownTargetsUnderKeyZone`, used by `finishPlan` under `TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` "on" (the default).
  - **Why.** The 12:00Z decision's pattern_lines BUY (a 1H symmetrical triangle, scalp) took the pattern's measured target as TP1, about 12.7 ATR(1H) away (50.72R): TP1-KZ's floor applied only to the plans built from the key levels.
  - **Rule.** range_fade, the eight pattern families and pattern_inside take TP1 as the default agent does: the nearest key zone beyond the gap, never jumped, at least `TP1_MIN_R` (1R), else no plan (LOW_REWARD); without a key zone ahead, the nearest level, else the 2R projection, still at least 1R.
  - **TP2.** A pattern family's measured target becomes TP2 when it lies beyond the new TP1; else the agent's own TP2 when it is not short of TP1; else the key zones' second target. A measured target left short of TP1 stays named in TP2's note. `trade.tp2` only: no new EA or signal key, no `extended_tp`. `rrr` reads the new TP1, `rrr_all` both.
  - **Gates.** `agentPlanPasses` and the RR gates read the new targets unchanged, so an agent's RRR is honest.
  - **Switch.** `TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` "off" restores every agent's own targets, byte for byte (TK5's sample hash unchanged; the marker `measured` never reaches a plan).
  - **Hashes.** No Omega engine change: `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` and `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` unchanged (V13, V14 green).
  - **Tests.** test/tp1-key-zone-agents.test.mjs KZ2-1 to KZ2-5 (the 12:00Z case: off reproduces the recorded plan, on takes TP1 at a key zone and the measured target as TP2; a key zone under 1R gives no plan; pattern_peaks the same; the default agent unchanged; off byte-identical). TK4 now pins the new rule for an own-target agent; the Projection Agent council test checks both switch states.
  - **SSOT.** The guardrails' TP1-KZ paragraph.
- feat(pm): a 1m re-entry after a profit-lock stop (RE1; owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "Live: gerbang + re-entry (Rekomendasi)"; SLG1, the gate, went first). `src/reentry-1m.mjs`, live on merge behind the Omega setting `reentry_1m` ("on" by default; "off" is the engine before it byte for byte; the owner's switch only, Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; in `ERA_SWITCHES`; no environment variable; the brief's ERA_SWITCH `REENTRY_1M` follows the SLG1/CH1/PM-1M precedent). A re-entry is a new position, an open through every check of `planIntents`.
  - **When, without a cron.** Once per newly closed broker minute on Omega's bridge sync, before the plan (`runReentry`; the minute's trigger handed to every sync of that minute, looked for every 5 s until the bar arrives), only with the engine on, the terminal connected and no kill. The crons are unchanged.
  - **Eligible.** The latest Omega exit deal of the account in the last 240 minutes, closed by the broker's stop (`sl`), of an original `open:` entry, whose last applied Omega stop (the newest done `modify_sl`/`modify_sltp`) sat at or beyond the entry and came from a profit-lock rule (`break_even`, `ladder_lock_tp<n>`, `sl_key_level_<band>`, `pm_breakeven`, `pm_tp2_breakeven`, `pm_lock_tp1`, `pm_fast_tp`). Never the initial stop, a stop in loss or trailed (`trail_5m`, `pm_fast_cut`, a loosening), `pm_cut_loss` or `pm_take_profit_weakening` (they keep `reentryGate`, which binds a re-entry too), a re-entry's own exit or a delegated position.
  - **Valid, trigger, stop, target.** The latest decision on the same side; the original stop untouched and TP1 not reached since the exit; within 240 minutes of the plan's issue; once per setup; no other Omega entry since the exit. A closed broker 1m bar at least 5 bars after the exit's closes back beyond the lock (a SELL's ask, a BUY's bid). The stop at the pullback's extreme plus max(2 x spread, 0.1 x ATR1m(14)), skipped when wider than the original stop; the target the original TP1, at least 1R (TP1-KZ), else skipped; both checked again at the live price (`reentry_stop_wide`, `reentry_reward`, `reentry_stops_level`). At market, no 5m or adaptive stop. A re-entry the broker refuses does not use the setup's one, but every attempt counts: at most 3 open intents per setup whatever their status (then invalid, `attempts`, noted once), at least 5 minutes apart (orchestrator review of #543).
  - **Risk.** At most min(the profile's cap, the original trade's money risk), and at most 1.0R of the original over the setup (its net loss taken off): `sizeEntry`'s `maxRisk` (`lot_capped_reentry`, `reentry_risk_cap`). `PLATFORM_MAX_OPEN` applies to a re-entry in every entry order; the daily loss limit and budget, the profile's caps, `tick_value_mismatch`, `volume_below_min`, kill, pause, the engine switch and every other entry gate apply unchanged.
  - **Blocked.** The rollover 21:55-22:15 UTC, the reopen 23:00-23:15 UTC and 15 minutes after any daily or weekly reopen, the market paused, a reconnect batch, a stale stream, the warm-up (PM-1M's `fastNoise` with RE1's 15-minute reopen; its why text now names the window from the knob, "23:00-23:05" unchanged for PM-1M); the spread above 60 points or above 2 x the 60-minute median bar spread, the high-impact release window (noted once per setup and reason).
  - **Records, ids, lines.** Omega's open intent `re:<ticket>:<minute>` (reason `pm_reentry`, `stop_source` `reentry_1m`, `reentry_of`, `reentry_lock`, `reentry_rule`, the original's `signal_issued_ms`, mode and strategy); `position_actions` the same id, rule `pm_reentry`, action `open`, `applied`/`skipped` (with the plan's reason)/`noted`, never `pending`. One Telegram line per re-entry when the broker answers ("XAUUSD RE-ENTRY · #<ticket> <side> · …"), in place of the generic executed line (also when reconciled from the open position); no account number. `isEntryIntentId` makes a `re:` position Omega's own everywhere an `open:` one is read (`isOmegaOwned`, `positionAgents`, `positionMeta`, `pmLoosenInputs`, `reconcileFilledOpens`, the Position Manager's run, PM-1M's step).
  - **v4.** `replayOmega4` keeps the latest exit's setup from its own exits and last stop rule, runs `reentryCheck` at every minute after it on its 1m bars, plans a trigger even while flat, and records the re-entry as its own trade (`re:<decision key>`, `omega4_json.reentry`); the checkpoint carries the setup (`re_setup`). `omegaParity` pairs a live trade whose open re-entered with v4's `re:` row; `OMEGA4_RULES` says v4 has no reconnect quarantine or live spread for it.
  - **Simulation (orchestrator, SELECT-only reads and a 1m-bar simulation over 80 broker trades opened 2026-11-11 to 11-23).** 31 of 47 locks re-entered, 58% hit TP, +0.60R on their own risk, +10.5R in total. Confidence: the direction is strong (+0.25 to +0.33R, 3-4 standard errors, holding in both halves of the window), the exact parameters are weak (n 80, nearly all SELL scalps in one downtrend).
  - **SSOT.** The Position Manager section (the new RE1 entry), the same-side re-entry guard, the v4 Position Manager line and the switch signature. RFC: docs/RFC_PM_A8.md §9.

- feat(pm): a 1m fast-protect layer on the MT5 broker stream (PM-1M; owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "deploy semuanya live. Tf 1m diperlukan position manager untuk antisipasi TP lebih cepat dan mengurangi kerugian. Meski concern dengan noise perlu penanganan."). `src/pm-fast.mjs`, live on merge behind the Omega setting `pm_fast_1m` ("on" by default; "off" is the engine before it byte for byte; the owner's switch only, Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; in `ERA_SWITCHES`; no environment variable). A layer beside the 5-minute Position Manager, not a re-base: A8, `pmBand` and Omega's buffers keep their calibrations; no council.
  - **When, without a cron.** Once per newly closed broker minute on Omega's bridge sync (the full plan runs on every sync while a position is open), before the plan reads the pending actions (`runFastProtect`; the closed minute's bar looked for every 5 s until it arrives, one evaluation per minute per isolate, INSERT OR IGNORE across isolates), only with the engine on, the terminal connected and no kill. The crons are unchanged.
  - **The stream.** `broker_bars_1m`, the last 90 minutes, pinned by `account_ref`, source and digits (the liquidity lens's baseline key), never by `connection_version`; a SELL's bid bars plus each bar's spread.
  - **`pm_fast_tp`.** Within the mode's near zone of TP1 or the broker's target (0.25/0.3/0.3/0.4R scalp/intraday/range/swing), in profit, 2 of the last 3 closed 1m bars against it with a body of at least 0.5 x ATR1m(14): the stop to protect `lock_r` (0.6/0.5/0.5/0.4) of the open profit when the SLG1 profit-lock gate is open; before the gate the target nearer (the recent extreme less the spread), not a stop into profit.
  - **`pm_fast_cut`.** A 1m impulse (true range at least 2.0 x ATR1m) closing beyond the last 5m swing, the next bar not reclaiming it: at or below -0.5R an early close, else the stop to the 1m swing plus max(2 x spread, 0.25 x ATR1m), only tighter and never beyond the original stop; in profit it waits for the gate.
  - **Noise.** Inert: the rollover 21:55-22:15 UTC, the reopen 23:00-23:05 UTC and 5 minutes after any reopen, the market paused, 3 minutes after a reconnect batch (a bar received more than 60 s after its close), a latest bar older than 90 s, fewer than 20 contiguous bars since a gap. Held and recorded: the spread-spike deferral (up to 10 minutes from the spike's start, a fast close too), one fast action per position per 3 minutes, no other-trigger action within 10 minutes, a position the 5-minute Position Manager acted on in this 5m bar. One per-mode table, `PM_FAST_1M`.
  - **Records, Omega, lines.** `position_actions` id `pm1:<ticket>:<bar minute>`, rule `pm_fast_tp` or `pm_fast_cut`, bar the minute, the why with ATR1m and its source (`broker_m1`, v4 `feed_m1`), a held trigger `noted`. Omega's intent has the same id (`fastIntent` in `positionManagerIntents`: a close, a stop only tighter, a target only nearer, never with a stop; SLG1 drops a stop into profit before the gate); `actionIdOfIntent`, the sync's applied mapping and the expiry read `pm1:` ids. One Telegram line per action, the Position Manager's ("1m lock", "1m stop tightened", "1m TP nearer", "1m early close"), no account number.
  - **v4.** `replayOmega4` runs `fastProtect` at every minute with a position, after the Position Manager, on its 1m bars, with the rate limit, hysteresis and 5m-bar hold from the intents it planned; no reconnect quarantine or spread-spike hold (v4 has neither receipt times nor a live spread: an expected difference in `omegaParity`).
  - **SSOT.** The Position Manager section; and the liquidity lens's archive sentence now says the first one-time backfill did not copy the corpus live (every batch rolled back above D1's 100 bound parameters) and B2 (#530) backfills it.

- fix(mode): no env mutation; a recorded daily outcome; deterministic order (MS1; orchestrator brief, no owner decision needed). Off: revert the change (no runtime switch).
  - **No env mutation.** `runDecisionEngine` wrote `env.TRADING_MODE` (with `env.COUNCIL_KNOBS` and `env.ROOM_CORRIDOR_ATR`) on the isolate's env, so the mode read from KV `trading:mode` then read as the server's pin to later code in the same isolate: the ledger cron's `runModeSelector` returned `pinned_by_server` without running, `ownerModeAction`, `rangeExitTick` and `setTradingMode` refused, `readStrategy` kept the decision's mode and `GET /api/trading-mode` answered `pinned: true`. It now reads the mode in force (`tradingMode`: the configured var, else KV, else intraday), the knobs and the room once and runs the decision on a request copy `{ ...env, TRADING_MODE: mode, COUNCIL_KNOBS, ROOM_CORRIDOR_ATR }`; env is never written. Only the configured `TRADING_MODE` is a server pin. The selected mode is the same as before; only how it is carried changed. `readStrategy`'s mode is `strategyMode(env)` (the same rule, exported for the test).
  - **Daily outcome.** Every daily `runModeSelector` outcome is one Omega event (`omega_events`, actor `agent:mode`, action `mode_selector_run`, through the `log` the ledger cron's `modeSelectorTick` passes; no new table): `{ day, status, reason, action, mode, to, proposed, regime, evidence }` for `pinned_by_server`, `pinned`, `unchanged` (the step's `none` or a refused proposal), `changed` and `failed`; `evidence` per mode `{ n, mean_r, status, scope }` (`outcomeEvidence`, no price), read on a pinned day too. `fresh` records nothing. Best effort: a write that fails never changes the outcome. `mode_selector_run` is in the change audit's `NOT_A_CHANGE` (no Telegram line; a switch keeps its own `mode_changed`).
  - **Deterministic order.** `loadSetups` orders by `opened_at`, then `setup_key`, so the walk-forward's holdout is the same whatever order the rows were written in.
  - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` and `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` unchanged (no hashed function changed).
  - **Tests.** test/mode-carry.test.mjs MS1-1 to MS1-7: each reader in the decision gets the selected mode (modeThresholds, guardrailsFor, riskChecks, room-tuning `activeRoom`, `lensKnobs`) and env keeps its keys; after a decision `tradingMode`, `strategyMode` and the selector's paths (`runModeSelector`, `rangeExitTick`, `ownerModeAction`, `setTradingMode`) follow KV; the configured var still pins; every outcome's event, its evidence and the tick's `omega_events` row; the order. No existing test changed. No EA, TradingView or Pine change.

Without one the rules below run (under SLG1 the gate, `lock_tp1`, the break-even) and the why says "change threshold tpw held the close back".

The place_limit intent records `entry_zone` and `zone_from` (note `limit_in_zone`); a council move never leaves the zone (Omega's `limitActionIntents` and `pendingFacts`' levels); a conversion's market leg waits once the zone is passed. The Signal Ledger's limit counterfactual places as `zoneLimit` does. v4 (the market replay) is unchanged by the zone (EZ12). `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` refreshed (`signalEntryZone`, `zoneLimit`, `ZONE_MIN_RR`, `ZONE_MIN_RISK_SHARE`, `entryZoneFields` added); `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` unchanged. Switch `ENTRY_ZONE_MODE` (`on`; `off` byte-identical, EZ11 against main's hashed sample). Test: `test/entry-zone.test.mjs` EZ1-EZ12 (EZ5b: zone_tight at its boundary).
- feat(public): the public track record's managed exit from the Signal Ledger's own policy (PUB-V3; owner 2026-11-23: "yang ditampilkan di cta public harusnya omega (v3) bukan yang live omega v4"). `src/public-track-record.mjs`: with `PUBLIC_MANAGED_SOURCE` `v3` (default) the managed scenario reads `signal_ledger` (`source = 'decision'`, `omega_status = 'closed'`, `r_omega` sized by `omega_size` as `performance(items, "omega")` sizes it, closed at `omega_closed_at`, `since` from `opened_at`), after the same public delay, with no parity gate: `replay_check` is `{ status: "not_required", shown: true }`, so the CTA page shows the column. The shape is unchanged (version `cryptophys.public-track-record.v1`, the same keys at every level, the same labels); TP1 and TP2 are unchanged; R and aggregates only, no forbidden key and no Omega in the output. `v4` is today's behaviour (the v4 replay, shown only on a passing parity check, the parity read from KV). Tests: `test/public-track-record.test.mjs` TR6-TR9 (TR2, TR3, TR5 pinned to `v4`).
- feat(strategy): TP1 at the nearest key zone, at least 1R (TP1-KZ; owner 2026-11-23: "ok setuju, TP1 ke key zone minimal 1R, karena di perjalanan position manager juga mengawal jika perlu close lebih cepat untuk mengamankan profit", replacing the 2026-11-12 scalp rule for TP1). In every mode the strategy agents' TP1 from the key levels (`targetsAhead`; `tradeLevels`/`planForDirection` with `keyZone` for the zone_pullback agent's plan) is the nearest Key Level Agent candidate beyond the mode's gap (`tp1KeyZoneOf`; `zoneLevels` marks candidates `key`), and pays at least `TP1_MIN_R` = 1; a nearer key zone under 1R leaves that agent without a plan; without a key zone ahead, today's nearest level or the 2R projection, still at least 1R. Agents with their own targets keep them. `agentPlanPasses` takes `tp1_min_r` (set on the guards while on), `liveAgentPlans` applies the floor to the default agent too, and the fallback trades the default agent's own plan. The guardrails keep their own plan, so one agent's geometry never blocks the side. `buildStrategy`'s plan follows the switch through `modes.tp1KeyZone` (`loadStrategyInputs`; `slimInputs` keeps it only when off); under 1R it keeps today's plan for its location and waits (`LOW_REWARD`). `ownTimeframePlan` (advisory) is unchanged. Trigger case: decision 2026-11-22 23:45Z (scalp, sweep_reversal SELL) took TP1 at a qualified level 3562.60-3569.96 (0.9R); the key zone 3553.73-3560.96 gives 1.71R. Switch `TP1_KEY_ZONE_MODE` (`on`; `off` is byte-identical, checked against a sample hashed on main). No Position Manager, EA or hashed-function change. Test: `test/tp1-key-zone.test.mjs`.

- fix(liquidity): the backfill within D1's 100 bound parameters (Track B B2). `archiveBrokerBars` bound 13 columns x 8 rows = 104 parameters, above D1's 100, so every backfill batch rolled back: 2,419 of the 3,083 connection-90 `corpus_bars` minutes never reached `broker_bars_1m`, and they are pruned from 2026-12-02 16:36 UTC. `BROKER_ROWS_PER_STATEMENT` is 7 (91), the INSERT built from `BROKER_BAR_COLUMNS`. `backfillBrokerBars` reads the active connection version and every version already archived for the account_ref and source (the list kept in KV `liquidity:backfill:versions:v2:<ref>|<source>`, the archive scanned only for a new version), copies each group under its own version (INSERT OR IGNORE, with the legacy flag of the account's live rows), counts the rows added from each INSERT's `meta.changes`, and runs once per identity (KV `liquidity:backfill:v2:<ref>|<source>|v<hash of the versions>`, the versions in the value; the v1 marker no longer stops it). The marker keeps the rows added, the minutes they span and `rebuilt`; the ledger step drives the baseline rebuild (not a reset) and the replay of the added minutes' sweeps (`liquidity:replay:v2:<identity>`, readings through `db.batch`) from it, so a step that fails after the marker still does both, and a copy cut off before its final marker is resumed over its whole span. A failure is retried at the next step and logs one `liquidity_backfill_failed` (not a change of settings) per identity. The backfill replay is expected to floor lens B on day one (production's backfilled history scores about 30 % hits): RFC §7.2-§7.3 working as designed, the floor only lowers lens B's weight. Tests: PL11, PL22 (1 to 2,419 bars on a D1 whose batch is one transaction refusing more than 100 parameters), PL23 (versions, legacy flag, cached version list, short keys, failure and retry), PL24 (rebuild, not reset; replay of the added span only), PL25 (a failure after the marker, a cut-off marker), PL26 (`runLensStep` over a backfill floors lens B on a replay that scores 40 of 93).

The peak (from 0, as `src/signal-ledger.mjs` counts it) a thin grey line (`off`), no longer dashed. The drawdown from that peak with its own R ticks, its deepest point marked with value and date.

- fix(nexus): #520 follow-up (the Orchestrator's round-6 nits). UI only, English: no hub, API or server change; `components/HeaderStatus.tsx`, `lib/headerStatus.ts` and the `AppShell` header unchanged.
  - **Recovery reads are spaced.** A recovery read (a replay arrives, the push turns live) goes only when the route's last recovery read is at least its fast cadence ago (`lib/pollState.ts` `recoveryGate`, `recoveryDue`: the decision and the rooms 60 s; `usePoll`'s `nudge`, the 15m room, 10 s): at most one recovery read per fast cadence per route on top of the regular reads, so the push turning live after an offline start or a transient 502 still reads at once. A hub that accepted the socket, replayed and closed it, with the AI Worker answering 502 fast, used to get one read per errored route per reconnect, about 6–7 requests/s per tab.
  - **The push backoff survives open-replay-close.** `lib/push.ts` resets its reconnect pause only once a socket has stayed open 5 s (`PUSH_STEADY_MS`) or delivered a live (`replay: false`) snapshot (`reconnectBackoff`), never on the open itself: such a socket now backs off 1 s, 2 s, 4 s … 60 s. In a browser against such a hub (every AI Worker read 502), the War Room page sent 7.1 requests/s and opened 116 sockets in 60 s before, 0.65 requests/s (the load and the first recovery reads; 0.23 in the second half-minute) and 12 sockets after.
  - **"checking" expires.** A route keeps its first send's time (`RouteState.firstSent`; `usePoll`'s `firstSentAt`), and a replay of unknown age is "checking" only within `CHECKING_MS` (10 s) of it (`checkingWithin`, `firstPending`); one render is armed for its end (`useWakeAt`), so with every dated read hung a reload's 3-hour-old replay turns stale with the plan hidden instead of staying dimmed as "checking".
  - **One timer chain.** The read schedule of `usePoll` and `useSchedule` is `cadenceTimer` (pure, run on fake timers in the tests): synced after every render with the cadence in force, it restarts with a check at once only when that cadence changed. The Panel's and the PollStrip's tag conditions are one rule (`showsTag`).
  - **Tests.** test/nexus-poll-states.test.mjs PS46-PS48 (the open-replay-close loop with a fast 502, the backoff, "checking" expiring, the schedule's re-arm on fake timers), and pins in PS35 (a chart read with a later `Date` leaves the offset as it was; api.ts calls no `.note(` and imports nothing), PS36 (recovery reads at once, spaced from the last recovery read, never from the last send) and PS38 (`showsTag`). Each named mutation (the re-arm, the sync, the effect deps, a `.note` through an alias, the checking tag condition, the spacing, the backoff reset, the checking bound) now fails a test.
- feat(nexus): panel data states, part A of the owner-approved mockup "Nexus data states" (owner 2026-11-22: "lanjutkan semua dan tetap berkoordinasi dengan orkestrator"): the polled panels listed under Pages and Review fixes can say loading, empty, error, stale or live, and a failed read no longer reads as "No … yet" or as live data (the rest are follow-ups, below). UI only, English: no API, engine, D1 or Worker change; the API has no `generated_at`, so every age is the browser clock's time since the last good read. Part B (the Performance charts) is a later PR; the chart code is untouched.
  - **The error is a code.** `ui/src/lib/api.ts` throws an `ApiError` with the route (no query), the HTTP status and the server's error code (at most 24 characters): its message is the line every panel shows, "/api/ledger 502", "/api/calendar 503 not_configured", "/api/ledger net" offline. `get()` keeps its signature and its not-found answer.
  - **The poll knows its times.** `ui/src/lib/usePoll.ts` adds `lastOk`, `lastFail`, `failures` (in a row) and `stale` = data on screen AND (the last read failed OR no good read for 2 × the interval), with one timer for the second case; the rules are pure in `ui/src/lib/pollState.ts`. The contract stays: the interval apart from the deps, refresh only while visible, the skeleton only on the first load.
  - **Shared pieces** (`components/ui.tsx`). `Panel` takes a `poll`: its header's right slot carries the state first, then the panel's own controls: amber "stale 7m" (tooltip: last ok HH:MM:SS UTC · local, what failed, how often, the retry cadence; on a phone the tag opens that line) with "ok HH:MM UTC · local" and Retry now; red "error"; with `live`, the cyan time of the last good read. `PollBody` picks the skeleton, the error box, the empty line or the content; an error with nothing on screen stays an error while it retries (no skeleton flash). The error box: the route and code, "retry 60s", the last good time and Retry now (44 px on a phone). `Empty` takes `when` (what is missing and when it fills); `Skeleton` has a chart shape.
  - **Pages.** Model checks for an error before "No baseline yet" and "No decision model yet", each of its four polls with its own tag. `lib/market.tsx` keeps each read's own catch and exposes which routes failed, so the War Room's News tab (and the 1H, 4H, D1 tabs) show "/api/news/daily 502" instead of "No news analysis yet". Performance: one poll feeds every panel, so the tag shows once, beside Refresh; the equity panel's empty line reads "0 closed trades · 30D · TP1 / Draws from the 2nd closed trade. The ledger runs every 5m." The raw "Unavailable: …" / "Failed to load: …" text is the shared error line on Performance, Calendar, Correlations, Alerts, Audit, Status (overview and parity), Scorecards, Shadow, Neurons, Strategy and Call for Councils (suggestions and the evidence pack).
  - **Unchanged.** `components/HeaderStatus.tsx`, `lib/headerStatus.ts`, the header of `AppShell.tsx`, the mesh background and the logo glow.
  - **Tests.** test/nexus-poll-states.test.mjs PS1-PS11 (the stale rule and phases in Node, `ApiError` and `get()` against a stubbed fetch, and source checks that the listed pages print the shared line, never "Unavailable: HTTP").
  - **Review fixes.** The Decision Engine card keeps its last decision when /api/conclusion/global fails after a good read (`lib/market.tsx` keeps each route's last good answer and its own times, `pollOf(path)`), with the stale tag open (age, last good read, route and code, Retry now); a first-load failure is the error box, and "No decision yet." only follows a good read. System status "Workers and lanes" shows the /api/status error box above the lane's rows, dims old rows under the stale tag, and its badge (`lib/healthBadge.ts`) never says "all nominal" while /api/status or /api/local-lane failed or is stale; the War Room's System issues lists a failed or stale status read. Stale after a sleep or a clock jump (`lib/usePoll.ts`, rules in `lib/pollState.ts`): the tab becoming visible re-evaluates stale before the refresh, a 20 s tick does while data is on screen (it renders only when the verdict changes), and a stale timer that fires early is armed again (`staleWake`). No "nothing yet" on an error: the War Room 15m tab, Roster, Key levels, Councils sessions and a session, the Shadow KPI cards ("—") and the Model learning pipeline ("—", "baseline error"). The tag now also on Status (overview, pull adapter, quota, news sources, parity), Shadow (and its tuning grid), Councils (sessions, a session, suggestions, the evidence pack), Calendar, Alerts, Audit, Correlations, Scorecards, Neurons, Strategy agents and the War Room tabs (15m, News, 1H, 4H, D1; a push counts as a good arrival). Retry now is busy-guarded: disabled while a read is in flight, and `refresh()` joins the read in flight (`singleFlight`), so two clicks on the War Room box make six requests, not twelve. Tests PS12-PS19. Follow-ups (still no tag): the War Room right-hand panels (Market sentiment, Trend by timeframe, Key levels, the HUD levels read, which still catches silently), Priority Queue, the Lens panel, the Macro War Room on Correlations, Audit's rule breaks and Aether sync, Scorecards' rule compliance, Strategy's other panels, and the Aether and Omega pages.
  - **Re-review fixes.** A push replay never clears a live failure: `lib/push.ts` keeps each snapshot's own `at` (the hub's publish time; the hub replays the stored snapshot on every connect), and a push applies only when newer than the data on screen and counts as a good arrival only when newer than the last failed read (`pushApplies`, `withPush`), on the decision, the rooms and the 15m room; the hub is unchanged. Each route of `lib/market.tsx` applies its own answer as it resolves and records lastOk only then (`RouteState`, `routeSettled`: an overtaken read is dropped, a failed read never becomes null data), so a hung D1 read no longer freezes the decision while it reads as live. The War Room chart dims the plan lines and zones of a stale decision and labels them ("Entry · stale 1h"), and past 2 h drops them with "plan hidden: decision stale" (`planMark`; Omega's chart draws the EA signal, not this decision). Retry now is guarded by its own click only (at most 10 s), `refresh()` joins a read in flight only while it is younger than the interval (`joinsInFlight`), busy and Retry now are per path, and reads skip the browser cache (its lock held a retry behind the hung read for ~20 s). A last good read more than 60 s in the future (the clock moved back) is stale. Key levels shows the decision read's error instead of "No zones"; System issues lists a failed or stale /api/local-lane. Tests PS20-PS24 (and PS9, PS14-PS16 updated).
  - **Third review fixes.** One local monotonic order for reads and pushes: `lib/pollState.ts` `nextSeq` is taken when a read is sent and when a push arrives (`lib/push.ts` keeps each snapshot's arrival, steady local time and whether it is the replay of a connect, beside the hub's `at`); a settled read replaces the data unless a push arrived after it was sent, and every push applies unless the hub's `at` marks it a replay older than what is on screen (`pushApplies`: a connect's replay must be newer than the newest applied `at`, and nothing more than 120 s older applies). The hub's `at` is never compared with a browser time (no clamp to the browser clock), so a fast or slow browser neither drops fresh pushes nor lets an old replay clear a failure. A push's arrival counts toward lastOk for good (`withPush`); only clearing the error needs the push to have arrived after the failed read was sent, so REST 502s with pushes every 15 minutes read "stale" at most the push interval and never hide the plan. Every local time and age (lastOk, lastFail, the stale tag, the plan lines) is on a steady clock (`localNow`: the wall clock forward, the monotonic clock on a backward step), so a backward clock step freezes nothing; shown clock times follow the wall clock (`wallOf`). The War Room's 15m room uses the same order (`usePoll` exposes `sentSeq`, `failSeq`). Retry now joins only a read sent within 2.5 s (`RETRY_JOIN_MS`), so a click during a hang sends a request (`usePoll` and `lib/market.tsx`). `get()` is no-store for the poll and retry reads only; /api/ohlcv and the chart plane read keep the browser cache (`CHART_READ`, `CACHED_ROUTES`). Tests PS25-PS30; PS6, PS7, PS9, PS12-PS16, PS20, PS21 and PS23 updated from the wall-clock semantics.
  - **Fourth review fixes.** A hub replay (the stored snapshot the hub sends on every connect) may show data but never clears an error or moves lastOk: only a live push or a good read does (`lib/pollState.ts` `takePush` keeps a replay out of the live mark that `withPush` reads), so a reload or a second tab during an outage no longer reads "live" for five minutes. A replay is aged on the server clock: each no-store, same-origin poll or retry read records its response's `Date` (a 502's too) and its local settle (`api.ts` `getDated`, `usePoll.ts` `readDated`, `serverClock`: the minimum of (local settle) - Date over the last 6 reads), and the replay's age is its `at` plus that offset (`replayLocal`, `ageBase`), so the stale tag and the plan lines read "stale 2h" and "plan hidden" from the first second; before any read brought a Date it is of unknown age (stale, the plan hidden, never live). A replay replaces a read's data only when its `at` is newer than that read's Date less 1 s (`pushWins`), in either arrival order (a read in flight is compared when it settles); a live push keeps the arrival-order rule. The 15m room (`lib/warroom.tsx`) uses the same functions. A forward clock jump (an NTP step, a wake from sleep: the steady clock outran the monotonic one by more than the interval, `clockJump`) reads once at once in `usePoll` and in `lib/market.tsx`'s schedules, never beside a read in flight. Tests PS31-PS35; PS20 restored to the original reproduction (with the "between" case), PS25's load case no longer clears a 502, and PS7, PS9, PS21, PS23 and PS30 updated.
  - **Fifth review fixes.** A route whose last read failed stays on the fast cadence while the push is live (`lib/pollState.ts` `cadenceOf`: the decision and the rooms 60 s, the 15m room 10 s, `usePoll`'s `errorMs`), and reads once at once when the push turns live or a replay arrives for it, never beside its read in flight (`recoveryReads`, `turnedLive`, `usePoll`'s `nudge`), so a transient 502 or an offline start (a tab restored before the Wi-Fi) recovers within the fast cadence instead of about 5 minutes. A cadence change keeps the phase: each read is due at max(now, last send + cadence) (`dueReads`, a timeout chain in `usePoll` and in `lib/market.tsx`'s `useSchedule`, now per path), which also ends the old bug where a socket flapping every 1-4 minutes postponed the fallback read forever; the clock-jump detector lives as long as the poll (a wake with the socket dropped reads at once). A hub replay that lands before the route's first dated read settles is "checking" (`isChecking`, `firstPending`: a muted tag, no stale chip, the plan dimmed " · checking"), stale and hidden only if that read fails without a Date or brings none; the outage case is unchanged. `lib/push.ts` uses the snapshot's `replay` flag when it is a boolean and falls back to the first message per socket otherwise (`isReplay`), and the hub now marks them (`workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/src/push-hub.mjs` `pushMessage`: `replay: true` on a connect's stored snapshots, `replay: false` on a live publish; the one Worker change of this PR, additive, owner-approved on 2026-11-22 at the Orchestrator's request; test `push-hub.test.mjs`), so a topic's first live publish with nothing stored clears an error as a live push should; the fallback keeps a page working while the UI is deployed before the hub. The chart's plan mark follows the card's 1 s clock (`PLAN_TICK_MS`, rendering only when the mark changes) and a data time more than 60 s ahead hides the plan; the server clock ignores samples more than a day off. `sameOrigin` resolves the path against the page (a safe base under Node) and refuses control characters ("/\t/evil.example/x"); an ok answer that is not JSON is `ApiError` `not_json`; the steady clock is anchored (t0 + max(wall, monotonic elapsed), re-anchored on a backward step), so ages no longer run 0.1-0.3% old. The server-to-local offset errs younger, not older: Date is truncated and stamped before the response leaves the server, so a replay's age is underestimated by the latency plus up to 1 s. Known limit of the fourth review's (c), as on main: a read that leaves the server more than 1 s after reading its data can beat a newer replay of a publish the socket missed. Tests PS36-PS45; PS6, PS13, PS20, PS21, PS25, PS26, PS30, PS31, PS34 and PS35 updated.
- refactor(clock): one session clock (Track A PR 2). `SESSIONS` and `nyHour` move from `src/decision-engine.mjs` to `src/market-hours.mjs` (the Decision Engine re-exports `SESSIONS`); new `sessionOpenMs(name, ms)` (the latest open at or before `ms`, in New York time) and `sessionWindow(name, ms, { rangeBars = 3 })` → `{ open_ms, range_end_ms, end_ms }` (end: the close of the last 15-minute bar before the session's end, London 11:45 and New York 16:45 New York time), for the opening ranges. `sessionAt` is identical (swept across both 2026 clock changes), `src/liquidity-lens.mjs` is unchanged and a test pins its `SESSION_OPEN_HOURS_NY` to the `SESSIONS` opens, and `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` is unchanged. With `off`: no pattern zone member, no pattern_* or pattern_inside plan, empty price action, rooms keep their projection levels. With `on` or unset the output is unchanged. - feat(lens): the Positioning & Liquidity analyst, both lenses live on merge (owner 2026-11-20: "Sekarang breakglass urgent jadi langsung live", "Setuju", "rekomendasi"; docs/RFC_POSITIONING_LIQUIDITY.md §15 as built, §16 deviations; `src/positioning-lens.mjs`, `src/liquidity-lens.mjs`, `src/lens-policy.mjs`, `src/lens-steps.mjs`).
  - **Switches.** Omega settings `positioning_enabled`, `liquidity_enabled`, default on, owner only; off, the lens contributes nothing. In `ERA_SWITCHES` (signature against the defaults: off starts a new era).
  - **Lens A.** CFTC COT (disaggregated, legacy fallback), D1 `cot_reports`, backfill 260 weeks, fetched when due; `cotAt` point in time; the `POSITIONING` witness at its mode's weight outside the tuner; 4H and 1D room line; mode and profile pack fact; `cot_stale`.

Old-shape eras are adopted as the first fingerprint's behaviour (`ERA_ADOPTABLE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH`). v4 replays `POSITIONING` from the stored decisions and treats a gate-held signal as an expected difference.

- feat(omega): the Position Council's bounded loosening and two-way take profit (platform ADR-0020 Amendment 8, owner 2026-11-20: "setuju", "keduanya"; docs/RFC_PM_A8.md; `src/position-manager.mjs`, `src/omega-engine.mjs`, `src/pm-loosen-knobs.mjs`).

  - **Knobs.** Start values per mode clamped to ADR-0020 A7's bounds (no platform reader of `omega.limit_*` yet).
  - **Storage.** `limit_*` and `regime` columns on v4's own `ledger_omega4` rows, so maker and taker pair on one row (`makerTaker`).
  - **Run and cost.** After v4 in the ledger step, never past v4's last minute, within what v4 left of the 720-minute bound; in the steady state it reuses v4's loaded bars and context. About 0.8 to 1.5 ms a signal in node.
  - **Report.** `cryptophys.execution-report.v1` with an added top-level `limit` block per mode and regime (fill rate, missed by reason, maker and taker R per trade, profit factor and total R, maker better, taker better, mean ΔR), R only, no price key at any depth (test). The schema stays v1 (Aether's validator allows extra top-level fields); its validator still accepts three modes only and so refuses reports that carry `range`, which needs an Aether change.
  - **Nexus.** A "Maker vs taker" panel on the Performance page.
  - **RFC.** `entry_order` becomes `"market" | "limit" | "council"` (default market); the council's enter_market, place_limit or skip, one limit→market conversion per signal; ADR-0020 A7 (merged, ed43279) folded into §4, §6 and §10. Stage 3 flips `LIMIT_ENTRY_AVAILABLE`; this stage does not.
  - **Live effect.** None. test/ledger-omega-limit.test.mjs L1–L12.
- feat(public): `PublicProjection.trackRecord()`, the public track record for the CTA's Performance page (owner 2026-11-20, confirmed: "Yes, build it"): win rate (won, lost, even), expectancy, net R (average win and loss) and profit factor per exit scenario (TP1, TP2, managed), in R only, aggregates only, 24 h delay; the managed exit only once the v4 parity check passes. `src/public-track-record.mjs`, test/public-track-record.test.mjs TR1–TR5.
- feat(ledger): Omega policy v4, the live engine replayed, with a parity check against the live account (owner 2026-11-20: "pastikan omega performance sama dengan omega yang live sekarang"; `src/ledger-omega4.mjs`, `cryptophys.ledger-omega.v4`).
  - **Why.** v3 re-implements a few of Omega's rules on each ledger row. It has no TP ladder, Position Manager, re-entry cooldown, adaptive or 5m stop, or profile caps, and it trades only the ledger's rows: on 2026-11-19 the ledger's no-stacking rule held back 5 of the 11 decisions Omega traded live.
  - **What.** One simulated account, minute by minute on the feed's 1m bars, through the live functions: `eaSignalFromDecision`, `managementFields`, `planIntents` (its intents applied to the simulated positions), and `positionFacts` with `positionAction` 3 minutes past each 5m close (the council's recorded verdict where the live account had one, else the mechanical fallback). Also the re-entry cooldown from the replay's own exits, the live account's profile in force then and its balance rebuilt from its deals. v3 runs unchanged beside it.
  - **Storage.** D1 `ledger_omega4` (one row per decision) and `omega4_*` columns on the ledger's rows. KV `ledger:omega4:checkpoint:v1` holds the last flat minute; each 5-minute run replays from there (`LEDGER_OMEGA4` "off" stops it). Measured: about 1.3 ms a signal on two days of 1m bars.
  - **Parity.** `omegaParity` runs at most hourly against Omega's real trades on the live USD account since 2026-11-19 12:00 UTC (KV `ledger:omega4:parity:v1`, `GET /api/ledger` `parity`). It passes with the same exit on at least 90 % and a mean |ΔR| of at most 0.1. Live trades before #491 (17:48 UTC that day) ran older Position Manager rules: they are shown apart and not counted (see the review below). No price, ticket or account in it; nothing goes to Aether or the platform.
  - **Checked by hand on 2026-11-19 data.** The 19:15 UTC SELL (current code) matched: the same take-profit at TP2, R 0.684 in v4 against 0.697 live, the entries 30 s apart. The 12:00 and 13:15 SELLs (before #488 to #491) did not: live cut the second on a WAIT alone, which the current rules no longer do.
  - **Nexus.** The Performance page gets an "Omega v4 (live engine)" policy, v4 and v4 against v3 in the per-mode table, a v4 column, the new exit codes, and a parity panel (pass or fail, trades compared, same-exit share, mean |ΔR|, and the per-trade rows with their causes).
  - **Review of #498 (orchestrator).**
    - **Parity per code era.** Only the live trades opened under the current engine and Position Manager code count. The era comes from `ENGINE_CODE_HASH`, a committed SHA-256 of the listed live functions' source that a test recomputes; it is seeded from 2026-11-19 17:48 UTC, and a new hash starts a new era at the deploy (`DEPLOYED_AT`). Earlier trades are shown apart. The status is `insufficient` below 20 live trades (`PARITY_MIN_TRADES`), never `pass`.
    - **1m archive.** D1 `ledger_bars_1m`: each run appends only the new closed minutes (batched, kept 120 days). The replay merges it with the KV series where KV no longer reaches back, so it stays reproducible after the two days roll off.
    - **Bound.** The checkpoint is now the whole replay state at its next minute. A run replays at most 720 minutes (`MAX_REPLAY_MINUTES`) and logs its work (decisions, minutes, `planIntents` and `positionAction` calls, wall time). Resumed runs equal one long run (test).
    - **Nexus.** The parity panel shows pass, fail or insufficient with the counts against the minimum of 20, the era (since, commit, code hash), the earlier trades apart, and that a later code change starts a new window. A collapsible note by the v4 option lists the approximations.
- feat(omega): limit entries, stage 1: the switch and EA 1.21, no behaviour change (owner 2026-11-20, relayed; scope confirmed: "Yes, that scope"; docs/RFC_OMEGA_LIMIT_ENTRY.md).
  - **Switch.** `entry_order` ("market" default); "limit" is refused until stage 3.
  - **Accounting.** Omega's own pending limits count as positions and as risk at their stops in every entry gate, and are deleted at once (`delete_order`) on a hard reason; with the switch on market all of them are, so none can survive. Kill's `close_all` also runs when only a pending order exists; Omega switched off deletes them, and so do disarm on a real account (`disarmed`, ADR-0020 Amendment 7) and a missing stop (`pending_without_stop`).
  - **OmegaExecutor 1.21.** `place_limit` (`<lot>@<price>` in the volume field, the expiry in the ticket field; refused through the price, inside stops or freeze level, without an expiry ahead, on netting accounts) and `delete_order`; its own expiry timer (never GTC), a 12 h maximum life and a 120 s dead-man switch. The bridge is unchanged.
  - **Live effect.** None: no pending Omega order exists, and nothing places one until stage 3 and the owner's switch.

## 2026-11-19
- perf(omega): bridge calm mode, omega-bridge 1.7.0 (owner 2026-11-19: "Keduanya, bertahap", step 2; owner "lanjutkan terkait vps").
  - **Why.** The EA writes state.json every 2 s, and the bridge synced on every write (about 1.7 syncs a second) even with nothing to manage.
  - **What changes.** Each sync response carries `stream_mode`. While calm (terminal connected, no position, order, intent, recovery or review), a 1.7.0 bridge syncs every 5 s and on results instead (about 0.2 a second). Anything else, or a position or an order in the bridge's own state, is fast as before.
  - **Trade-off.** An entry planned while calm reaches the terminal at most 5 s later; open positions are managed at the fast pace.
  - **Rollout.** The server goes first, then the owner updates the bridge on the VPS (`docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md`, "Calm mode"). The EA is unchanged. An older bridge ignores the field.
- perf(market-data): a published bar is not rebuilt every minute (owner 2026-11-19, relayed "setuju semua": profile first, nothing the agents or parity read may change).
  - **Profile.** Inside each freshness window (H1 25 min, M15 9, H4 30, D1 60) every 1-minute cron rebuilt the bar's engines and HUD snapshots before it read the bar's published marker, about 24 wasted H1 builds an hour.
  - **What changes.** Each publisher reads the marker first, keyed by the producer's own candle key of the last candle. In node, a minute with every bar already sent fell from about 4.5 s to 0.1 ms; the first build of a bar is unchanged (about 4.3 s for H1).
  - **Outputs.** The build code is untouched (no hashed producer file changes); the peeked key equals the built key (test); `force` still rebuilds.
- perf(omega): a light bridge sync when there is nothing to manage (owner 2026-11-19, relayed "setuju semua", then directly "Keduanya, bertahap": step 1, no VPS step).
  - **Why.** The bridge syncs about 1.7 times a second (on every state.json write), and a full plan costs about 15 D1 statements.
  - **What changes.** An idle sync now reuses the isolate's last full plan, at most 5 s old, and only delivers the open intents. It uses about 3 statements; in node the measured time per idle sync fell from 1.88 ms to 0.53 ms.
  - **When it does not apply.** Any open position, pending order, terminal result, corpus, macro or calendar data, or a state change runs the full plan as before.
  - **Trade-off.** A new signal or a settings change with nothing open is planned at most 5 s later.
  - **Step 2.** The bridge honouring a calm mode from the server follows with a bridge release.
- feat(war-room): Order Blocks opens live in the metric format (owner 2026-11-19: "Promosi Order Blocks sekarang").
  - **What changes.** On the 15m War Room, Order Blocks' live opening now uses the metric prompt tested in shadow. The answer becomes a plain room opening (bias from the weighted implications, the readings as the claim, the table kept as `metric_table`) and passes the room's own validation.
  - **The evidence.** It was the only agent better than its prose control in both slices: discovery 36 vs 20 (p 0.022) and holdout 24 vs 16. Its discovery slice had 175 of the 200 bars the rule asks for.
  - **Risk.** In shadow it took a side on 219 of 275 bars where its prose control took one on 39, so expect Order Blocks to call BUY or SELL far more often.
  - **What stays.** Every other agent stays on prose.
  - **Switch.** `METRIC_LIVE` "off" reverts it.
- chore(shadow): retire the shadow arms that were not better, to cut model load (owner 2026-11-19, directly: "1+2+3 hentikan semua", then "Pertahankan, hentikan sisanya" and "Hentikan seluruh arm"). Before (ai_usage_daily, 18 Nov): shadow sources about 1.63M prompt and 0.20M completion tokens. Record per arm (sign test on decided pairs that differ, one-sided, Holm over the eight tested arms; D1 to 2026-11-19 17:47 UTC):
  - **Kept: metric opening shadow, M15.** Better: 238 vs 170, p 0.0004, Holm 0.0032; accuracy 47.3% vs 45.2% over 3,038 scored bars. It takes a side twice as often as its control (1,591 vs 824 bars) and misses twice as often (770 vs 396). About 0.62M prompt tokens a day.
  - **Retired on "not better": metric rebuttals** (19 vs 26, Holm 1). New switch `METRIC_REBUTTAL` "off"; `METRIC_SHADOW` still stops both.
  - **Retired on "worse" or "no effect": the learned-note arm** (`SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED` "false"). chronos_matrix 15m was worse (9 vs 26). chronos_matrix 1h (4 vs 4), trade_management 1h (3 vs 5) and aegis_footprint 15m (0 vs 1) were not better. risk, news, decision_matrix, prophet, h1, supertrend, trade_management 15m and aegis_footprint 1h never changed a decided stance. New notes from the curator are not tested while it is off.
  - **Stopped, measurement done: the A/A arms** (chronos_matrix 15m 17 of 387 changed, 1h 11 of 83; risk 0 of 23).
  - **Retired, no verdict: the model arena** (`MODEL_ARENA` "off"; 37 and 36 scored bars, no decided difference).
  - Expected drop: about 0.87M prompt tokens a day, roughly 53% of the shadow total. That is the note arm (586 of the 739 `shadow:war_room_opening` and `shadow:tf_room_opening` calls on 18 Nov), the rebuttals (0.21M) and the arena (0.13M). The measured drop over a full weekday follows from `ai_usage_daily`.
  - Unchanged: the benched agents' shadow seats of the neuron allocation (the remaining ~150 calls a day under the same meter labels).
  - Stored rows stay. Nothing on the live path changes.
- feat(position-manager): TP2 with a break-even stop in one modification, before the ladder's TP1 lock (platform ADR-0020 Amendment 6; owner 2026-11-19: "Merge #490 + kerjakan opsi BE"). When the council approves TP2 (momentum not weakening, thesis intact) and the position is in profit, the Position Manager sends the target to TP2 and the stop to break-even plus the spread (or keeps a stop already past it) as one `modify_sltp` (`tp2_breakeven`; OmegaExecutor 1.18+; Omega refuses a stop looser than the broker's or below the entry, and one without room at the live price). Since the ladder went live, 0 of 8 Omega positions had their stop at the ladder's TP1 (five locked at the entry), so TP2 behind that lock never went out. A Position Manager `modify_sltp` counts as a commanded stop for the ladder (`commandedStops`); the owner's own does not.
- feat(position-manager): TP2 with the council, and only when it can reach the broker (PM PR 2; owner 2026-11-19, relayed plan, approved). The Position Manager proposes the outward target to TP2 only when the council answered with the momentum not weakening and the thesis intact, and, under the TP ladder, once the stop locks the ladder's TP1 (ADR-0020 Amendment 4, `ladderTp1Locked`, read from Omega's `tp_ladder` setting). Before, it proposed TP2 every five minutes from the first pip of profit and the ladder dropped it, so the owner read "target out to TP2" (and, after #488, "not applied") each bar. The same "not applied" reason for the same ticket and rule is said once an hour.
- feat(position-manager): the stop locks go through the council, with the mechanical lock as the fallback (owner 2026-11-19: "Lewat council juga", then "ya"). Break-even and the TP1 lock read the council's answer: beyond TP1 by 30 pips with the momentum weakening the position is closed (`take_profit_weakening`) instead of locked; otherwise the lock applies with the council's reading in its line. When the council does not answer (failure, timeout, the daily cap) the lock still applies, said in the line ("the lock applies as the fallback"); before, a silent council left break-even undone. The TP ladder's stop locks in Omega are unchanged (PM PR 3).
- fix(position-manager): no cut on a WAIT alone, the council confirms every cut, and Telegram says what reached the broker (owner 2026-11-19, relayed: "prioritas perbaikan karena ini running live"; "WAIT bisa jadi HOLD, harus divalidasi jangan langsung diexit").
  - **cut_loss.** Inside the stop, a cut needs a measured reason (the decision on the other side, the agent's confidence or the quorum's share on the position's side dropped) and the council's thesis weakening or broken. A WAIT alone holds (`hold_to_stop`, said in the line); the thesis intact holds (`hold_thesis_intact`); no council answer cuts nothing (`council_unavailable`). Position 362862558 was cut on a WAIT alone at -10.11; holding would have made about +5.63.
  - **The Position Manager's line.** Sent by the Omega sync when the broker answers, or when Omega drops the action, with the reason (`pm_dropped` of the plan, the 6-minute action age, the 2-minute terminal timeout). Before, it was sent when the action was decided, so "target out to TP2" was read while the TP ladder dropped it.
  - **Every exit said.** `src/position-exit.mjs`: each Omega or delegated position that leaves the book (the broker's take-profit and stop included, which said nothing before) gets one line with the reason, entry, exit, pips and the result, and one `omega_events` row `position_closed`. A full Omega close no longer sends a second "Closed" line.
  - The daily report marks an action that was not applied.
- fix(council): the quorum knobs stay inside the doctrine's bounds 0.45 to 0.7 (owner: "ya"): scalp 0.45-0.65 (was 0.40), intraday 0.45-0.7 (was 0.40), swing 0.45-0.7 (was 0.75). No knob was ever live, so no value in force changes. History 10-19 Nov: no scalp side between 0.40 and 0.45; swing trades at 0.70-0.80 of the weight hit 5 of 6.
- feat(doctrine): the Decision Engine's thresholds per trading mode in the doctrine facts (owner, relayed: "mau"): `decision.quorum_min_<mode>`, `decision.lean_<mode>`, `decision.min_rrr_<mode>` for scalp, intraday, swing and range, as the engine reads them (the council's live knobs included), beside the single facts of the mode in force. Only the reporting changes; no value, guardrail or rule.
- fix(tuning): the room guardrail's hourly step is recorded in `auto_tune_runs` (step `room`, each mode's counts and the changes); the run log accepted only the weighting and witness steps, so the first live room runs left no row.
- fix(platform): the kernel's 4 reports an hour per agent are kept here (D1 `platform_publish_log`): a fifth report in the hour is held and retried later instead of denied, since 5 denials in an hour get an agent suspended (the cause of the Risk Manager's suspension on 18 Nov). The doctrine facts also carry the room per mode (`decision.room_min_atr_scalp`, `_intraday`, `_swing`, `_range`).
- fix(platform): a suspended platform agent backs off (orchestrator, platform audit: xauusd-risk-manager denied `agent_enabled` dozens of times since 18 Nov). `publishToPlatform` waits an hour after such a denial, then one `whoami` read decides; `platform_backoff` in D1, listed on `/api/platform/status`. Test in platform-reports.
- feat(engine): the room guardrail per trading mode, tuned by the learning protocol (owner: "guardrail room biarkan protocol learning dan knowledge yang melakukan adjustment secara dinamis", then "sesuai rekomendasimu"). `src/room-tuning.mjs`: replay of the stored decisions with the room recomputed from the stored corridor, arms of plus and minus 0.25 inside the owner's bounds 0.5 to 1.5, sign test with Holm, discovery 200 and holdout 100, activation per mode with a 7-day cooldown, reverse shadow, Telegram, the Aether report, the doctrine facts `decision.room_min_atr*`. Every mode starts at 1; `ROOM_TUNING_MODE` "default" holds it. Tests RT1-RT7.
- fix(omega): the ladder lock never loosens a stop from a stale snapshot. The management reads the tightest of the position's stop and the `modify_sl` intents of the last 5 minutes still in flight or done with an ok result (`commandedStops`, `withCommandedStop`), and the break-even lock (TP0) is taken once. Found by the orchestrator in live D1 on the first Omega trade of the account: three modifications in seven seconds (3719.19, 3719.23, 3719.19), the middle one looser. Tests TL8, TL9.
- feat(omega): the pages list the deals of the account the terminal last reported only (owner: "hapus" the mixed history). `listDeals(..., { account: "last" })` on the Omega overview, the deals RPC and `/api/deals`; the deals the connected account reports (its last 100) take its `account_ref` once. Deals of other accounts and deals stored before the account was recorded stay in D1 for Omega's own statistics (policy rollback, profile agent, strategy evidence) but are on no page.
- feat(omega): symbol identity, money and the account card (orchestrator review of #477; owner relayed: "harus diperbaiki semua sampai ultimate ... kosmetic hardcoded ke broker/server/account tertentu"). OmegaExecutor 1.20 + omega-bridge 1.6.0: the price path against the reference's past H4 closes, no expiring contracts, no choice between different contracts without the owner, the chosen symbol re-checked against a fresh reference, the reference aged on its own time and never sent during the MT5 fallback. Money: the owner's value converts when the trades confirm it; a measurement near a power of ten is snapped; the stored state is never converted twice (a cent account with the owner's value and no trade would have been divided by 100 twice on the overview). A daily-loss block records its evidence and is lifted when the corrected reference no longer supports it. The Risk profile card, its confirm and history, Telegram lines and refusals name the connected account by what the terminal reports, never by internal id or server; the pages' broker label is the broker's own name. Test data uses no real broker names.
- feat(omega): universal broker, server and symbol (owner: "desain tolol kalau hardcoded ke broker/server tertentu seharusnya berlaku universal"). No list of broker, server or symbol names anywhere: gold is identified by profit currency USD, a 1-100 oz contract and a price within 2% of the reference; the account's units per US dollar are 1 for USD, measured from its own trades otherwise, or the owner's value with the connection, else nothing opens (`money_unverified`, `money_mismatch`). The broker label is the EA's `ACCOUNT_COMPANY`. OmegaExecutor 1.19 scans every symbol on the server by these checks (reference price from bridge 1.5.0, `omega/reference.txt`); the bridge's names only open a first chart. The Console drops its broker presets and server lists; the gold symbol is automatic with an optional hint; a "Units per US dollar" field. The tick-value, minimum-volume and profile caps are unchanged.
- feat(omega): a refused owner control is recorded as the Omega event `profile_control_refused` (action, value, account, the connected account, whether the terminal was live, the reason; never a login). The owner tried to change the agent mode after the 03:50 UTC deploy and no attempt reached the database or left a trace (orchestrator, D1 read-only). Test AS20 extended.
- fix(omega): two account bugs found by the orchestrator in live D1 (read-only) and confirmed here.
  - **A deposit counted twice in the daily loss** (regression from #467). The EA's day start is the balance less today's closed deals (`OmegaExecutor.mq5`), so it already holds today's deposits and withdrawals; `dailyLossExecutor` added them again on the broker's start. The $300 deposit of 01:23 UTC made the reference 652.64 against a 369.13 balance: the daily stop read as hit and the profile agent downgraded the account to Safe at 01:32 while it was in profit. A withdrawal would have hidden a loss of its size. The broker's start is now used as it is; the onboarding reference and the owner's reset keep their rules. AB9 rewritten with the EA's real numbers, plus the 19 Nov case.
  - **Two accounts on one server were mixed up.** The 5-minute profile agent, the mode selector, the owner's reset and the overview found "the connected account" as the first row on the terminal's server: with a USD and a cent account on FxPro-MT5 they used the cent account's row, so the cron wrote the USD account's balance, deposit and daily loss there, and the owner's ceiling, profile, agent mode and reset of 00:00-01:37 UTC landed on it while the connected account stayed Safe. The bridge sync now stores the account's opaque reference with the state, and `connectedAccount` finds the account by it. The Console picked its account by the server too (`Omega.tsx`): it now shows the account the server marks connected.
  - **Cent or USD, always visible** (owner 2026-11-19, relayed: "lakukan perbaikan bugs sekarang harus bisa membedakan mana akun sen dan usd"). Each account in the overview carries `identity` (`accountIdentity`: currency, cent or not, gold symbol, e.g. "USD account · GOLD" or "Cent account (USX) · GoldOne"); the Console lists every account with it and which one is connected, and heads the controls with it. Every owner control (profile, ceiling, agent mode, accept, decline, reset) acts on the connected account only and refuses any other with a message naming it. Test AS20. Rows already written are not repaired by this change (owner-only controls).
- fix(public): `warroomScene()` reads each room's statements from its run document's steps (`room_runs.body_json.steps`). The 1H, 4H and 1D rooms keep them only there, so #473 found no claim or rebuttal for them (seen in D1 for the 1H room of 02:00 UTC and the 4H and 1D rooms of 22:00 UTC); `agent_thread_messages` is read only for a document without steps. The filter also refuses "enough/safe/ready/clear to trade" and "tradeable". Test PW6.
- feat(public): `PublicProjection.warroomScene()` for the CTA's holo war room homepage (owner 2026-11-19: "ok lanjutkan ... Cukup info yang perlu dan layak untuk konsumsi public"; confirmed in the session). The latest scene of the 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D rooms: roster in speaking order, per analyst its bias, metric names, at most two filtered sentences of its claim and of its rebuttal and whom it rebutted; the Moderator's public reasons. Never a stance, confidence, decision, price or order wording. The public filter is stricter: whole sentences are checked and an over-long one is dropped, never clipped (a cut once hid "a high-confidence sell"); positional wording (flat, waiting, trade-ready, the sidelines, "#1") is refused; "Decision Matrix" reads "Matrix"; a ratio such as 0.618 is no price. Tests PW4, PW5.
- feat(omega): Modify and Delegate on the Trade tab (owner 2026-11-19, Human-AI interaction; confirmed: "Posisi + pending order", "Omega lanjut biasa", "Tombol + toggle", "Kelola penuh"). OmegaExecutor 1.18.
  - **Modify:** an open position's S/L and T/P, and a pending limit or stop order's price, S/L and T/P, checked against the live price and the broker's stop distance, queued as one `manual:` intent (`modify_sltp`, `modify_order`) and audited. Omega keeps managing its own positions afterwards.
  - **Delegate:** a manual position handed to Omega by its button, or every manual position opened while the toggle is on; "TAKE BACK" releases it (never re-adopted by the toggle). Managed like Omega's own: a stop from the plan when none, break-even, TP ladder, Position Manager, opposite-signal close; never an add, a larger size or a reversal. Its intents carry `d:` ids.
  - **EA 1.18:** `modify_sltp`, `modify_order` (the new price in the volume field: no bridge change), and foreign-magic actions only for `manual:` and `d:` ids. Before 1.18 Nexus refuses edits and delegated positions wait.
  - `src/omega-hitl.mjs`, routes `POST /omega/api/position/modify`, `/position/delegate`, `/delegate-mode` (owner only), tests HI1–HI5 and the route test.

## 2026-11-18
- fix(decision): 1H and News no longer read as stale after the daily reopen (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Perbaiki keduanya").
  - **Rooms.** A room's age leaves out the daily break as well as the weekend (`goldPausedAt`, `goldPausedMsBetween` in `src/market-hours.mjs`). The cause: the 1H room of 22:00 UTC was 2 hours old at the 00:00 decision, which ran about 4 minutes before the first 1H room after the reopen concluded, so the "fresh" guardrail held that decision.
  - **News.** News Daily records each hourly run that finds its reading unchanged (D1 `news_daily_check`), and `readNewsDaily` returns `checked_at`; the News witness ages from it. The cause: on quiet evenings no new gold headline came from about 19:06 to 00:06 or 01:06 UTC, so the reading went stale at about 22:06 every day although News Daily ran every hour.
