- fix(nexus): the setup card's Omega line says it is the Signal Ledger's paper replay of Omega's policy ("Omega policy (paper)"), not the live EA, and names its gate in words ("another paper trade still open (max positions)"). Owner 2026-11-18: "omega selalu info max positions" while the live EA's own reasons were range_breakout and volume_below_min; the live reason stays on the Omega page.
- feat(public): `PublicProjection.warroom({ source })` for the CTA studio's Create → War Room (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Real-time analysis"). Sources `15m` (the War Room), `1h`, `4h`, `1d` (room_runs `tf:H1`, `tf:H4`, `tf:D1`) and `news` (the 24-hour digest). Analysis only: the bias, reasons and metric names of the latest concluded run, or the digest's bias, summary, reasons, counts and headlines; never the decision (BUY, SELL, WAIT), its confidence, a price or order wording (`publicPhrase`; "order flow", "order block" and "order book" are analysis). `src/public-warroom.mjs`, test PW1–PW3.
- feat(omega): the Position Manager's profit and loss band in R per mode (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Per mode"; RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES section 8, the last part of phase 4).
  - The ±30 pips of take-profit-when-weakening, break-even and cut-loss become the position's stop distance at entry times its mode's band (`PM_R_BAND`: scalp 0.3R, intraday and range 0.5R, swing 0.7R; `pmBand`). With Omega's 139-pip average stop that is 42 / 70 / 98 pips instead of 30.
  - The mode is the open intent's, else the decision's, else intraday; a position without a known stop distance keeps 30 pips. The TP1 lock (30 pips beyond TP1) is unchanged.
  - Each action's line and the council's evidence carry the R, the band and the mode; `GET /api/omega/position-manager` adds `band_r`.
- fix(omega): same-side re-entry waits one closed M5 candle only; deposits and withdrawals never count as the day's loss (owner 2026-11-18, relayed and confirmed: "Setuju keduanya").
  - **Re-entry.** After a `pm_take_profit_weakening` or `pm_cut_loss` exit, a same-side entry waits only until the next M5 candle closes after the exit (`reentry_cooldown_bars`, 1, whole candles 0 to 12; note `reentry_cooldown` names the time it frees). The 0.5×ATR(M15) pullback (`reentry_pullback_atr`) and the confidence bypass are gone, and so is the note `reentry_needs_pullback`.
  - **Daily loss.** Each deposit or withdrawal is kept for its UTC day (D1 `omega_accounts.day_ops`); `dailyLossExecutor` moves the day's reference by those after it (the broker's day start, or the owner's reset; the onboarding balance already moves with them). A withdrawal no longer trips the daily floor or shrinks the risk budget, and a deposit no longer hides a loss.
  - **Zero balance.** A 0 balance reading (a terminal loading an account) is no longer a withdrawal: the 2026-11-18 16:41–16:44 `balance_operation` events (−98.03, +52.64, −52.64) were such readings during an account switch.
- feat(omega): Range, the 4th trading mode (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; live: "Ya, lanjut di live").
  - **What it does.** In range mode only the range strategies (`range_fade`, `sweep_reversal`) trade, still through the H4/D1 filter and the cell gate of #464, and only while the decision's 1H regime ranges (`applyRangeMode`: `range_mode_agent`, `range_breakout`). It uses the intraday thresholds and ladders as intraday (+0.5R on M15).
  - **Entry.** On Auto, the daily selector proposes range only while the 1H regime ranges and a range strategy's cell with the trend or neutral passes (`rangeEvidence`), two runs in a row, then the mode council approves (risk seat + 3 of 4). The owner may pin range from the Decision card.
  - **Exit.** Every 5 minutes (`rangeExitTick`): in range with the 1H regime no longer ranging, the mode returns to the one it came from (an owner's pin of range included; the pin then holds that mode), recorded in `omega_mode_changes`, an event and a Telegram line. No council: a fail-safe move.
  - **Lists.** "range" joins the trading modes (the ledger, the plan learner's contexts, the Performance page, the Conservative and Aggressive profiles' modes).
- feat(omega): counter-trend reversal probation (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; live: "Ya, lanjut di live"). `src/htf-cells.mjs`.
  - **Who.** The reversal strategy (`sweep_reversal`) against the H4/D1 trend, before its cell passes. Its plan already needs a sweep of a key zone reclaimed at the 15m close and puts the stop beyond the sweep's extreme; probation adds a change of structure on a closed H1 or H4 candle (the last close beyond the latest swing point against the trade; `probation_unconfirmed` until then). A range fade against the trend still never trades.
  - **How much.** Half the risk and half the profile's hard cap per trade (so a minimum lot that passes the full cap may not pass the half), one probation position at a time (`probation_busy`), never against a position on the other side (`probation_hedge`). Every other check is unchanged.
  - **Graduate or revert.** Once the cell passes it trades at full risk; a cell whose last 5 probation trades average under 0R (their R from the deals over the money risked at the stop) is reverted (`probation_reverted`) until it passes, checked in the 5-minute ledger step, with an event and a Telegram line.
  - **Owner.** Setup → Engine "Reversal probation" on/off (`POST /omega/api/probation`, owner only; on clears reverted cells). The "Order Executed" Telegram line says PROBATION ½ risk; the open intent carries `probation`, `cell` and `risk_usd`.
- feat(omega): Range mode step 1, the H4/D1 trend filter and the evidence gate per cell (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; live on the owner's account: "Ya, lanjut di live"). `src/htf-cells.mjs`.
  - **HTF trend.** H4 and D1 each "up", "down" or "flat" from the EMA(50) and its slope over 5 closed bars, combined ("mixed" when they disagree). A side is "with", "against" or "neutral" to it.
  - **Range strategies only** (`range_fade`, `sweep_reversal`): against a clear trend no trade (`htf_against`); an unknown trend no trade (`htf_unknown`); otherwise only in a cell (agent × side × HTF context) whose replayed record has 30+ setups at +0.10R or better (`cell_collecting`, `cell_below_bar`). Applied at signal time like the strategy arm: a held trade has no side, so it opens nothing and nothing it does bypasses a sizing or risk check.
  - **Evidence.** The plan learner tags each range-strategy setup with its side's HTF context (`plan_setups.htf`, from the cached 4h and 1D bars) and keeps the cells in its state (`cells`, `cell_gate`).
  - **Page.** The Omega page says why a range trade is held, with the cell's count and mean R. `RANGE_CELL_GATE` "off" stops the gate (emergency only).
- feat(omega): the owner's reset over today's daily loss stop (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Reset + buka kunci hari ini", after the cent-account sizing bug).
  - **Action.** `reset_override` (owner only, same origin, the page asks first): the baseline reset, and on a day the daily loss limit fired, today's stop lifted. Never over the kill switch, and refused while it is on.
  - **Still protected.** For the rest of that UTC day the daily loss counts from the equity at the reset (`day_ref_override`, `overrideDayRef` in `dailyLossExecutor`), so a new loss of the day's limit stops it again; the next UTC day ignores it.
  - **Audit.** Reason `owner_reset_override_daily_lock` at severity warning (the previous block in its evidence), the event `profile_baseline_reset_override`, and a Telegram line that says the stop was lifted.
  - **Page.** Setup → Risk profile: "Reset + lift today's stop" beside "Reset baseline" while a daily loss stop holds.
- fix(omega): cent deals read in USD, and an exposure line on every position (owner 2026-11-18: "keduanya").
  - **Deals.** A deal stored before #461 with a cent currency (USX, USC) kept its amounts in cents (the incident's −1254 USX). The money readers (loss streak, daily loss, the balance-operation check, the profile council, the policy rollback stats, the deals list and its stats) now read such rows ÷100 in USD (`DEAL_MONEY_COLUMNS_SQL`, `DEAL_NET_SQL`, `dealInUsd`). Nothing stored is rewritten.
  - **Exposure.** Each open position carries `exposure`: USD per $1 move in gold, and the loss at its stop in USD and % of equity (`positionExposure`). The Omega page shows it under the volume; the "Order Executed" Telegram line says it too, e.g. "≈ $3 per $1 move · risk at SL $44.91 (82.4%)".
- fix(omega): size on the real dollar risk on cent accounts (incident 2026-11-18 13:21 UTC).
  - **What happened.** On the FxPro GoldOne account (currency "USX", 5450 = $54.50) Omega opened SELL 3 lots (1 oz each) at 3771.45, stop 3786.42: about $44.9 at the stop, ~82% of the account, while the engine counted ~0.8%. "USX" was not known as cents, and the broker reported the tick value in USD while balance and profit were in cents.
  - **USX** is US cents, like USC.
  - **Tick value.** For a USD-profit symbol on a USD or cent account, Omega now sizes on contract size × tick size dollars and keeps the broker's figure as `tick_value_reported`. When the broker's figure is neither that nor 100 times it, nothing opens (`tick_value_mismatch`).
- feat(omega): cent accounts and the owner's gold symbol (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"). OmegaExecutor 1.17, omega-bridge 1.4.0.
  - **Why.** A cent account's gold is "GoldOne", on no list: the bridge opened a GOLD chart the account lacks, the EA failed to start ("symbol synchronization timeout") and the balance read 0.
  - **Owner symbol.** Setup → Connection & EA's "Symbol" selector is now sent with the connection (`trade_symbol`; "Other" for a typed name). The server, the bridge and the EA accept that one exact name under the same contract checks; the bridge opens its chart first and writes `omega/trade_symbol.txt` for the EA.
  - **Fail-closed.** A name off the list opens nothing until its bid is within 2% of the signal's entry (`symbol_price_mismatch`).
  - **USC.** Amounts are divided by 100 once where the bridge state enters (`centToUsd`); limits, sizing and pages are in USD; the account is labelled CENT. A USC profit currency passes only on a USC account.
  - **Terminal errors.** With no EA state, the terminal's last journal error is reported (`terminal_error` event, shown on the Omega page), account digits redacted.
- fix(omega): the ladder locks TPx again (owner 2026-11-18: "TP1+1 langkah geser ke TP1, TP2+1 langkah, geser ke TP2, dan seterusnya"). A step past TPx moves the stop to TPx. This replaces the TPx−1 rule of #458.
- fix(omega): the ladder locks to TPx−1 (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "TPx−1"). One step past TP1 the stop stays at break-even; past TP2 it goes to TP1; past TP3 to TP2. It was TPx since #456.
- fix(omega): the ladder's closed-candle confirmation (review of #456).
  - A candle needs its final minute bar.
  - Candles come only from the connected terminal's own server and symbol, and the cache keys on that identity.
  - A candle must close after the position opened to confirm a lock.
- feat(omega): the TP ladder's lock per mode, on closed candles (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju").
  - **Step per mode.** Scalp keeps +30 pips. Intraday uses +0.5R and swing +0.3R, where R is the stop distance at entry (open intents now record `mode` and `r_dist`).
  - **Lock.** A step past the entry puts the stop at break-even; a step past TPx puts it at TPx.
  - **Trigger.** The mode's last closed broker candle (M5, M15 or H1, from the M1 corpus), closing beyond the level in the trade's direction. With no such candle there is no new lock.
  - **Knobs.** `ladder_r_intraday`, `ladder_r_swing`.
- feat(omega): the same-side re-entry guard (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju").
  - **Why.** Four times on 2026-11-18 the Position Manager closed a SELL on weakening and the next 15m SELL reopened seconds later.
  - **Cooldown.** After a PM exit for `pm_take_profit_weakening` or `pm_cut_loss`, a new entry on the same side waits for `reentry_cooldown_bars` (1) closed 15m bar (`reentry_cooldown`).
  - **After the cooldown.** The entry also needs a pullback of `reentry_pullback_atr` (0.5) × ATR(M15) from the exit price, or the strategy agent's confidence back at its level when the closed position opened (`reentry_needs_pullback`, with the price needed).
  - **Exempt.** Exits by TP, the ladder or the stop.
  - **Entry confidence.** The open intent now records `agent_conf` at entry.
- feat(omega): phase 4, the trading-mode selector (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"). See `docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md` "Phase 4".
  - **Daily run.** On Auto, once a UTC day: the replay evidence per mode and regime, feasibility at the minimum lot against the profile cap, and fail-safe moves without the council (revert on failed evidence, drop an infeasible mode, back to the baseline after the kill switch or the daily loss).
  - **Council.** A better mode switches only after two passing runs and the mode council's approval: the risk seat and three of four.
  - **Owner.** Owner-only pin, Auto and veto, from the Decision card.
  - **Display.** The Omega header shows the mode.
  - **Record.** `omega_mode_changes`, Telegram and `mode.v1`.
- fix(omega): the Position Manager's outward TP2 now follows ADR-0020 Amendment 4 under the TP ladder too. It waits until the stop locks a profit at or beyond TP1. On 2026-11-18 at ~09:45 it moved ticket 389881390's target outward before that.
- fix(omega): a stop or target change the terminal applied without its result line is recorded done from the position (`reconcileModifies`). Seen 2026-11-18: the TP of 389881390 moved while its intent expired.
- feat(omega): the profile council's downgrade veto is on, and the TP ladder follows Amendment 4. Both follow platform ADR-0020 Amendments 4 and 5 (merged 2026-11-18 08:38 UTC).
  - **Downgrade veto.** `OMEGA_PROFILE_COUNCIL_DOWNGRADE` is `"on"`. The bridge sync convenes the council at once. The pending cap is now 2 minutes (it was 10), and the seat deadline is 25 s. A late review, a failure or an invalid verdict applies the downgrade. A veto's digest item carries its trigger, the profile it held back and `veto_until`.
  - **TP ladder.** The take-profit moves outward (trailing TP by key level, extended target) only while the stop locks a profit at or beyond TP1.
- fix(omega): the onboarding day's daily loss counts from the balance at onboarding (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju").
  - The real account connected at 08:29 started at $54.50 after −$3,316.19 of manual losses that day, so the broker's day start blocked every entry with `daily_loss_limit`.
  - On the day Omega first sees an account, every daily-loss check (arm auto-disarm, profile agent, planner) now uses the onboarding balance. Later losses still count.
  - The block that pre-onboarding losses set today is lifted once.
- feat(omega): the owner's override of the daily lock (confirmed: "Ya, boleh override"): a warning confirm, recorded as `owner_override_daily_lock`.
- feat(omega): approval proposals carry the council's advice and a 24 h expiry. The Telegram line links to `#setup/profile`, which opens Setup → Risk profile, and a badge on Setup shows pending items.
- feat(omega): account baselines and the owner's reset (owner 2026-11-18, relayed and confirmed: "setuju semua").
  - **Baselines.** A login is its own account with its own baseline. The row an older EA left under the server's name is no longer adopted.
  - **Omega deals only.** The loss streak, the upgrade evidence and the council's record count Omega's deals only.
  - **Deposits and withdrawals** move the drawdown reference and the peak (event `balance_operation`) instead of reading as P&L.
  - **Reset baseline** (Setup → Risk profile, owner only, named confirm) restarts the connected account's peak, drawdown reference and loss streak. The profile and ceiling are kept.
    - It is refused while the kill switch is on or after the day's stop.
    - It is recorded as an audit row (`owner_reset`), an event, a Telegram line and a `risk_profile.v1` item.
  - **Seen before.** The panel shows whether Omega saw the account before, and when it first and last saw it.
- feat(omega): the TP ladder (owner 2026-11-18, relayed and confirmed: "a, langsung live", every mode, Scalp included). Omega's own positions get TP1..TPn: the key levels between the entry and the take-profit, else thirds, then the take-profit. Once price is 30 pips past TPx, the stop locks at TPx-1 (TP0 = the entry). At each rung, a progressive partial close takes 25% to 50% of the volume: 25% with a strong power behind the position, 50% when it fades. Partials keep the broker's volume step and minimum, so a 0.01 position gets the lock only, and there are at most three. Exposure only goes down: the ladder never adds, never widens a stop and never moves the take-profit outward (Amendment 4). It replaces the single 50% scale-out (`tp_ladder` on by default; knobs `partial_min_pct`, `partial_max_pct`, `ladder_lock_pips`, `ladder_max_partials`). OmegaExecutor already runs `partial_close`, so no new EA is needed. The "Scaled Out" Telegram line now names the lot instead of "50%".
- feat(omega): the profile council (owner 2026-11-18, relayed: "libatkan council untuk naik profil"; confirmed in the session).
  - **Upgrades (live).** An autonomous profile upgrade now needs the council's approval after the measured gates pass.
    - Three seats (risk, strategy, trade management) review an evidence pack with no price.
    - A fixed moderator rule applies the upgrade only when the risk seat and at least two of three approve.
    - A failed council, the daily cap (`profile_council`, `PROFILE_COUNCIL_DAILY_USD`) or a second review the same day means no upgrade.
  - **Downgrades (off).** The owner's "Council boleh menolak" is built behind `OMEGA_PROFILE_COUNCIL_DOWNGRADE` (`"off"`) until a platform ADR-0020 amendment allows a veto.
    - A soft trigger holds new entries (`profile_review_pending`) until the council decides.
    - A veto keeps the profile for up to 24 h, or until the trigger worsens.
    - A failure or a late review applies the downgrade.
    - The kill switch and the daily loss limit never wait.
  - **Record.**
    - Each review is a row in `omega_profile_councils`, whose id is the session id.
    - Each review sends a Telegram line naming the verdict and each seat.
    - The change's evidence and the `risk_profile.v1` item carry the council's id and verdict.
    - Omega → Setup → Risk profile lists the last three reviews.
  - See `docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md` "Profile council".
- fix(omega): an open the terminal filled is recorded done even when its result line never reaches the Worker. Seen 2026-11-18 07:47 UTC: ticket 389746494 opened with intent `open:1794987900000:S`, its result never arrived, the intent expired at the TTL and no "Order Executed" line went to Telegram. `reconcileFilledOpens` (`src/omega-broker.mjs`) now records such an intent as done from the open position that carries its intent id (ticket, fill, `reconciled: true`), once, with an `intent_reconciled` event and the executed line ("Recorded from the open position"). A later result does not change a done intent.
- fix(nexus): the Omega page goes back to the MT5-terminal layout (owner 2026-11-18 on #444: "saya lebih suka tampilan sebelumnya dengan market watch di sebelah kiri chart. yang saya minta hanya risk profile yang jangan ditempatkan di bawah market watch"; then "header lama, spec tetap di bawah market watch"). Restored: the old header, the MT5 title bar, the toolbar, Market Watch with the symbol spec and the Navigator in a left column beside the chart, the toolbox below. Kept: the risk profile in Setup → Risk profile (not under Market Watch), Setup's sub-tabs, the dynamic broker label and DEMO/LIVE, the settings-save fix. Literal duplicates removed: "Algo Trading" shows once (the toolbar toggle), the server name no longer repeats in the header and the title bar. Added (owner): the header's profile badge carries the applied risk ("Aggressive · 5%", the other limits in its tooltip), and each position shows its risk to the SL ("SL −15.00 USD · 1.5%"), or "locked +X" once the stop is at or beyond the entry. On a phone the Market Watch column folds above the chart (toggle), no horizontal scroll.
- fix(omega): broker labels follow the connected broker (owner 2026-11-18, relayed: "label notif alert juga cek ulang harus dinamis sesuai dengan brokernya"). The Telegram line "Omega Order Executed (Monex)" was hard-coded while the account was FxPro. Every Omega line (executed, closed or scaled out, TP extended, rejected) now ends with `brokerLabel` (`src/account-spec.mjs`), e.g. "FxPro (DEMO) · GOLD": the first word of the server the terminal reports, the account mode and the broker's symbol; "broker unknown" while the spec is not validated. The Omega page preselects no broker (it follows the connected server) and its placeholders name none. Guard test `test/broker-labels.test.mjs`: no broker name in the server's code or the Omega page outside comments and the setup presets.
- feat(nexus): Omega page rebuilt for monitoring (owner 2026-11-18: "setuju, lanjutkan rebuild UI omega"). One status strip replaces the three status blocks: broker named from the connected server ("broker unknown" until its spec is validated), DEMO/LIVE, symbol, MT5, EA version, engine/armed, kill or blackout, the profile, equity and the day's P&L. The Windows-style title bar, the duplicated algo and health pills and the account readout are gone. The chart is full width at a fixed height (320 px on a phone, 460 px from sm). The symbol and account spec, Market Watch and Navigator move to Setup, which has sub-tabs: Engine, Risk profile, Connection & EA. The trade footer shows the risk and positions that apply under the profile. No horizontal scroll at 390 px. Guard test `test/nexus-omega-layout.test.mjs`.
- feat(nexus): the account profile controls move to Omega → Setup → "Risk profile" (owner 2026-11-18: "hilangkan text cosmetic", "tambahkan tombol update ... sebagai konfirmasi dari owner", "tempatkan pada menu tab terpisah di setup"). A select only edits a draft; Update names the change ("Profile Aggressive → Custom, Ceiling Aggressive → Custom. Confirm?") and applies it after Confirm, in an order the server accepts; it is disabled while nothing changed. The monitoring view keeps one profile line and two badges (spec, EA version) with their detail in tooltips; the explanatory paragraphs are gone (the rules are in the panel's (i)). The engine card shows the applied limits in one line.
- fix(omega): the engine settings could not be saved from Nexus. The form's browser range check (risk max 2) silently cancelled a save, and the card did not say what the account profile applies. The form now leaves the checks to the server, which says why it refuses, and shows the limits the profile applies now. The risk setting goes to 10% (owner 2026-11-18: "Naikkan ke 10%, dikunci profil"): what applies is the smaller of the setting and the profile's target (Safe 1%, Conservative 2%, Aggressive 5%), and Custom uses the setting itself. `RISK_PCT_MAX` is 10 (it was 2), so the doctrine fact `omega.risk_pct_max` reported to Aether changes with it.
- fix(ea): OmegaExecutor 1.16. 1.15 refused every open on the FxPro demo (`refused: margin_unknown`, four intents 05:12-05:47 UTC): under unlimited leverage (1:2,000,000,000) 0.01 lot needs about USD 0.004, which the terminal rounds to 0.00, and 1.15 read a margin of 0 as unknown. 1.16 refuses only when `OrderCalcMargin` itself fails (`refused: margin_unknown_<error>`) and leaves the margin to `OrderCheck`. The server's minimum EA version for entries is now 1.16 (`ea_outdated` for 1.15, so no order the EA can only refuse is sent), and a margin per lot of 0 is a valid spec.
- feat(omega): Account Profiles phase 3, the profile agent (`src/profile-agent.mjs`, `docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md` §10, ADR-0020 Amendment 3, merged on the platform 2026-11-18). Ladder safe < conservative < aggressive < custom. Downgrades are never blocked and apply to new entries at once, in every agent mode: the kill switch or the daily loss sends the account to Safe (warning; no autonomous upgrade before the next UTC day); a 20% drawdown, 4 losses in a row or an M15 ATR of 1000 points step down once each, with hysteresis. Upgrades go one step on evidence (equity at or above the prior balance peak, 20+ closed deals since the switch with a positive mean), only below the owner's ceiling, never with a position or order open, at most one per 7 days; approval mode makes them proposals. The ceiling, profile, agent mode and proposals are the owner's (`POST /omega/api/account-profile`, same origin, checked twice). Each switch is recorded in D1 `omega_profile_changes`, as an Omega event and a Telegram line, and in a `risk_profile.v1` digest with the opaque account reference only. The Omega page has the controls.
- fix(nexus): the Omega bridge card shows a reported leverage of 1:100,000 or more as "unlimited" (the FxPro demo reports 1:2,000,000,000). Leverage is no input to sizing; the margin check is the broker's `OrderCheck` in OmegaExecutor 1.15.
- feat(omega): Account Profiles phase 2, sizing within the profile (`docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md` §3-4). Each limit (risk per trade, positions, daily loss) is the smaller of the profile's cap and the setting; Custom keeps the settings unchanged, so the live account sizes as before. Below the broker's minimum lot, the minimum lot is used only when its real risk is within the profile's hard cap (`min_lot_within_cap`), otherwise the setup is skipped with the numbers (`volume_below_min`, e.g. "the minimum lot 0.01 risks 5% of 100, over the Safe cap of 2%"). The profile's total open risk caps the lot (`lot_capped_open_risk`) or skips the entry (`open_risk_cap`). The Omega page shows each reason with its numbers. `OMEGA_EA_VERSION_GATE` is now "on": OmegaExecutor 1.15 runs on the VPS (seen 04:17 UTC, its spec validated 04:18 UTC), so an older EA opens nothing (`ea_outdated`).
- feat(omega): Account Profiles phase 1 (`docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md`, owner 2026-11-18: live with gates). OmegaExecutor 1.15 reports leverage, margin mode, free margin, freeze level, the symbol's trade mode, the margin per lot and an opaque login hash, and is the last gate: before an open it checks the trade mode, the stops level, `OrderCalcMargin` and `OrderCheck`; before a stop or target change the freeze level; a failure is `refused: <what>`, never a resize or a moved stop. The server (`src/account-spec.mjs`, D1 `omega_accounts`) stores each account's spec with its hash under an opaque reference: the first usable spec validates at once, a changed spec opens nothing (`spec_unvalidated`) until it is usable and has held 60 s, open positions keep their management; events and a Telegram line on each change. The live account is stored as Custom (its current settings), ceiling Custom, autonomous; phase 1 changes no lot size. `OMEGA_EA_VERSION_GATE` (`ea_outdated` for an EA before 1.15) ships off until the owner has compiled 1.15 on the VPS. The Omega page shows the spec, the profile and the gate.
- fix(omega): `POST /api/settings` (risk %, max positions, daily loss, enable and arm) is owner only, like the strategy arm: `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB` must match the Access user id, and the request must be same origin. Without an owner configured, nobody can change them. Before, any signed-in person could (docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md §9; requested by the orchestrator under ADR-0020 Amendment 3's gates). The D1 `omega_events` row still audits each change.
- docs(rfc): `docs/RFC_ACCOUNT_PROFILES.md`, Account Profiles & Dynamic Mode for Omega. Owner decisions (2026-11-18): live with gates and automatic rollback, no shadow phase; the live account starts as Custom (its current D1 settings: risk 2%, 5 positions, daily loss 10%); the profile agent runs autonomously with the proposed guardrails and a ceiling of Custom. Four phases: EA spec and checks (EA 1.15); sizing within profile caps; the profile agent; the dynamic mode selector and an R-based position manager.
- fix(news-shadow): the daily news comparison runs while the day's shadow spend leaves a 600-neuron reserve under the shadow budget (its two readings take about 160), instead of only under 80% like the model arena. On 2026-11-17 the shadow arms had used 24,200 of 25,000 neurons by 21:00 UTC, so the first comparison was skipped every hour (`shadow_budget_headroom`) and `news_shadow_compare` stayed empty. It still never pushes the day over the budget, and the shadow switch and breaker still hold it.

## 2026-11-17
- feat(council): the return path is on. The platform's V3 is live on the Forge and the AI Kernel (83a352e, relayed 2026-11-17 22:35 UTC), so `organism.json` grants xauusd-moderator `council.decisions.read` (the platform's `XAUUSD_CEILING` allows it to the moderator alone; the census raises `agent_capabilities_changed` and the routine `update_agent_capabilities` grants it) and `COUNCIL_DECISIONS_POLL` is "on". Until the platform's migration 0077 is deployed its intake records nothing, so the read returns an empty list; then the vol_filter proposal should show as waiting_external. Also a guard test (`test/nexus-tailwind-guard.test.mjs`): no `shadow-sm` in the Nexus UI source, and the live "shadow" values the Tailwind upgrade tool once rewrote stay intact. A check of #432 found none left: the word "shadow" occurs 84 times in the UI source before and after it, and its fresh build has no `shadow-sm`.
- build(nexus-ui): vite is replaced by esbuild and Tailwind 3 by Tailwind 4 (owner 2026-11-17, picked over an audit exception). npm advisory GHSA-fc5q-39xc-vjpf (picomatch, high, no fixed release) reached the Nexus UI through vite (every release, 6 to 8, depends on picomatch) and Tailwind 3, and `dependency-audit`, a required check, failed every PR. `ui/build.mjs` bundles `src/main.tsx` with esbuild, runs `src/index.css` through PostCSS with `@tailwindcss/postcss`, and writes `dist/index.html` with the hashed `/assets/` script and stylesheet (no inline script, as before); `vite.config.ts` and `postcss.config.js` are gone, `tailwind.config.js` stays (loaded with `@config`). The classes Tailwind 4 renamed are renamed (`rounded`→`rounded-sm`, `rounded-sm`→`rounded-xs`, `outline-none`→`outline-hidden`, `outline`→`outline-solid`, `bg-gradient-to-*`→`bg-linear-to-*`, `backdrop-blur-sm`→`backdrop-blur-xs`, `shadow-sm`→`shadow-xs`, `bg-white/[0.0n]`→`bg-white/n`); the upgrade tool also renamed the word "shadow" in texts and in one status comparison, which was undone. v3's default border colour and button cursor are kept in `@layer base`. The UI's npm audit: 11 high → 0. Fifteen pages, screenshotted against the vite build with the API mocked: the same layout; Tailwind 4's palette (oklch) is slightly more vivid.
- feat(council): the platform's return path for accepted proposals, prepared behind `COUNCIL_DECISIONS_POLL` ("off"). Once an hour (`src/council-decisions.mjs`), xauusd-moderator reads `council.decisions.read` through `AGENTS.observe` from the stored cursor (at most 5 pages of 20), keeps the `typed_proposal.v1` updates about this repo's proposals (`council_proposal_updates`: state, reason, the known refs, at) and skips the rest. `GET /api/council/proposals` adds each proposal's latest platform state, and Nexus shows it beside the proposal (received, queued, waiting_external, in progress, merged, deployed, refused, reverted and so on). Nothing xauusd does changes with it. The capability is not in organism.json yet: the platform (relayed 2026-11-17) asks that it be added only once V3 is live on the Forge and the AI Kernel, or the census refuses the organ; then the flag is turned on in the same PR.
- docs(backlog): B-11 (issue #226) marked done; it was built and is live (`GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED`, the extended witnesses, the witness-weight auto-tune with rollback).
- fix(nexus): themed confirmations instead of the browser's own pop-ups (owner 2026-11-17: "windows pop up agar menyesuaikan tema utama"). A reusable `ConfirmDialog` / `useConfirm` (`components/ConfirmDialog.tsx`), in the style of the Omega close-position modal, with Esc to cancel. On the Councils page: Accept (it says what happens for a knob change, a code task or no change), Close and turning sessions off. No native `confirm`/`alert`/`prompt` is left in the UI.
- fix(councils): a code task's `typed_proposal.v1` report carries `state: "handed_off"`, the state it has once the platform holds it (the first one said `handoff_pending`).
- feat(councils): council proposals, phase 2 (docs/RFC_COUNCIL_PROPOSALS.md; owner: "Live otomatis + rollback otomatis").
  - **Shadow test.** An accepted knob change is replayed over 60 days of decisions in plan-R and checked against grid-adapt's gate.
  - **Live and rollback.** A value that passes goes live by itself (KV `council-knobs:v1`, read by the Decision Engine at each decision, clamped to the bounds). It rolls back by itself when the decisions since then show the old value would have done better, and is never tried again.
  - **Reporting.** Each step sends a Telegram line and a platform state update.
  - **Registry.** It lists only the 6 testable Decision Engine knobs; `near_atr` and `max_risk_atr` become repository tasks.
  - **Value in force.** The moderator and Accept compare against the value in force.
  - **Page.** The Councils page shows the live knob values.
- feat(councils): council proposals, phase 1 (docs/RFC_COUNCIL_PROPOSALS.md; the owner confirmed in the session: "Nexus dulu, Cerebrum nanti", "Live otomatis + rollback otomatis", and code changes "dikerjakan oleh cryptophys bukan dari external").
  - **Typed options.** The moderator writes each option as a `policy_parameter` (a listed knob of `src/council-knobs.mjs` with a value inside its bounds), a `repository_task` (new code, with a spec) or `no_change`. An invalid knob change becomes a `repository_task`.
  - **Accept.** The owner-only Accept in Nexus decides the session, stores the proposal (D1 `council_proposals`) and sends a `typed_proposal.v1` report to the platform (`xauusd-moderator`). A failed send is retried hourly.
  - **Destinations by kind.** A `repository_task` carries `executor: "cryptophys"` and is meant for the platform's evolution loop, never for Claude. A knob change waits for its shadow test (phase 2).
  - **Evidence the seats can use.** The seats get a fair share of every pack section, where before only the first 7,000 characters were sent, plus up to 30 citable refs. A ref without its `@as_of` part still counts.
  - **Reading.** `GET /api/council/proposals` lists the accepted proposals.
- fix(councils-page): the sessions switch reads as an action, and a closed session no longer fills the screen (owner 2026-11-17: "sudah saya re-open, cek pendapat agen masih ngacau dan gak dibersihkan layarnya"). D1 showed the switch turned off at 14:15:43, on at 14:16:12 and off again at 14:16:15, so the Re-open was refused ("council sessions are off") and no new session started; the old empty session's cards stayed on the page. Now the state ("On"/"Off") and the action ("Turn on"/"Turn off", with a confirm before turning off) are separate; Re-open is disabled while sessions are off and says so; a closed session's messages are hidden behind "Show them"; the page opens the newest live session by default and starts clean when every session is closed.
- feat(councils): the owner's **Close** and **Re-open with fresh evidence** per session (owner 2026-11-17, confirmed in the session: "Ya, buat keduanya"; closed sessions "Tidak dihitung" toward the 4 a day; re-open "Ya, tampilkan"). Close ends a session (state `closed`, reason `owner_closed`) and frees its slot; Re-open starts the same topic and seats on a fresh pack, then closes the old one. Why: "kenapa tidak bisa reset" (there was no way to end a session), and the owner's question at 13:10 was never answered: the addressed seat's answer used all 700 tokens, was cut off, and was dropped without a trace. Now an answer to the owner gets 1,400 tokens; a seat that cannot answer leaves a visible notice (`round = 'notice'`) with the reason; a message must address 1 or 2 seats; a message to a session that ran on no evidence is refused with the offer to re-open; the page flags a pack that is empty or older than 24 hours. The daily neuron cap (8,000) still bounds the cost. Owner-only `POST /omega/api/council` actions `close` and `reopen`.
- config(strategy-council): `STRATEGY_COUNCIL_ARM` "allowed". Platform ADR-0020 Amendment 2 (Council auto-arm) merged 2026-11-17 (platform main 6df9a34, the owner's merge_owner_change), so the code ceiling is raised and the owner's "Council auto-arm" toggle on the Strategy Agents page can act. The toggle stays off until the owner turns it on (owner-only, audited); nothing is armed by this change. Each click of the toggle is now audited in D1 `strategy_council_toggle_events` (on/off, actor, Access user id), as an arm or disarm is. Gates unchanged: walk-forward pass to arm, at most 3 armed, one change per strategy per UTC day, no council arm on a day the system auto-disarmed (kill switch, daily loss) or while an arm is blocked, the owner's manual arm or disarm pins the strategy, a failed council changes nothing, every action is an arm event with actor `council`, a Telegram line and a `strategy_arm.v1` item (`council_auto`).
- docs(agents): hard rule 9, everything built or emitted is English (owner 2026-11-17; confirmed in the session). A sweep found no Indonesian UI or output string (#423).
- fix(councils): a free topic reads a default evidence pack (the 15m engine, the Zone pullback exits, the fills, plus packs chosen by words in the text, the strategy council's verdicts and the arm state); a session on a pack with no ref is refused before any call and does not count toward the 4 a day; the strategy pack cites the plan learner's state only when it is stored (#422).
- feat(councils): Call for Councils P2 sessions (owner 2026-11-17: "pendapat masing-masing agent bukan detail evidences"; confirmed "Setuju + topik bebas"). Each agent's own opinion as a card (stance, first-person view, what it would try; evidence behind a link), rebuttals by name, the moderator's options with `no_change`, the owner's messages answered; sessions on a suggested topic or the owner's free text; 16 calls a session; owner switch (off by default) and `CFC_MODE` "on"; the options go to Cerebrum (`council_decision.v1`) for the pick. See RFC_CALL_FOR_COUNCILS §12.
- feat(strategy-council): the cards' `verdicts` (daily walk-forward per strategy and a council proposal) and the owner's "Council auto-arm" toggle (owner 2026-11-17: "council boleh langsung arm/disarm otomatis jika owner mengaktifkan mode auto"; confirmed "Setuju, kunci manual permanen"). The council arms or disarms only while `STRATEGY_COUNCIL_ARM` is "allowed" (off until platform ADR-0020 Amendment 2) and the owner's toggle is on, within max 3, one change a day, no arm after a system disarm, never a strategy the owner pinned by a manual click (until unpinned). See RFC_STRATEGY_RECOMMENDATION.
- feat(grid-adapt): automatic entry/stop per strategy x 1H regime from the Entry and Stop Grid (owner 2026-11-17, relayed: "keputusan jangan ditangan saya, council strategy agent yang harus otomatis menyesuaikan"; confirmed in the session: "Setuju, bangun begitu"). `src/grid-adapt.mjs`, once a UTC day on the ledger step: a cell is used only with >= 40 fills, a per-setup gain over the plan in R of the PLAN's stop on the same setups above +0.05 with a bootstrap 2.5% bound above 0, a positive latest-quarter holdout and agreeing neighbours; hysteresis 0.05R; a slice whose last 10 live grid trades lose reverts and is held 7 days; one Nexus Telegram line per change. The signal moves entry/stop (the Decision Engine's plan and the learner are unchanged); Omega records `stop_source = grid:<cell>`. `GRID_ADAPT_MODE` "live". On 2026-11-17's data no slice passes: the plan everywhere (the grid's +1.34R cell was 9 setups in its own R; -0.50R per setup against the plan in plan R).
- feat(strategy-agents): recommendation cards (RFC §3; owner 2026-11-17 "Ya, semua, live"). `src/strategy-recommendation.mjs` builds one deterministic, read-only card per strategy (record overall and in the present context, R distribution, Omega's stop/locked-stop shares, real Omega entries and closes, breaker, arm) with `recommended` / `neutral` / `not_recommended` and every rule checked with its evidence ref; KV `strategy-recommendations:v1`, served by `GET /api/strategy-agents` and shown on the Nexus Strategy Agents cards. No price field.
- feat(strategy-arm): the platform record `strategy_arm.v1` (RFC §10; owner 2026-11-17 "Ya, semua, live"; platform ADR-0020 Amendment 1). `src/strategy-arm-relay.mjs` publishes the arm/disarm events and the Decision Engine's strategy picks (mode, chosen strategy, confidence, confluence) as one `xauusd_digest` report from `xauusd-risk-manager` at most every 30 minutes; the cursor moves only on success. New doctrine facts `omega.strategy_arm_required` and `omega.max_armed_strategies`.
- feat(strategy-arm): attribution and migration (RFC §4/§5; owner 2026-11-17 "Ya, semua, live"). `ea_deals.strategy` is filled when a deal is first stored, from the Omega open intent whose result ticket is the deal's position. `migrations/0025_strategy_arm.sql` records the `strategy_arm_state`, `strategy_arms` and `strategy_arm_events` tables (the same statements as the run-time schema; production applies no migration).
- feat(omega): the strategy arm's position gates (RFC §5; owner 2026-11-17 "Ya, semua, live"). While the arm is live, `planIntents` refuses an entry when the signal's strategy already holds an Omega position (`strategy_has_position`) or an Omega position on the other side is open or closing (`opposing_position`). Nexus explains both on the Omega "why" panel.
- feat(strategy-arm): owner-only arm/disarm (RFC §11.3; owner 2026-11-17 "Ya, semua, live"). The Omega route compares the Access JWT `sub` with `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB` (403 `owner_only`), the AI Worker's `setStrategyArm` checks `actor_sub` again, and Nexus hides the button when `can_arm` is false. While the var is unset (bootstrap) behaviour is unchanged and each visitor's sub is recorded once in `omega_events` (`owner_sub_seen`), so a follow-up PR can set it.
- feat(decision-engine): the hourly "still <stance>" heartbeat goes to the Cryptophys-Cerebrum chat (owner 2026-11-17: only the heartbeat moves; trading information stays in Nexus; no silent fallback). `notifyDecision` publishes it as a kernel report (`xauusd-reasoner`, kind `xauusd_digest`, severity `info`, `body.telegram_topic = "ea_heartbeat"`, plain text) instead of the Nexus send; a failure is logged (`decision_heartbeat_relay_failed`) and not retried until the next hour. See `docs/SSOT.md` §1.
- feat(news): measurement phase B for the news shadow (owner 2026-11-17, relayed: "A dan B"). Once a UTC day after 21:00 the AI Worker reads the last 24 hours of headlines twice with the news analyst's prompt, TradingView's set and the free sources' set (the news Worker's new read-only RPC `shadowItems`), as a small structured answer each (gold and dollar bias, impact, up to three catalysts), and keeps both in D1 `news_shadow_compare` with their agreement; `GET /api/news/shadow-compare` gives the rows and the agreement rates. Two calls a day metered as `shadow:news_compare` (the shadow switch and its daily neuron breaker hold it; it also waits past 80% of the shadow budget); about 160 neurons a day, below USD 0.002. `NEWS_SHADOW_COMPARE` "off" stops it. It feeds no room, decision or News Daily.
- feat(news): measurement phase A for the news shadow (owner 2026-11-17, relayed: "setuju c, bandingkan dulu", then "A dan B"). Every hour each relevant shadow headline and each TradingView headline is kept once per UTC day with its source, address, publication time and first-seen time, 14 days in KV (`news:shadow:items:<date>`), plus the hour's TradingView pull status (ok, empty, or why it was refused: `reserved_for_chart`, `news_daily_cap`, a cooldown). The day totals gain per-source items, relevant items, rate limits, timeouts and response time, and the duplicates dropped. `GET /shadow/report?days=N` (1-14, read-only, public headlines only) computes coverage, overlap (same address, or a title similarity of at least 0.8 within six hours), latency (first seen less published) median and p90 per source and for TradingView, the shadow's lead over TradingView, duplicates, relevance per source, the script mix, the high-impact stories one side carried and the other missed, and per-source reliability with an `unreliable` flag (GDELT today). Measurement only: nothing reads these items for rooms, decisions or News Daily, and the news path is unchanged.
- docs(rfc): `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_RECOMMENDATION.md` records that platform ADR-0020 "Standing trading authorization (arm)" is merged and in effect (owner's `merge_owner_change` `cns_7ed29426`, 2026-11-17 01:08 UTC, platform main `e051489`, relayed). The P0 precondition holds; P2 (shadow) and P3 (live) still start only on the owner's word, behind `STRATEGY_ARM_MODE`, with every ADR-0020 guard. No code.
- feat(quota): one TradingView budget for the account, chart first (owner 2026-11-17, relayed: "setuju perbaikan alert dan kuota"; task #89). On 2026-11-16 the chart alone had made 443 calls by 23:45 UTC and the provider refused the 501st call of the day, with news and the calendar beside it. Every provider call passes the gateway (`fundamental-news-ingestion`), which now admits each job against today's calls of every caller (`admitCalls`, `src/provider-usage.mjs`): the account cap is the quota less a safety margin (500 - 20 = 480, for calls outside this system and the counts' floor error); the chart (pull adapter, gold's 1H history) is admitted up to the cap; the calendar within 30 a day and only while the chart's need to midnight fits; news last, within 60 a day and only while the chart's need and the calendar's rest fit. A refused chart job answers `account_budget_exhausted` until the next UTC midnight, which the pull adapter treats like the provider's quota (no budget spent, cooldown kept, the MT5 fallback takes over); news and the calendar wait. The chart's reserve assumes 18 calls an hour on a weekday before the day has a completed hour (it let news run unchecked at 00:xx UTC). The pull adapter's own budget is the cap (`TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET` 480). `/health` and `gatewayStatus()` report `account_budget` (cap, chart calls and need, calendar left, news room, headroom). `TV_ACCOUNT_BUDGET` "off" leaves the check out. Steady weekday chart demand is about 420 calls (measured 443 on Monday 11-16 with the reopen), so the expected headroom is about 30-60 calls a day, most of it news.
- fix(alerts): the MT5 fallback alert says the real cause instead of "TradingView is down" (owner 2026-11-17, relayed: "setuju perbaikan alert dan kuota"; on 2026-11-16 23:45 UTC the account's TradingView quota had run out, back at 00:00). `fallbackCause` (`src/feed.mjs`) reads the gateway's cooldown and reason, OAuth and the pull adapter's last refresh: the quota used up, a rate limit or pause, the chart's daily budget, OAuth, or a real outage ("TradingView is down or not answering", the only case that says down). The `market_data_fallback` subject (and so its Telegram message) names the broker source (e.g. `fxpro-mt5 demo GOLD`) and when TradingView is expected back; the War Room card "Data source · MT5 fallback" shows the same line; the owner-switch Telegram text and the council's fallback caution no longer claim an outage.
- fix(omega): USD gold only, at every layer (owner 2026-11-17, relayed by the orchestrator: "setuju", then "b", PR #405 hardened before merge). OmegaExecutor 1.14 trades a symbol only when its name is on one exact list (shared with the bridge and `src/gold-symbol.mjs`), its profit currency is USD and its contract size is 1-100 oz; otherwise it trades nothing and reports `symbol_error` (no prefix match, so `GOLDEURO` is refused; no fallback to a priceless or the chart symbol). omega-bridge 1.3.0 remembers a chart symbol only for a verified EA, never forgets a proven one for a quiet terminal, ends an exhausted or login-failed search sticky (no restart loop) and reports `bridge_symbol` with each sync. The server opens nothing while the symbol is unverified (`symbol_unverified`, so an EA before 1.14 opens nothing), shows the check on the Omega page and `/api/health`, records `symbol_changed` / `symbol_refused` / `bridge_symbol` events and raises the health alert `omega_symbol`. The profile drop-in fails loudly on an empty `WINEPREFIX`. Also in PR #405: the bridge picks the gold chart symbol per broker server and gives the EA its own chart profile (bridge 1.2.0).

## 2026-11-16
- docs(rfc): Strategy Recommendation / Armed: the owner's answers ("1–10 setuju"): `OWNER_ACCESS_SUB` now; open positions stay managed to close on disarm; auto-disarm also on the breaker and the engine toggle, a lost executor only blocks entries; after an `opposite` close the next decision only; `DECISION_ENGINE = "off"` trades nothing; a promoted change keeps the arm; reports by `xauusd-risk-manager` with three more reasons; a Telegram line in the Nexus chat; the default thresholds re-checked after a month. P2 and P3 wait for platform ADR-0020.
- feat(councils): Call for Councils phase P1, evidence only (owner 2026-11-16: "tahap P1 mulai"; `docs/RFC_CALL_FOR_COUNCILS.md` §12). No model call, no session, no write route, no change to any trading or decision path. `src/cfc-pack.mjs` checks a topic key strictly against the topic grammar of RFC §1.0 (every strategy agent and every pattern it trades, seats, the engine, risk by session, mode or regime, news, execution, data, cost, knowledge, free) and builds its evidence pack (`council_pack.v1`) from the tables that exist today, bounded and by index: for a strategy topic the agent's resolved trades with both SL+ definitions side by side (Omega's locked stop the headline, the ledger's breakeven beside it), the limit entry at the key level (`ENTER_LIMIT`) apart from the market entry, excursions, the stop distance in ATR, the plan learner's arms and record, held decisions, outcome scores, fills and releases; a section without a source says `unavailable: <reason>`. Every price field is dropped; refs follow `ev:xauusd/<source>/<id>[@<as_of>][#<field>]`; the sha256 is that of the canonical JSON. `src/cfc-triggers.mjs` runs the deterministic suggestion flags of RFC §1.2 for every type once an hour in the parity sweep (skipped while the market is closed): the strategy rule is the last 20 resolved trades ending at the stop or SL+ 65 % of the time or more, under Omega's policy, the ledger's plan or the plan learner's replay; one open suggestion per topic, a 7-day fingerprint cooldown, at most 3 new a day; stored in D1 `cfc_suggestions` (migration `0024`, also made at run time). `CFC_MODE` (`suggest` by default, `off`, `on` kept for P2). Read-only routes `GET /api/council/suggestions` and `GET /api/council/evidence?topic=`; the Nexus page **Call for Councils** (`#/councils`) with its own navigator item, nothing on the Omega page. See `docs/SSOT.md` "Call for Councils, phase P1".
- docs(rfc): `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_RECOMMENDATION.md`, Strategy Recommendation / Armed (owner 2026-11-16: "1b, 2tidak, maks 3strategi, ya arm disarm cukup 1 click, jika tidak dipilih maka no position, ya.."; governing decision: platform ADR-0020 "Standing trading authorization (arm)"). Draft for owner review; no code. Armed is a new layer over "live": an armed strategy may own Omega entries and closes, at most 3 armed, one click to arm or disarm on the Strategy Agents page (owner only, CSRF, server-side check, append-only audit), no strategy armed means no new position, the Decision Engine keeps its veto, auto-disarm on the kill switch or the daily loss limit; one `strategy_arm.v1` report per event, best effort. Ten open questions.
- docs(rfc): `docs/RFC_CALL_FOR_COUNCILS.md`, the Nexus side of "Call for Councils" (owner 2026-11-16, relayed by the orchestrator: the owner calls the relevant agents into a retrospective on a topic, e.g. the Key Level entry often stopped at SL or SL+, and together they choose what to try). Draft for owner review; no code. Aligned with the shared contract `council.v1` and the owner's HAIC decisions (picks in Cerebrum only, a shadow window of 14 days and 30 outcomes, councils started by the owner only, transcripts 90 days hot then R2); the page is its own Nexus navigator item, Call for Councils, never on the Omega page (owner 2026-11-16). A session fits the contract's 16 calls (6 participants, one rebuttal round). Topics are general (owner 2026-11-16: "jangan persempit call for action hanya ke salah satu agent strategy"): any strategy agent or pattern, any room seat, the Decision Engine, risk by session or regime, news handling, Omega/EA execution, data quality, cost, knowledge, or a free topic, each with its own evidence pack and suggestion flags. A council only produces a proposal, a proposal only starts a shadow measurement with criteria fixed up front, and only the owner turns a result live; the council is never a witness or decision evidence. Covers triggers (the owner, and deterministic flags from the breakers, the Signal Ledger, the plan learner, outcome scoring and reputation), seats per topic from the real roster, a frozen evidence pack every claim must cite, the session flow, storage per ADR-0015 (one D1 row, transcript in R2), audit, cost caps reusing the shadow budget and switch, placeholders for the shared Cerebrum contract, owner corrections per ADR-0017 decision 4 (never refused, deduplicated by content hash, high weight to Aether, a bounded reputation term), and nine open questions.
- feat(shadow): the shadow knowledge arm tests 6 learned notes at once (was 2), and its hourly call cap doubles to 32 (at most 48) (owner 2026-11-16: "opsi A"). Six curator items of 2026-11-16 (three role reminders, three New York cautions) sat in the pack with 0 samples because the two oldest items held both slots until their verdict (143 and 96 of 200 discovery bars on 2026-11-16). The kill switch and the daily neuron breaker still bound every shadow call.
- chore(ci): `dependency-audit` is a required check on `main` (BACKLOG B-14). The owner added it to the live "main protection" ruleset, and `protection-guard` failed every PR until `ops/branch-ruleset.json` declared it ("main requires dependency-audit, which ops/branch-ruleset.json does not declare"); the declaration, its test, the workflow note, SSOT and BACKLOG now follow.
- feat(omega): EA health to the Cryptophys-Cerebrum chat through the platform kernel (owner 2026-11-16: "info terkait HeartBeat EA di Telegram jangan masuk ke Cryptophys-Nexus, tapi ke Cryptophys-Cerebrum"; "pakai cara 2 lewat kernel"; "pindahkan yang nomor 1 saja"). The "Cerebrum Self-Healing Action" message no longer goes to the Nexus chat. `src/ea-heartbeat-relay.mjs` publishes the recoveries recorded in `omega_events` as one `xauusd_digest` report from `xauusd-risk-manager` with `body.telegram_topic = "ea_heartbeat"`, at most one per 30 minutes; the kernel forwards it to "Cerebrum alerts" (platform CR claude/ea-heartbeat-relay). Every other Telegram message stays in Nexus. See `docs/SSOT.md` §1.

## 2026-11-15
- chore(ci): `main` is protected by the repository ruleset declared in `ops/branch-ruleset.json` (owner 2026-11-15: "kerjakan hardening branch protection xauusd"; #377; applied by the owner as ruleset 27335916, since the automation cannot write rulesets). The ruleset blocks deletion and force pushes and requires linear history and a pull request with 0 approvals. Its required checks must pass on an up-to-date branch: `evidence-unit-tests`, `preview-e2e` and `protection-guard`. No bypass actor. `test-evidence-fusion.yml` runs on every PR, and its job is now named `evidence-unit-tests`. `deploy-preview-worker.yml` skips `preview-e2e` (which then passes) when no preview path changed. `branch-protection-guard.yml` fails when main's live rules differ from the declaration, or when a required check is no longer exactly one job that reports on every PR. See `docs/SSOT.md` §5 rule 7.
- fix(d1): the AI Worker archives D1 `bus_messages` and drops it (owner 2026-11-15: "setujui semua"; #375, #376). The workflow route failed on its first D1 call because the CI token has no D1 access; nothing was written. `src/bus-archive.mjs` runs on the hourly cron through the new R2 binding `ROOM_TRANSCRIPTS`. At 16:06 UTC it wrote 335 rows (434,782 bytes, sha256 `d9e15f48…a81f1eff1`) to `xauusd-room-transcripts/archive/d1/bus_messages.ndjson`, read them back equal, and dropped the table and `idx_bus_candle`. `archive-bus-messages.yml` now only reads the archive back from R2. Migration `0023` drops the table from fresh databases.
- fix(deploy): the Nexus, Omega, News and Market Data deploy workflows (and their dry-runs and `provision-r2.yml`) pin `wrangler@4.156.2`, because `wrangler@4.45.0` rejected `--tag` / `--message` and those four P5 deploys had failed (#373). Test D5 fails any deploy line pinned below it.
- refactor(api): the authenticated `/v1/analyze` evidence-bundle path is removed (owner 2026-11-15: "setujui semua"; no call since 2026-11-11, no caller in this repository or the platform's). Gone: `handleAnalyze`, `processAnalysisTask` and `runAnalysisFromBundle` (its Analyst/Counselor/Reasoner chain), the route and its replay cache; a stale `analysis` queue task is acknowledged and logged. The engines `h1_analyst` / `h1_counselor` leave `organism.json`. The hourly news chain's Reasoner is metered as `news_reasoner` (it shared `h1_reasoner` with the H1 Reasoner, which keeps it). `lib/` contracts stay for the webhook path.
- feat(rooms): ADR-0015 phase 2a in shadow (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya", item c; `docs/RFC_ROOM_CELLS.md`). Once an hour (`ROOM_RUNS_SHADOW` = `on`) the concluded runs of the 15m War Room and the 1H/4H/D1 rooms of the last 48 hours are derived from their stored messages into `room_runs` (one row per run: room, run, candle, bar close, stance, bias, confidence, counts, usage, and a JSON body with every agent's final position and the conclusion; migration `0022`, also made at run time). `compareRoomRuns` then checks, for the 15m and 1H rooms, that the statements outcome scoring would build from the documents equal the ones it reads from `agent_thread_messages`, and stores the counts in `room_runs_compare` (`GET /api/room-runs/status`). Nothing reads the documents yet; no reader moves and no room changes. `transcript_key` stays null until the R2 bucket is bound.
- chore(r2): `provision-r2.yml` creates the private R2 bucket `xauusd-room-transcripts` for the rooms' transcripts (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya", item d; `docs/RFC_ROOM_CELLS.md` §4). Idempotent, runs once on merge (or by hand), no public access, no lifecycle rule. The AI Worker binds the bucket in a later PR, after it exists.
- chore(deploy): version provenance for every Worker (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju P5"). Each production deploy (`deploy-worker`, `deploy-nexus-dashboard`, `deploy-omega`, `deploy-fundamental-news-ingestion`, `deploy-market-data-ingestion`) tags its version with the commit's first 12 characters (`--tag`), names the commit (`--message`) and injects `DEPLOY_COMMIT` / `DEPLOYED_AT`. `/health` of all five Workers returns `commit` (12) and `deployed_at` (Nexus gains a `/health`). The OrganismDna RPC adds `deployed_commit` (40 hex) and `deployed_at` to the AI Worker's own organ (the platform's field contract); without a valid sha both are omitted. No behaviour change.
- feat(api): `GET /api/agents/inventory` (`xauusd-agents-inventory.v1`) and `GET /api/ai/logic` (`xauusd-ai-logic.v1`), generated from the code (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya", items a and P4; `src/agent-inventory.mjs`). The inventory lists the 28 room seats from the code rosters (War Room desks and HUD seats, 1H/4H/D1 desks, macro agents, the news thread, moderator, system) with their rooms, role models, triggers, switches, tables and kernel ids, plus each one's last message and 7-day count from D1. The matrix lists every AI task of `organism.json` with its source, trigger, model, inputs, output, validation and consumers, and its use in `ai_usage_daily` under all its meter labels (`<task>`, `shadow:<task>`, `shadow:arena:<task>`), with a verdict (active, dormant_flagged, declared_only). The DNA links the inventory with the top-level `agents_inventory` field (the platform's contract; never inside an organ, the seats are not kernel agents). `test/agent-inventory.test.mjs` keeps both equal to the rosters and the DNA. Read-only.
- chore(meter): the hourly news chain's Analyst and Counselor are metered as `news_analyst` / `news_counselor` (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya"; they were labelled `h1_analyst` / `h1_counselor`, which read as the retired H1 chain), declared in `organism.json`. Since 2026-11-11 all 53 calls under the old labels were the news chain's (56 news analyses). The authenticated `/v1/analyze` bundle path (`runAnalysisFromBundle`) is still reachable and keeps the `h1_*` labels; it made no call in that window. No behaviour change.
- refactor(agents): the per-H1 HUD agents are retired in code (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya"; none ran since the War Room, 2026-11-05: `enqueueHudAgents` returned early whenever `WAR_ROOM_ENABLED` was on). Removed: `runHudAgents`, `buildHudAgentsTask`, their prompts, schemas and validator, `HUD_AGENTS_ENABLED` and `HUD_AGENTS_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR`, and the engines `hud_agent` / `hud_moderator` from `organism.json`. A stale `hud_agents` queue task is acknowledged. The HUDs speak as War Room and 1H/4H/D1 room seats (`hudEvidenceLines` stays); stored Threads 1H HUD messages stay readable. The retried-step and refs-rule tests now call the step machinery directly.
- refactor(agents): the legacy Live 15m room is retired in code (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya"; the War Room has met in its place since 2026-11-05, #98). Removed: `runLiveRoomBar`, its agent and moderator schemas and prompts, and its four live HUD seats; the engines `room_agent`, `room_moderator` and `live_hud_agent` leave `organism.json`. A `live_room` task runs the War Room, or is skipped and logged when `WAR_ROOM_ENABLED` is off. Kept: `buildLiveRoomTask`, `LIVE_ROOM_ENABLED` (it still gates enqueueing the 15m task), `liveEvidence` (the War Room's evidence) and `readLive` (stored rows). The tests of the shared step machinery (corrections, the moderator's fallback, budgets) and of `liveEvidence` now call them directly.
- refactor(agents): the H1 multi-agent bus is retired in code (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju semuanya"; no producer since 2026-11-07). `callBusModel` and the news thread's schemas, validators and evidence move to `src/model-call.mjs` (its fallback, timeout and rate-limit tests move to `test/model-call.test.mjs`, the runner-based ones now call it directly). Removed: the bus runner, `GET /api/bus`, the `bus:*` statements in outcome scoring, the bus consensus in `/api/tldr` (null) and the bus replies in Threads 1H (`h1:<candle>` keeps the decision and the HUD agents), and the engines `bus_agent` / `bus_moderator` from `organism.json`. A stale `multi_agent_bus` queue task is acknowledged. `MULTI_AGENT_BUS_ENABLED` became `NEWS_THREADS_ENABLED`; for one deploy the old name still counts when the new one is unset. D1 `bus_messages` is not touched (archive and drop wait for the owner).
- perf(d1): outcome scoring's room statements and the weighting replay read only their window of the room tables (owner 2026-11-15: "setuju", `docs/RFC_ROOM_CELLS.md` §6, the interim option). The 15m War Room's daily threads of the window are named (`src/thread-windows.mjs`, at most 62 days, else the whole range) and every room read is bounded by `bar_time` (a day before the window, since a message is never older than its bar), searched by the new index `agent_thread_messages(thread_id, bar_time)` (migration `0021`, also made at run time). The same rows and results; production read 13,842–14,056 rows per call before. Test fixtures of the 1H/4H rooms now carry their bar close, as production rows do.
- docs(rfc): `docs/RFC_ROOM_CELLS.md`, the ADR-0015 phase 2 blueprint for the room tables (relayed owner approval 2026-11-15, blueprint only; the owner approves it before any implementation). It lists every writer and reader of `agent_threads`/`agent_thread_messages`, what is permanent evidence and what is transient per thread kind, the `RoomCell` Durable Object contract, the `room_runs` run document with the transcript in R2, the shadow and retirement plan, and the D1 rows measured in production (about 3,000–3,800 message rows written and an estimated 2.4 M rows read per market day, mostly two unindexed scans in outcome scoring and the weighting replay). Nothing changes in production.
- feat(scoring): the Decision Engine's own stances are a scored subject, `engine:15` (each 15m bar's decision, its own run marker `15:engine`, so recent bars are backfilled), and `GET /api/engine/decisions` and `GET /api/decisions` carry `outcome_summary` ({ scoring_version, subject, timeframe, windows: { 7d, 30d }: n_directional, hit, miss, hit_rate, wait_n, wait_accuracy, as_of }) from the stored scorecards (#332). No price, entry or ATR. For the kernel's Signal Analyst hit_rate_7d / hit_rate_30d (owner 2026-11-15, platform session; confirmed in this session).
- feat(patterns): each detected pattern carries where to draw its lines (`line_from`, `line_to`: the start and last bar close times; `upper_from`, `lower_from`: the lines' prices at the start; fitted lines slope, the others are flat), and the public analysis gives them as `lines` (owner 2026-11-15: "gambar juga garis pattern di chart"). cryptophys-cta draws them on TradingView's chart.
- feat(public-analysis): each zone carries the names of its metrics (`zoneMetrics`: the zone's heaviest key levels by name, at most three; a round number is "round number", a pattern's "measured target" is "measured projection", any other name with a trade word is left out; no price). Owner 2026-11-15: "tidak perlu dicantumkan sumber playbook, cukup nama metrics sumbernya"; cryptophys-cta shows these instead of a source count and drops the catalogue reference.
- fix(security): the decision-model trainer pins `lightgbm==4.6.0` (Dependabot, high: CVE-2024-43598 / GHSA-2586-f3p4-hq84, remote code execution, fixed from 4.6.0). Used only by `train-model.yml` on the runner, never by a Worker. On a synthetic 700-row dataset 4.5.0 and 4.6.0 give byte-identical trees and walk-forward checks, and the trainer's own check (the compact trees reproduce LightGBM's predictions) passes.

## 2026-11-14
- chore(content): retire the deployed `xauusd-content` Worker (owner 2026-11-15: "pindah content queue ke cta, nexus hanya khusus war room"). The one-off workflow `retire-content-worker.yml` deletes it by explicit name, idempotently, retrying while Cloudflare still sees Omega's former binding. Its engine and studio live in cryptophys-adm/cryptophys-cta; it never published. Never to be redeployed.
- refactor(content): the content queue moves to cryptophys-cta; Nexus is the War Room only (owner 2026-11-15: "pindah content queue ke cta, nexus hanya khusus war room"). The content engine and its studio now live in the repository `cryptophys-adm/cryptophys-cta` (the Worker `cryptophys-cta` at `cta.cryptophys.work`: studio behind Access, public CTA page), which reads this repository only through the AI Worker's read-only `PublicProjection` entrypoint (kept). Removed here: the Nexus page "Content queue" and its nav entry, Omega's `/api/content*` routes and its `CONTENT` binding, the `xauusd-content` Worker's code with its deploy and validate workflows and tests, and its organ in `organism.json`. The topology is five Workers again (SSOT, AGENTS.md, README, the topology, DNA and inventory tests). The deployed `xauusd-content` Worker is deleted by a one-off workflow in the next PR, once Omega no longer binds it. Trading behaviour unchanged.
- feat(content): real-time analysis posts, the content policy, and the approval page wired (owner 2026-11-14 for the Threads account xauusd-ai-cta: "fokus pada hasil analytics dan key level. Tidak boleh ada advices buy/sell, kecuali info buy/sell zone"; "Real-time, hanya analisa"; the chart image a TradingView screenshot, not Yahoo data). The AI Worker's `PublicProjection` gains `analysis()` (`src/public-analysis.mjs`): the Key Level Agent's candidate zones as demand ("area beli") and supply ("area jual") zones with strength and sources, the Projection Agent's H1/H4/D1 patterns by catalogue name and chapter with their lines and, once confirmed, the measured projection, and the structure per chart; never the decision, the council's reasons, a plan, an entry, a stop, a lot or the account (`analysisLeaks`). The content Worker drafts, every hour at :09 Monday to Friday, pattern posts (confirmed on H1/H4/D1, forming on H4/D1; once per pattern and status; at most four a day per channel) and at 06:09 UTC the key-level map (`analysis-drafts.mjs`, fixed Indonesian templates). The guardian gains `signal_words` (entry, SL, TP, stop loss, take profit, cut loss, BUY/SELL/LONG/SHORT as a signal; a buy or sell zone stays information) and allows price levels for these posts. Omega binds `CONTENT` (xauusd-content's `ContentApi`), so the Nexus Content queue works. The policy is recorded in `docs/RFC_CONTENT_AGENT.md` §0.1. Publishing stays off; no model call, no cost; trading behaviour unchanged.
- feat(content): the content engine for Threads and Instagram, prepared with publishing off (owner 2026-11-14: "persiapan engine post ke Instagram/threads"; `docs/RFC_CONTENT_AGENT.md` P2). A sixth Worker, `xauusd-content` (`workers/xauusd-content/`, `deploy-content.yml`, `validate-content.yml`), so the social accounts' tokens never sit beside the trading paths; no public URL. It reads only the AI Worker's new `PublicProjection.recaps` RPC entrypoint (the public projection: decisions older than 24 hours, no level, plan or account data). Tuesday to Saturday at 22:17 UTC it drafts the previous trading day's recap in fixed Indonesian templates (`drafts.mjs`; no model writes public text), each checked by the content guardian; drafts wait in the `ContentDesk` Durable Object (SQLite) for a person, with every step an event, and an approval runs the guardian again (`desk.mjs`). The publishers (`publishers.mjs`) use Meta's two-step flow (Threads text posts; Instagram image posts, whose card image is the next step, so Instagram drafts wait as `needs_image`), send the token only in POST bodies, offer a dry run with the token redacted, read the publishing quota from Meta's own limit endpoints, and refresh long-lived tokens after 30 days into the desk. `CONTENT_PUBLISH` is "off": an approved draft waits; turning it on is an owner PR after the lawyer's check. Tokens are Worker secrets set by the deploy workflow from GitHub secrets the owner adds. Meta's documentation could not be read from the build session, so the endpoint shapes follow the published guides and are to be confirmed by one real post on a test account. organism.json gains the organ (drift binding `EXT_XAUUSD_CONTENT`, to be bound on the platform side). No model call, no cost; trading behaviour unchanged.
- docs(ssot): ADR-0015 phases 3 (telemetry to Analytics Engine) and 5 (retention with an R2 archive) recorded as deferred, with the measurements of 2026-11-14 (owner: "lanjutkan semua"): about 8,700 rows written on a weekday (room messages 3,241, memo 2,139, scores 1,985, parity 703), of which about 4,000 are telemetry-like and each read as a record of truth; the database at 66 MB after 11 days (about 2 GB a year against the 5 GB included). Re-evaluation triggers are in `docs/SSOT.md`. And test(organism): `test/organism-inventory.test.mjs` keeps each Worker's wrangler bindings and crons equal to its code (no unread binding, no binding the code calls that the config lacks, every cron branch fired, a cron for every scheduled handler, no preview binding production lacks), until the platform's DNA schema has fields for an organ's bindings and crons (it has none at `$schema_version` 1).
- feat(content): the public projection and the content guardian, content roadmap P1 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjutkan semua"; `docs/RFC_CONTENT_AGENT.md` §2.1–2.2, BACKLOG item 26). `GET /api/public-projection` (`src/public-projection.mjs`; behind Access like the rest of `/api`) returns recaps of Decision Engine decisions older than `PUBLIC_DELAY_HOURS` (default 24): time and session, stance, bias, the quorum's shares, chart patterns by name and status (never bounds or targets), reasons and risks with every sentence that names a price or uses order wording removed (production's summaries name prices and "limit order", so the summary is not used at all), the names of the guardrail checks that held a side back (never their details, which name levels), and the 15m outcome. A key or string that would still leak drops the recap (`recapLeaks`). `checkContentDraft` (`src/content-guardian.mjs`) checks a draft deterministically: capacity ("edukasi"), disclaimer, promised returns, live instructions, price levels (off until the owner allows them as commentary), account data, Nexus or Omega, key-like strings, TradingView's attribution on a chart, and the delay. Nothing publishes: there is no content agent yet; the lawyer's check of POJK 6/2026 stays with the owner. No model call, no cost; trading behaviour unchanged.
- fix(news): GDELT in the news shadow gets its own time limit and backs off (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjutkan semua"; `fundamental-news-ingestion/src/news-sources.mjs`). Before: every source had 6 seconds, and GDELT failed 17 of 17 runs on 2026-11-14 (`timeout` at 6,000 ms in `/health` `news_shadow`); asked from outside, its DOC API answered "429" (rate limited) after about 10 seconds. GDELT now has 20 seconds (`timeoutMs`; the feeds keep 6), a 429 is named `rate_limited`, and after 3 failed runs in a row it is asked only on every 6th UTC hour until it answers (`backoff`, `inBackoff`; the run records `consecutive_failures`, and the day counts `skipped_runs` apart from `failed_runs`). The Nexus Status page shows "rate limited" and "backing off". Shadow only: nothing reads these headlines yet; no key, no cost.
- feat(agents): the model arena, in shadow, AI Neuron B (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut B"; `src/model-arena.mjs`). The two heavy steps that run on gpt-oss-120b, the Decision Engine's council (`decision_council`: 90 calls, 0.70M input and 58k output tokens on 2026-11-13) and the 15m War Room conclusion (`war_room_conclusion`: 82 calls, 0.28M input, 43k output), are asked again, on every 8th 15m bar after the decision, of `@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017` (Workers AI: 27,273 neurons per M input, 545 per M cached input, 109,091 per M output; default limit 20 requests a minute), with the live step's first messages, once, at reasoning effort "low". The council's answer goes through the same guardrails as the live one; the conclusion through the room's own validation. Each answer is stored beside the live step's (D1 `model_arena_outputs`), both stances are scored on the same 15m outcome in the sweep, and `GET /api/model-arena` and the Nexus Neuron allocation page compare validity at the first try, agreement, hit rate, latency, tokens and neurons per call. Cost: about 12 sampled bars on a full weekday, so about 24 calls; at the live steps' token sizes about 300 neurons per council call and 160 per conclusion, about 5,500 neurons a day (about USD 0.06 at the paid rate), metered as `shadow:arena:<schema>` within the shadow kill switch's budget. It stops for the day at its own cap (`ARENA_NEURONS_PER_DAY_MAX`, 6,000) and once all shadow calls reach 80% of the kill switch's 25,000, so it never trips the breaker by itself; `MODEL_ARENA` "off" and the kill switch turn it off. The meter now prices cached input tokens at a model's cached rate when one is published (only the challenger here; `neuronsOf`), and the model text layer has the challenger's request form (`modelRequests` family `deepseek-v4`). **It changes no prompt, room, council or decision; trading behaviour is unchanged.**
- fix(market-data): the pull adapter's daily TradingView budget is the account's provider quota, 500 (owner 2026-11-14: "sesuaikan batas dengan kuota TV agar sama dan ssot"); ADR-0015 phase 2 deferred (owner: "setuju tunda"). `TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET` was 800 (2026-11-05) against the provider's 500 a day (`PROVIDER_DAILY_LIMIT` of the news Worker, which its chart reserve uses), so it never held: on 2026-11-12/13 the provider refused at the 501st call while the budget still read room (the pull adapter had made 379). It is now 500 in `market-data-ingestion/wrangler.jsonc` and as the code default, the stale one-day override (2026-11-08) is removed, and `test/tv-quota-alignment.test.mjs` fails if the two numbers part. The chart's modelled demand at each series' cadence is about 420 calls over a full day (379 measured on 2026-11-13), inside the 500 but leaving about 80 for news, so the news pulls yield to the chart near the limit; the demand test now models the cadence (it had counted USOIL every 5 minutes, 612) and allows up to 90% of the quota. The quota is the account's, shared with the news pulls, which yield to the chart near the limit; the same number alone does not keep the account under it, so the Monday check of a full weekday stays. `docs/SSOT.md` and `docs/MARKET_DATA_INGESTION.md` say 500, and SSOT records phase 2 as deferred, with the measured reason (about 0.4 million row writes a month of 50 million; the hot reads already about one row; 66 MB) and the re-evaluation trigger. No decision or trading behaviour changes.
- fix(market-data): the pull adapter's daily TradingView budget is counted atomically; ADR-0015 phase 4 recorded as deferred (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut keduanya"; review from the platform session). The budget was a KV read-modify-write (`reserveBudget`), so two runs close together, or a KV read that had not yet seen the last write, could both reserve. It now goes through the AI Worker over the binding the pull adapter already has (`LocalComputationIngress.reserveTvCalls` / `releaseTvCalls`, `src/tv-budget.mjs`, D1 `tv_call_budget`, run-time schema): one statement grants `min(wanted, daily − used)` and adds it, returning both; the KV key keeps a copy for diagnostics, and the KV count is used when the binding fails (`budget.counter`). The pull adapter still owns no D1. Checked on the way: the H1 parity gaps of 2026-11-12/13 (19:00–22:00 UTC, 39 of 46 trading hours compared) came from the provider's quota running out at about 500 calls (health alerts `market_data_stale`/`market_data_fallback`), not from KV consistency, so no parity change is made; the internal budget of 800 sits above the provider's quota (the TradingView quota task). `docs/SSOT.md` records phase 4 as deferred, with the corrected reason (no billed saving, change on the trading path; the Durable Object requests would likely stay inside the included million) and the re-evaluation trigger (D1 or KV past half its allowance, or minute-exact timing needing alarms). No decision or trading behaviour changes.
- feat(nexus): the UI/UX audit's 18 remediations, landed on `main` (owner 2026-11-14 pushed `feat/ui-ux-e2e-platform-audit`, commit 19f1a3d, and said "sambil lanjut PR"; `docs/ui-ux-e2e-platform-audit.md`).
