Only the audit commit was taken onto current `main`, its five conflicts reconciled with the push hub (#337–#348) and the metric chips (#344). Kept as written: the War Room chart opens on 75 bars instead of fitting 400 (DEF-01), the trade plan lines no longer hide with the Clean preset (DEF-02), mobile shows System Issues, the chart and the decision before the admin cards (DEF-03), the assistant panel's sections remember their open state (DEF-05), the trend ladder switches the War Room tab (DEF-06), DXY and USDT.D bars are coloured by their effect on gold (DEF-07), the SL+ line on the chart (DEF-11), the active preset highlighted (DEF-12), layer chips scroll on mobile (DEF-13), the unlinked chart in amber (DEF-14), the crowd line's percentages inline (DEF-15), a link from the priority queue to Alerts (DEF-16), and the Aether link at `/#/aether` (DEF-09). Changed on landing: the Omega badge (DEF-10) reads Omega's new light status `GET /omega/api/status` (`OmegaGateway.status`, `brokerStatus`: one bridge row and the settings) every 60 s while visible, instead of the full overview every 30 s on every page (which also read two fields that do not exist); `usePoll` (DEF-08) refreshes on tab refocus and never flashes the skeleton on a background refresh, with the interval kept apart from the deps so the pages that slow their read once the push hub delivers keep their data; DEF-04, DEF-17 and DEF-18 were already on `main`. The audit document's claims are corrected: repository-relative paths, §5 on the push hub, §7.2 with the whole suite (1,050 tests), the typecheck and the build, §7.3 on the integration, and no "certified" sign-off. No decision or trading behaviour changes.
- fix(omega): the Omega page's prices and chart move at the bridge's 5-second sync again, at no added cost (owner 2026-11-14: "fase 4 asalkan tidak impact peningkatan biaya"). Since #336 the bridge row is written on a change that matters or every 30 seconds, and the overview read its quote, equity and positions from that row, so the chart's live candle, the bid/ask and the open positions' profit moved about every 30 seconds (also under the old 0.5-second SSE stream). The overview pushed after each sync now carries that sync's own live numbers (`liveBridgeFields` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`: bid, ask, spread, balance, equity, the day's realized P/L, positions, orders, ATR), with nothing written; the 30-second write rule stands. The hub's subscriber count is remembered per isolate for 30 seconds (`SUBSCRIBERS_TTL_MS`): each ask is a billed Durable Object request, and the bridge syncs every 5 seconds (17,280 asks a day → about 2,900). ADR-0015 phase 4 for XAUUSD (Market Feed and Omega Bridge as Durable Objects) is not built: Workers Paid includes 1 million Durable Object requests a month for the whole account ($0.15 a million after), every RPC call is one request, and the D1 and KV use those cells would replace is inside the included D1/KV allowances, so the cells would add about 0.5 million billed-quota requests a month (a call per bridge sync, an alarm per minute) without lowering the bill.
- feat(omega): the Omega overview over the push hub, ADR-0015 phase 1, the rest, part 2 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut"). Before: the Omega page opened `/omega/api/stream`, an SSE stream that computed the whole overview (deals, the 30-day deal list, the EA signal, control events, the broker state) every 0.5 seconds for 15 seconds and was reopened by the browser at once, plus a read every 8 seconds: about 6,000 overview computations an hour per open Omega page, while the data cannot change faster than the bridge's 5-second sync. Now: after each bridge sync (`OmegaGateway.bridgeSync`, after the bridge has its answer) the AI Worker computes the overview once and publishes it to the hub's new `omega-overview` topic, only while a page listens (`hubSubscribers`, the hub's `subscribers`); the page subscribes through Omega's own `GET /omega/api/push`, which checks the Access identity like `/omega/api/overview` and forwards the WebSocket to the hub (cross-script binding `PUSH_HUB` in `workers/xauusd-omega/wrangler.jsonc`) for that one topic. Nexus' `/api/stream` strips the topic (`nexusStreamRequest`), so account data never reaches a socket the Omega Worker did not authorize; the hub replays a stored overview on connect only while it is under 60 seconds old (`FRESH_ON_CONNECT_MS`). The page's read runs every 8 seconds until the first push, then every 60 seconds as a fallback; the pushed snapshot carries no viewer and the page keeps the last read's. Freshness is unchanged (the bridge's 5 seconds); with one page open: about 6,000 → 720 overview computations an hour, and none while no page is open. The SSE route stays for an old page still open; the new page no longer uses it. **No trading behaviour changes**: the bridge's answer and every control are untouched.
- feat(nexus): the 1H/4H/D1 rooms and the global decision over the push hub, ADR-0015 phase 1, the rest, part 1 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut"). Two new topics in the Nexus Worker's `PushHub` (`workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/src/push-hub.mjs`): `threads` (`{ D1, H4, H1 }`, each the body of `/api/threads/timeframe?chart=…`) and `decision` (the body of `/api/conclusion/global`). The AI Worker publishes them (`publishMarket` in `src/index.ts`, through the bodies the routes now share: `timeframeThreadBody`, `globalConclusionBody`) when a timeframe room concludes (the sweep's rooms and the H1 room's queue task) and when the Decision Engine decides a bar. Nexus' market provider (`ui/src/lib/market.tsx`) subscribes to both on one socket (`usePushMany`; `usePush` now wraps it) and reads them every 5 minutes as a fallback once both have arrived, instead of every 60 seconds; News Daily and the 5m context stay on the 60-second read. Before → after, per open War Room page: 6 reads a minute (360 an hour) → 2 a minute plus 4 every 5 minutes (168 an hour, −53%); the AI Worker publishes about 4 decision and 1 to 3 room snapshots an hour. The Omega overview (account data) is not a Nexus stream topic: `/api/stream` sits only behind the edge Access sign-in, which a service token also passes, while Omega checks the person itself; it follows in its own PR through Omega's own route. **No decision changes.**
- feat(agents): Workers AI prompt caching, AI Neuron A5 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut"; Workers AI docs "Prompt caching": prefix caching matches the prompt from its start, `x-session-affinity` routes calls to the instance holding the prefix, cached input tokens are reported in `usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens` and billed lower). Every bus call now sends `x-session-affinity`, one id per room kind and step (for example `xauusd:warroom:open:technical`; `affinityId` in `src/multi-agent-bus.mjs`, through `runModel`'s `extraHeaders`), so one agent's step goes to the instance holding its prompt prefix from bar to bar; `AI_SESSION_AFFINITY` "off" sends none. The meter records the cached input tokens (`usageSplit` `cached`; `ai_usage_daily.cached_tokens`, added at run time by `ALTER TABLE`, since production applies no migration), and the Nexus "Neuron allocation" page shows the share of input tokens served from the cache (`/api/neuron-allocation` `prompt_cache`). Before: not measured (the meter had no cached tokens). Prompt order: the room prompts already put the role and rules first and the bar's data last (`chatMessages`: role line as the system message, the rules, then YOUR DATA); the only semi-static block after the data is the learned notes, and moving it would change the live prompt, so it is left for the owner. Neurons stay computed at the input rate (no published cached rate for these models here), so a saving shows as the cached share, not in the neuron estimate. **It changes no prompt and no decision**: the header only routes the call.
- feat(agents): the room memo keyed on the evidence, in shadow, AI Neuron A4 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut"; `src/room-memo.mjs` `evidenceShadow`). The live memo (`ROOM_MEMO_ENABLED`) reuses an opening only when the whole prompt is the same: on 2026-11-13 the 15m War Room's openings made 1,130 model calls and reused 444 answers (28%), the 1H/4H/D1 rooms' 382 and 98 (20%); a bar whose closes moved slightly misses. The evidence key is the agent's metric menu (`metricMenu` of its data: HUD statements with price levels masked, fields, candles as distances and ratios) and its learned notes, hashed with the model and schema. It never reuses an answer: on each fresh opening the prompt key missed, it records whether the evidence key had an answer within the memo's TTL (`memo_hits`) and whether the model then gave the same stance and bias (`memo_same`), under the meter source `evidence_memo:<schema>` (kept out of the live memo's daily totals), and stores the answer in D1 `room_memo_evidence` (run-time schema). `/api/neuron-allocation` returns `memo` and the Nexus "Neuron allocation" page shows "Memo: prompt key (live) and evidence key (shadow)": live reuse, evidence hits, same-answer rate and the extra neurons a day the evidence key would save, counting only same answers. Keying the live memo on the evidence is a later step and the owner's. Cost: one D1 read and one upsert per fresh opening, no model call. `EVIDENCE_MEMO_SHADOW` "off" stops it. **It changes no decision.**
- feat(nexus): metric chips in the War Room, AI Neuron A3 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut"). Under each post of the 15m room's communication bus and Consensus Discussion, the same agent's shadow answer of that bar in metric form (A1, A2) is shown as chips: each metric's short id, reading and value, coloured by its implication (green bullish, red bearish, grey neutral), dimmed by its weight, ⚑ on the metric that would invalidate the view; a rebuttal shows which agent's metric it disputes, how (contradicts, outweighs, confirms) and with which metric of its own. Each block is marked SHADOW · METRICS with its stance, "differs from live" when the stance is not the live post's, and its grounding (n/m). A toggle above the bus hides them (remembered in the browser). Backend: `readWarRoom(db, { metrics: true })` adds `metric_shadow` (`readMetricShadowBar`, two reads on the primary key's (room, bar_time) prefix; valid answers only; null before the tables exist) for `/api/war-room/latest` and the push snapshot only; decision cards and the strategy engine read the room without it. The War Room publishes once more after its metric jobs, so open pages get the chips at once. Fix: the kill-switch card shows "State unknown" instead of "Shadow OFF" when the server's answer lacks the switch's state. **It changes no decision.**
- feat(agents): rebuttals in metric form, in shadow, AI Neuron A2 (owner 2026-11-14: "lanjut kerjakan A2"; `src/metric-shadow.mjs` `runMetricRebuttals`). On every fourth 15m bar (the hour's first, `METRIC_REBUTTAL_EVERY`), after the bar's metric openings, each agent that rebutted live answers the other agents' metric openings, shown anonymously as #n with their metric tables, in metric form: `{ stance, confidence, rebuts: [{ ref, their_metric, my_metric, relation }] }` with relation contradicts, outweighs or confirms (AI task `metric_rebuttal`, metered `shadow:metric_rebuttal`). No sentences and no values are written, so no price level can appear. A rebut is grounded when its #n is on the bus, the disputed metric is in that opening and the answering metric is on the agent's own menu. Answers go to D1 `metric_shadow_rebuttals` beside the agent's live rebuttal stance on the same bar (with the disputed agent kept beside each #n) and are scored on the same 15m outcome by the sweep. `/api/metric-shadow` returns `rebuttals`, and the Nexus "Neuron allocation" page has a "Rebuttals in metrics" table: validity, grounding, agreement with the live rebuttal, hit rate against the live hit rate, tokens and latency. Cost: at most `METRIC_REBUTTAL_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (default 12) calls; with up to 8 rebutters on one bar an hour, about 4,000 to 5,000 neurons a day, which keeps all shadow arms together under the kill switch's 25,000 budget. `METRIC_SHADOW` "off" or the kill switch stops it. **It changes no decision.**
- feat(agents): the shadow kill switch (owner 2026-11-14: "berikan kill switch untuk mode shadow jika ternyata biaya membengkak"; `src/shadow-switch.mjs`). One switch stops every shadow path that calls a model: the metric-format arm, the learned notes under test (15m War Room, 1H room, council) and a benched agent's turn beside its room. The owner flips it from the card "Shadow mode · kill switch" (War Room right column and the Neuron allocation page) through `GET`/`POST /omega/api/shadow-mode` (Cloudflare Access, a person, the same origin, a reason), stored in KV `shadow-mode:switch:v1`, audited in D1 `shadow_switch_events` and sent to Telegram. A cost breaker runs hourly in the sweep: when today's (UTC) shadow neurons pass `SHADOW_NEURONS_PER_DAY_MAX` (default 25,000, about USD 0.28 a day at the paid rate; the whole system used about 107,000 a day on 2026-11-13), it switches shadow off itself and tells the owner; it stays off until a person switches it on. `SHADOW_MODE` "off" holds it off by variable. To count them, shadow calls are now metered apart in `ai_usage_daily` as `shadow:<schema name>` (`callBusModel` `meterSource`), so the AI usage panel shows them on their own rows. Off changes no live room, quorum or decision; a benched agent stays benched and its record pauses until shadow is on again. The War Room shadow log carries `shadow_off` when the switch is off.
- feat(agents): metric readings instead of prose, in shadow (owner 2026-11-14 in the platform session: the AI should "membawa interpretasi metrics yang dia propose bukan narasi", and "Ya" here to doing it in shadow first; `src/metric-shadow.mjs`). On each 15m War Room bar, every agent that opened live answers once more from the same data as a table of metric readings (`{ stance, confidence, metrics: [{ id, value, reading, implication, weight }], invalidation }`, AI task `metric_opening`). Each agent gets a menu of the metrics its own data holds: its HUD statements as L1..Ln, the fields of its data by path, and its candles as distances and ratios only (change over 1 and 4 bars, range against the 20-bar average, distance below the 20-bar high and above the 20-bar low, up bars of 8, body ratio), never a price level; price-like numbers in a statement become "[level]". A cited id on the menu is grounded; the share that is, is the agent's grounding rate. Answers are stored in D1 `metric_shadow_outputs` beside the agent's live stance on the same bar and both are scored on the same 15m outcome (the sweep's `scoreMetricShadow`). `GET /api/metric-shadow` and a panel on the Nexus "Neuron allocation" page compare, per agent: validity, grounding, stance agreement with the live answer, hit rate against the live hit rate, tokens and latency. About one extra light model call per answering agent per 15m bar, at most `METRIC_SHADOW_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (default 48) an hour; `METRIC_SHADOW` "off" stops it. **It changes no decision**: nothing reads the shadow answers but the comparison. Promoting the format, room by room, is a later step and the owner's.
- feat(agents): neuron allocation by reputation (owner 2026-11-14: "setuju poin 4", then "Setuju" to the thresholds; `src/agent-allocation.mjs`). Per room (M15, H1, H4, D1) and agent, on the directional calls of the last 30 days (the scorecards snapshot): below 45% over at least 60 decided calls the agent goes to shadow in that room, and back to live at 50% or more (the gap keeps it from flapping). In shadow it leaves the room's bus, quorum and discussion and answers only on every fourth bar of the room's timeframe, beside the bus (step `shadow:<agent>`), scored as its own calls (source `room_shadow`), so its record keeps moving and its neurons drop by about three quarters. The moderator is exempt, a room keeps at least 4 live agents with a record, and a room that would keep fewer than two live agents keeps everyone. The rules run hourly in the sweep; each move is stored (KV `agent-allocation:state:v1`), logged (D1 `agent_allocation_events`), sent to the owner on Telegram and shown on the Nexus "Neuron allocation" page. `AGENT_ALLOCATION` "off" keeps every agent live. **This changes which agents speak in a room, and so the room's quorum and conclusion.** On the records of 2026-11-14 the first run would move `projection` (31.7% of 60) and `interstellar_eyes` (44.98% of 209) to shadow in the 15m room and `technical` (44.6% of 65) in the 1H room.
- feat(nexus): Neuron allocation, read-only (owner 2026-11-14: "setuju poin 4, tapi perlu visualisasi yang jelas di dashboard", then "Ya" to doing this before the structured metrics and the model arena). `GET /api/neuron-allocation` (`src/neuron-allocation.mjs`) and the Nexus page "Neuron allocation" (`#/neurons`): per agent its status, rooms, 30-day hit rate with the number of scored calls (the scorecards snapshot), neurons per day and share (its room messages' tokens priced with its model's input/output mix of the day from `ai_usage_daily`, so the agents add up to the meter), neurons per correct call and a daily trend; a quadrant of accuracy against neurons per day; neurons per room per day stacked by agent; and the allocation changes. Grounding is shown as "not yet" until agents answer in metrics. Nothing changes an agent: every agent stays live; allocation by reputation is its own step.
- feat(war-room): the War Room publishes to the push hub, ADR-0015 phase 1, part 2 (owner 2026-11-14: "C. mulai kerjakan"). The AI Worker binds the Nexus Worker's `PushHub` class across scripts (`PUSH_HUB`, `script_name: xauusd-nexus-dashboard`; no migration here) and the 15m War Room publishes the room exactly as `GET /api/war-room/latest` returns it at each step of a run: the brief, the openings, the discussion and the conclusion (`publishWarRoom`, `src/push-hub.mjs`). An open Nexus page follows the room from the hub and polls only every 5 minutes as a fallback; per open tab about 12 reads an hour instead of 360, plus one room read per step published (four a run). The preview Worker has no binding and publishes nothing. A publish that fails never stops the room; the page then polls as before.
- feat(nexus): the push hub, ADR-0015 "Organs as cells" phase 1, part 1 (owner 2026-11-14: "C. mulai kerjakan"). A Durable Object in the Nexus Worker (`PushHub`, `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/src/push-hub.mjs`, WebSocket Hibernation) holds the open pages' WebSockets and each topic's latest snapshot; a page connects at `GET /api/stream?topics=war-room` (behind the same Access sign-in as Nexus), gets the topic's latest snapshot at once and every new one as it is published, and keeps the socket alive with a ping the hub answers without waking. The War Room page reads the room from the hub and polls `/api/war-room/latest` every 5 minutes as a fallback once the hub has delivered a snapshot (every 10 seconds until then, and whenever the socket is down). Nothing publishes yet: the AI Worker's War Room publishes in part 2 (a cross-script binding to this class, which needs the class deployed first), so until then the page polls as before. No AI Worker, decision or trading change.
- perf(omega): the bridge sync writes its row on a change that matters, a reported deal once, and reads the kill switch once (owner 2026-11-14: "B. penuh tanpa fase shadow"). The bridge syncs every 5 seconds. Each sync wrote the `omega_bridge` row (the quote and equity change at every tick), sent every closed deal the terminal reports to `storeDeal` (an insert attempt and, with a source, an update: 36 deals on 2026-11-13, 72 statements a sync), read `ea:control` twice (the sync's plan and the signal) and walked `omega_events` back for the last plan note. Now: the row is written when its key, version, connection, permission, error, executor, account mode, positions, orders or deals change, else every 30 seconds; a deal stored with its source is remembered per isolate; the control is read once and handed to `eaSignal`; `omega_events(action, id)` is indexed. Measured on the sync as production runs it (`test/bridge-sync-cost.test.mjs`, one minute of 12 syncs with 36 reported deals): D1 statements 984 to 110, rows written 12 to 2, KV reads 72 to 60. No intent, order, stop or kill changes: the planner plans on the sync's own state, and a KILL set between two syncs is read by the next one. Omega's page and the health check show the last quote and sync time up to 30 seconds old (the watchdog's thresholds are 2 minutes for the bridge and 1 minute for the terminal state).
- perf(market-data): a published bar's marker is read from KV once, not every minute (owner 2026-11-14: "A. ya"). Each minute every publisher (HUD snapshots of four charts, the H1, 5m, 15m and HTF lanes) read its latest bar's "already published" marker, and the derived-series check read its record, to learn what was done minutes or hours before: 13 keys besides the series on a quiet minute. A marker this isolate wrote or found, and the day whose derived check is done, are remembered per KV binding (presence only, never content), so a quiet minute reads 5 (the refresh summary, the owner's fallback switch, and the H1 revision check's three markers, whose content it compares). A bar is still published at most once; an unknown marker is read as before.
- perf(news): `/health` reads KV at most once a minute per isolate, and the provider calls from one record per day (owner 2026-11-14: "A. ya", the KV part of the resource audit). Nexus reads `/health` every 30 seconds through `/api/status`, and each call read 19 KV keys, 14 of them the seven callers' usage records of today and yesterday. `recordUsage` now also writes its caller's record into the day's summary (`tv_mcp:calls-day:<date>`), which `/health` reads (2 keys; a day without a summary falls back to the caller keys); the body is kept per isolate for 60 seconds (`TV_HEALTH_CACHE_SECONDS`, "0" for none). A `/health` now reads 7 keys at most, and none for the rest of the minute. The chart's reserve still reads the exact per-caller keys before a news pull; two callers recording at once can leave one's latest count out of the summary until its next record (the shown figures, not the reserve). Each recorded invocation costs one more KV read and write.
- perf(strategy-inputs): an hour in which no gold bar opened is not loaded (resource audit P0). The strategy capture loaded the engine inputs at every sweep after an hour's capture window only to find the series stale when the market was closed; it now returns `market_closed` before the load (`goldClosedAt`). With the closed-market idle the sweep is already still from two hours after the Friday close; this covers those two hours and any sweep run with `CLOSED_MARKET_IDLE` off. Nothing is captured that was captured before.
- perf(d1): the scorecards are stored once and read as one row (resource audit P0). Every War Room read (Nexus every 10 seconds, each decision card, each room's prompt), the indicator registry, the analytics page and `/api/scorecards` computed the 30-day scorecards from every score of the window: 15,675 rows read per call in production on 2026-11-14. `scorecards(db, { snapshot: true })` now returns the stored copy (`scorecard_snapshots`, one row per window of 7, 14 or 30 days) while it is younger than 20 minutes and computes and stores it otherwise; the outcome scoring refreshes the stored windows whenever it writes scores, so a reader sees new scores at once. The numbers are the same as computed (`test/scorecard-snapshot.test.mjs`); a window may lag the clock by up to 20 minutes at its far edge (rows older than 30 days drop out at the next refresh). Callers without `snapshot` compute as before.
- fix(d1): the read indexes are made by the run-time schemas, since production applies no migrations (found after the deploy of the indexes: `deploy-worker.yml` runs `d1 migrations apply` only with a D1 token, which is not configured, so production's tables come from `schema.sql` and each module's run-time schema and `0020_read_indexes.sql` never ran there). `outcome-scoring`, `agent-threads` and `parity-registry` add their indexes to their schemas; `webhookLaneStatus` makes the `webhook_sources(received_at)` index once per isolate. `test/read-indexes.test.mjs` builds a database without the migrations and checks every read index is made.
- perf(d1): thread prefixes read as index ranges, and the latest room and News Daily runs as one index step (resource audit P0). `thread_id LIKE 'warroom:%'` (and `newsdaily:`, `live15m:`, `tf:`) could not use an index and scanned every room message; it is now `thread_id >= 'warroom:' AND thread_id < 'warroom;'`. A thread's id carries its run's date, so the latest run is found by reading the (thread_id, run_id, step) index backwards from the end of the range (`ORDER BY thread_id DESC, run_id DESC LIMIT 1`) instead of `MAX(run_id)` over the table: the War Room's latest run (`readWarRoom`, which Nexus reads every 10 seconds; the health alert) 20,906 rows read to 1; the decision cards' 12 latest 15m rooms 20,919 to 323; the latest News Daily 21,050 to 3; the outcome scoring's live 15m statements 20,906 to 206, its War Room statements 20,906 to 14,056; the compliance summary 20,985 to 18,771 (production `rows_read` of 2026-11-14, the same results before and after). `test/read-indexes.test.mjs` checks the plans (index, no sort) and that each lookup returns what `MAX(run_id)` returned. No result or behaviour changes.
- perf(d1): indexes for the queries that scanned whole tables (resource audit P0; owner 2026-11-14: "lanjutkan P0"). Migration `0020_read_indexes.sql`: `outcome_scores(timeframe, candle_key)` (the decision cards' outcomes, 40 a push, read 17,389 rows each for no result), `agent_thread_messages(ts)` (AI calls in the last hour, 20,906 rows), `engine_parity_comparisons(compared_at)` (latest comparisons, 7,200) and `(candle_key)` (a candle's comparisons, 3,600), `webhook_sources(received_at)` (the last webhook, 9,930); and `omega_intents(status, created_ms)` in the run-time Omega schema (the bridge sync's pending intents, every 5 seconds). Rows read are production's of 2026-11-14 before the change; `test/read-indexes.test.mjs` checks that each query searches its index. Indexes only: no query, result or behaviour changes; each index costs one extra write per inserted row.
- perf(worker): the AI Worker's crons stand still while gold is closed (owner 2026-11-14, through the platform orchestrator: "lebih hemat read/write D1 dan KV"; `src/market-idle.mjs`). From two hours after the Friday close to the Sunday reopen the parity sweep (every 15 minutes) ran its whole chain on inputs that cannot change: the war rooms and their fallback, the H1 finalize, the reconcile and backfill, outcome scoring, the hourly replays with the cache off (weighting per timeframe, witness tuning, shadow versions, learned promotion, the Aether pack), pattern calibration, strategy captures and replays, compliance, the Aether and platform pushes, the Omega policy; and the ledger step (every 5 minutes) replayed the ledger, the plan learner, the model dataset and the baseline. While idle the sweep keeps only the macro rooms (a High-impact event can still be due) and, on the first sweep of each hour, a heartbeat (the economic calendar refresh, so the coming week's events are in before the reopen, and the health alerts); the ledger step does nothing. The reopen needs no catch-up of its own: the first sweep after it is the first of its hour, and every step reads its own cursors. Measured on the deployed entry point on a Saturday (`test/market-idle.test.mjs`, an empty D1): a sweep from 121 D1 statements and 67 KV reads to 7 and 3, a ledger step from 48 statements to none; on production data the skipped replays read far more rows than the empty database shows. The hourly news cron is unchanged. `CLOSED_MARKET_IDLE` "off" runs every cron in full. No decision, trade or Omega order changes: the market is closed.
- perf(market-data): the pull adapter's cron stands still while gold is closed, and reads each series once a minute while it trades (owner 2026-11-14, through the platform orchestrator: "lebih hemat read/write D1 dan KV"). Every minute's run read all 15 series twice (the refresh, then the bundle), the fallback switch and every publisher's dedupe key, whatever the market: about 31 KV reads a minute in a counting test (47 on a 1m pull minute), about 45,000 a day, weekends included. (1) **Closed market:** once a run of the weekend closure (Friday 17:30 to Sunday 18:00 New York) left nothing to pull, bootstrap or heal, the refresh summary is marked `closed_idle` and every later minute of that closure reads only the summary and stops: 1 KV read a minute, the summary rewritten every 30 minutes as a heartbeat (status stays `up_to_date`, `market_closed: true`, so Nexus stays green). A closure with MT5 fallback bars to heal, or an empty series, keeps running in full; the reopen runs the full cycle, the first post-open pull included. USDT (crypto, its 4H bars) is no longer pulled during an idle weekend and is caught up at the reopen, before any gold bar is built. (2) **Open market:** the bundle is built from the records the refresh left, so a quiet minute reads 17 keys, not 31 (33 on a pull minute, not 47); after an MT5 fallback step that rewrote series, the bundle is read from KV as before. Measured with a counting KV in `market-data-ingestion/test/closed-market.test.mjs`. No bar, record, decision or TradingView call changes on an open market.
- feat(news): news without TradingView, in shadow (owner 2026-11-14; `docs/RFC_CONTENT_AGENT.md` P0, BACKLOG item 26). Every hour, beside TradingView's news pull, the news worker reads publisher RSS feeds (FXStreet, investingLive, Investing.com commodities and forex, the Fed's press releases and speeches) and GDELT, keeps the headlines about gold, oil or the dollar, de-duplicates them and compares the last hour with TradingView's (`src/news-sources.mjs`): headlines on each side, the same story on both, and which side had it first. Nothing reads them yet: the rooms, News Daily and the council still read TradingView's news. No key, no quota; the feed addresses are measured here first (a feed that moved or blocks Workers shows as failing). Results in the news worker's `/health` (`news_shadow`) and Nexus Status ("News sources (shadow)"). `NEWS_SHADOW_MODE` "off" stops it.
- docs(content): the content agent roadmap (`docs/RFC_CONTENT_AGENT.md`, BACKLOG item 26; owner 2026-11-14). What may be shown to the public (TradingView charts with attribution, the system's own analysis as education, never the pulled data, news text or account data), the regulation to check first (POJK 6/2026), the public projection, the content guardian in Aether, posts to X and Instagram through an approval queue, and the automated livestream parked: the owner streams manually from a desktop for now, and an automated stream will never run on the execution VPS. No code change.
- feat(parity, nexus): what a GMI parity mismatch was computed on, and the TradingView quota in Nexus (owner 2026-11-14: "kerjakan 1 dan 2"). (1) On 2026-11-13 18:45 UTC one mismatch in 301 comparisons revoked the 15m GMI identity, and the cause could not be confirmed: the TradingView alert carries only GMI's final fields (pinescript/ssot is never edited) and the comparison kept only the fields that differed. A mismatch now keeps every compared field on both sides (`values`, with TradingView's `shared_core_status`; no input bar, so no price), and a GMI mismatch keeps a fingerprint of the intrabars its footprint read, as cached at the comparison (`intrabars`: per intrabar its minute, volume and direction, and one hash; no price). The parity sweep reads them again once, 30 minutes to 6 hours later, and records whether any minute changed (`intrabars_later`; `src/parity-intrabars.mjs`). The local engines are not touched, so no parity identity changes. Diagnostics only: no result, count or status changes. (2) Nexus Status has a "TradingView quota" panel: today's calls against the account's daily limit (500), per caller today and yesterday, per UTC hour, the time the quota ran out, the gateway's cooldown, and whether a news pull is held back for the chart now. The news worker's `/health` (relayed by `/api/status`) adds `provider_daily_limit` and `chart_reserve`, and names its cron :04.
- fix(news): a fresh shared copy with no headline of the last hour is still a hit. Production 2026-11-14 04:04/04:06 UTC (a Saturday): the scheduled pull found no headline of the last hour, so its shared copy held none, and the AI Worker's pull two minutes later asked the provider again (3 calls). Within the copy's 15 minutes the AI Worker now takes the empty answer; the edge cache keeps its rule (old headlines only: ask upstream).
- fix(market-data, news): a weekday's TradingView calls fit under the provider's quota (owner 2026-11-14: "kerjakan juga 1-3"). The account's quota refuses the 501st call of the UTC day, and a weekday spent about 590 (pull adapter about 455, news 144), so it ran out at about 20:00 UTC on 2026-11-12 and 2026-11-13 and the gold chart stood still until midnight. (1) The AI Worker's hourly news pull (:06) read the scheduled pull's edge cache from another data centre, missed it and asked the provider again for the same headlines (60 calls on 2026-11-13): the pull keeps a shared copy in KV (`tv_mcp:news:last:<symbols>`, 900 s) and the news cron moves to :04. (2) The XAUUSD 1H bars are built from the 1m pull like the 5m and 15m ones (24 calls a weekday); the 4H, daily and weekly bars, which open at the session's start, stay native. (3) The chart comes first: a news pull is refused while it would leave the chart without calls before midnight (`chartReserve`, `TV_PROVIDER_DAILY_LIMIT` 500, `TV_NEWS_RESERVE` "off" to leave it out). A weekday is now about 490 calls. Not changed: the owner's MT5 fallback switch, which was off on 2026-11-13 (set off at 00:01 UTC), so the fallback did not cover 20:00 to 22:00 that day.
- docs(strategy-agents): the default agent stays in the strategy agents' consensus as a balancer (owner 2026-11-14: "tidak setuju, tetap ikut konsensus sebagai penyeimbang"); the proposal to leave it out is closed (BACKLOG item 25, `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_AGENTS.md` §4a). No code change.
- feat(shadow): the GMI latch candidates stop (owner 2026-11-14, "setuju", at the scheduled P5 review). `gmi_v12_70@latch8` and `@latch12` move to `RETIRED_CANDIDATES` with the review's numbers: 116 shared scored bars of the 300 a verdict needs, 0 differing stances, 0 decisive pairs, both 0.44 right. GMI was WAIT on 114 of the bars and the latch changed only its "Prophet risk hold" fields (16 and 8 of 124 bars), so a candidate could never be shown better. The GMI control keeps running, as the baseline of the next grid point if Aether's calibrator proposes one (its rule needs 3 stuck episodes over 10 hourly cards; GMI had 6 cards). `prophet_r37@latch8` keeps running to 2026-12-07 (3 decisive pairs of 30). This changes no decision: shadow candidates never trade.

## 2026-11-13
- feat(decision): no trade while price is squeezed between key zones (owner 2026-11-13: "jika masuk zone kejepit abstain, sehingga akan no trade", then "no trade"). A seventh deterministic guardrail, `room` (`squeezeOf`, `src/strategy-engine.mjs`): it fails for both sides while the nearest Key Level Agent candidate below price and the nearest one above it are less than one 1H average range apart, so the decision is WAIT. Only candidates count (the qualified zones are too dense: on 2026-11-13 the default plan read "price at a key zone" on both sides in 22 of 22 decisions, so that reading cannot define a squeeze); a candidate price is at is the place of the trade, not a wall; no candidate on one side is room. `SQUEEZE_GUARD` "off" leaves the check out. Every decision record keeps the corridor (`squeeze`: the two zones, their distance, in 1H ranges), so the rule's effect can be measured. This blocks trades. From the first targets of the 22 decisions that recorded both sides' plans the corridor was 1.3 to 2.9 ranges in the intraday and swing modes, so the rule is expected to fire in a minority of bars; the exact share is measured from `squeeze` after the market opens. Nexus names the check; the bundled doctrine mirror follows the canon's guardrail list (the stale `confirmation` is gone from it); Aether's doctrine names the guardrail (cryptophys-aether #30).
- fix(test): the scheduled-worker tests no longer fail every weekend. `test/worker-scheduled.test.mjs` ran the crons on the wall clock, and the worker analyses nothing while the market is closed, so two tests failed from Friday 22:00 UTC (found 2026-11-13). The file's clock now starts on a Wednesday in the London session; no assertion changed.
- feat(decision): the strategy agents join the quorum as a witness, weighted like a timeframe room (owner 2026-11-13: "libatkan agen strategi dalam decision council. Atur ulang pembobotannya. agen strategi ini sejajar dengan TF karena merupakan konsensus dari beberapa agen"). A new witness `STRATEGY_AGENTS` (`agentsConsensus`, `src/strategy-agents.mjs`): a live agent speaks for the side it sees its own setup on when its plan passes the reward guardrail; the default agent only where price already holds at its zone; a voice counts 1, moved between 0.5 and 1.5 by the agent's own record from 15 resolved setups; lean = (buy voices − sell voices) / (all voices + 1); silent, the witness drops out. Weights re-set: D1, 4H, 1H and the strategy agents 0.1263 each (was 0.1469 for the rooms), 15m 0.0947, News and the strategy engine 0.0632, Macro 0.0315, key levels, regime and trend 0.0395, macro drivers 0.0237; the calendar's 0.08 and the session's 0.02 kept (the base set of seven likewise: 0.1667 for the three rooms and the agents). `assess` makes the agents' plans before the witness cards (they never read the quorum). The council's prompt names the witness and may list it in `weighed`; the auto-tune treats it like any tuned witness, and a set stored before it is migrated (`withCurrentWitnesses`). Nexus names it in the decision card and the Aether page. This changes the quorum and therefore which side may trade. Aether accepts the witness and carries the weights in the doctrine (cryptophys-aether #29).
- feat(strategy-agents): breaks named for both directions, and a strategy for patterns that have not broken yet (owner 2026-11-13: "kenapa hanya break out apa break down sudah?", then "approve all"). (1) Every agent already planned a short on a pattern broken downward as it plans a long on one broken upward; the wording now says so: `breakout_retest` is labelled "Break and retest" and the council's price-action reading says "broke upward", "broke downward" or "not broken yet". (2) New agent `pattern_inside`: a line pattern still forming (rectangle, broadening formation, wide triangle, wedge or diamond, at least 0.8 ATR between its lines) is traded from one line toward the other, a long from the lower line or a short from the upper one, the stop 0.25 ATR beyond the line, the targets the middle and the far line. Live with the other agents. (3) The per-pattern breaker is kept per agent and pattern, so a pattern's swings inside and its breaks are judged apart; Telegram names both.
- feat(strategy-agents): one strategy per pattern family, over the Projection Agent's whole catalogue (owner 2026-11-13, asked what "libatkan Projection Agent" covers: 53 chart patterns and 10 event patterns; "benar"). The generic `pattern_breakout` of the same day is replaced by eight agents, `pattern_peaks` (16 patterns), `pattern_lines` (15), `pattern_curve` (10), `pattern_bars` (4), `pattern_pole` (3), `pattern_gaps` (3), `pattern_legs` (2) and `pattern_event` (5): the 58 patterns that apply to gold, each in exactly one family, each family with its own entry (the return to the broken line, or near the breakout for flags, measured moves and release patterns), stop (a share of the pattern's height, or the pattern's other line, capped in ATR) and first target (half the measured move for the rounded turns). **Record and breaker per pattern:** the plan learner keeps each pattern's own trades (`plan_setups.pattern`); a pattern whose record is negative over 15 resolved setups is held back while the rest of its family trades on; the council sees the pattern's record beside its family's; Telegram tells the owner. The Nexus Strategy agents page lists the families with their patterns and a Pattern record table. All eight live (`STRATEGY_AGENTS_LIVE`). The family rules are a first reading of each family's method and are unproven on gold: the record decides.
- feat(strategy-agents): the Projection Agent joins the strategy layer (owner 2026-11-13: "libatkan Projection Agent karena dia yang punya knowledge terkait price action"). (1) A fifth agent, `pattern_breakout`, trades the chart patterns the Projection Agent detects broken out or busted on the 15m and 1H charts: the entry at the broken line (at price within 0.15 ATR of it), the stop half the pattern's height behind the line (at most 1.25 ATR), the first target the pattern's measured target. The Projection Agent's own record on gold decides: a pattern measured below the rate of all patterns is left out, the best measured one is taken first, and the setup names the record. Live with the other agents (`STRATEGY_AGENTS_LIVE`), under the same breaker. (2) When the council has strategies to choose from, its evidence carries `price_action`, the Projection Agent's reading of the 15m, 1H and 4H charts (each pattern, whether and which way it broke, its record on gold; no price), and the prompt tells it to choose the strategy that matches that price action.
- feat(strategy-agents): every strategy agent is live (owner 2026-11-13: "live sekarang langsung implementasi karena strategi lebih related dengan key level bukan decision"). `STRATEGY_AGENTS_LIVE` = `sweep_reversal,breakout_retest,range_fade` in `wrangler.jsonc`: when one of them sees its setup on a side and its plan passes the reward guardrail, the council finds it beside the default plan and may choose it; the trade then takes that agent's entry, stop and targets. **This changes what the Decision Engine signals and Omega trades.** With it: a breaker (a live agent whose measured record is negative over 30 resolved setups goes back to shadow by itself and returns when the record recovers; Telegram tells the owner once each way), each agent's record in the council's evidence (overall and in the present trading mode and 1H regime), and a "suspended" badge on the Nexus Strategy agents page.
- feat(nexus): the Strategy agents page (`#/strategy`, sidebar "Strategy agents"; owner 2026-11-13: "pastikan semua ada visualisasi"). The registry with each agent's status, what it is made for (hover) and its measured record (setups, mean R, fill and target rates); the latest decision with what each agent would trade and which plan the trade took; each agent's mean R per market condition; the plan learner's entry and stop grid against the plan (mean R, the difference and the fill rate per arm), its pick per market condition and the referee (independent setups, pick against plan, p-value, held-out part, verdict). Read-only, from `GET /api/strategy-agents` and `GET /api/plan-learning`.
- fix(plan-learning): the replay enters a plan at its level at market, and the referee counts independent setups (version v2). The first production run (189 setups from four days of decisions) showed two things. One plan in four counted as never filled, because price had left the exact entry in the minute the council took, while Omega enters such a plan at market within 0.35 of its stop distance: the replay now does the same on the first bar and keeps the arm's stop and target distances from that fill. And the engine writes a new plan at almost every 15m close (1.2 decisions per setup), so setups overlap and share one price move: the sign test now counts only setups that opened after the one counted before them had ended (60 independent setups, 20 decisive, the newest quarter held out). Stored setups are replayed again under the new rules.
- feat(strategy-agents): pluggable strategy agents, chosen by the council (owner 2026-11-13: "bukan hanya logicnya, itu static, kita perlu agent yang bisa dibongkar pasang menyesuaikan strategy yang tepat yang dipilih oleh council"; `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_AGENTS.md`, `src/strategy-agents.mjs`). One interface (`fits` for the council, a pure `plan(dir, ctx)`), a registry, and four agents: `zone_pullback` (the plan of before, always live), `sweep_reversal`, `breakout_retest` and `range_fade` (in shadow). Every decision records each agent's plan per side (`strategy_plans`); the plan learner measures each agent's own setups; `GET /api/strategy-agents` shows the registry with the records. The council chooses among live agents (`strategy` in its answer; the trade takes that agent's entry, stop and targets inside the reward guardrail, and Telegram names it). No agent but the default is live, so the evidence, the prompt and every trade are unchanged; switches `STRATEGY_AGENTS_LIVE`, `STRATEGY_AGENTS_OFF`, `STRATEGY_AGENTS_MODE`. Making an agent live is BACKLOG item 24.
- feat(plan-learning): the plan learner, shadow only (owner 2026-11-13: "Kita butuh strategy modul learning yang adaptif terhadap price action supaya entry dan resiko lebih presisi"; `docs/RFC_PLAN_LEARNING.md`, `src/plan-learning.mjs`). Each plan the Decision Engine held is replayed on the bars that followed with 20 arms (the entry 0 to 0.3 ATR deeper, the stop 0.5 to 1.25 × the plan's distance) by the Signal Ledger's own simulation; per trading mode and 1H regime the learner picks the arm with the largest shrunk gain over the plan, or keeps the plan; a walk-forward referee (sign test, held-out newest quarter, 100 setups) says whether the learner beats the plan. D1 `plan_setups`, KV `plan-learning:state:v1`, `GET /api/plan-learning`, switch `PLAN_LEARNING_MODE`. It runs in the ledger's 5-minute step and replays the stored history at its first run. No decision, EA signal or Omega order reads it; applying a learned plan is BACKLOG item 23 and needs the owner.
- fix(calendar): a core release is never matched to the headline print. Found in the PPI check of 2026-11-13: TradingView listed only "PPI MoM" (actual 0.3 against 0.4, surprise -0.32 SD), and the ForexFactory event "Core PPI m/m" took that row, so the CALENDAR witness and the council's calendar context read the same surprise twice under two names. `matchRelease` now keeps the core and the headline apart and returns no release when only the other one is listed. The rest of the release behaved as designed: the "before" lean 0.24 (consensus, drift 56 % over 8 h) from 05:30 UTC, the event caution from 11:30 to 13:45 UTC, the "after" lean +0.22 (bullish, lower producer prices) from 13:45 UTC until 15:30 UTC (the learned 2-hour horizon). Backlog item 5 is closed with it.
- fix(nexus): the Aether and Model & Learning pages use the same page margins as every other page (they sat flush against the sidebar and the window edge), and status badges on the Aether page appear only when there is something measured: "in sync" only when sync checks exist, Fidelity and Agent accuracy "OK" only with a sample, the regime badge only when a regime is reported. The same rule elsewhere: Scorecards no longer shows a 50 % average accuracy with no agent scored (n/a instead) and hides the empty Leaderboard panel; the Economic calendar shows "no event risk" only after the calendar was fetched.
- style(nexus): the dashboard reads as a production console, not an annotated prototype (owner 2026-11-13: "masih kotor, terlalu banyak footnote ataupun headnote"). Panel explanations and footnotes moved into a hover info mark (`Hint`, `Panel hint`); panel titles shortened (Engine status, Shadow testing, Parameter tuning, Execution analytics, Agent scorecards, Learning pipeline, Omega, Performance, Signal vs Fill); decorative badges, slogans, the sidebar tagline and the footer list removed; hardcoded schedule notes dropped. Values, states, confirmations (KILL, LIVE) and warnings (the OANDA vs broker price note on the Omega key levels) stay. Tests that pinned old labels now pin the new ones; their behaviour checks are unchanged.
- fix(nexus): stale descriptions and fallbacks (owner 2026-11-13, PR 3 of the hardcoded audit). Macro War Room: no longer "context only · never decides"; it is the MACRO witness, with its weight in force from the latest decision. Shadow versions: the arena runs on 1H and 15m bars (not "identical closed H1 bars"); the holdout target is the backend's (no `?? 30`). Calendar: "imminent" is inside the event's pre-release window (not "within 2 h"), and the gate text says Omega opens no trade inside its own news window. Scorecards: the decorative "Primary Consumers" badges are gone, the reputation weight's range comes from the API, an unscored agent shows "—" instead of a neutral 50 %. Header: "Room live" only while the 15m room is under 45 minutes old ("Room behind", or "Market closed"). Status: the MCP budget and the parity target come from the API (no `800` or `24` fallbacks). Threads: the H1 fallback minutes from the record. Key levels: the tolerance as returned. Lean labels: the neutral band is the quorum's 0.15.
- fix(nexus): Performance, Aether and the decision card show measured values and the decision's own rules, not invented ones (owner 2026-11-13, PR 2 of the hardcoded audit). Performance: the "Alpha Fidelity Score", its friction drag and "governance directive", and the "Aether ingress payload" (built in the browser, never sent) are gone; slippage, latency and spread read "no sample" instead of 0; sizing reads `risk_pct` of equity at the stop (not "ATR multiplier"); the broker name comes from the bridge. Aether: no fidelity 92 / spread 16 / latency 240 fallbacks; the guardrail quorum shown is the decision's `rules.quorum_min` for its mode, and the analytics floor is labelled a recommendation that is not applied (the guardrail never used it); the B-11 reference weights come from `GET /api/weighting/engine` (`default_weights`). Decision card: the reward chip reads the decision's `rules.min_rrr` ("target at a key level" in scalp), the quorum and confidence chips their thresholds, the lean band `rules.quorum_lean_band` (new in each record, with the mode's `rules.lean`), and the countdown is the next decision (Omega's own signal-age limit applies to entries).
- fix(security, nexus, omega): the trading mode no longer changes on a GET, and the Omega page shows the backend's values, not invented ones (owner 2026-11-13: "ya, kerjakan semua mulai PR 1"). (1) `GET /api/trading-mode?mode=` used to write KV `trading:mode` through Nexus's read-only proxy (a link opened while signed in could switch the mode); it now answers 405 and the selector posts to the Omega Worker's `POST /api/trading-mode` (Access person, same origin; `OmegaGateway.setTradingMode`, Telegram on each change, refused while the server pins `TRADING_MODE`). The decision card shows the mode of that decision beside the selector for the next one. (2) Omega's execution tab reads `route` (one route, no failover; the invented "Monex-Demo / HotStandby-Backup / failover YES" are gone), the TCA without "< 150 ms" or "0.0 pts" defaults ("no sample"), the real stop management (`management`: key-level SL+, break-even at `trailing_stop_r` R or off, the 5m fallback, scale-out, the 0.75 ATR noise floor; not "1.0x / 1.5x ATR"), the news gate's window from `BLACKOUT_*` (`macro_risk.window_minutes`, and the broker overview now applies that same window) and the events of the next 24 hours (`upcoming_24h`). The account type is "unknown" when not reported (never "demo"), and the watchdog says what it does and when it last acted (no "zero human intervention", "all nodes nominal" or "DISPLAY=:99").
- docs(backlog): item 22 closed. The owner recreated the Prophet R38 1H TradingView alert; it arrived again at 11:00 UTC, the 1H parity matched (24 fields, approved, 42 in a row) and both `webhook_silent` alerts resolved by themselves at 11:13 UTC. Item 15 notes the `gmi_m5` mismatch pattern (the higher-timeframe gate on the 5m chart).
- feat(health): a TradingView alert that went silent is a health alert (`webhook_silent`). Per chart (5m, 15m, 1H, 4H), a source seen in the last 7 days whose newest alert is more than 3 bars older than the newest of the other sources there, while that chart's alerts still arrive. Found 2026-11-13: Prophet R38 1H (and its HUD) has sent nothing since 2026-11-10 07:00 UTC, and the 1H parity record stood still with no alert. BACKLOG reviewed: item 22 added (restore that alert), items 12, 15 and 21 brought up to date.
- fix(omega): a deal stored without its source takes it from the next report. The clean-up of the duplicate deals (#302) kept each ticket's first row; for 25 deals that row predates the `source` column, so the Omega policy's rollback check (source `omega`) would have missed six Omega deals. The bridge re-reports every deal with its source, so they heal on the next sync; a source once set never changes. Verified: 36 rows for 36 deals and the unique index `ea_deals_ticket` in production at 09:36 UTC.
- fix(omega): a closed deal is stored once per deal ticket. The bridge re-reports each closed deal on every sync and its times can move by a second, so the `(deal_ticket, closed_ms)` key let every report through: on 2026-11-13 D1 `ea_deals` held 116,821 rows for 36 deals. `storeDeal` now skips a ticket it already has, and every reader counts the first row of each ticket only (`FIRST_OF_DEAL`: the deal list, Omega's statistics, the Aether policy's rollback check, the organism status). The old duplicate rows are removed by the 5-minute ledger step (`dedupeDeals`, owner 2026-11-13: "setuju, bersihkan duplikat deal lewat PR"): at most 20,000 rows a run, keeping the first stored row of each ticket, then a unique index `ea_deals_ticket` holds one row per deal ticket.
- feat(nexus, ledger): why Omega is or is not entering, and the decisions the ledger held back (owner 2026-11-13: "pastikan semua ada visualisasi"). The broker overview carries the planner's current notes (`planner`: codes and since when), and the Omega page's Signal panel shows them as plain sentences ("why", "adjust", "info"). A BUY or SELL the no-stacking rule holds back is now recorded (D1 `signal_ledger_held`: the decision, the row that held it, why) and `/api/ledger` returns `held`; the Performance page lists them. Before, such a decision left no trace.
- feat(nexus): Model & Learning page and the model's advice in the decision card (owner 2026-11-13: "pastikan semua ada visualisasi, sehingga owner/user mengetahui dan memahami"). `#/model` shows the learning loop (record, learn, prove, use) with live states, the dataset, the baseline with its council gate and per-model walk-forward and prospective scores (progress to 300 decisions), the LightGBM decision model (shadow), and the notes that went live by themselves. The Decision Engine card shows the model's advice (`context.model`) or that it is not connected yet, and the CALENDAR witness's phase with the pre-release drift. The Shadow versions page groups the knowledge arm by 1H room, 15m War Room and council, and its copy no longer says a note needs a PR.
- feat(model): the model report carries the daily decision model (owner 2026-11-13: "lanjutkan perluas model report gbm ke Aether"). The hourly `cryptophys.model-report.v1` gains an optional `gbm` block (`gbmReport`, `src/model-gbm.mjs`): its `model_id`, training time, rows, feature count, walk-forward checks, prospective record (scored on the same labeled rows as the baseline's) and top features by gain, `connected: false`; never a tree, threshold or leaf. Aether validates and keeps it (cryptophys-aether, `overview().model.gbm`) for Cerebrum's Training panel.
- fix(nexus): the Cerebrum Console links point at the Nexus mount `https://nexus.cryptophys.work/console/` instead of `console.cryptophys.work`, before the platform retires the old hostnames (owner 2026-11-13, "setuju pension domain lama"; ADR-0010).
- feat(model): the decision model, trained daily by GitHub Actions (owner 2026-11-13: "1-3"; RFC step 3). `.github/workflows/train-model.yml` (04:17 UTC) fetches the labeled dataset through the Access-protected read API, trains LightGBM per label set and side on pre-decision features (`scripts/train_gbm.py`, walk-forward checked, deterministic) and writes compact trees to KV `model:gbm:v1`; the trainer refuses to write unless its JSON reproduces LightGBM exactly. The AI Worker evaluates the trees itself (`src/model-gbm.mjs`), stores their probabilities per new decision beside the baseline's (shadow only) and shows the checks and the prospective record at `GET /api/model/gbm`. Nothing from the dataset or the model reaches the public log or an artifact. ONNX for the EA is step 4.
- feat(knowledge): the shadow arm on the 15m War Room and the council; fix(news): /health inside the Drift Detector's probe (owner 2026-11-13: "1-3"). (1) A learned note addressed to a 15m War Room agent or to the Decision Engine council is now tested like the 1H ones: one extra call with the note on the same bar, against the real answer (for the council, put through the same guardrails), scored on the 15m bars, subjects ending in `@15`; promotion goes live on the chart tested, and a live council note is watched on the council's own decisions. `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` defaults to 16 (shared by the three). (2) The news Worker's `/health` read about fifteen KV keys one after another; on a cold isolate that once passed the Drift Detector's 5-second probe (2026-11-13 02:00 UTC, "drifted unavailable"). They are read in parallel now.
- fix(ledger, omega): the Signal Ledger and Omega follow the same signals (owner 2026-11-13: "check kenapa posisi yang dilakukan oleh signal engine berbeda dengan omega", then "setuju"). (1) The ledger held new decisions back while a same-side row was `open`, and a row stays open while it is followed to TP2: one row past TP1 and at break-even since 14:57 on 2026-11-12 kept every SELL out of the ledger until 00:49 (4 of 70 traded decisions recorded since 2026-11-12). A row now holds signals back only while it waits for its fill or is open, before TP1 and without SL+, as Omega's own no-stacking rule. (2) Omega entered a limit plan at market when price was past the entry toward the stop and kept the plan's stop distance from that fill, which put the stop beyond the plan's key level (a SELL at 3903.98 against a 3888.48 entry, its stop 14 past the plan's). It now waits while price is more than 0.35 × the plan's stop past the entry (`price_past_entry`), and the ledger's Omega policy fills a limit only inside the same band.
- feat(model): the baseline model connects itself to the Decision Engine council (owner 2026-11-13: "buat agar autonomous"). The model now reads only what was known before the council answered (never the decision's stance, bias, confidence or plan). Each hour a gate reads its prospective record; when both sides of a label set hold 300 unseen decisions, beat the base rate significantly (paired Brier gain p < 0.05) and reach AUC 0.55, the council receives `context.model` (each side's chance of target before stop, against the base rates) as advice; it disconnects itself when that no longer holds, and the owner hears of each change on Telegram. The guardrails still decide what can trade. Switch `MODEL_AUTO_CONNECT` (on).
- feat(knowledge): learned notes promote and retire themselves (owner 2026-11-13: "Apakah menyambungkan ini masih manual? Jika iya, buat agar autonomous"). With cryptophys-aether#26, a text proposal is shadow-tested without waiting for a person; `src/learned-live.mjs` then puts a note whose shadow verdict reads better than active live for exactly the agent and chart it was tested on (the 1H room, never the moderator), retires it when Aether drops it (a rejection) or when the agent turns significantly worse live (binomial p < 0.05 from 40 decided calls), tells the owner on Telegram, and stops the shadow arm testing it. D1 `learned_live`, `/api/tuning` `learned_live`, switch `LEARNED_AUTO_PROMOTION` (on). The prompts read the bundled pack plus the live items.
- feat(model): the learning loop strengthened (owner 2026-11-13: "strengthen autonomous learning & knowledge looping"). (1) A self-trained baseline (`src/model-baseline.mjs`, RFC step 2): every hour a logistic regression per label set and side learns from the labeled dataset, is judged walk-forward (Brier skill against the base rate, AUC, R of its picks), predicts each new decision in shadow (`model_dataset.shadow_json`, point in time) and builds a prospective record once those decisions are labeled; KV `model:baseline:v1`, `GET /api/model/baseline`, switch `MODEL_BASELINE_MODE` (on). (2) A wide label set (stop 3 ATR, target 4.5 ATR, 12 hours) next to the tight one, whose 1 ATR stop ended most rows at a stop on both sides. (3) The model report (`cryptophys.model-report.v1`, no price) goes to Aether's `ingestModelReport` after each retraining (cryptophys-aether stores it). (4) Approved learned notes now also reach the 15m War Room's agents and moderator and the Decision Engine council (consumer `council`), as they reached the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms; the bundled pack is still empty, so no prompt changes today.
- feat(model): the custom model's dataset, step 1 of `docs/RFC_CUSTOM_MODEL.md` (owner 2026-11-13: "end goalnya mampu membuat custom model sendiri dari knowledge ini khusus untuk EA dan XAUUSD Trading Assistant"; "setuju"). `src/model-dataset.mjs` turns every Decision Engine decision, the stored history included, into a D1 `model_dataset` row: around a hundred numeric, point-in-time features (witnesses, quorum, guardrails, the plan in ATR, structure, regime, session, news, calendar, cross assets, recent moves in ATR; no price) and, once 4 hours have passed, triple-barrier labels for a long and a short (target 1.5 ATR, stop 1 ATR, or expired; on 1m bars where they reach). Run by the Signal Ledger's 5-minute step; `GET /api/model/dataset/summary` and `GET /api/model/dataset`; switch `MODEL_DATASET_MODE` (on). It feeds nothing. BACKLOG item 21 holds the next steps (baseline, a decision model trained in GitHub Actions to ONNX, a LoRA language model).

## 2026-11-12
- feat(projection): the chart patterns' weights calibrated on gold, and the Projection Agent in the rooms (owner 2026-11-12: "ya kerjakan kalibrasi bobot pola chart; Projection Agent sekarang harus masuk ke room"). `src/pattern-calibration.mjs` replays the Key Level Agent's own detector over every bar of the cached 15m, 1H, 4H and daily series each hour (about half a second), follows each breakout to its target, its other line or a horizon of twice its width (30 to 120 bars), and turns each pattern's shrunk success rate into a weight factor (0.5 to 1.5, from 20 resolved breakouts) that scales its broken-line, target and other-line levels in `buildKeyLevels`; KV `chart-patterns:calibration:v1`, `GET /api/patterns/calibration`, switch `PATTERN_CALIBRATION_MODE` (auto). The Projection Agent (`src/projection-agent.mjs`, manifest desk `projection`) speaks in the 15m Live War Room and the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms after the Risk Manager: its data is the room chart's patterns with their record on gold and the HUD plane's projection levels, each as a distance from the last close in average bar ranges (rooms state no price). War Room cap 185 calls an hour (170).
- chore(prod): Aether's learned knowledge on in production, and the backlog reviewed (owner 2026-11-12: "ya kerjakan keduanya, mode production harus active semua"). `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED` and `LEARNED_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED` are "true": the shadow arm tests the three approved role reminders (htf, risk, chronos matrix), and the rooms read the bundled learned pack (empty until an owner PR adds an item the shadow arm found better). Every other production switch of the AI Worker and the pull adapter was already on or at its fullest mode (Decision Engine, weighted witnesses, Omega engine and 5m execution, Corpus Oracle shadow, broker trigger and shadow, feed fallback auto, rooms, news, macro, room memo, calendar contrarian, chart patterns, ledger alerts, weighting and witness auto-tune); `PREVIEW_FIXTURES_ENABLED` stays off (test fixtures only). `docs/BACKLOG.md`: item 19 (GMI re-approval) and the Telegram part of item 5 closed, item 15 updated (1H, 15m and 5m approved; 4H at 17 of 24, D1 at 2 to 3), item 6 replaced by the review of the Decision Engine and Omega per mode about 2026-11-19, item 12's switch done.
- feat(nexus): the live setup's lifecycle, locked levels, swing structure and the bias split on the Decision Engine card (owner 2026-11-12, after comparing Nexus with another assistant's screen: "ya kerjakan semua nomor jika memang lebih baik, awas jangan sampai regresi, redundancy dan duplikasi"). `GET /api/conclusion/global` carries `setups` (`activeSetups`, src/signal-ledger.mjs): the Decision Engine's ledger rows still live, or ended within 2 hours, each with its levels as locked at its decision, its lifecycle on the ledger's 1m bars (signal, entry, SL+, TP1, TP2, invalidation or cancellation, with times), the R so far, Omega's trade of it, and the decisions since (same side confirms, opposite calls against). The ledger keeps each policy's own exit time (`tp1_closed_at`, `tp2_closed_at`, `tp2_exit_reason`). The decision record carries `structure` (src/market-structure.mjs: the 4H, 1H and 15m swing structure, HH/HL, LH/LL, from the Projection Agent's confirmed turns; labels only, not a witness, not read by the council). The card shows the setup with its steps, the structure strip, a countdown to the next 15m close, the witnesses' technical and fundamental lean with their weight shares, the quorum's weight as one bar (long, neither, short), and the entry's key-level zone; the decision's own plan ladder shows only when no live setup of its side already holds locked levels. Left out as not better: a third target (not traded or measured; Omega's trailing TP already moves to the next key level) and a separate market-conditions header (the card's market context and the Omega page already carry it). The "Live metrics" line repeating the confidence and the quorum is removed.
- feat(ledger): the Signal Ledger on 1m bars, every 5 minutes (owner 2026-11-12: "kenapa status entry/exit/tp selalu telat", "ya kerjakan keduanya"). Entries, exits and targets were recorded at the close of the 15m bar that held them and only at the parity sweep (:10, :25, :40, :55), up to about 25 minutes late. The simulation now runs on the feed's 1m bars where the 1m series reaches (about two days), 15m and 1H bars before them (`stitchBars`), and the ledger (signals, Omega policy, Telegram alerts, execution report) runs on its own cron `2-59/5 * * * *` (`runLedgerStep`), no longer in the parity sweep. The Omega policy (`cryptophys.ledger-omega.v2`) enters the minute after the decision, waits for a limit only within the signal's age, manages every minute, and reads a decision from when it was made (`decided_at`) rather than its bar's close.
- feat(omega): OmegaExecutor 1.13 writes no macro bars by default (owner 2026-11-12: "seharusnya memang tidak perlu selain XAUUSD saja", "data makro dimatikan di mt5"): `CorpusMacroEnabled` is false, so the broker supplies XAUUSD only (its quotes, `corpus.json`) and the USD calendar (`calendar.json`, unchanged). The demo broker had no oil and no USDSEK anyway, so the DXY and USOIL fallback could never be built from it; during a TradingView outage XAGUSD, USOIL and DXY now wait for TradingView. The server side of stage 2 stays (it takes macro bars when an EA sends them).
- feat(ledger): the Omega policy per trading mode in the Signal Ledger, reported to Aether (owner 2026-11-12: "ya kerjakan kebijakan Omega di Signal Ledger sesuai dengan mode intraday, swing, dan scalping. Ini digunakan untuk inputan ke aether dan selanjutnya akan menjadi knowledge"). Each Decision Engine signal now keeps its `trading_mode` and `entry_action`, and a third policy follows it beside TP1 and TP2: what Omega would have done (`simulateOmega`): market entry on the bar after the decision (a limit plan within 0.35 × the stop distance), the plan's stop and target distances from the fill, the day's gates in R (`max_daily_loss_pct / risk_pct` of the Omega settings, open risk at the stops, max positions, no stacking; the budget caps the size), and at every 15m close the latest decision's quorum power and key levels through Omega's own `keyLevelPlan` (SL+, trailing TP, the close on a reversed power), an opposite decision, 72 h. New columns (`omega_status`, `omega_gate`, `omega_filled_at`, `omega_closed_at`, `omega_exit`, `r_omega`, `omega_size`, `omega_json`), decisions still in the 15m series backfilled once. `GET /api/ledger` adds `by_mode` (scalp, intraday, swing × TP1, TP2, Omega, the paired comparison on the same signals and the gates that skipped) and an `omega` block per source; Nexus Performance gets an "Omega policy" target, a per-mode table and an Omega column. The sweep sends `cryptophys.execution-report.v1` (per mode and per trade: bar, session, mode, side, R and exit, never a price) to Aether's `KnowledgeIngress.ingestExecutionReport` when it changed (D1 `aether_execution_report_pushes`). Approximations: feed bars, a fixed spread, no 5m or adaptive stop, no scale-out.
- feat(platform): HUD agents now publish a `hud_digest` per 15-minute sweep across the 15m, 1H, 4H and 1D rooms — on a stance move into/out of BUY/SELL or a directional bias flip (BULLISH↔BEARISH), otherwise a 6-hour heartbeat — so Prophet and Tactical Muscles, which had published nothing since 2026-11-06 because their 15m stance is always WAIT under the confirmation rule while their bias kept moving (21 and 31 flips in 72 h), stop showing "idle"; per-agent/per-timeframe cursors in `platform_hud_cursors` (migration `0019`), and the report timeout is 15 s (a 5 s timeout recorded reports the kernel had stored as failed and sent them again).
- fix(decision): the scalp target finds the nearest key level (owner 2026-11-12: "bugs lagi?"). At 13:00 UTC the council's SELL turned WAIT on "no key level ahead for the scalp target": the nearest support zone sat 8.13 below the entry, 0.46 × the 1H ATR, under the 0.5 ATR the first target needed, and the next support was a zone the Key Level Agent had left out by its cap of two candidates per side. In scalp the first target is now the nearest zone 0.25 ATR or more ahead, qualified zones included (`SCALP_TARGET`); intraday and swing are unchanged. The decision record's `key_levels` keep the qualified zones too, for Omega's stop and target.
- fix(omega-health): the EA liveness is read from OmegaExecutor's bridge-relayed state when that is fresher than the legacy heartbeat, so the AI Mesh and the Nexus organism card no longer show a live OmegaExecutor as "never seen"/degraded; `source` added to the `ea` block, and its `version` (with `mode` "local_stops"/"omega_only") from OmegaExecutor 1.12's `executor` report (schema `omega-health.v1` unchanged otherwise).
- fix(nexus): the Omega chart fills its box (owner 2026-11-12: "tidak ada space kosong dibawah sumbu waktu, hilangkan label yang menutupi volume"). The chart was a fixed 430 px inside a row as tall as the Market Watch sidebar, which left empty space under the time axis; it now stretches to the row's height (at least 430 px). The key-level legend that sat over the volume bars is gone; its text is the Key Levels toggle's tooltip.
- feat(omega): OmegaExecutor 1.12 reports its version and its local stop inputs, and Omega checks them (owner 2026-11-12: "ya tambahkan versi EA dan status input ke server"). After the owner compiled 1.11 the server could not tell which EA ran or whether its local break-even and trailing were off: a chart keeps the inputs of its old attachment. The EA writes `executor` (name, version, `AutoBreakEvenEnabled`, `TrailingStopEnabled`, `DynamicAtrStopEnabled`) into `state.json`; the AI Worker keeps it (`executorInfoFrom`) and the overview reports `bridge.executor` as ok, local_management, outdated or unknown; the Omega page and the Nexus Omega card show the version and who controls the stops; the plan notes `ea_local_management` while the EA still trails by itself. Needs the EA recompiled once more (1.12).
- feat(projection): chart patterns are the Projection Agent's main knowledge (owner 2026-11-12: "adopsi semua yang ada di buku untuk main muscle knowledge Projection Agent"; "Fibonacci, cone, dan trendline, dan tambahan 53 pola chart ... dan 10 pola event"). `src/chart-patterns.mjs` catalogues the 53 chart patterns and 10 event patterns of Bulkowski's *Encyclopedia of Chart Patterns* by chapter and recognises them on each chart's closed bars with detectors written from scratch: boundary lines (rectangles, triangles, wedges, broadening formations, diamonds), peaks and valleys (double and triple tops and bottoms with the Adam and Eve shapes, head and shoulders and its complex form, three falling peaks, three rising valleys), tall bars (pipes, horns), a pole and a pause (flags, pennants, the high and tight flag), gaps and islands, measured moves, and curves (rounding turns, cups with handle, scallops, bump and run). What is adopted is the method: the breakout, the measure rule, the return to the breakout price and the busted pattern; the book's statistics (daily US stocks, 1991 to 2004) are not copied and sizes are in the chart's average true range. Each pattern gives the Key Level Agent levels in the projection family (breakout lines 0.6, a broken line 0.8, the measured target 0.7). Five event patterns have a gold reading from the released USD figures (`recentReleases`); the five stock-only ones are marked not applicable. The decision record keeps `chart_patterns` for measuring each pattern on XAUUSD. Switch `CHART_PATTERNS_MODE` (on). Notes: `docs/books/bulkowski-chart-patterns/NOTES.md`; the book file is not kept in the repository.
- feat(omega): Omega controls the stop and the target from the key levels and the quorum power; scalp targets the nearest key level (owner 2026-11-12: "semua dikontrol dari omega bukan EA, untuk mode scalping jangan TP berdasarkan RRR tapi target di Key Level terdekat, atur SL+ dan Trailing TP juga harus berdasarkan keylevel menyesuaikan Bull/Bear Power"; answers: Power is the Decision Engine's quorum, no RRR limit, close when the power reverses). **Scalp:** the target is the plan's first target, the nearest key level ahead, whatever it pays (`MODE_THRESHOLDS.scalp` `target: "nearest"`, `min_rrr` 0; a projection is no target); an adaptive stop keeps the target at the level. **Signal:** the latest decision's quorum score (`power`, `power_ms`), its 12 key zones (`key_levels`, recorded in the decision), `trading_mode` and the broker's basis against the feed (`feed_basis`, median of matched closes). **Omega** (`keyLevelPlan`): SL+ behind the key levels price passed (further back with a strong power, tighter with a weak one, never back), trailing TP to the next key level near the target with a strong power, and a close of a winner when the power is -0.5 or worse against it; the 5m trail and the extended target remain only without a known basis. **OmegaExecutor 1.11:** its local break-even and micro trailing are off by default (they had moved two shorts' stops on 2026-11-11 beside Omega). Needs the EA recompiled and re-attached on the MT5 host. Doctrine `engine_thresholds` and `omega_execution_gates` amended (cryptophys-aether companion PR).
- fix(omega): a daily risk budget and no stacking on an unprotected side (owner 2026-11-12: "ya kerjakan budget risiko harian dan larangan tumpuk posisi"). On the demo account four SELL positions stopped out between 23:01 and 00:56 UTC for −42.21 (−4.06 % of the day's 1039.42), twice the 2 % limit: after −10.35 at 23:01 only 10.44 of the day was left, yet two shorts opened 13 minutes apart (00:19 at a 13.49 stop, 00:32 at 0.02 lot on an 8.74 stop), because the limit compared only the equity with the floor and a second short could join an unprotected one. Now a new entry's lot is capped to what the day can still lose (the equity above the floor less the open positions' loss at their stops, `openRiskAtStops`; notes `lot_capped_daily_budget`, `daily_risk_budget`), and a second position on a side waits until every position on that side is protected, its stop at or beyond its open price (`same_side_unprotected`). Replayed, both 00:19 and 00:32 entries are refused (`test/omega-daily-budget.test.mjs`).
- fix(decision): Prophet's ruthless cut loss (t_enc 4) vetoes a long only and supports a short (owner 2026-11-12: "setuju … justru jadikan ini sebagai opportunity"). t_enc 4 comes only from d_enc 3, a bearish structure break on the 1-hour chart, so it tells longs to get out; `riskChecks` vetoed both sides, and from 07:01 to 08:46 UTC eight bearish decisions (quorum −0.65 to −0.76, 93 % of the weight short, every other short guardrail passing, 4.51R) were held at WAIT: a false-positive veto and missed shorts. Now: a veto for a long, a support for a short (+0.1 strategy confidence; `supports` per side in the decision record); the knowledge base's short arguments list it as for, and the HUD reading says it cuts longs.
- feat(nexus): each key-level block on the Omega chart is labelled above with its role, strength and price range (owner 2026-11-12: "label rentang harga juga ditampilkan di atas zone"), e.g. "KL R · strong · 3972.88–3976.88"; the labels are drawn in front of the candles on a dark backing (a second, top-layer view), the bands stay behind them.
- fix(nexus): the Omega chart's key levels are transparent blocks, not lines (owner 2026-11-12: "ubah key level di chart omega menjadi block zone transparans, jangan berupa garis supaya tidak ambigu dengan garis posisi"). A series primitive (`ui/src/lib/keyZoneBands.ts`, lightweight-charts 4.2 `attachPrimitive`) draws each of the War Room's six zones nearest to price as a translucent band from its low to its high behind the candles, coloured by role and shaded by strength, with its label inside (or just above a thin zone); no price line and no axis label, so every line on the chart is the bid, ask, plan or a position.
- fix(calendar): the reaction horizon after a release is its peak (owner 2026-11-12: "ya perbaiki horizon reaksi pakai peak 2 jam"). Among the hours where gold followed the rule at least 55 % of the time with a positive mean, the one with the largest mean move, and its hit rate for the strength; it was the longest such hour. Production: inflation 2 h at 91 % (strength 1, was 24 h at 56 %, 0.24), jobs 1 h at 67 % (0.68, was 24 h, 0.32); the other kinds are unchanged. The release's surprise is weighed, by the CALENDAR witness, the cautions and the Calendar Agent, until its peak.
- feat(calendar): the contrarian option for the pre-release drift (owner 2026-11-12: "ya buatkan opsi kontrarian untuk survei"; `CALENDAR_CONTRARIAN = "on"`). A kind whose gold drifted against the consensus significantly before its releases (at least 55 %, a negative mean, one-sided binomial p < 0.05) leans the other way, by the same scale, from its contrarian horizon; the Calendar Agent's projection, the Macro War Room and the CALENDAR witness follow it. Measured in production: surveys, 72 % against over the 4 hours before (21 of 29, p 0.012), lean the other way at 0.9; no other kind qualifies. Off, they are held at 0 as before.
