- feat(ai-usage): the room memo reuses answers (`ROOM_MEMO_ENABLED = "true"`; owner Tahap 1: "kerjakan tahap 0 lalu tahap 1", reuse once the shadow shows `memo_same / memo_hits` ≥ 0.9). In the 24 h before the switch, `war_room_opening` repeated 444 of 1,303 requests with the same stance and bias 436 times (0.98) and `tf_room_opening` 103 of 450, all the same (1.00). At those rates the memo saves about 550 calls and about 500,000 tokens a day; a reused answer is validated by the room again, and answers expire after 24 hours.
- feat(calendar): the pre-release drift and the CALENDAR witness (owner 2026-11-12: "ada tendensi market bergerak sesuai proyeksi horizon menjelang release economic calendar", "ya kerjakan 1-3 dan 4a", "sekaligus horizon news release mempengaruhi bobot", CALENDAR 0.08). The sweep measures gold's drift 1/2/4/8 hours before every release in the consensus direction (D1 `econ_releases.pre_json`, history included); per event kind the hit rate and a learned pre-release horizon. The Calendar Agent's consensus lean takes its strength from it (whole while unmeasured, 0 when gold did not drift the consensus way reliably) and its imminent window from the learned horizon; the projection, the Macro War Room and the council read the evidence. A new CALENDAR witness weighs 0.08 only inside a release's horizon (the consensus before, the surprise × the measured reaction after); the other witnesses were scaled to make room, so their shares outside a horizon are unchanged. The auto-tuning includes it; a stored set is migrated. Nexus shows it as context outside a horizon.
- feat(organism): the XAUUSD organs' DNA for the platform census (owner 2026-11-12: "kerjakan B dulu lalu A"; A: "DNA untuk XAUUSD dan Omega dengan auto-register"). `organism.json` (`xauusd-organism.v1`) declares the five Workers (Omega included), the 13 agents that report to the platform kernel and the 25 AI tasks of the code; `test/organism-dna.test.mjs` keeps it equal to the code, so a new agent or AI task cannot ship without reaching the census. The new RPC entrypoint `OrganismDna` (`dna()` only) serves it to the platform Forge (`EXT_ORGANISM_XAUUSD`), which registers what is new by consensus, within its external-organ ceiling (tier 1, no council seat, no privileged capability).
- feat(nexus): the War Room's key levels on the Omega chart (owner 2026-11-12: "apakah di omega chart bisa ditampilkan key level yang diambil dari war room ?", "kerjakan sekarang, tampilan sesuai usulan"). The Key Level Agent's six candidate zones nearest to price (`GET /api/conclusion/global` → `strategy.key_levels`, every 60 s), each as its upper and lower edge, coloured by role (support, resistance, at price) and shaded by strength, behind a "Key Levels" toggle that is on by default. The War Room panel and the chart share one selection (`ui/src/lib/keyZones.ts`). The zones are on the feed's prices (OANDA via TradingView), like the chart's candles; the legend says the broker's prices may differ by a few cents.
- fix(nexus): the Performance page shows no money (owner 2026-11-12: "hapus broker capital, sesuai aturan never money; catatan kita akan membuat modul sendiri terkait Financial Management"). Removed: Broker Capital and Live Valuation (read through a key spelled in pieces that slipped past the page's never-money test), Floating P&L, the positions' swap and P&L columns, the financing and P&L rows of the disparity table, the P&L in the exit confirmation, and the dollar fields of the Aether ingress card. The test now refuses any dollar template or split key on the page. Account figures will live in a Financial Management module of their own.
- fix(truthfulness): no invented numbers in the organism status or the order route (owner 2026-11-12: "ya bersihkan sekalian"). `GET /api/organism/status` returned a fixed $500 treasury, a burn and runway derived from it, a 150% AI quota and labels nothing measured (power mode, "canon sync: synchronized"); it now returns `organism-status.v2` from `src/organism-status.mjs`: the XAUUSD session (open or closed), the Decision Engine's state and latest decision time, Omega's control, EA heartbeat and bridge sync (from `omegaHealth`), and the deals closed in the last 7 days (null when the read fails, not 0). This Worker holds no treasury; the platform's Metabolism does. Omega's overview replaced `sor` (hardcoded "Monex-Demo" and "HotStandby-Backup" brokers and `failover_ready: true`, while no failover exists) with `route`: the active route from the terminal's own server, its status (`no_connection`, `unknown`, `disconnected`, `trade_disabled`, `trading`), `failover: "none"` and the bridge sync age. The Nexus Digital Organism card and the Performance page show only those values, with "—" where nothing was measured.
- feat(decision): News leans with its TLDR, and the Decision Engine card tells stale from no direction (owner 2026-11-12: "ya bedakan status antara stale dan tidak ada keputusan, setuju kerjakan 1 dan 2"). Production 00:32 UTC: News, Regime and Session looked stale but were fresh: News read −0.10 from its headline balance under a BEARISH TLDR at 0.72, Regime leaned 0 because the 1H chart is chaotic (by design), and Session never leans (context). News now leans 0.7 × the TLDR's stance × confidence + 0.3 × the headlines' balance (the headlines alone without a TLDR); it feeds the Global Conclusion and the NEWS witness (0.08). The card shows each witness's status: stale, no direction, context or no data; only a witness leaning against the side shows a cross.
- fix(decision): MACRO DRIVERS counts only fresh data (owner 2026-11-12: "kerjakan perbaikan cek kesegaran macro drivers"). The DXY 24-hour change (News Daily's context) and the US 10-year daily change (the correlations) each count only while their last 4-hour bar closed within 8 hours; a stale one drops out, and with both stale the witness is shown stale and leaves the quorum. Before, the witness had no freshness check: on 2026-11-11, with the TradingView quota spent from 13:05 UTC, the last dollar change kept counting for hours. The market card's dollar change and the dollar caution follow the same rule. Reviewed the overlap with the Macro War Room: kept as two witnesses (a reasoned 4-24 hour bias and the mechanical 24-hour pull, 0.07 together), the overlap left to the auto-tuning.
- feat(omega): 5m execution for Omega, timed by the broker (owner 2026-11-11 "ya rancang A+C, timing dari broker langsung"; 2026-11-12: a 3-bar window, a lot cap of 1.5x, "Semua langsung aktif"; `docs/RFC_OMEGA_5M_EXECUTION.md`). The pull adapter runs the 5m Prophet producer on the broker's M5 bars at each broker 5m close and sends the confirmations and the 3-bar 5m structure to the AI Worker (KV `trigger:broker:m5:latest`). Once 400 broker M5 bars are there, a market entry waits for a broker 5m Prophet confirmation on its side within 3 bars of the decision; until then, or when the lane is stale, TradingView's 5m or 15m confirmation. Omega tightens a market entry's stop to the 5m swing (never wider than the plan's, a floor of 0.5 × the 5m ATR), keeps the plan's target and caps the lot at 1.5 × the plan's; it trails its positions on the 5m structure from 1R and closes one on an opposite broker 5m confirmation from 0.5R. Switches `OMEGA_EXEC_5M` and `BROKER_TRIGGER_MODE`, both on by default. Doctrine fact `decision.execution_trigger` = `prophet_candle_for_market_orders_broker_5m`.
- feat(omega): `OmegaHealth`, the execution host's liveness for the platform's AI Mesh (owner 2026-11-12: "bagaimana kita juga ingin menampilkan remote vps atau omega engine dengan EA nya bisa termonitor di Ai Mesh"; "kerjakan B dulu"). A new RPC entrypoint on the AI Worker with one method, `health()` (`src/omega-health.mjs`, `omega-health.v1`): the EA heartbeat's age and freshness (15 minutes), its version, mode, trading allowed and open positions; the Omega control mode; the VPS bridge's sync age and freshness (2 minutes), version, and the terminal's connected and trade-allowed while its state is fresh. Read-only, measured only (an unmeasured value is null), no account figure, server name or control. The platform kernel binds it as `XAUUSD_OMEGA_HEALTH`.

## 2026-11-11
- feat(corpus): Corpus Oracle phase 2, the broker shadow lane (owner "All", the deferred plans). At each published 15m close the pull adapter runs the 15m engines once more on the broker's M15 bars (cached, extended from the corpus) and sends the field-by-field agreement with TradingView's records to the AI Worker (D1 `broker_shadow`, `GET /api/corpus/status` `shadow`). It feeds no parity, room, decision or signal; it needs 400 broker M15 bars and pauses while the MT5 fallback runs. `BROKER_SHADOW_MODE` on|off.
- feat(omega): B-12, Omega's risk policy from Aether (issue #227; owner "All"; cryptophys-aether #18). Hourly pull of `AETHER.omegaPolicy()` into KV `omega:policy`; `applyOmegaPolicy` applies it behind `OMEGA_POLICY_AUTO`/`policy_auto` (off by default) within Omega's hard bounds, audited (actor `aether`) and sent to Telegram; after 50 Omega trades a policy whose profit factor is below 1 and below the previous 50 is rolled back and its doctrine sha refused. `ea_deals.source` records who opened each position.
- feat(feed): MT5 fallback stage 2, the macro series and the economic calendar from the broker (owner 2026-11-11: "intinya input untuk AI Worker bisa sama persis tidak ada regresi"). OmegaExecutor 1.10 writes the broker's M1 bars of silver, oil and the dollar index's six pairs and the MQL5 USD calendar; bridge 1.1.0 sends them; the AI Worker keeps them from Omega's active broker and user only. During a fallback the pull adapter rebuilds USOIL 5m/15m/1H, XAGUSD 4H and DXY 4H (the ICE formula), and the econ step takes released values from the MQL5 calendar when TradingView's fails. US10Y, USDT dominance and news have no broker source. Needs the new EA and bridge on the MT5 host.
- feat(feed): the MT5 fallback switch in the War Room, and 4H and daily bars from the broker (owner 2026-11-11: "possible kita sediakan menu toggle data source di war room", "owner akan mengaktifkan untuk fallback", "input untuk AI Worker bisa sama persis tidak ada regresi"). A Nexus card shows the source in force, the switch and the broker corpus' freshness; a person switches it through Omega (`/omega/api/feed-fallback`: Access, same origin, a reason), audited in D1 `feed_fallback_events` and sent to Telegram; the pull adapter reads it (KV `market-data:v2:feed-fallback-mode`) before each fallback step. During a fallback the 4H and daily series are rebuilt from the broker's minutes on the grid of their last native bar (22:00 UTC session start), only for buckets the 1m series covers; the weekly bar waits for TradingView.
- feat(gateway): who spends the TradingView quota, and a cooldown spends no budget (owner 2026-11-11: "check dulu penyebab utama hari ini reach kuota limit apa ada perubahan jumlah pull ?"). The code showed no change in the system's pulls since 2026-11-08 (the calendar of #240, about 25-45 calls a day, began the evening before), and nothing recorded who had spent the account's quota by 13:05 UTC. The gateway now counts its provider calls per caller and hour (`provider_calls` in its `/health`, `gatewayStatus().calls_today`, named in the `market_data_stale` alert). The pull adapter no longer reserves its daily budget while the gateway's cooldown holds, and gives back a reservation the gateway refused without calling TradingView (the budget read exhausted by evening).
- feat(macro): the Macro War Room meets after every 1-hour close (owner 2026-11-11: "ubah nexus macro war room agar session update per 1H close"). `dueSessions` starts a session 20 minutes after the latest closed `OANDA:XAUUSD|1h` bar (`goldLastHourClose`, run id `1h:<close>`, kind `1h`) and keeps it due until the next 1-hour close; it met 20 minutes after each 4-hour close before. The event sessions (30 minutes and 2 hours after a High-impact USD event) are unchanged. `macroSessionWord` names the session in the agents' prompt, the desks' evidence and Nexus (stored `4h` sessions keep their name). About 12 calls a session, within `MACRO_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` 30. Nexus `#/correlations` says "20 minutes after every 1-hour close".
- feat(feed): the MT5 fallback (owner 2026-11-11: "ya rancang jalur cadangan dari bar MT5"; trade as usual, the broker's raw price). While the TradingView gateway is down and the market trades, the pull adapter merges the broker's closed M1 bars from the Corpus Oracle (`LocalComputationIngress.corpusBars`) into `OANDA:XAUUSD|1m` and rebuilds 5m, 15m and 1H from them (`FEED_FALLBACK_MODE = auto`); records, the 5m context and decisions carry `feed: "mt5_fallback"`, parity skips them, the decision adds a caution, and a `market_data_fallback` alert goes to Telegram. Native pulls heal the range once TradingView answers (once per 5 minutes, so a cooldown does not spend the daily budget).
- feat(corpus): `CORPUS_ORACLE_MODE = shadow` (owner: "Nyalakan, host sudah #243"): the broker's closed M1 bars from Omega's active broker, pair and user are kept (D1 `corpus_bars`) and measured against TradingView, for the MT5 fallback. docs(rfc): `docs/RFC_MT5_FALLBACK.md`, the broker's M1 stands in for TradingView's while the gateway is down (owner choices: trade as usual, the broker's raw price).
- feat(health): a `market_data_stale` alert, with the cause, to the owner's Telegram. Production 2026-11-11 13:05 UTC: the TradingView provider quota ran out (gateway cooldown until 00:00 UTC), so prices, the rooms, News Daily and parity stood still for hours; decisions held WAIT on stale rooms and no alert said why. The news-ingestion Worker gains the read-only RPC `NewsIngestionService.gatewayStatus()` (quota cooldown, OAuth state, last news pull; never a token); the AI Worker's health check opens `market_data_stale` when the latest XAUUSD 5m bar is over 30 minutes old while the market trades, names the gateway's cooldown, and sends its opening and resolution to Telegram.
- feat(decision-engine): the confirmation candle is the entry trigger, not a guardrail (owner 2026-11-11: "jadi pemicu entry"). In 87 decisions since 2026-11-10 14:45 UTC the 5m Prophet confirmation never printed at a 15m close (the 5m context arrived verified 84 times) and the 15m one never either; the gate passed only through the rooms and held back 82 longs. The decision now records `entry_trigger` per side and decides without it; `GET /api/ea/signal` (EA and Omega) sends a market entry only after a verified 5m or a 15m Prophet confirmation on its side (recorded, or read live from the 5m context), with flat `awaiting_*` fields until then; a limit at the key level needs no candle. Doctrine facts, the council prompt, the witness-weight replay and tests follow.
- feat(tuning): every auto-tuning step leaves a row in D1 `auto_tune_runs` (owner 2026-11-11: "ya buat catatan hasil auto-tune di D1"). The first witness-weighting run (11:10 UTC) sent nothing to Aether and left no trace a person or an agent could read; the deployed bundle showed a deploy outside GitHub Actions at 10:49 and 10:50 UTC had put back the build before #252 (main redeployed through ops-dispatch at 11:55). Each run of the room weighting per timeframe and of the witness weights now records the deployed version, the replay's counts, the applied and pushed status and the error; `GET /api/tuning/runs`; 30 days. Decision records carry `worker_version`.
- feat(omega): support MT5 mobile manual order observation and per-position manual exit override (owner 2026-11-11: "check kenapa omega tidak bisa membaca order atau activity yang saya lakukan di mt5 mobile phone", and "pada panel trade tambahkan tombol exit di masing-masing open posistion supaya user bisa interupsi TP atau SL"). (1) OmegaExecutor EA (`ea/mt5/OmegaExecutor.mq5`) scans all positions and orders via `PositionsTotal()` and `OrdersTotal()`, tags source (`magic == 920200 ? "omega" : (magic == 0 ? "manual" : "external")`), writes them to `state.json`, and allows manual intent closure (`StringFind(id, "manual") >= 0` or kill switch) while protecting manual positions from automated bot exits. (2) Engine safety boundary (`src/omega-engine.mjs` `isOmegaOwned`): automated mean-reversal exits, opposite-side closes, and position management (break-even, trailing stop, TP1 scale-out) strictly operate on Omega-owned trades only (`magic 920200` or `source: "omega"`), never altering discretionary manual/mobile trades. (3) Broker engine & RPC (`src/omega-broker.mjs`, `src/index.ts`, `workers/xauusd-omega`): exposes `orders`, adds `closePosition` RPC and `POST /omega/api/position/close` with Cloudflare Access authentication, creating `manual:close:<ticket>:<timestamp>` pending intents. (4) UI (`Omega.tsx`, `Performance.tsx`): renders `Source` column badges (`[📱 MANUAL / MOBILE]`, `[🤖 OMEGA AI]`, `[EXTERNAL]`), lists pending orders table, and adds an `EXIT ✕` button on each open position row with confirmation modal and feedback toast. Deploy verified to production on `omega.cryptophys.work` and `nexus.cryptophys.work`.
- feat(nexus): the Aether page shows the Decision Engine's witness weights (owner 2026-11-11: "apa ada visualisasi ini di aether ?"): the set in force against B-11 per witness, where it came from, the replay fidelity, the candidates with discovery, Holm and holdout, the reverse shadow and the history, from the report Aether keeps (timeframe `engine`).
- feat(decision-engine): the witness weights are auto-tuned (owner 2026-11-11: "harusnya bobot itu dinamis sesuai dengan auto tune yang ada di aether"). `src/witness-tuning.mjs` replays the stored decisions under the sets next to the active one (each witness but the session times 1.5 or divided by 1.5, bounds 0.02-0.28, sum 1), with the rooms' statistics (paired sign test, Holm, 200 discovery and 100 holdout decisions), activates the better set automatically (at most once per 7 days), rolls it back by reverse shadow after 100 decisions, tells the owner on Telegram and reports to Aether (timeframe `engine`). The engine uses the activated set (`witness_weights_source` `auto_tuned`, else `default` B-11); the doctrine facts report it; `GET /api/weighting/engine`; `WITNESS_TUNING_MODE = "default"` freezes B-11. Replaces the hand-made `deriveWitnessWeights` path in `assess()`, whose `aether_adaptive` label came from no Aether data and whose weights never left B-11 (the regime fields it read do not exist).
- feat(telegram): `OwnerNotifier`, the Cerebrum kernel's path to the owner's Telegram (owner 2026-11-11: "buat notif telegram jika ada yang perlu approve owner di semua cerebrum"). A new RPC entrypoint on the AI Worker: `notify({ change, title, lines, link })` sends a plain-text notice with this Worker's Telegram bot (`src/owner-notify.mjs`: control characters stripped, 10 lines of 300 characters, links only to `https://nexus.cryptophys.work/console/`, at most 12 an hour). The kernel binds it as `OWNER_TELEGRAM` (cryptophys-cloud-platform) and calls it when an approval opens, changes or is resolved.
- fix(omega-ui): translate remaining Indonesian broker authorization warning banner to English (owner 2026-11-11: "all in english sebagai standar bahasa platform").
- feat(decision-engine): Aether dynamic witness weighting engine (owner 2026-11-11: "pilihan 2", dynamic weighting based on aether). `src/witness-weighting.mjs`: derives dynamic witness weights based on market regime (trending boosts D1, H4, and Trend TF; mean-reverting/chop boosts Key Levels and M15 structure; high volatility/expansion boosts News Daily and Macro War Room) and Nexus analytics friction (high spread/slippage discounts breakout strategy and prioritizes Key Levels pullback limits). Bounded strictly within [0.02, 0.28] and re-normalised to 1.000. Wired into `assess()` and `buildDecisionRecord()` in `src/decision-engine.mjs` when `GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED` is enabled, falling back to baseline priors or KV cache `aether:witness_weights:effective`.
- feat(decision-engine): a Telegram heartbeat and a council that recovers from a cut-off answer (owner 2026-11-11: "check kenapa telegram bot lebih dari 1 jam tidak mengirim pesan", then "kerjakan keduanya"). The bot was healthy: the 15m stance had stayed WAIT since 01:30 UTC and Telegram is sent only on a change. (1) Heartbeat: after `DECISION_TELEGRAM_HEARTBEAT_MINUTES` (60) without a Decision Engine message, one "still WAIT/BUY/SELL" message gives the bar since which the stance holds and this bar's reading; then hourly while nothing changes. A state written before this change starts the hour at deploy. (2) Council: `COUNCIL_MAX_TOKENS` 6144 (4096 before; 2 of 14 answers were cut off on 2026-11-11 while an answer uses about 1,000 tokens); a cut-off answer is asked once more, briefly, with twice the budget; an answer that stays invalid is asked once from the moderator role's fallback model, within the 90 s deadline, before the deterministic fallback decides.
- feat(rooms): four HUD agents dismantled (owner 2026-11-11: "hardening juga untuk dismantle dan delete agent yang sudah tidak berkontribusi", "dismantle sekarang"). R37 Funnel, GABFX Price Action, Playbook Execution and Grid Bot Protocol leave every room: 15m War Room 2026-11-08..11, NEUTRAL in 87-97 % of about 190 openings each, never a BUY or SELL. Manifests: `dismantled` and `rooms: []`; the War Room, the 1H/4H/D1 rooms and the HUD threads take their HUD rosters from `hudAgentsIn(room)`. The HUD sections are still computed. The quorum no longer counts their neutral weight, so a BUY or SELL needs the matching majority of the agents who still speak. Saves about 380 gemma openings a day (about 4,000-5,000 neurons), and four fewer entries in every Consensus Discussion and conclusion prompt; the platform's `hud-*` identities of the three that reported are dropped from `HUD_PLATFORM_IDS` (platform migration 0047 deletes them).
- feat(ai-usage): neurons per day measured, the room memo in shadow, and the tf rooms' missing metrics (owner 2026-11-11: "kurangi neuron/hari tanpa mengurangi kapabilitas agent", Tahap 0). (1) Fix: the strict JSON schemas of the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms had no `metrics`, which the room validators require since 2026-11-09, so the model dropped the list every time: each answer cost a correction call with the whole prompt again and was then kept with inferred metrics (production 2026-11-10: 438 corrections for 874 tf answers; 73 for 31 conclusions on gpt-oss-120b, and 18 of those conclusions fell to the fallback). The schemas now carry `metrics` (`TF_ROOM_SCHEMAS`). (2) `src/ai-usage.mjs`: every Workers AI call records input and output tokens, failures and timeouts per day, source and model in D1 `ai_usage_daily`; `GET /api/ai-usage` shows the estimated neurons. (3) `src/room-memo.mjs`: a room step whose whole request equals a stored answer is counted (shadow, `ROOM_MEMO_ENABLED = "false"`), or reused with the flag on; reused steps do not count against the rooms' hourly call caps. Migration 0018.
- perf(kv): the pull adapter writes its refresh summary only when something happened (owner 2026-11-11, resource management). The cron runs every minute and wrote `market-data:v2:last-refresh` 1,440 times a day; an idle `up_to_date` minute now writes nothing, a pull, an update, a derived bar or a new status is written, and an unchanged summary is refreshed every 30 minutes (`writeRefreshStatus`, `STATUS_REWRITE_MS`).
- chore(models): no retired Workers AI model can come back (owner 2026-11-11: "ganti juga semua model yang berpotensi dihentikan"; Reasoner stays on gpt-oss-120b, "karena punya kelas yang sama"). Every configured and default model was already off the 2026-11-20 retirement list (gpt-oss-120b, gpt-oss-20b, gemma-4-26b-a4b-it, glm-4.7-flash, bge-small-en-v1.5). `RETIRED_MODELS` in `src/model-text.mjs` lists the 24 models of the changelog of 2026-11-05; the glm-5 and nemotron request forms are removed; a test fails if a default or either wrangler config names a retired model. No runtime change.
- fix(omega): audit findings, owner "ya perbaiki". (1) Position management (`manageIntents`) runs on every sync, whatever the entry gates say: a blackout, a pause, a wide spread, the rollover quarantine, a full book or a WAIT signal stopped break-even, the extended target and the scale-out of the positions already open. (2) The adaptive-volatility stop moves the target in proportion, so the executed trade keeps the plan's reward-to-risk that the Decision Engine checked (a stop widened to 0.75 ATR had kept the plan's target). (3) Break-even reads the executor's `open_price` (it read `entry`, which the executor never sends, so it never fired) and waits for `trailing_stop_r` R instead of any profit. (4) The TP1 scale-out banks half once per position (the remaining half was halved again at the next sync). (5) `max_positions` counts only the positions being closed in the same plan.
- feat(corpus-oracle): phase 1, the broker corpus (owner 2026-11-11: "persiapkan engine baru bernama corpus oracle yang akan melakukan feeding data ke local lane dari live mt5 terminal sesuai dengan broker, pair dan user active dari omega"; docs/RFC_CORPUS_ORACLE.md). The Omega executor writes the broker's closed M1 bars of its symbol (`omega/corpus.json`, UTC, no login); the bridge sends those after the server's cursor with each sync; the AI Worker keeps a bar only from Omega's active broker (the stored connection's server), pair (the executor's symbol) and user (the stored connection, version recorded) in D1 `corpus_bars` (14 days) and answers `corpus_cursor_ms`; `GET /api/corpus/status` measures freshness, missing minutes and the basis against the TradingView 15m series. `CORPUS_ORACLE_MODE = "off"` by default; phase 1 feeds no room, decision or plan; phase 2 computes the engines on broker bars as shadow identities that earn parity.
- feat(doctrine): the Knowledge Council's Nexus and Omega seats, and an audit fix (owner 2026-11-11: "beberapa doctrine sudah obsolete dengan state dan logic terbaru … audit dan lakukan update doctrin, sekaligus buatkan protocol doctrine update yang dilakukan autonomous oleh agent"; then "autonomous doctrine protocol harus dilakukan oleh knowledge council, dilakukan harian dan di propose ke cerebrum untuk eksekusinya"). (1) `src/doctrine-facts.mjs`: every hour the AI Worker reports to Aether (`ingestDoctrineFacts`) the facts it runs with, for the Nexus seat (Decision Engine switch, cadence, witness weights, guardrails, trading mode and thresholds, room caps, weighting cadence, EA blackout, parity, lane) and the Omega seat (the settings in force and the fixed gates), tagged with the deployed version (new `version_metadata` binding). Aether's council (cryptophys-aether, doctrine v2) proposes lasting differences to the Cerebrum consensus, which executes them (cryptophys-cloud-platform, migration 0045). (2) Audit fix: `tradeFor` honours the trading mode's reward threshold (`minRrr`; `MODE_THRESHOLDS`, `modeThresholds`): in scalp mode a 1.2R plan passed its guardrail and was then turned into WAIT by a fixed 1.5R. (3) SSOT reconciled with the code: the session witness weighs 0.02, the quorum guardrail also passes on the mode's lean, the trading modes' thresholds, and Omega's rollover quarantine, adaptive-volatility stop, limit pullback, early exit, break-even, extended target and TP1 scale-out.

## 2026-11-10
- fix(calendar): TradingView's titles (production 2026-11-10, the first back-fill: 13 releases since 28 Oct, all with an actual). "Non Farm Payrolls" (with a space) was read as an "other" event: the jobs rule now matches it, and stored "other" rows are re-classified at the next sweep. ForexFactory and TradingView name the same release differently ("Core CPI m/m" and "Core Inflation Rate MoM"), and the headline and core prints share a minute: `matchRelease` now prefers the same "core" word and the same period (m/m, q/q, y/y).
- feat(calendar): the Calendar Agent reads the released value, measures the surprise and learns each event kind's horizon; the Macro War Room and the Decision Engine follow it (owner 2026-11-10: "apakah economic calendar sudah punya agent yang bisa memprediksi horizon dan update analytic setelah news release beserta data releasenya?" and "apakah macro war rooms sudah ada wiring dengan macro drivers yang menjadi inputan global decision?"). (1) Releases: the news-ingestion Worker's `TvGateway` and `NewsIngestionService` gain `economicCalendar` (TradingView's `mcp-tv-get-economic-calendar`, the same OAuth grant and quota cooldown; actual, forecast, previous) and `goldHistory` (gold 1H, one pull a day). `src/econ-releases.mjs` stores high-importance USD releases in D1 `econ_releases`: the day of a High-impact USD event released 2 minutes to 3 hours ago when its actual is missing (at most every 10 minutes), and a back-fill of about 400 days, one 14-day window an hour. (2) Surprise: actual - forecast in the series' own standard deviations (from 6 past surprises; a tenth of the forecast or the forecast-previous gap before), within +-3, its effect on gold by the event kind's fixed rule. (3) Horizon: gold's move 1, 2, 4, 8 and 24 hours after each release in 1H ATR, signed by the rule; per event kind the mean signed move and hit rate at each horizon, and the learned horizon (the longest with a hit rate of at least 55 % and a positive mean; the fixed 4 hours below 5 releases). (4) Wiring: `projectEvent` and `calendarOutlook` carry the release, its surprise, the learned horizon and the history text, and `surprise_lean`; after a release with its value the Calendar Agent's bias follows the surprise by rule (`calendarBiasRule`); the Macro War Room's evidence carries the release values and USDT dominance (Dollar Analyst), and it meets again 2 hours after a High-impact USD event (`event2:`); the Decision Engine's `context.market.calendar` and a caution when a release surprises against the trade inside its horizon; `/api/calendar` and the Nexus Calendar page show actual, surprise and the learned horizons; the Decision Engine card shows the release line.
- chore(market-data): Workers Logs on for `xauusd-tradingview-pull-adapter` (`market-data-ingestion/wrangler.jsonc` `observability`, persisted logs and invocation logs, no traces). The 5m local records stopped publishing 2026-11-10 13:10-14:10 UTC and the Worker kept no logs to show why; the next incident can be read from its `local_computation_publish_m5`, `m5_context_submit_failed` and run errors.
- fix(war-room): the sweep runs a missed 15m room (`runMissedWarRoom`). Production 2026-11-10 13:10-14:10 UTC: the 5m bars kept arriving in the cache (the `war_room_stale` alert saw the 13:40 bar) while the 5m local records and their context were not published, so the queue never got a 15m close and the rooms of 13:15 to 14:00 never met; the Decision Engine decided those bars about 10 minutes late without them. Now the sweep (:10, :25, :40, :55), at least 4 minutes after a 15m close with the market open, runs the room of that bar when it never started, building its 15m candles from the cached closed 5m series (`m5ContextFromSeries`; no bias and no Prophet 5m confirmation is claimed from bare bars), and the Decision Engine decides after it. A room that already started is left to its queue task; without cached bars nothing meets (`no_m5_bars`). The root cause of the unpublished 5m records needs the market-data Worker's logs.
- fix(war-room): `WAR_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` 136 → 170. The cap counts a rolling 60 minutes; production 2026-11-10 15:15: the room of 14:15 had started 4 minutes late, so the window held four rooms (113 calls) and the 15m conclusion ran out of budget (`budget_exhausted`), which left the Decision Engine without the 15m room's decision (WAIT for lack of a confirmation). Five rooms of about 32 calls fit now; a room still makes the same calls.
- fix(decision-engine): KEY_LEVELS no longer reads a squeeze between two zones as a full lean. Production 2026-11-10 15:00: price sat inside a zone 0.09 ATR under resistance, and 0.6 x (distance to resistance - distance to support) / (sum) gave +0.6 bullish. The gap now counts in at least one 1H ATR, so a squeeze is near neutral (+0.05 there); distances wider than an ATR are unchanged.
- feat(decision-engine): the extended witnesses are independent (owner 2026-11-10: "ya buat witness baru benar-benar independen"). Before, KEY_LEVELS, REGIME and TREND_TF were derived from the strategy engine's direction, alignment and trend (the D1/4H room leans), and MACRO_DRIVERS from the macro room, so they repeated witnesses already counted (the strategy's share rose from 0.10 to about 0.23). Now each reads its own data (`independentWitnesses`): KEY_LEVELS, price against the Key Level Agent's zones (near support bullish, near resistance bearish, at most 0.6); REGIME, the direction of the last 20 closed 1H bars times their efficiency, scaled by the Hurst sensor (1 persistent, 0.6 between, 0.3 mean-reverting; 0 in chaos); TREND_TF, price against its 20-bar mean in 2 ATR on D1, 4H and 1H (0.4/0.35/0.25); MACRO_DRIVERS, the dollar's 24-hour change and the US 10-year yield's daily change (a stronger dollar or higher yields weigh on gold). A witness without its own data drops out. A test flips the strategy's direction and checks that none of them changes.
- feat(decision-engine): `GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED` is on in production (owner 2026-11-10: "nyalakan GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED"). The B-11 extended witnesses (key levels 0.05, regime 0.05, cross-timeframe trend 0.05, macro drivers 0.03; session as context) count in the quorum. While the flag is on every witness takes its B-11 weight (D1/4H/1H 0.16, 15m 0.12, News 0.08, Macro 0.04, strategy 0.08; sum 1, `activeWitnessWeights`), so the extended witnesses hold their designed 20 % instead of being added on top of the full base set; the record carries `weighted` and the weights in force. `"false"` restores the seven base witnesses.

## 2026-11-09
- feat(decision-engine): the right panel's market sentiment, regime, macro drivers and session are part of the decision (owner 2026-11-09: "masukan sentiment, regime, macro drivers dan juga session (perlu ditambah hari tanggal dan market open/closed) menjadi pertimbangan global decision juga"). `src/decision-engine.mjs`: `marketContextOf` builds `context.market` for the council and the record: the sentiment (the witnesses' weighted lean, the news and macro composite, the 24-hour headline counts), the regime of the 15m and 1H charts (the Nexus radar's sensors: trending or mean-reverting, chaos, the stretch z-score, and the bar regime label), the macro drivers (24-hour change of gold, the dollar, oil and USDT dominance, the gold-dollar link, cross-asset correlations) and the session (`sessionAt`: day, date, UTC time, market open or closed, the active sessions and their liquidity, the next weekly close or reopen). New guardrail `market_open` (no entry while the market is closed); new cautions (thin liquidity between sessions, the weekend close within 2 hours, a 1H stretch of 2.5 standard deviations in the trade's direction, a chaotic 15m regime, the dollar moving 0.3 % against the trade). With `GLOBAL_DECISION_WEIGHTED` the MACRO_DRIVERS witness falls back to the dollar's 24-hour pull when the macro room carries no lean, and SESSION carries its text (still unweighted: a session has no direction). Nexus: the Decision Engine card shows the market context the council read; the session clock shows the day, the date, the UTC time, a market open or closed badge and the time to the next close or open.

Includes an interactive MT5 window header, Algo Trading status bar, timeframe selector (`M5`/`M15`/`H1`/`H4`/`D1`), Market Watch (`Ctrl+M`) with live broker Bid/Ask boxes, spread, and symbol contract specification, Navigator (`Ctrl+N`) tree, interactive `lightweight-charts` candlestick + volume canvas with live Bid/Ask, 15m Decision Engine Entry/SL/TP, and open MT5 position price lines, and a 5-tab MT5 Toolbox (`Ctrl+T`: `Trade` with the classic MT5 account summary bar, `History`, `Order Intents`, `Experts`, and `Journal & Bridge`). Extended `brokerOverview().bridge` in `src/omega-broker.mjs` with non-sensitive symbol and account fields (`bid`, `ask`, `digits`, `point`, `tick_size`, `tick_value`, `volume_min`, `volume_max`, `volume_step`, `stops_level_points`, `day_start_balance`, `day_realized_pl`).

Added `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui/src/pages/Omega.tsx` (Signal, Control, Expert Advisor, Broker connection with browser WebCrypto RSA-OAEP + AES-GCM encryption, Broker engine settings, Bridge & terminal, Intents, Open positions, Closed deals, and Audit), wired `omega: Omega` in `App.tsx` and `/#/omega` in `AppShell.tsx` with in-place SPA navigation, and updated `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/src/index.ts` to serve the Nexus SPA shell for `/omega` and `/omega/` while forwarding `/omega/api/*` and `/omega/health` unchanged to `xauusd-omega` (`OMEGA_PAGE`).
- feat(omega)!: the Omega broker engine (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "Omega memegang akses broker"; "halaman omega masih di nexus.cryptophys.work/omega; di halaman ini bisa input server, user, dan password untuk terhubung broker yang dipilih"; "Bridge di host ini"; "Mati sampai Anda aktifkan"). `src/omega-engine.mjs` plans and sizes the trades (the EA's gates, risk % of equity at the stop, one open per signal, kill closes all), `src/omega-broker.mjs` keeps the settings, the connection, the bridge state, the intents and the audit in D1 and runs the plan on each bridge sync (`OmegaGateway`: `setSettings`, `setConnection`, `clearConnection`, `bridgeKey`, `bridgeSync`). The page encrypts the broker credentials in the browser to the bridge's key, so no Worker can read them. Nexus forwards `/omega/*` to `xauusd-omega` (`OMEGA_PAGE` binding) and links it. The bridge (`bridge/omega-bridge`, `ea/mt5/OmegaExecutor.mq5`, `docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md`, `docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE_PROTOCOL.md`) runs on the owner's MT5 host and only makes outbound requests. Off by default: `OMEGA_ENGINE_ENABLED = "false"`, the page toggle, and `LIVE` for a real account. AGENTS.md rule 8 updated.
- fix(5m-trigger): the 5m trigger read "no data" and never counted (owner 2026-11-09: "kenapa 5m sebagai trigger selalu no data?"). Two causes: the Nexus row of the 5m layer showed a lean, and the trigger has none (it is a confirmation), so it always read "no data"; and the 5m Prophet confirmation counted as verified only while BOTH the Prophet and the GMI 5m implementations were parity-approved, and `gmi_m5` was revoked since 11:15 UTC (a verdict mismatch), so the strategy kept "waiting for a verified 5m confirmation" while `prophet_m5` stood approved with 112 matching bars. Now the trigger needs the Prophet 5m parity only (`verifiedM5Confirmation`; the combined 5m reading still needs both), the Decision Engine record carries `context.m5_trigger`, and the Nexus card shows the 5m row as the trigger's state (buy or sell confirmed, unverified, no confirmation, no fresh 5m bar). Also: the council read the weighted lean (0.42) as the quorum at 13:15; the evidence now names `bullish_share` / `bearish_share` against `share_needed_above` and says the lean is only its strength.
- feat(decision-engine): ONE Decision Engine as the single SSOT, replacing the H1 decision / Global decision dualism (owner 2026-11-09: "kesalahan fatal di Global Decision tidak ada Model Ai … hanya ada 1 decision engine sebagai ssot"; choices: AI within guardrails, switch now, every 15m close). `src/decision-engine.mjs`: the witnesses (the D1, 4H, 1H and 15m rooms, News Daily, the Macro room, the strategy engine; weights D1/4H/1H 0.20, 15m 0.15, News 0.10, Macro 0.05, strategy 0.10) and the context (event gate, regime, correlations, previous decision) go to one AI council call (moderator model), which chooses stance, entry type, target, confidence and reasons inside deterministic guardrails (fresh 15m and 1H rooms, a weighted quorum above 50 %, a room or verified Prophet confirmation, no strategy veto, a key-level plan, 1.5R, confidence 0.40; market entry only at the level); a broken guardrail turns it into WAIT (`overruled`), and a failed model call falls back to the strategy engine's decision when it passes. Runs right after each 15m War Room (and the sweep for a missed bar), once per bar (D1 `engine_decisions`, KV `decision-engine:latest`). The EA signal, Telegram, the Signal Ledger (source `decision`) and the Nexus card ("Decision Engine", witnesses with weights, guardrails, the plan) all follow it; while it is on the H1 Telegram message, the `h1_decision`/`strategy` ledger rows and the 5m strategy evaluation stop. New `GET /api/engine/decision`, `/api/engine/decisions`; `/api/conclusion/global` carries `engine`. Kill switch `DECISION_ENGINE = "off"`. EA 1.11: `MaxSignalAgeMinutes` default 10 (the signal expires at the next 15m close). `riskChecks` and `triggerOf` of the strategy engine are exported for the guardrails.
- perf(h1): the H1 decision within 1 to 2 minutes of the close (owner 2026-11-09: "45 menit … harusnya max < 1-2m"). Measured before: the room started about 6 minutes after the close and the decision came at about 6 to 7 minutes in normal hours, because the market-data cron ran every 5 minutes and an H1 bar counted as closed only 90 seconds after its close; the HUD snapshot was published after the H1 record, so the room often lost a 60-second retry. Now: the market-data cron runs every minute (the gold 1m chart keeps `cadence: 300`, so the TradingView quota does not change); `SETTLE_SECONDS` is 60 (a later revision is still republished for parity, `republishRevisedH1`); the HUD snapshot is published before the H1 record; the h1_room task retries every 15 seconds and waits at most 2 minutes for the HUD (`H1_ROOM_HUD_WAIT_MS`); the queue batch timeout is 1 second. The 45 to 55 minutes of 10:00-12:00 UTC were the room failures fixed in #210.
- fix(rooms): the independent rooms lost most answers after #202 and stopped concluding (production 2026-11-09, 10:00-12:00 UTC: 27 of 56 1H and 13 of 28 4H messages `invalid_output`, the 1H and 4H conclusions `budget_exhausted`, the H1 decision falling back to WAIT about 55 minutes after the close, and fewer cards for Aether). Causes: models left out `metrics` or wrote it inside the claim (`","metrics":[...]}` plus tokens such as `<|file_separator|>`), and agents named after their indicator (Supertrend, Prophet, Order Blocks) made a cited metric count as naming the agent. Fix (`src/war-room.mjs`, `src/agent-threads.mjs`): leaked metrics are recovered and stray tokens stripped before any check; a role name never appears, while an indicator's name counts only as an actor ("says", "'s view", "according to"); an answer whose only remaining error after its correction is a missing metrics list is kept with its own data source's metrics, marked `metrics_inferred`. Tests use the stored production answers.
- feat(aether-sync): end-to-end sync hardening between the AI Worker, Aether and Nexus (owner 2026-11-09). Aether's new `AetherReader.sync()` (cryptophys-adm/cryptophys-aether#9) checks card ingest, the canon index, the embedding backlog, each timeframe's weighting report and the AI budget (ok, warn, or idle while the market was closed); the AI Worker reads it every 15 minutes through a second read-only binding `AETHER_READER` and adds Aether's reachability, the KB v4 hash match between the repositories and its own card push (`src/aether-sync.mjs`). Each warning is a health alert (`aether_sync:<check>`); `/api/aether/status` and the Nexus Aether page show the combined status; the Nexus deploy smoke test now reads `/api/aether/overview` and refuses a price field. The topology test allows exactly the two Aether bindings of the AI Worker (KnowledgeIngress, AetherReader) and the read-only one of Nexus.
- docs: the Aether RFC (§0.7) records the Aether page and `AetherReader`, the end of Aether's public URL and its canon rule `advisory_only` aligned with the EA decision (cryptophys-adm/cryptophys-aether#7); backlog item 12 points to the Aether page for the weighting.
- feat(nexus): an Aether page at `nexus.cryptophys.work/aether` (owner 2026-11-09), behind the same Cloudflare Access sign-in as Nexus. Read-only, from the new `GET /api/aether/overview` (Service Binding `AETHER` to Aether's new RPC entrypoint `AetherReader`, cryptophys-adm/cryptophys-aether#6). It shows the canon and doctrine, the decision cards and their ingest, the AI budget and indexes, each timeframe's automatic room weighting (active point, regimes, agent weights, candidates, reverse shadow, history), the guardian's compliance findings of the last 24 hours and the knowledge proposals. The menu links use `/#/…` so they work from any path, and the app reads its page from the path when there is no hash.
- feat(omega): Omega Execution Engine, a page separate from Nexus and Aether (owner decisions 2026-11-09: monitor + control, its own subdomain, per-position data from the EA). New Worker `xauusd-omega` at `omega.cryptophys.work` (static page, no build; `deploy-omega.yml`, `validate-omega.yml`) reads and writes only through the Service Binding to the AI Worker's new RPC entrypoint `OmegaGateway`. It verifies the Cloudflare Access JWT itself on every request (fail closed; audience checked when the deploy can resolve it), and a control change needs a signed-in person, a same-origin JSON request, a reason, and `KILL` typed to kill. The EA control (`src/ea-execution.mjs`, KV `ea:control`) is active, paused (the signal's new `paused`: no new entry, pending orders deleted) or killed (`kill_source` omega); every change is audited in D1 `ea_control_events` with the Access email. The heartbeat now keeps each open position and the floating and today's P&L (16 KiB), and the new `POST /api/ea/deal` stores each closed position once in D1 `ea_deals`; Omega shows them with today's, 7-day and 30-day statistics. EA 1.10 (`ea/mt5/CryptophysXAUUSD.mq5`): honours the pause, reports its positions and closed deals (queued and retried), records why it closed a position (opposite, kill). The hostname joins the platform's Access application in `cryptophys-cloud-platform` (ADR-0010 amendment). The server still never places an order, never sizes and holds no broker credential; unrelated to the platform's prohibited legacy `omega` swarm.
- feat(parity): the mismatch diagnostics also keep the JMA and kinetics inputs (`pct_rank`, `s_theta`, `s_omega`, `s_alpha`, `global_h`), the close's position against the value area (`value_area`: above_vah, inside, below_val) and the zone flags; never a price. Why: the first 5m mismatch with diagnostics (bar 08:35 UTC on 2026-11-09) showed the local branch `jma_long` (+3) where Pine's verdict before the conflict reset was -3 (`gmi_dir` -1 against `prophet_dir` +1); the branch alone cannot tell whether the JMA trigger or an earlier zone or Fabio branch differs.
- feat(ea): MetaTrader 5 Expert Advisor, live-ready (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "Langsung siap live", "EA MT5 di terminal Anda"). `GET /api/ea/signal` (`src/ea-signal.mjs`) serves the latest H1 decision as one flat object (side, action, entry, stop, first target, issue and expiry times, the feed price, the event-gate blackout and the `EA_SIGNALS_ENABLED` kill switch); `POST /api/ea/heartbeat` and `GET /api/ea/status` monitor the EA. `ea/mt5/CryptophysXAUUSD.mq5` executes it on the owner's terminal: risk-percent sizing from the stop distance, the plan's distances on the broker's price, limit plans as expiring pending orders, a chase guard, spread, age, blackout, daily-loss and position limits, opposite-signal close, kill switch, and live trading only with `AllowLiveAccount`. AGENTS.md rule 8 now says the server never trades, holds no broker credential and gives no size; the owner's EA may execute. Install guide `docs/EA_MT5.md` (Access service token `cryptophys-agent-mt5-ea`). The MQL5 source was not compiled here (no MetaEditor in CI): compile it in MetaEditor and run it on a demo account first.
- feat(weighting)!: auto-tuning per timeframe and regime, without an operator (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "Otomatis + pengaman"; "Per regime + shrinkage … berlaku masing-masing timeframe independent"). Equation `cryptophys.weighting.v3` adds a regime component (each agent's accuracy in the bar's volatility and trend regime, shrunk toward its own record, `regime_prior`) and a high-volatility quorum bonus; `src/market-regime.mjs` labels each bar from its own timeframe's bars (ATR share tercile x 20-bar efficiency ratio). Every room (15m, 1H, 4H, D1) uses its own timeframe's active point in its own bar's regime; the 4H and daily rooms are now scored (`SCORED_TIMEFRAMES` 240 and 1D; a weekend is no gap on the daily chart). The replay runs per timeframe (`readWeightingReplay({ timeframe })`, 16 candidates); the best point rated better on discovery and holdout is activated automatically (`autoDecision`, 7-day cooldown per timeframe, never a rolled-back point; D1 `weighting_states`), the reverse shadow rolls back by itself, and each change is a Telegram message. Aether receives each timeframe's report as an audit (`mode: "auto"`); approval in the Console is no longer used. `GET /api/weighting?timeframe=`. `WEIGHTING_MODE = "default"` stays the kill switch.
- feat(rooms)!: independent rooms (owner decisions 2026-11-09: each timeframe and each agent independent, never referring to another agent or timeframe; outputs name the metrics they used, never an agent; the Consensus Discussion stays inside each timeframe). The Higher-Timeframe Desk is retired from the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms and the 15m room; the 15m room no longer reads the H1 decision, the 1H room or the 5m lane and is judged by its own quorum; the News Analyst no longer reads the Macro War Room; the Risk Manager reads only its chart. The Consensus Discussion runs over an anonymous bus (`#n` references, `busRefs`, `anonymousBus`); every opening, rebuttal and conclusion carries `metrics`, and a text naming another agent (`names_an_agent`) or without metrics (`metrics_missing`) gets one correction. The rooms use `ROOM_RULES_INDEPENDENT` (no "H1 decision chain is authoritative", no 5m lane); the shared natural-language examples no longer cite the H1 decision or the 5m bias. The Reasoner reads the anonymous consensus without the 5m context (`ownTimeframeEvidence`) and the H1 plan comes from the 1-hour chart's own key levels (`ownTimeframePlan`). Tests updated for the new rooms; new tests for the validators, the anonymous bus, the 1H-only plan and the Reasoner's evidence.
- feat(weighting): Aether tunes the weighting of the H1 quorum, activated by approval in the Console (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "Approve di Console", "Parameter + komponen"). `src/weighting.mjs` is the equation `cryptophys.weighting.v2`: each 1H agent's own weight from its scored calls, as the shrunk accuracy times a confidence-calibration term (Brier, `calibration_beta`), with an optional half-life, clamped to a weight range, and the quorum share; the default point is exactly the P7 weight with quorum 0.5, so nothing changes until a point is approved. The 1H room builds its crowd line with the active point and records it (`crowd.weighting`, `crowd.quorum_share`), so the moderator's validation and `quorumOf` use the same quorum; the other rooms keep P7. `src/weighting-replay.mjs` replays the H1 quorum over 30 days of concluded 1H rooms, point in time, for the active point and the 12 grid points one parameter away, with the sign test, Holm and the 100-room holdout, and the reverse shadow after an activation. The sweep runs it hourly, activates an approved `param_candidate` of family `weighting` from the learned pack when it is a grid point the replay still rates `better_than_active` (7-day cooldown, D1 `weighting_state` with history), rolls back automatically when the previous point wins the reverse shadow after 100 rooms (p < 0.05), and sends the report to Aether (`pushWeightingReport`, `KnowledgeIngress.ingestWeightingReport`; parameters and statistics only). `WEIGHTING_MODE = "default"` is the kill switch. New `GET /api/weighting`. The calibrator rule lives in `cryptophys-aether`.
- feat(parity): a mismatch keeps the local engine's diagnostics in its detail (`local_diagnostics`: branch, timeframe states, chaos, entropy, intrabars; unverified internals, never compared). Why: the B4 follow-up of 2026-11-09 found the only GMI mismatches of the day (5m, `gmi_dir` local 0 against TradingView ±1, 2 before B4 and 1 after, so not a B4 regression) all on conflict bars (gate -1). Pine and the local engine both take `gmi_dir` from the verdict and then reset the verdict to 0 on a conflict, so the verdict matches while `gmi_dir` shows that the verdict branch BEFORE the reset differed. The detail now names the local branch, so the next such bar shows which branch disagrees with Pine. The H1 GMI identity (`gmi` ef7e6563a253) and `gmi_hud` matched 2 of 2 after B4.
- feat(h1)!: the H1 decision is the quorum of the 1H War Room, independent of the TradingView webhooks, of parity approval and of any single indicator signal (owner decision 2026-11-09: "H1 Decision harus independen … murni dari konsensus agent"; "H1 Decision adalah kuorum"). BUY or SELL only when the moderator concluded BUY or SELL and more than half of the weight of the agents who spoke holds the matching bias, each agent weighted by its 30-day track record (the P7 crowd line; Aether tunes the weighting later): `quorumOf` / `applyQuorum` in `src/h1-consensus.mjs`, `QUORUM_SHARE`, `quorumAllows` and `quorumRule` in `src/reputation.mjs`. The prices come from the Key Level Agent's zones through the strategy engine (`planForDirection`); a quorum without a valid entry, stop and target stays WAIT, and no position size is given. The direction and prices are set deterministically; the Reasoner writes the reasoning. Indicators are arguments, not gates or vetoes: `consensusGuard`, the Reasoner's Prophet-trigger rule and its `t_enc` 4 / GMI ±3 vetoes are retired, and the knowledge base's `decision_policy` lists `long_arguments` / `short_arguments` instead of `actionable_*_requires`. The 1H, 4H and D1 rooms conclude BUY or SELL under the same quorum (`entry_without_quorum` replaces `tfEntryAllowed`'s confirmation rule) and drop the parity confidence cap of 0.4 (owner: "confidences sesuai kapasitasnya"; the calibration stays a label). The chain reads the local records approved or not (`decisionLocalRecords`: the approved copy, else the observed record as `not_parity_approved`), so a GMI identity re-earning parity after B4 no longer fails every H1 bar closed; in quorum mode only a missing or stale closed candle degrades the bar, and a missing news analysis or engine record is noted while the room still meets (owner choice). The synthesis records `consensus.quorum` and `plan_available`. Not changed: the 15m Live War Room's "no confirmation, no entry" and the strategy engine's own rules. Tests for the quorum, the plan, the prompts, the room validator, quorum-mode fusion and the local records.
- refactor(gmi)!: B4, one GMI engine for every chart (backlog item 11; owner go for the full re-approval, 2026-11-09). `local-engines/gmi/src/gmi-core.mjs` is now the single engine, `computeGmiOnChart(bundle, { chart })` for 5m, 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 with Pine's `dyn_ltf` intrabars, and `computeGmi(bundle)` the 1H chart. It replaces three copies (the H1 core, `gmi-core-tf.mjs`, `gmi-core-htf.mjs`; about 830 duplicated lines). Before the swap the merged engine was compared with all three on 80 synthetic bundles across the five charts: every output identical, diagnostics included; `test/gmi-core-htf.test.mjs` pins golden fingerprints of the former modules' outputs. `gmi-core-tf.mjs` and `gmi-core-htf.mjs` remain as one-line re-exports, so no producer changed: the Prophet and MizanLib identities keep their approval. The six GMI identities change (`gmi` d9518534b38d → ef7e6563a253, `gmi_m5`, `gmi_m15`, `gmi_h4`, `gmi_d1`, `gmi_hud`) and re-earn 24 parity matches each; until `gmi` is approved again (about 24 trading hours) every H1 bar is a fail-closed WAIT, the 5m confirmation reads UNVERIFIED and the 15m desk not calibrated (backlog item 19). Provenance and knowledge base regenerated.
- feat(nexus): one Key levels panel (backlog item 18, owner approval 2026-11-09). The Levels card and the Key Level Agent panel of the right column become `KeyLevels.tsx`: one price axis with the Camarilla set and the daily and weekly pivot ranges of the active tab's chart on the left lane, the Key Level Agent's zones of every chart on the right lane, and the price as one dashed line across both. The axis is fitted to price, the candidate zones and H3/L3/H4/L4 (plus anything within half again of that reach), so it has no empty band; a level beyond it is listed at its edge with its price, each in its colour. The readers, the 15m setup, the set picker, the calibration note, the close's place in the Camarilla, the nearest candidates and the summary stay. Without the Global Conclusion the Camarilla side shows alone. Two drawing defects of the old Key Level Agent ladder are gone: its price line never painted (a `useId` with colons in `url(#…)`, and an objectBoundingBox gradient on a zero-height line). `LevelLadder` and `KeyLevelAgent.tsx` are removed. Checked with screenshots of the built UI on mocked API data (1-hour set, Daily set, no Global Conclusion).
- docs(backlog): item 5 narrowed. The economic calendar (#157) and the correlations (#156) are verified on live data from D1: the Macro War Room of 02:00 UTC read this week's calendar (no High-impact USD event within 24 hours, 2 within 48, Core CPI named) and live 4h context (DXY -0.14 % over 4 hours, silver +2.48 %, the weekly correlation with gold). What stays open waits for an event: the event gate firing at the first High-impact USD release of the week, and the first Telegram ledger event.
- feat(ops): `ops-dispatch.yml` (backlog item 17, modelled on the platform repository's). The session's GitHub access cannot call `workflow_dispatch` or re-run a job (403), so after a secret change the owner had to click "Run workflow". Now a push to the operator branch `claude/vibrant-mccarthy-dq5xs7` whose head commit adds exactly one `ops/requests/<UTC timestamp>-<name>.json` (`{"workflow": "deploy-worker.yml"}`) dispatches that workflow on `main` and follows it to completion. Allow-list: the four deploy workflows and `inspect-ai-worker.yml`; no inputs; never `set-webhook-secret.yml` or `decommission-legacy-workers.yml`. A branch reset onto `main` never replays a request. AGENTS.md rule 1 holds: deploys still run the deploy workflows on `main`. `test/ops-dispatch.test.mjs` keeps the allow-list honest.
- feat(rooms): the Prophet R38 setup READY state on the 15m, H4 and D1 charts (backlog item 16). `prophet-setup.mjs` gains `chartProphetSetup(bundle, chart)` with the producers' bar slices and Pine periods (`SETUP_CHARTS`: H1 "60", M15 "15", H4 "240", D1 "1D"); the 15m and H4/D1 publishers attach `values.setup` to their prophet records as the H1 publisher does (consistent with the record's events or not at all; `prophet_setup` in each publish summary). The Threads already read `values.setup` of their chart's record (`tfEntryAllowed`), so the 15m, 4H and D1 rooms may now conclude BUY or SELL while the setup is READY after its one-bar event. The desk reading (`chartDeskReading`) passes the state to the plain-language reading and returns `buy_setup_ready` / `sell_setup_ready`. No hashed engine file changed. Tests: the three charts attach, keyed like their producers; the wrong chart never attaches.
- docs(backlog): the post-reopen checks of `docs/BACKLOG.md` items 0 to 4 passed on production D1 by 04:00 UTC and leave the backlog: `strategy_inputs` holds one row per closed H1 bar from 00:41 UTC; the H1 decisions of 01:00 to 04:00 UTC ran by Consensus Discussion (`consensus_reasoner`, `concluded`, guard 0 of 4, decided 392 to 439 s after the close, 2478 s for the bar whose news retried); conclusions 24 ok on gpt-oss-120b, 1 via the gemma fallback, 2 `budget_exhausted` before #188 deployed; openings 687 ok against 8 `invalid_output` (about 1 %), no 3021 rate limits; P7 scored 351 statements of the 15m room and 22 of the 1H room. Owner items closed: the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 alerts and the GMI HUD candidate are installed (items 8 and 13), and the Telegram bot secrets are set (item 9: the `ledger_alerts` seed row of 2026-11-08 10:55 UTC). New items: 14 rotate the webhook secret before publish (owner decision), 15 the 4H and D1 calibration in progress, 16 the setup READY state on the 15m, 4H and D1 charts, 17 an ops-dispatch workflow for this repository, 18 the Nexus Levels + Key Level Agent panel merge. Item 6 is checked early, about 2026-11-12, since #192 is the first change aimed at "always WAIT".
- fix(h1): the Prophet R38 setup READY state reaches the decision (owner report 2026-11-09: the H1 HUD showed "BUY · READY" while the H1 decision stayed WAIT). `buy_confirmed` / `sell_confirmed` are one-bar events, the HUD's READY is a state that lasts while the setup is inside its confirmation window (3 closed bars on 1H), the gate passed within the setup and the cycle alignment holds; only the events travel in the alerts, so every later bar read as "no signal". New `local-engines/producer/src/prophet-setup.mjs` (outside the hashed engine files, so no parity identity changes) reproduces the state from the same R38 core outputs; `local-publisher` attaches it to the H1 prophet record as `values.setup`, consistent with the record's events or not at all. It surfaces as `prophet_r38.setup_state` in the decision evidence (`indicatorPayloadsFromLocal`), the bus (`WEBHOOK_FIELDS`), the plain reading (`interpretWebhooks`: "Its BUY setup is still READY: the confirmation passed N bars ago..."), the 1H War Room (`entryAllowed`, `NO_CONFIRMATION_RULE`, `confirmations.h1_prophet_r38.buy_setup_ready`) and the Threads (`tfEntryAllowed`, `chart_prophet_r38.buy_setup_ready`). The knowledge base says every field describes the closed candle being decided, never the running one, accepts the READY setup as the entry trigger, and says GMI gate 1 is sufficient (the Reasoner had written "gate 2 and verdict 3" into an invalidation). The Reasoner prompt carries the same rules. Tests for the state, the attach, the payload, the rooms and the reading.
- fix(pine): the GMI R11 candidate (`pinescript/candidates/Global-Multiverse-Interceptor-v12.70.hud.v1.txt`) sends each of its three CTA webhook payloads (`prophet_r37_ssot`, `gmi_v12_70`, `gmi_v12_70_hud`) once per bar close (`alert.freq_once_per_bar_close`, as Prophet R38 already does). With `alert.freq_all` inside `barstate.isconfirmed` a 5m bar delivered each GMI payload twice (8 messages per bar from 2 alerts instead of 5). D1 `webhook_sources` upserts on (candle_key, source), so stored data was never doubled; the fix saves TradingView alert quota and log noise. The owner reinstalls the script and recreates the GMI alert. Engine provenance and knowledge base regenerated.
- feat(ops): manual workflow `set-webhook-secret.yml` sets the AI Worker's `WEBHOOK_SECRET` from the GitHub Actions secret `TV_WEBHOOK_SECRET` byte for byte, prints its fingerprint (sha256, first 8, as in `webhook_rejections`), and proves the Worker accepts a body that carries it exactly as the Pine payload does (a `diagnostic_probe` source refused only after the secret check, so nothing is stored). Owner report 2026-11-09: after several dashboard edits the alerts still sent fingerprint f5209fb1 and the Worker held f5405a14.
- fix(health, h1): no false alarm or record for a closed market (post-reopen audit 2026-11-09). The stale Live War Room check counts its 45 minutes from the first 5m bar after a market gap, and names a meeting older than 12 hours with its date (2026-11-08 23:12 read "has not met since 22:00 UTC", which was Friday). The H1 backfill skips hours that open while gold is closed (Friday 17:00 to Sunday 18:00 New York, `goldClosedAt`): 48 fail-closed WAIT runs had been recorded for the weekend.
- fix(bus): a primary model call leaves time for the fallback (post-reopen audit 2026-11-09: the 15m room's conclusion on gpt-oss-120b timed out at the deadline, the fallback never ran, and the `budget_exhausted` row named the fallback with `fallback_used` = 1). `callBusModel` caps the primary's timeout at the remaining time minus `FALLBACK_RESERVE_MS` (20 s) when a fallback follows, skips the primary when less than that is left, and records `model` null and `fallback_used` false when the budget ran out before a model was asked.
- fix(rooms): a rebuttal may name an agent by any spelling of its id or name (post-reopen audit 2026-11-09: 3 of 54 debate turns lost as `rebuts_invalid` for "aegis_footprint_matrix", "macro_aether_sensors" and "the kinetic_engine"). `src/rebut-names.mjs` matches targets by slug (lower case, a leading "the" dropped, non-alphanumerics as "_") against each bus agent's id and display name; the War Rooms and the Macro War Room (which matched nothing before) use it.
- fix(p7): no score across a market gap (post-reopen audit 2026-11-09: about 109 Friday statements were scored on Sunday evening's bars, all WAIT read as missed opportunities). `scoreStance` returns `no_data` (`market_gap`) when a bar of the horizon opens more than 2 hours after the previous one closed (`crossesGap`, `MAX_BAR_GAP_MS`); the one-hour daily break is not a gap. The scoring pass removes the rows of such bars already written, so the reputation forgets them.
- fix(market-data): after a gap, a pull that brought no new bar backs off (post-reopen audit 2026-11-09: after the reopen, gold 1h/4h/1D, DXY, US10Y and XAGUSD were pulled at every 5-minute run because `planRequest` expected the next bar two bar-lengths after the cached one, which ignores the weekend; each pull returned no new bar, the D1 alone about 250 of the 800 daily calls). When the cached bar is more than three bars old and the last pull already came after the expected close, the next pull waits `gapBackoffSeconds` (a quarter of the bar, 5 minutes to 1 hour). A normal bar is pulled on schedule.
- fix(h1): a degraded H1 run is retried while its evidence can still arrive (post-reopen audit 2026-11-09: every H1 bar since the reopen ended as a degraded WAIT, `news_analysis_stale`, because the local engines publish at about :05:35 and the hourly news analysis lands at about :06:50; a degraded run was final, so the Consensus Discussion never ran). The parity sweep's backfill now retries a degraded run within 45 minutes of the close (`DEGRADED_RETRY_MS`, attempt cap 4); the claim only succeeds when the evidence fingerprint changed (fresh news), so an unchanged bar is not analysed twice.
- feat(webhook): rejection fingerprints (owner report 2026-11-09: after the secret was rotated and set in the Worker and the indicator inputs, the 5m alerts still answered 401 with two different 32-character secrets). Each `webhook_rejections` row also keeps the first 8 hex characters of the SHA-256 of the received secret and of `WEBHOOK_SECRET` (columns added to the existing table). The owner hashes the candidate values locally (`printf '%s' "<value>" | sha256sum`) to see which value the alerts send and which the Worker holds. Never the secret; the fingerprints stay in D1.
- feat(nexus): System status shows a "TradingView alerts" lane (owner report 2026-11-09: the page did not show that every alert was refused). GET /api/status carries `ai_worker.webhook` (the last accepted alert from D1 `webhook_sources` and the last hour's 401s with their reason, never the secret); the lane is red while alerts are rejected, green when one was accepted in the last 2 hours, grey while the market is closed. The `webhook_rejected` health alert names the lengths of the most frequent reason only.
- feat(webhook): a rejected TradingView alert says why (owner report 2026-11-09: every alert since the week open answered "401 Unauthorized"; the Pine candidates' five payloads are valid JSON with the `secret` field, so the Worker kept no trace of what it refused). POST /webhook now stores each 401 in D1 `webhook_rejections` with its reason: no secret (empty CTA Webhook Secret input), extra whitespace, letter case, a different length, or a different secret of the same length. It keeps lengths, source, timeframe, candle time, content type and user agent, never a character of either secret; at most 300 rows an hour, pruned after 14 days. The response stays a bare "Unauthorized". A `webhook_rejected` health alert (Priority Queue, `xauusd-risk-manager`) opens while rejections arrive and resolves an hour after the last. New read-only workflow `inspect-ai-worker.yml` (manual) lists the AI Worker's deployments, versions (an upload or a secret change) and secret names, never a value.

## 2026-11-08

- fix(key-levels): fewer, independent candidate zones (owner report 2026-11-08: 11 candidates, too noisy). The four chart-plane readers added about eleven level kinds on four charts, and a source was a (kind, timeframe) pair, so one 15m chart's KWW mean, Fibonacci and PA line made three "sources". A source is now a level family (twelve families), a family on one timeframe adds only its heaviest level to the score, and at most two candidates per side remain (strongest, then nearest; a zone at price stays; the rest read `qualified`). `cryptophys.key-level-agent.v2`. The Nexus panel and chart say "families". Tests added.
- fix(market-data): the closed-market window follows New York time: no non-crypto pull from Friday 17:30 to Sunday 18:00 New York (Friday 22:30 to Sunday 23:00 UTC in winter, an hour earlier in summer). The fixed Sunday 22:00 UTC reopen was right only in summer: on 2026-11-08 the pull adapter pulled eight series every 5 minutes for an hour before gold opened at 23:00 UTC. The Nexus System status page uses the same window. Test updated for both seasons.
- fix(news,ohlcv): the TradingView gateway allows the context series and validates each request alone. US10Y and XAGUSD (added for the Nexus correlations) were never on the gateway's allowlist, and one invalid request rejected the whole batch. At 22:40 UTC on 2026-11-08, after the reopen, a batch carrying them failed whole, gold included (`symbol_not_allowed` for every series); this was also why US10Y stayed empty. The defaults now include `TVC:US10Y` and `OANDA:XAGUSD`, `TV_GATEWAY_SYMBOLS` adds to the defaults instead of replacing them, and an invalid request is answered in its own slot while the valid ones run. Test added.
- feat(macro): oil in the Macro War Room (owner request 2026-11-08, "geopolitics causes inflation").
  - **Correlations:** a WTI crude oil row (`TVC:USOIL`). It reuses the 1h series derived from the USOIL 5m pull that GMI already makes, sampled at gold's 4h closes, so it needs no new TradingView call.
  - **Oil Analyst:** a new agent in the room. It reads oil's changes, its correlation with gold, and the News Daily headlines on oil or geopolitics (`geopoliticalHeadlines`). Its rule: a supply shock or conflict that lifts oil raises inflation expectations and safe-haven demand, which usually supports gold, unless the same rise pushes yields and Fed expectations higher.
  - **Correlation Analyst:** now covers oil too.
  - **Nexus:** the Macro War Room page lists the Oil Analyst and the oil row.
  - **Cost:** about 12 model calls a session, within the 30-per-hour cap. Tests added.
- feat(nexus): the Key Level Agent panel follows the key-level plan (owner question 2026-11-08). It shows how many levels each reader added (Band, Liquidity, Projection, Session), says when they wait for the chart plane, and shows the strategy's setup type at the 15m location with its evidence. A zone's hover names each level's reader. The candidate rule text now reads three or more sources and at least medium strength (it said two).
- fix(market-data): a one-day TradingView budget override (owner decision 2026-11-08). The weekend pulls made before #165 spent the day's 800 calls, and the market reopens at 22:00 UTC, two hours before the budget resets. `TV_DAILY_CALL_BUDGET_OVERRIDE` (`"YYYY-MM-DD:<calls>"`) raises the budget for that UTC date only; it is set to `2026-11-08:1000` and lapses by itself at 00:00 UTC. The 800 base is unchanged. Diagnostics report the effective budget. Test added.
- feat(nexus): the GMI chart layers (owner decision 2026-11-08, PR 3 of the key-level plan). A row of toggles above the Nexus chart, kept per viewer:
  - **On by default:** Zones, Bands (Z-KWW mean, ±1σ and ±1.618σ, EMA 114; `lib/indicators.ts` computes them from the chart's closed bars with the Mizan formulas) and Volume (the session POC, VAH and VAL).
  - **Fib:** Auto shows the OTE band and the extensions while a setup forms.
  - **Toggles:** VWAP (5m to 1H), Liquidity (whale zones, FVG targets, QM, RBS/SBR) and Session (true opens).
  - **Backend:** `GET /api/hud/levels` serves the snapshot's `chart_plane`; each level is named on the price axis.
- feat(key-levels): the four level readers and the setup type (owner decision 2026-11-08, PR 2 of the key-level plan). `src/level-readers.mjs` reads the chart plane of each HUD snapshot:
  - **Readers:** the Band, Liquidity, Projection and Session agents hand the Key Level Agent their levels with base weights (SSOT §Key levels). `GET /api/key-levels` reports how many levels each reader added, and each zone member names its reader.
  - **Setup type:** `setupType` labels the 15m location as *continuation_pullback* (OTE band or KWW mean, with liquidity with the trade) or *reversal_sweep* (a 1.618σ stretch into a QM level or a whale zone). A setup type counts as a location with the trade. The strategy reports it as `setup`, and the captured strategy inputs keep the chart plane for the replay.
  - Deterministic: no model call. Tests: `test/level-readers.test.mjs`.
- feat(producer): the chart plane in every HUD snapshot (owner decision 2026-11-08, PR 1 of the key-level plan). `local-engines/producer/src/chart-plane.mjs` computes every price object GMI v12.70 draws on the chart at the closed 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 bar, from the cached bars (no new TradingView call):
  - bands: Z-KWW MA, ±1σ and ±1.618σ, EMA 114, VWAP, the TVA channel;
  - liquidity: FVG open targets, whale zones and mitigation blocks, QM/RBS/SBR, TDM POC/VAH/VAL, LVN and HVN;
  - projection: the SMC Fibonacci grid, the probability cone, the tactical trendlines and PA lines;
  - session: True Openings, day and week opens, previous day and week high/low, and the Daily session volume profile.
  - It rides as `chart_plane`, outside `huds`, so no room agent changes. About 2 KB and under 90 ms per chart. The hashed engine files are untouched. `/health` names the plane's version per chart. Tests: `local-engines/producer/test/chart-plane.test.mjs`.
- fix(alerts): canonical engine names with their timeframe in the Priority Queue (owner request 2026-11-08). One backend map, `src/engine-names.mjs` (`engineLabel`), names a registry id by its indicator name, chart timeframe and id, e.g. "Parity revoked: GMI v12.70 15m (gmi_m15)". The health alert subjects (and so their kernel reports), `GET /api/parity` (`name`, `label`) and the Nexus parity tables and local-lane line use it; an input mismatch names the bar by its open time, and a room by its SSOT name ("the 1H War Room"). Open alerts take the new subject at the next sweep. A test keeps the Nexus map (`lib/names.ts`) equal to the backend one. Tests: `test/engine-names.test.mjs`, `test/health-alerts.test.mjs`, `test/parity-view.test.mjs`.
- feat(nexus): the System status page (`#/status`, owner request 2026-11-08), under the War Room in the Live group of the sidebar. It replaces the Worker status panel of the War Room, so the War Room shows only issues.
  - **Workers and lanes:** the AI Worker, the news ingestion and its last hourly pull, TradingView OAuth, the local lane (with the re-earning progress), calibration, the pull adapter's last refresh, the TradingView budget of the day and the freshness of the gold bars. On the weekend, lanes that are expected to be idle show grey.
  - **Priority Queue.**
  - **Pull adapter:** the budget bar, the last refresh, the last H1 publish, the derived check, and a table of every series with its role, pull cadence, last close and bar count (diagnostics now include `cadence_seconds`).
  - **Parity:** the new read-only `GET /api/parity` (`src/parity-view.mjs`), with every registry identity (current or older), its streak, totals, last result and implementation hash, the counts of the last 24 hours and the recent comparisons with the mismatched fields.
  - **War Room:** a "System issues" panel appears only when a lane is amber or red, and links to the page.
  - **Canonical names (owner request 2026-11-08):** every label uses the SSOT names (`lib/names.ts`). Each lane names its Worker (`xauusd-ai-trading-assistant`, `xauusd-fundamental-news-ingestion`, `xauusd-tradingview-pull-adapter`), and each engine its indicator (MizanLib, Prophet R38 + R37, GMI v12.70, Prophet HUD, GMI HUD) beside its registry id.
  - Tests: `test/parity-view.test.mjs`.
- feat(strategy): the 5m trigger is checked at every closed 5m bar (owner decision 2026-11-08, "the confirmation is critical").
  - **Before:** the strategy read the 5m Prophet confirmation only from the 15m room, so a confirmation on the first or second 5m bar of a 15-minute window was missed.
  - **Now:** each 5m context from the pull adapter re-evaluates the engine (`evaluateStrategyAtM5Close`) with that bar's confirmation (VERIFIED only while the 5m parity holds) and close. A TRIGGERED setup is recorded in the Signal Ledger at once, and its Telegram alert is sent.
  - **Also:** `liveM5Inputs` feeds `/api/strategy` and the Global decision; the trigger note names the 5m bar; the strategy inputs capture keeps the 5m price for the replay.
  - Deterministic, no model call. Tests added.
- fix(market-data): the gold chart stays live (owner decision 2026-11-08, after #166). XAUUSD 1m is pulled every 5 minutes again, so the 5m chart and 5m parity are real-time; only USOIL 5m (macro) keeps the 15-minute cadence. A weekday costs about 440 calls, plus 24 for the news.
- perf(market-data): the TradingView pulls recalculated for the quota (owner request 2026-11-08). XAUUSD 1m and USOIL 5m are now pulled once per 15 minutes, at the 15m close, instead of every 5 minutes (`cadence: 900`).
  - Every bar is still pulled, so the 5m and 15m engines, the derived series and their parity are unchanged. A local record is compared only when it exists, so skipped 5m bars never break an approval streak.
  - 5m freshness drops from 5 to 15 minutes, and 5m parity samples one 5m bar in three.
  - Calls on a weekday:
    | Series | Before | After |
    |---|---|---|
    | 1m | 288 | 96 |
    | USOIL 5m | about 276 | 92 |
    | Rest (H1, H4, D1, W1, DXY, USDT, US10Y, XAGUSD, the daily derived check) | about 60 | about 60 |
    | **Total** | **about 620** | **about 250** |
  - News stays at 24 a day (hourly). A weekend now costs about 12 calls (USDT only).
  - Test added.
- fix(market-data): no TradingView pull while the market is closed (owner report 2026-11-08: the Macro War Room page showed no US 10-year data). On the weekend the pull adapter re-requested every gold, oil and dollar series each 5 minutes although no new bar could arrive. That spent the daily budget of 800 TradingView calls (from the owner's quota), and the context series last in line (US 10-year, silver) never got a turn.
  - Now `marketClosed` skips non-crypto series from Friday 22:30 to Sunday 22:00 UTC (both daylight-saving offsets).
