  - An empty series still bootstraps once, and crypto is pulled every day. The gap is pulled at the reopen. Test added.
- feat(nexus): the Macro War Room page lists its agents and their roles before the first session, and says when the first session is.
- feat(macro): the Macro War Room (owner decisions 2026-11-08: the correlations become a room for the news and events that move gold; it meets after every 4-hour close and after a High-impact USD event; context plus a MACRO row in the Global decision), in `src/macro-room.mjs`.
  - **Agents:**
    - the Calendar Agent, with the fixed-rule projections of #163: before a release its bias must follow the consensus lean, and after a release it reads the headlines;
    - the News Analyst;
    - the Dollar Analyst (DXY), the Rates Analyst (US 10-year) and the Metals Analyst (silver), each with the 4-hour, 1-day and 5-day change and the correlation with gold over a week and a month;
    - the Correlation Analyst.
  - **No gold price level** reaches the agents.
  - **Phases:**
    - the bus;
    - a Consensus Discussion where the agents whose bias differs from the room's answer, at most 4 (if none differ, the Correlation Analyst stress-tests the room);
    - the moderator's macro bias for gold over the next 4 to 24 hours, with its reasons and its drivers.
    - A BULLISH or BEARISH conclusion needs an agent holding it.
  - **Schedule:**
    - 20 minutes after each 4-hour gold close, after the timeframe rooms' burst;
    - 30 minutes after a High-impact USD event, with events at the same minute sharing one session;
    - each session within 3 hours, once, at most one per sweep, in its own `waitUntil`;
    - behind `MACRO_ROOM_ENABLED` (on in production and preview), within `MACRO_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` 30.
    - A session costs about 11 model calls.
  - **Consumers (context only):**
    - the News Analyst of the 15m War Room and of the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms reads the latest conclusion (8 hours fresh);
    - the Global decision shows a MACRO row that never enters the weighted score;
    - the strategy engine's decision is unchanged.
  - **Read and UI:** `GET /api/macro`; the Nexus page `#/correlations` is now the "Macro War Room" (conclusion, bus, discussion, then the correlations table).
  - Tests: `test/macro-room.test.mjs`.
- feat(calendar): the Calendar Agent (owner decisions 2026-11-08: fixed rules plus an AI narrative; context plus a MACRO row in the Global decision), in `src/calendar-agent.mjs`.
  - **Projection by fixed rules per event kind:** inflation, jobs, wages, growth, surveys, housing, the rate decision, unemployment and claims, Fed speeches. For each USD event it gives:
    - which way gold usually moves if the actual is above or below the forecast (stronger US data or a hawkish Fed: gold down; unemployment and claims the other way; a speech by its tone);
    - the consensus lean (forecast against previous, largely priced in);
    - the status (upcoming, within 2 hours, released, past);
    - the horizon, from 2 hours before to 4 hours after the event.
  - **Outcome after release:** the export has no actual figure, so the outcome is read from the headlines.
  - **Where it is used:**
    - `GET /api/calendar` adds `projection` per USD event and the `outlook` (High-impact USD events from 6 hours back to 48 hours ahead, and the net consensus lean of the next 24 hours);
    - the News Daily TLDR prompt reads the outlook and may not reverse a projected direction;
    - the Risk Desk's calendar lines in the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms carry the projection;
    - the Nexus calendar page shows the Calendar Agent panel and an "above / below forecast" column.
  - The Macro War Room that discusses it follows in its own PR. Tests: `test/calendar-agent.test.mjs`.
- feat(telegram): the H1 decision message is sent only for a BUY or SELL, or when the decision changes from the previous bar's (for example BUY to WAIT); a WAIT after a WAIT is not sent (owner decision 2026-11-08, `src/h1-telegram.mjs`). The same bar processed twice is never sent twice. `H1_TELEGRAM_MODE = "all"` restores one message per bar. Tests: `test/h1-telegram.test.mjs`.
- fix(nexus): smaller type in the conclusion card (News and room tabs) and the Global decision panel (owner request 2026-11-08). A long decision word (BEARISH, BULLISH or NEUTRAL in the News TLDR) was 52 px wide in a 176 px column and ran into the summary. Now the decision is 36 px, or 26 px for words longer than 5 letters; the bias is 18 px; the summary is 12 px and the reasons and headlines 11.5–12 px. The Global decision is 32 px, or 24 px for long words. Checked at 1440, 1024 and 390 px: no overlap.
- feat(nexus): the Decision audit and Alerts pages; the Intelligence group no longer reads "soon".
  - **Decision audit (`#/audit`):** the decision cards of the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 rooms, from `/api/decision-cards`. Each card shows the conclusion, the crowd line, the H1 decision, the open stuck states, every agent's final stance and opening claim, the rule breaks, and the outcomes once scored. The page also shows the rule breaks of the last 7 days and the push status to Aether. No levels.
  - **Alerts (`#/alerts`):** the Telegram alert state from the new read-only `GET /api/alerts`: whether the bot is configured, the events the next sweep would send (with their text), and the recorded ones.
  - Tests: `readLedgerAlerts` (read-only, no secret value, recorded and pending events).
- feat(ledger): Telegram alerts from the Signal Ledger (backlog 11.2, owner decision 2026-11-08), in `src/ledger-alerts.mjs`, D1 `ledger_alerts` (migration 0017).
  - **Events.** A strategy setup triggered, the entry reached, TP1 / the stop / the time limit reached (with R), the TP2 part closed, and a setup cancelled. Each is sent once, over the existing Telegram bot.
  - **Safe start.** The first run records the existing events without sending them, so the deploy does not flood the chat. At most 5 messages a run; a failed send is retried by the next run.
  - **Advisory only.** Every message says no order is placed and no position size is given. Off with `LEDGER_ALERTS_ENABLED = "false"`.
  - Tests: `test/ledger-alerts.test.mjs`.
- feat(strategy): the economic calendar and the event-risk gate (backlog 11.1, owner decision 2026-11-08), in `src/econ-calendar.mjs`.
  - **Source.** The ForexFactory weekly export, fetched by the sweep at most once an hour and cached. A failed fetch keeps the cache.
  - **Gate.** A High-impact USD event is a caution for the strategy engine (−0.1 confidence) from 2 hours before to 30 minutes after it. It never forces WAIT and never changes the side.
  - **Rooms.** The Risk Desk of the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms sees the events due now and in the next 24 hours.
  - **Replay.** The strategy inputs record carries the gated events, so the replay reproduces the caution.
  - **Read model.** `GET /api/calendar` and the Nexus page `#/calendar`.

- feat(nexus): the correlations panel (backlog 11.3), `#/correlations` and `GET /api/correlations` (`src/correlations.mjs`).
  - **What it shows.** Gold beside DXY, the US 10-year yield and silver on closed 4h bars:
    - the last close and the change over 4h, 1 day and 5 days, in basis points for the yield;
    - the Pearson correlation of 4h returns with gold over 30 and 120 bars;
    - each series is as-of aligned to gold's 4h closes.
  - **New series.** The pull adapter pulls `TVC:US10Y` and `OANDA:XAGUSD` 4h as `context` series: about 12 TradingView calls a day, last in priority, never in the engine bundle.
  - **Isolation.** The panel is context only; nothing reaches a room or a decision.

- feat(aether): phase A9 groundwork. The bundled learned pack and its retrieval, in `src/learned-knowledge.mjs` and `scripts/build-learned-knowledge.mjs`.
  - **The pack.** `knowledge/learned/pack.json` (empty) is generated into `src/learned-knowledge.generated.mjs`. The script adds an item only when its shadow verdict is `better_than_active`.
  - **Test gates.** Tests check that the generated module is fresh and that every item follows the rules: plain words, no levels, its numbers in its evidence, provenance and a shadow verdict.
  - **Retrieval.** Selection is deterministic, by audience, chart and session. The budgets are 2 notes and 400 characters for agents, and 3 notes and 600 characters for the moderator.
  - **Where it goes.** The block follows the data in the 1H, 4H and D1 room openings and conclusions. The brief records `learned_pack`.
  - **Switch.** It is off unless `LEARNED_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED`. With the flag off, the prompts are unchanged.

- feat(aether): phase A7, second part. The shadow knowledge arm for approved text items, `src/shadow-knowledge.mjs`.
  - **Default.** Off; it runs only with `SHADOW_KNOWLEDGE_ENABLED = "true"`.
  - **The call.** For up to 2 items, the target agent of the 1H room makes one extra opening call per bar with the note. An A/A call without the note measures the noise (O13).
  - **Timing and budget.** The calls run after the H1 decision and are capped at 8 calls an hour.
  - **Storage.** D1 `shadow_agent_outputs` (migration `0016`) holds the answers, with the real opening as the control. Scoring happens in that table, never in `outcome_scores`.
  - **Visibility.** `/api/tuning` shows `replays.knowledge`, and Nexus shows it in the "Tuning grid" panel.
  - **Prompt.** The live opening prompt is unchanged: the note is only added in the shadow call.

- feat(aether): phase A8, part 4. The strategy-threshold replay arm, `src/strategy-replay.mjs`.
  - **Engine change.** `buildStrategy(inputs, params)` takes its thresholds; the default `STRATEGY_PARAMS` keeps the live behaviour.
  - **Replay.** Each stored hour gets one decision per arm (control, `strategy@rrr1.2`, `strategy@conf0.3`, `strategy@near0.5`). The decisions are computed once in the sweep and kept in D1 `strategy_replays` (migration `0015`).
  - **Scoring.** Each decision is scored with the hour's stored outcome, using the sign test, Holm and the holdout.
  - **Drift check.** The control is checked against the live decision stored at capture.
  - **Visibility.** `/api/tuning` shows `replays.strategy`, and Nexus shows it in the "Tuning grid" panel.

- feat(aether): phase A8, part 3 (owner go 2026-11-08). The strategy engine's inputs are now recorded once per closed H1 bar, for the strategy-threshold replay.
  - **Capture.** D1 `strategy_inputs` (migration `0014`) holds a slimmed, gzip-compressed record, captured at the first sweep 40 or more minutes after the close, together with the live decision and state.
  - **Equivalence.** A test proves `buildStrategy` on the slim record equals `buildStrategy` on the full inputs, across several engine states.
  - **Cost.** The inputs are loaded once an hour, not at every sweep.
  - **Outcomes.** Each stance's 4-hour outcome is scored once on the 15m bars. A capture whose bars left the cache is marked `no_data` after a day.
  - **Retention and visibility.** Kept 60 days. `/api/tuning` shows the counts under `strategy_inputs`.
  - **Isolation.** It never reaches a room, a decision, the platform or Aether.

- feat(aether): phase A8, part 2. `src/tuning-replay.mjs` adds a deterministic replay of the reputation parameters behind the 1H crowd line, exposed as `replays.crowd` in `GET /api/tuning`.
  - **Arms:** the control is the active parameters; the candidates are `crowd@prior20`, `crowd@weight0.5-1.5` and `crowd@band0.2`.
  - **Point-in-time reputation:** a score counts only once its 4-bar horizon has closed before the room. Each room's crowd stance is scored with that stance's outcome on that candle.
  - **Statistics:** the sign test, Holm, the discovery minimum and the holdout, as for the shadow versions. The crowd is also compared with the moderator on the same rooms.
  - **Status:** 18 scored 1H rooms on 2026-11-08, so the replay is `collecting` until about 300 rooms.
  - **Strategy thresholds:** their replay needs the hourly strategy inputs to be stored first, which is an owner decision.

- feat(aether): phase A8, first part. The shadow comparison gains Holm–Bonferroni and a holdout, and `GET /api/tuning` is added (RFC_AETHER §9.3, K7, O12).
  - **Holdout:** the newest 100 shared bars. The discovery test (the 200-bar minimum, the sign test) uses only the bars before them. A candidate must be no worse on the holdout.
  - **Holm:** the discovery p is adjusted across the family's tests of the calendar month, counting live candidates and those stopped that month.
  - **Verdicts:** `collecting`, `not_better`, `holdout_failed`, `better_than_active: ready for owner review`.
  - **Tuning view:** `src/tuning.mjs` lists every `TUNING_GRID` point as running, stopped or untested. It is read-only and starts nothing. Nexus `/shadow` shows it as the "Tuning grid" panel.

- ci: the deploy smoke tests check the CI Access service token strictly.
  - **Why:** the AI Worker's token probe followed redirects, so a refused token still ended on the Access login page with HTTP 200 and passed. The first Nexus run with the token (after #148) failed with "dashboard page not served", the same refusal.
  - **Now:** `scripts/access-token.mjs` normalises the secrets like the platform's `access-headers.sh` (CR/LF, whitespace, a header prefix). Swapped or wrong-shaped values are reported by shape, never by value. The token probes never follow a redirect, so an Access refusal fails with an explicit message.
  - **Access on `/api/*`:** `ai.cryptophys.work/api/*` is a path destination of the platform's Access application (platform #207, owner option (a)). `/health`, `/ready` and `/webhook` stay public.

## 2026-11-07

- feat(security): the AI Worker and Nexus have no public `workers.dev` or preview URL (`workers_dev: false`, `preview_urls: false`).
  - **Why:** both served the read API and the dashboard to anyone, bypassing the Cloudflare Access on their custom domains.
  - **Nexus smoke test:** it now needs the CI service token for its content checks.
  - **Unaffected:** the webhook at `ai.cryptophys.work/webhook` and the isolated `preview-e2e` Worker.
- feat(nexus): Nexus reaches the AI Worker through a Service Binding (topology change, owner go 2026-11-07, step 1 of locking the read API).
  - **Binding:** `AI_WORKER` → `xauusd-ai-trading-assistant` (default `fetch`) replaces the public fetch of `https://ai.cryptophys.work`.
  - **Why:** the AI Worker's `/api` can now go behind Cloudflare Access while Nexus keeps working. The webhook stays public and secret-protected.
  - **Read-only:** only GET and HEAD are forwarded. A missing binding fails closed with 503.
  - SSOT §2, AGENTS.md rule 7 and the topology test change together.
- feat(aether): phase A7, the AI Worker pulls Aether's learned pack; approved parameter candidates run as shadow versions.
  - **Pull:** `src/aether-pack.mjs`, hourly over the existing `AETHER` binding (`KnowledgeIngress.learnedPack()`). D1 `aether_learned_pack` (migration 0013) keeps the last good pack. A failure keeps it in use.
  - **Shadow:** an approved `param_candidate` in the reviewed `TUNING_GRID` (`stateMaxAge` 8, 12, 16, 24 for `prophet_r37` and `gmi_v12_70`) becomes a shadow version. At most 3 candidates run per family, and stopped points are never restarted. Nexus `/shadow` marks it "From Aether".
  - **Text items:** stored and listed in `GET /api/aether/status` only; they reach no prompt.
  - **Promotion:** stays an owner PR.
- feat(aether): phase A5, the AI Worker pushes its decision cards to Aether (topology change, owner go 2026-11-07).
  - **Binding:** `AETHER` → `cryptophys-aether` `KnowledgeIngress`, production AI Worker only, no props and no secret. SSOT §2, AGENTS.md rule 7 and the topology test change together.
  - **Push:** `src/aether-push.mjs`, once an hour in the first sweep after the H1 close. It sends the latest cards of each chart whose content changed, checked locally for leaks first. D1 `aether_card_pushes` (migration 0012) keeps the hash last sent and Aether's refusals.
  - **Status:** `GET /api/aether/status`.
  - **Non-fatal:** a push never changes a room, a decision or a card, and never waits on Aether.
- fix(rooms): agents stop talking about calibration (Aether's first compliance finding: more than half of the latest room messages broke the owner rule).
  - **Cause:** the natural-language rule and its correction prompt gave "verified by parity" as their example phrase. The example is replaced.
  - **Soft correction:** a valid room answer that still mentions parity, calibration, comparators, "unverified", "verified by" or "parity-verified" gets one soft correction within the budget. It is never rejected for this; the first answer is kept when the correction fails or changes the stance.
  - **One pattern:** the compliance check and the rooms use the same pattern (`CALIBRATION_TALK`).
- feat(aether): phase A1, agent compliance checks and decision cards (owner go on RFC_AETHER_KNOWLEDGE_GUARDIAN).
  - **Compliance:** `src/compliance.mjs` and D1 `compliance_checks` (migration 0011). Five deterministic checks run once over every stored room message: order or size wording, stance against bias, calibration talk, field names, and data scope. `GET /api/compliance` returns rule breaks per 100 messages; Nexus shows them on the Scorecards page.
  - **Decision cards:** `src/decision-cards.mjs` and `GET /api/decision-cards`. One card per room conclusion, for Aether, with no level, price, plan or size.
  - **Read-only:** neither changes a message or a decision.
- docs: RFC `docs/RFC_AETHER_KNOWLEDGE_GUARDIAN.md` (draft, revision 2). Aether is a separate Knowledge Guardian cluster: decision audit, compliance with role and doctrine, its own Canon (master knowledge) and learned knowledge, and self-calibration by proposal (shadow tests; the owner promotes by PR).
  - Vectorize is recommended separate from the platform's agent memory, so the state machine and the state market stay apart.
  - It is design only, and it lists the owner decisions needed.
- feat(nexus): Signal Ledger, the Performance page and a left sidebar.
  - **Ledger:** `src/signal-ledger.mjs` and D1 `signal_ledger` (migration 0010). Every H1 BUY/SELL decision and every triggered strategy setup is followed on closed bars to its outcome in R: fill, target, stop or time exit, with exit at TP1 and at TP2 tracked side by side. It is advisory only: R, never money. The parity sweep runs it.
  - **API:** `GET /api/ledger?days=30` returns the win rate, expectancy, total R, profit factor, drawdown, fill rate, equity curve and signals, per source and in total.
  - **Nexus:** a Performance page (`#/performance`). Navigation moves to a left sidebar grouped by layer: Live, Analytics, Market and Intelligence. The layers still to build are marked "soon".
  - **Backlog:** item 11 records the next layers. The proposed calendar source is the ForexFactory weekly export, which needs an owner decision.
- feat(shadow): P5 review (owner decision). Two latch candidates stopped, deadlines for the rest.
  - **Stopped:** `prophet_r37@latch12` (4 against 3 where the stances differed, p 0.5) and `prophet_r38@latch8` (no stance ever differed, because R38 takes a side only on a confirmed signal). They are no longer computed; their stored outputs stay. The view lists them under `retired`.
  - **Deadlines:** `prophet_r37@latch8` is reviewed at 30 decisive pairs or on 2026-12-07 at the latest. The GMI candidates are reviewed at their 200-bar sample, around 2026-11-14. Each running candidate shows a `review` in `GET /api/shadow-versions` and on the Nexus Shadow page.
- feat(parity): a comparator for the Grid Bot Protocol HUD, the `gmi_hud` identity.
  - **Pine candidate:** the GMI HUD candidate alert (`gmi_v12_70_hud`) also carries `cshi_viability`, `deploy_status`, `muddy_waters` and `grid_viable`. `pinescript/ssot` is unchanged.
  - **Local record:** the pull adapter posts a `gmi_hud` record after each H1 publish, and again when it republishes a revised bar. Its result never decides the publish marker.
  - **Parity:** `gmi_hud` is a separate identity with its own provenance. It is accepted at ingest and compared by the parity sweep, but stays out of the decision lane. The approved `prophet` and `gmi` hashes are unchanged.
  - **Readings:** the Grid Bot line is verified once `gmi_hud` is approved and its record equals the snapshot.
  - **Owner step:** install the GMI HUD candidate on the H1 chart.
- feat(agents): P6, Supertrend and Order Blocks, and the Grid Bot Protocol HUD ported. Three more agents in every room.
  - **Supertrend:** `ta.supertrend(3, 10)` on closed bars. Its H1 direction is scored as `indicator:supertrend`, and its line is a key level.
  - **Order Blocks:** it reads the Prophet R38 order-block filter and GMI's Aegis blocks, so no new definition was needed. Its best blocks are key levels.
  - **Grid Bot Protocol:** Mizan `cshi_engine` and GMI's deploy status, from the Pine source. It was the last HUD not computed.
  - **Hourly caps:** `WAR_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` goes from 112 to 136 and `TF_THREADS_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` from 120 to 140, for the three extra openings per room.
  - **Backlog:** the Grid Bot Protocol and P6 items are done. B4 stays: it needs a calibration window from the owner.
- feat(agents): P2 of RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM, indicator manifests and the registry.
  - **Manifests:** `src/indicator-manifests.mjs` gives one manifest per engine, library, HUD agent, desk and the moderator. No hashed file changes.
  - **Generated rosters:** the HUD roster and the agent families of the crowd line are now generated from the manifests.
  - **Registry:** `GET /api/indicator-agents` (`src/indicator-registry.mjs`) joins each manifest with its parity identities per chart, its 30-day scorecards, its open stuck states and its shadow versions.
  - **Next:** P6 (Supertrend, then Order Block) can use the manifest path.
- feat(agents): P7 of RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM, the crowd line, reputation weighting and Nexus scorecards (owner go 2026-11-07). The RFC's proposed values for decisions 2 to 4 are accepted.
  - **Scoring:** outcome scoring now covers the agents of the Live War Room 15m and the 1H War Room (`agent:<id>`, final stance after the Consensus Discussion). Before, only the retired legacy rooms were scored, so the current rooms had no track record.
  - **Reputation:** 30-day accuracy shrunk toward 50 % with 10 pseudo-observations. Weight = 2 × that, within 0.25 to 2. Open H1 stuck states add a caution flag in the 1H room.
  - **Crowd line:** for every room, a deterministic line with the head count, the reputation-weighted biases, a disagreement index and the families against the crowd. The moderator and the H1 Reasoner receive it.
  - **Nexus:** each post shows its agent's record, each room shows a crowd line card, and a new Scorecards page lists the agents.
  - **Backlog:** last week's lists are merged into `docs/BACKLOG.md`: B4, the Grid Bot Protocol HUD, P2, P6 and the P5 candidates' decision.
- feat(h1): the 1H room runs right after the close, so the H1 decision comes sooner (owner request 2026-11-07).
  - **Why:** the decision came 15-45 minutes after the close. The room waited for the next sweep (:10, :25, :40, :55) and up to 20 minutes for its HUD snapshot.
  - **Now:**
    - Parking the evidence enqueues an `h1_room` task.
    - The task waits for the HUD snapshot until 5 minutes after the close, runs the 1H room, and the Reasoner decides at once.
    - A late snapshot re-convenes the room; the decision already written stays.
  - **Fallback:** the sweep remains it, and skips a bar while the task holds its room (`h1room:lock:`).
  - **Measured:** `consensus.decided_after_close_s` (target about 10 minutes).
  - **Backlog:** `docs/BACKLOG.md` lists the open work that waits on the market, a date or the owner, starting with the Monday 2026-11-09 production checks.
- feat(models): the model migration (owner decision 2026-11-07: "as recommended").
  - **Why:** Workers AI retires `@cf/zai-org/glm-5.3-flash` and `@cf/nvidia/nemotron-3-120b-a12b` on 2026-11-20 (changelog 2026-11-05: HTTP 410, error 5028; first announced for 2026-11-15). glm-5.3-flash was also paid and limited to 20 requests a minute per account, the main source of the rooms' 3021 errors.
  - **Roles:**
    - Room agents: gemma-4-26b-a4b-it with thinking off (fallback gpt-oss-20b).
    - Moderators and the News Daily TLDR: gpt-oss-120b (fallback gemma-4).
    - News chain and the H1 Reasoner: gpt-oss-120b.
    - New per-role variables: `ROOM_AGENT_MODEL`, `ROOM_AGENT_FALLBACK_MODEL`, `MODERATOR_MODEL`, `MODERATOR_FALLBACK_MODEL`.
  - **Change to the recommendation:** it kept Nemotron as the Reasoner, but Nemotron is on the same retirement list, so the Reasoner moves to gpt-oss-120b too.
  - **Request forms per model family** (`modelRequests`, `runModel`): a form the model rejects falls back to the next one. `extractModelText` reads the Responses API shape of gpt-oss.
  - **Rooms:** they now use each role's fallback. Before, they ran on the primary only, to avoid the paid, slow glm-4.7 fallback. A pinned primary failure belongs to the failed model only.
  - **preview-e2e:** it asserts the role models. A preview-only `/preview/reasoner-probe` proves the Reasoner model.
  - **First preview-e2e run:**
    - It passed the Reasoner probe (gpt-oss-120b, first request form, 1.4 s), the news thread and the first War Room.
    - The second War Room's gpt-oss-120b conclusion was rejected. Most likely cause: gpt-oss names agents by id (`trade_management`), which the natural-language rule reads as a field name.
    - Fixes:
      - Bus agent ids in a moderator's text and in rebuttals are now written as the agents' names before validation.
      - A fourth reason is dropped instead of failing the conclusion.
      - A moderator answer that is still rejected after its correction is asked once from the moderator's fallback model.
      - A conclusion that failed now shows its errors, model and raw text in the room API.
- feat(h1): the Reasoner decides the H1 bar last, from the 1H War Room's Consensus Discussion; the Analyst, the Counselor and the old H1 bus are retired (owner decision 2026-11-07).
  - **Why:** the chain decided at the close, before any discussion, so the decision was ambiguous and almost always WAIT. The Analyst and the Counselor ran only in the background (no screen showed their output). In the last 32 production runs the Counselor returned no answer every time, and the Analyst 7 times.
  - **Flow:** complete evidence is parked at the close (no model call). The 1H War Room meets: bus, Consensus Discussion, conclusion. The Reasoner then decides when the room concludes, or 45 minutes after the close at the latest with whoever spoke. Degraded evidence is still a fail-closed WAIT at once.
  - **Rules:** the decision follows the room's consensus. A WAIT room with a clear bias may become BUY or SELL only with the matching confirmed 1-hour Prophet R38 signal and exact prices. A deterministic guard turns any BUY or SELL against the room into WAIT. Vetoes: Prophet `t_enc` 4, a GMI verdict of ∓3 against the trade, missing prices.
  - **Fail-closed:** an empty room gives WAIT without a model call. A Reasoner still failing 2 hours after the close gives WAIT.
  - **1H room:** the 1H Desk reads the local records of the bar (like the 4H and D1 Desks). No room prompt carries an H1 decision any more, and the H1 reference rule of the 1H conclusion is gone.
  - **Nexus:** the 1H tab ends with the Reasoner's H1 decision (`reasoner_decision` on `GET /api/threads/timeframe?chart=H1`).
  - **Terms:** "debate" is now "Consensus Discussion" in every room prompt, the Nexus labels and the docs. Stored step names (`debate:*`) and API fields stay for compatibility.
  - **preview-e2e:** the old bus and Threads 1H checks are replaced by a check that the old bus no longer runs.
- fix(bus): the Workers AI fallback is more reliable (owner decision 2026-11-07).
  - **Why:** three preview-e2e runs failed on 2026-11-06 (#122, #127, #128) when glm-5.3-flash timed out.
  - **Timeouts no longer pin the fallback at once:** one timeout sent every later step of a run to the fallback, as in the news thread of #127. Now one timeout falls back for its step only, and the second pins the fallback.
  - **A truncated fallback answer is retried:** a fallback answer cut off by its token limit, as with the H1 bus moderator of #128, is retried once with twice the budget and a brevity note.
## 2026-11-06

- fix(key-levels): each Camarilla level, pivot and CPR edge counts once, from the freshest snapshot. In production at 23:56 the D1 snapshot (still from 5 November) put a second "daily Camarilla L3" beside the current one, which inflated the source count of daily zones.
- feat(nexus): Key Level Agent zones on the chart; a candidate needs three sources and medium strength (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **Candidates:** a candidate now needs 3+ independent sources and a score of at least 3 (medium). At two sources, production marked 14 of 18 zones; now 9 of 18.
  - **Chart:** the price chart draws the candidate zones as bands behind the candles, coloured by role and shaded by strength. It also draws the strategy plan's entry, stop, TP1 and TP2 as price lines, with the RRR on the axis label. A "Zones" button toggles them.
- feat(strategy): the Key Level Agent. Key levels come from every chart, not only Camarilla, and the levels that stack in one zone become candidate key levels (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **Sources:** `src/key-level-agent.mjs` (`GET /api/key-levels`) gathers, each weighted by kind and timeframe:
    - Camarilla of the 15m, 1H, 4H, daily and weekly charts;
    - the daily and weekly pivot ranges;
    - the value area of each chart;
    - the swings of the 15m, 1H, 4H and D1 bars;
    - the previous day and week;
    - round numbers.
  - **Zones:** levels within 0.3 × the 1H ATR group into zones. A zone with two or more independent sources is a candidate, rated strong, medium or weak.
  - **Strategy:** it plans on the zones. The entry is at or pulls back to a zone edge, the stop sits beyond the far edge of the first zone against the trade, and the targets are the near edges of the next candidate zones. The strategy now also reads the 4H and D1 HUD snapshots and the 4H, D1 and W1 bars.
  - **Nexus:** a Key Level Agent card shows the zone ladder, the nearest candidate zones and a summary.
- feat(nexus): the Global Conclusion and the strategy engine are one "Global decision" card (owner request 2026-11-06: they were redundant).
  - `GET /api/conclusion/global` now carries the full strategy, so Nexus fetches one endpoint.
  - The card shows:
    - the decision, direction, global bias with its score, and confidence;
    - one row per timeframe plus News, with its lean (−100 to +100) and whether it passes;
    - the plan with its RRR;
    - vetoes and cautions;
    - the H1 chain as a reference.
  - The StrategyPanel and GlobalConclusion cards are removed.
- fix(strategy): a GMI and Prophet conflict and a closed GMI gate on the 1H chart are cautions (each −0.1 confidence), no longer vetoes (owner decision 2026-11-06, "longgarkan saja").
  - **Why:** at 14:15 they blocked a short setup that every room's agents backed (D1 −0.34, 1H −0.46, 15m −0.62).
  - **Still vetoes:** a Prophet cut loss (t_enc 4), and a strong GMI verdict against the trade.
- feat(strategy): the strategy engine. The decision is the consensus of every agent from D1 to 5m, with key levels and reward-to-risk (owner decision 2026-11-06: "full confluence and all agents"; "D1 trend down to the 5m entry, with key level prices and RRR").
  - **Why:** the H1 chain's three models need a Prophet trigger on the H1 candle itself, so the decision was almost always WAIT.
  - **What:** `src/strategy-engine.mjs` (`GET /api/strategy`) is deterministic. It reads:
    - the four War Rooms (every agent's bias, confidence weighted);
    - News Daily;
    - the 1H and 15m HUD snapshots;
    - the latest confirmations;
    - the closed 1H and 15m bars.
  - **Layers:** D1 and 4H give the trend, 1H the setup with the trend, 15m the location at a key level, and the 5m verified trigger.
  - **Plan:** an entry, a stop beyond the next level against the trade, and TP1/TP2 at the next major levels, with the RRR to each.
  - **Rules:** BUY/SELL needs the trigger, no veto, at least 1.5R and a confidence of at least 0.4. Otherwise it is WAIT with the state, the side and the levels.
  - **Global Conclusion:** its action is now the strategy decision. The H1 chain stays as a reference.
  - **Nexus:** a Strategy engine card leads the right panel: decision, the five-layer checklist, the plan ladder with RRR, vetoes and cautions.
- feat(rooms): decision and bias are separate; the debate follows the bias (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **Why many agents never spoke:** debate speakers were chosen by action alone. In production at 13:30, all 18 agents said WAIT, 11 leaned bearish and 3 bullish, and only the Risk Manager debated. The "could not speak this round" posts at 13:15 were the Workers AI limits, fixed by the quorum.
  - **Debate:** agents whose bias leans against the room's bias now debate, its strongest holder defends it, and the Risk Manager stays. Rebuttals carry a bias, and a changed bias is a concession.
  - **Conclusion:** gives the decision (`stance`) and the room's `bias` separately, and the summary states both. A bias needs an agent holding it.
  - **Nexus:** the conclusion card shows Decision and Bias side by side, and the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms follow the same rules.
- fix(war-room): the Live War Room 15m waits for a quorum before it concludes.
  - **Why:** in production at 13:15, Workers AI 3021 (the per-minute limit) and 3040 (capacity) failed 14 of 18 openings, even after the single retry, and the room concluded on four agents.
  - **Now:** within 5 minutes of the close, a room with fewer than half of its agents heard returns `waiting_for_quorum`. The queue redelivers it after 60 seconds, and only the failed openings are asked again. Past the wait, it concludes with whoever spoke, as the timeframe rooms do.
  - **Queue:** a task error may carry its own `retryDelaySeconds`.
- feat(rooms): D1 catch-up: a missed H4 or D1 HUD snapshot is sent late, once, and its room re-convenes with every agent (owner decision 2026-11-06, "Catch up now").
  - **Why:** the D1 room of the 5 November bar met with only the Technical Analyst and the Risk Manager. That bar closed (22:00 UTC) before the D1 HUD snapshot, the D1 Desk and News Daily existed, and the pull adapter sends a D1 snapshot only within 60 minutes of the close, so no D1 snapshot was ever stored.
  - **Pull adapter:** an H4 or D1 bar whose snapshot was never sent is sent once while it is still the latest close (H4 4 hours, D1 72 hours), built on the bundle cut at its close. `GET /health` shows `last_hud_publish` per chart.
  - **AI Worker:** a timeframe room that concluded with `hud_snapshot` false re-convenes once when the snapshot arrives, as run `<bar open>#hud`, with its HUD agents, the own desk and the News Analyst. The API keeps the bar open and adds `reconvened: true`.
  - **Not changed:** H1 and M15 snapshots and rooms; a room that had its snapshot never re-runs.
- fix(bus): a Workers AI capacity error (3040, "Capacity temporarily exceeded") gets the same single wait-and-retry as the per-minute limit (3021). Production 12:01: every opening of the 15m War Room and the News Daily TLDR failed on it, and the 12:15 room ran normally.
- feat(prophet): Prophet R38 and R37 read as one indicator (owner decision 2026-11-06, "so it is not ambiguous").
  - **Why one indicator:** both share one MizanLib structure core; their `d_enc` and subtype were identical on all 55 production H1 candles.
  - **What R37 adds:** its consensus direction was bullish or bearish on 29 of them, while R38's signal was set once. That consensus direction is the bias Prophet seemed to lack.
  - **The interpreter** (H1 chain, H1 bus, timeframe desks) now writes one "Prophet: …" line: R38's signal and trade management, R37's direction and confirmation candle, and the structure once.
  - **The Prophet HUD agent** reads its signal and direction first, then structure, management, setup and regime. The signal and direction come from the same snapshot, through the R37 Funnel and Trade Management panels, and are verified by parity.
  - **Prompts and desk roles** name "Prophet" instead of "Prophet R38, Prophet R37".
  - **Not changed:** the two TradingView alerts stay separate for calibration.
- feat(rooms): every agent states a bias beside its action; Prophet reads its state; a BUY or SELL conclusion needs support (owner decision 2026-11-06, Prophet audit).
  - **Audit:**
    - The AI Worker reads Prophet R38 correctly. The alert fields equal the local reading (verified by parity).
    - TradingView itself sent `direction 0 / t_enc 0 / d_enc 0` on H1 today. The cut-loss (`t_enc 4`), price-discovery (`t_enc 6`) and structure-break (`d_enc 3`) states of 4 and 5 November were read and reasoned about.
    - Prophet looked "always WAIT" for two reasons:
      - the room stance is an action, and without a confirmation the action is WAIT;
      - the Prophet agent never received its setup state.
    - Room leans therefore read near neutral.
    - The one confirmed sell (5 Nov 06:00) was blocked by the GMI gate conflict, by design.
  - **Bias:** every opening now gives `bias` (BULLISH, BEARISH or NEUTRAL) beside its action. Room leans and the Global Conclusion read it, and Nexus shows "WAIT · bias bullish".
  - **Prophet setup:** the Prophet reading gains its setup from the setup gate the snapshot computes for the R37 Funnel, for example "a buy setup is waiting for structure confirmation, and all three Prophet cycles point up", plus a risk veto (`t_enc 4`) or reversal watch (`t_enc 3`).
  - **Support rule:** a BUY or SELL conclusion needs at least one agent holding that stance. A conclusion that breaks it, or the confirmation rule, gets one correction call. The preview run had a BUY over a unanimous WAIT room.
- feat(war-room): each room has its own timeframe desk; the War Room conclusion looks as before in every tab (owner feedback 2026-11-06).
  - **15m room:**
    - The 15m Desk replaces the H1 Desk. It reads the parity-approved 15m Prophet R38, R37 and GMI records of the candle, in plain sentences.
    - The H1 chain and the latest 1H room conclusion move to a Higher-Timeframe Desk. The H1 decision still binds the moderator.
  - **1H, 4H and D1 rooms:**
    - The H1 Desk is now the 1H Desk, which also reads the local 1-hour records.
    - The 4H and D1 rooms gain a 4H Desk and a D1 Desk on their bar's local records.
    - Unapproved records are labelled and capped at confidence 0.4 (`src/timeframe-desk.mjs`).
  - **Nexus:** each tab's conclusion uses the original War Room conclusion design:
    - the glowing 52 px stance, with badges for the candle close, the phase and the 5m label;
    - the summary in its own box;
    - the H1 constraint and the entry confirmation in two boxes;
    - the advisory note.
  - **Stored summaries and reasons** now end at a full sentence (or a word with "…") instead of mid-word.
  - **The D1 room of 5 November had only two agents:** its bar closed before the D1 HUD snapshot and News Daily existed. The next daily close (22:00 UTC) runs with the full roster.
- feat(nexus): Global Conclusion, Camarilla/CPR level ladder and regime radar; each tab starts with its conclusion, and the agents panel follows the active tab (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **AI Worker:**
    - `GET /api/conclusion/global` (`src/global-conclusion.mjs`): a deterministic multi-timeframe summary of the D1, 4H, 1H and 15m rooms plus News Daily. It gives a weighted bias, agreement, a summary and an action bound by the H1 decision.
    - `GET /api/hud/levels?tf=15|60|240|1D` (`src/hud-levels.mjs`): Camarilla sets, daily and weekly pivot ranges, and six regime axes from the latest HUD snapshot. Each comes with its calibration.
    - Both routes answer before the `/api/hud/:candle_key` prefix. `/api/hud/latest?tf=` now also accepts 240 and 1D.
  - **Nexus:**
    - The right panel opens with the Global Conclusion and its sentiment gauge. The level ladder and regime radar follow the active tab.
    - The tab card shows the active timeframe's conclusion first.
    - The left panel lists the active room's agents.
- feat(nexus): the War Rooms move under the chart, and the right panel becomes a chart-only trading assistant (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **Center:** one card under the chart with the tabs `News | 15m | 1H | 4H | D1`, opening on News at every load. The separate 15m conclusion and 15m room cards are removed, because they repeated the 15m tab.
  - **Right:**
    - a market sentiment gauge (weighted room leans plus news) with a headline-bias donut;
    - trend by timeframe: D1 macro, 4H intraday, 1H swing, 15m structure, 5m confirmation;
    - the H1 decision ring with its levels and confirmations;
    - agent consensus bars;
    - macro drivers over 24 hours;
    - a session clock.
  - One provider fetches the 1H, 4H and D1 Threads, News Daily and the 5m context every 60 seconds for both panels.
- fix(war-room): a dissenter the bus persuaded now concedes in the debate instead of being dropped.
  - In production, every concession was rejected as `rebuts_invalid`. The dissenter changed its stance (for example, News Analyst SELL to WAIT), then wrote its own id or nothing in `rebuts`. This happened about every half hour in the 15m room, and also in the 4H room.
  - A rebuttal whose stance differs from the agent's opening may now name nobody. Its own id is dropped, and it is stored as `conceded: true`.
  - Nexus marks it "CONCEDES".
  - preview-e2e accepts it. On PR #116 the live preview model hit the same case.
- fix(bus): the H1 multi-agent bus gets the same single correction attempt as the Thread rooms.
  - An answer from an agent or the moderator that is valid except that its text reads like code (a field name or a key path) is asked once to rewrite in plain words.
  - The extra call is recorded in `corrections` and counted by the hourly budget. It never runs past the wall deadline.
  - On PR #116 the preview moderator named a field from the evidence, and the conclusion was lost.
- feat(hud): a third calibration tier, "computed from verified bars" (owner decision 2026-11-06, option 1).
  - HUD statements that are pure formulas of closed bars count as calibrated evidence when the candle's bar is proven equal to TradingView's. These are the Camarilla levels and bands, the daily and weekly pivot ranges, and the trading sessions.
  - The bar is proven when the candle has an approved local record and a matching parity comparison (which includes the input bar), and no input mismatch.
  - Such statements lift the 0.4 confidence cap in the 15m War Room and in the 1H, 4H and D1 rooms. Engine internals stay not yet calibrated.
  - Agents, the debate and the moderator see the tier. Nexus shows it in the badge, for example "1 verified · 3 from verified bars of 4".
  - CI: the preview deploy now also retries a Cloudflare API outage (5xx, "no healthy upstream") with a 30, 60 and 90 s backoff. Before, one outage failed preview-e2e before any test ran.
- feat(parity, health): architecture gaps 1 and 2 (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **Gap 1: one bad bar no longer revokes the H1 chain.**
    - When only the input bar differs from TradingView and every indicator field agrees, the result is `input_mismatch`. It neither counts nor revokes, and the candle stays out of the trusted lane.
    - The pull adapter republishes an H1 candle whose bar TradingView revised later. The new comparison replaces the open one and counts.
    - Three input mismatches in a row still revoke.
    - **One-time reconciliation (owner approval 2026-11-06):** the first parity sweep after deploy runs `reclassifyOnce` (KV marker `parity:reclassify-input-only:v1`).
      - It reclassifies the comparisons stored as `mismatch` under the old rule whose mismatches are all input-bar fields. In production these are three: GMI H1 01:00 volume, and Prophet and GMI H1 03:00 close and volume.
      - It restores the H1 `prophet` and `gmi` identities they revoked.
      - Indicator mismatches and escalated repeated-input revocations are never touched.
  - **Gap 2: the system watches itself** (`src/health-alerts.mjs`).
    - It checks for: a revoked parity identity, an input mismatch, a missing local H1 record, a room without a conclusion, Workers AI rate-limit losses, a stale News Daily, and a stale War Room.
    - It reports a `warning` when an alert opens and an `info` when it resolves, from `xauusd-risk-manager` to the platform kernel (at most 3 an hour, never `critical`).
    - Nexus shows the open alerts as its Priority Queue.
  - **Rate-limited model calls retry once.** A call that hits the Workers AI per-minute limit (3021) waits 12 seconds and retries the same model once, within its deadline. It no longer marks the primary model as failed for the rest of the run.
    - The limit is shared by every room and by the preview-e2e runs of the same account.
    - A preview run beside the 15m War Room lost two HUD openings to it.
- feat(nexus): Nexus rebuilt in the Cerebrum Console obsidian design (owner decision 2026-11-06), so it can merge into the Console control panel. The `nexus` subdomain will later replace `home`.
  - **Stack:** a React SPA in `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui` with the same stack and palette as `workers/console/ui`: React, Vite, Tailwind obsidian, lucide, framer-motion and the three.js neural mesh. It is served as Workers Static Assets.
  - **Layout:**
    - Same shell as the Console.
    - War Room agents and Worker status on the left.
    - Chart, War Room conclusion and Live War Room 15m in the center.
    - The Threads card (`D1 | 4H | 1H | 15m | News`) on the right; it moves under the center on narrower screens instead of hiding.
    - Shadow versions are at `#/shadow`.
  - **Worker:** it now forwards only GET/HEAD `/api/*` calls (read-only).
  - **CI:** the deploy workflow builds the UI, and `validate-nexus-dashboard.yml` builds it on pull requests.
- fix(market-data): native bars of 1 hour and longer count as closed only 90 seconds after their close.
  - **Cause:** the pull adapter took the H1 bar that closed at 04:00 UTC seconds after its close. That provisional bar had close 4045.145 and volume 48491. The final bar, and the TradingView alert, had 4044.815 and 48987.
  - **Effect:** the local H1 record built on the provisional bar failed parity on its input and revoked the approved H1 `prophet` and `gmi` identities. They re-earn 24 matches.
  - **Scope:** M1 and USOIL 5m keep no delay, so the 15m War Room is not held back.
- fix(threads): rooms of one sweep are 65 seconds apart, and a room concludes only with at least half of its agents heard.
  - In production, the 1H room started right after the 4H room and lost 14 of 18 openings to the Workers AI per-minute limit.
  - Failed openings are retried by the next sweep.
  - Two hours after the close, a room concludes with whoever spoke.
- fix(api): `/api/threads/timeframe` and `/api/news/daily` were answered by the `/api/threads/:id` and `/api/news/:key` prefix routes (404 in production). The prefix routes now exclude them, and a worker test covers it.
  - The timeframe rooms run one after another, D1, 4H, then 1H, instead of in parallel. In the first production run, three parallel rooms hit the Workers AI per-minute limit, and each room should read the higher timeframe's fresh conclusion.
- feat(threads): every Thread is a War Room of all agents; News Daily; tab bar on top of the right panel (owner decision 2026-11-06).
  - **Timeframe War Rooms.** The 1H, 4H and D1 Threads now run the Live War Room phases: every agent opens, a debate of up to 3 minutes, then the moderator's TLDR.
    - Agents: the desks plus one agent per HUD of that chart.
    - They run from local computation only, never from a webhook.
    - The pull adapter now also publishes the H4 and D1 HUD snapshots.
    - Budget `TF_THREADS_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR`: production 120.
  - **News Daily.** The last 24 hours of headlines, each with a gold bias (Bullish, Bearish or Neutral) and why, plus market context, and the News Analyst's TLDR.
    - The War Room and the timeframe rooms read it.
    - Read API: `GET /api/news/daily`.
  - **Nexus.** The right panel's tab bar sits on top of the card: D1 | 4H | 1H | 15m | News. Each tab shows the whole Thread instead of a short TLDR.
- feat(threads): Thread tabs in Nexus, D1, H4, H1 and 15m (step 4 of the owner's timeframe-thread plan; no 5m thread for now).
  - **H4 and D1 threads.** `src/timeframe-threads.mjs` runs one thread per closed H4 or D1 bar: Structure Desk, GMI Desk, Technical Analyst, Macro Desk and Risk Manager open once each, then the moderator writes the TLDR. Uncalibrated desks speak at confidence 0.4 or lower.
  - **Runs and budget.** The parity sweep cron runs it behind `TF_THREADS_ENABLED`, within `TF_THREADS_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` (production 24, preview 60). Read API: `GET /api/threads/timeframe?chart=H4|D1`.
  - **Nexus.** The right panel is one "Threads · TLDR" card with tabs D1, H4, H1, 15m and News. H1 and News come from `/api/tldr`, 15m from the Live War Room, H4 and D1 from the new API.
- feat(htf): H4 and D1 local lane (step 3 of the owner's timeframe-thread plan).
  - **Producer and publishing.** `htf-local-producer.mjs` builds mizan, prophet and gmi records for every closed H4 (`"240"`) and D1 (`"1D"`) bar. The pull adapter publishes them behind `HTF_PARITY_PUBLISH`.
  - **GMI engine.** GMI runs on the new `gmi-core-htf.mjs`, which adds H4/D1 and Pine's `dyn_ltf` intrabars (M5 on H4, M15 on D1). A test proves it equals `gmi-core-tf.mjs` on M15 and H1.
  - **Identities.** New provenance identities `prophet_h4`, `gmi_h4`, `prophet_d1` and `gmi_d1`. Every existing hash is unchanged.
  - **AI Worker.** The registry scopes `_h4` and `_d1`. The parity sweep reads the last two closed H4 and D1 keys from the cached native bars, because OANDA H4 and D1 bars open at 17:00 New York.

## 2026-11-05

- feat(hud): Macro & Aether Sensors complete (step 2 of the owner's timeframe-thread plan).
  - **New rows.** `macro-aether.mjs` ports CONSOL. GRAVITY, Z3 KINETIC SPREAD, MACRO CYCLE, EST. MACRO SHIFT, ASSET GRAVITY (WTI), Q-GRAVITY PULL and MACRO SMR from GMI v12.70 and MizanLib.
  - **Where they appear.** The H1 and M15 HUD snapshots carry them, and the Macro & Aether agent reads them in plain language.
  - **Unchanged.** No hashed engine file changed.

- feat(market-data): M5 and M15 derived locally from M1, and USOIL M15/H1 from USOIL M5; TradingView MCP budget 1200 → 800 (owner decision 2026-11-05).
  - **Why.** TradingView suspended the account's MCP access until 00:00 UTC for suspicious volume, which also stopped the pull adapter.
  - **Native pulls.**
    - Now: XAUUSD M1, H1, H4, D1 and W1; USOIL 5m; DXY and USDT 4h.
    - No longer pulled: XAUUSD M5 and M15, which were duplicates of M1.
    - USOIL 15m and 1h are new and derived.
  - **Demand.** About 620 calls a day, down from about 1000.
  - **Daily check.** A daily check compares the derived bars with a few native bars.
  - **Unchanged.** The cached history and every series key, so the engines, the AI Worker's scoring and Nexus read the same series.

- feat(shadow): GMI "Prophet risk hold" candidates `gmi_v12_70@latch8` and `gmi_v12_70@latch12`.
  - **How they work.** `src/gmi-regate.mjs` recomputes the two points of GMI v12.70 that the Prophet structure feeds: `mtf_state`, and the R10 gate with its risk hold. It starts from the approved local GMI record's diagnostics, so the hashed engine is not forked.
  - **Exactness.** With the active structure it reproduces GMI's comparable fields exactly (42 cases across two branches).
  - **Shadow family.** `gmi_v12_70` is a new shadow family. Its control is checked against the approved records.
- docs(rfc): owner decisions 2026-11-05. Decision 1: a local-only improved version is enough to prove it first. Decision 6: no limit on agents per room.

- ci(preview-e2e): a War Room proof draws its random 15m bar only when the shared preview D1 has no room for it yet. Run 42080341191 drew a bar an earlier run had used and read that run's room.
- ci(preview-e2e): transport errors between the runner and the preview edge (`ECONNRESET`, timeouts, socket errors) are retried up to 5 times. Every e2e request is idempotent. Run 42079728424 died on one reset right after a passing War Room proof.
- fix(war-room): blank conclusion reasons are dropped before validation. A preview run on 2026-11-05 returned three reasons and an empty string, which failed the whole conclusion as `reasons_invalid`. A list with no non-blank reason still fails.
- feat(shadow): RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P5, the first improvement candidates: structure-latch expiry.
  - **Candidates.** `prophet_r37@latch8`, `prophet_r37@latch12` and `prophet_r38@latch8`. The ±3 structure latch expires after 8 or 12 closed H1 bars instead of 50.
  - **Local-only.** They are option (b); owner decision 1 decides whether a promotion also needs a Pine candidate. They run in shadow only.
  - **Stricter criteria.**
    - A higher accuracy must pass a paired one-sided sign test (p < 0.05) on the bars where the versions disagree.
    - Stuck time is compared in candles, not episodes, because expiring a latch can split one episode in two.
  - **Real-data replay** (696 scored H1 bars, 2026-09-19 to 2026-11-05):

    | Version | Accuracy | Control | Bars that differ | Paired result |
    |---|---|---|---|---|
    | `prophet_r37@latch8` | 0.431 | 0.433 | 21 | 3 vs 4 |
    | `prophet_r37@latch12` | 0.434 | 0.433 | 6 | 3 vs 2 (p 0.5) |
    | `prophet_r38@latch8` | — | — | 0 | — |

    - `prophet_r38@latch8` changes no direction: R38 confirmed only 4 times in 696 bars.
    - Expiry alone is not yet an improvement. Its main effect would be on GMI's "Prophet risk hold", which needs a GMI variant: a future candidate.

- feat(shadow): RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P3, shadow indicator versions.
  - **Framework.** `src/shadow-versions.mjs` replays a control (the active Prophet R37 and R38 code with defaults) and candidate versions over the same cached H1 bars, once an hour. Outputs are stored after a 300-bar warm-up, and the first computation is final (`shadow_outputs`, migration `0008`).
  - **Scoring.** Outputs are scored as `shadow:<version>`.
  - **Comparison.** Candidates are compared with the control on the same bars using the RFC criteria: higher accuracy, no new stuck episodes, and at least 200 bars.
  - **Read model.** `GET /api/shadow-versions` and the Nexus `/shadow` page.
  - **Isolation.** Nothing reaches a room, a prompt or a decision, and the engine files are unchanged. P3 ships no candidate; P5 adds the first.

- feat(platform): RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P4, owner instruction 2026-11-05.
  - **Binding.** The AI Worker binds `AGENTS` to the Cryptophys Platform `cryptophys-ai-kernel` `AgentRpc` entrypoint. The binding pins `host_worker` and carries no secret. It is production only; preview has no binding, so the code is a no-op there.
  - **Reports.** One advisory report per meaningful event, sent from the parity sweep cron:
    - `xauusd-reasoner`: each new H1 decision; a BUY↔SELL flip is a `warning`.
    - `xauusd-moderator`: the War Room conclusion when its stance changes, otherwise at most once an hour.
    - `hud-*`: a HUD stance moving into or out of BUY or SELL.
  - **Failure handling.** A denial or a kernel rejection is recorded and never retried. Only a transport failure is retried, by the next sweep. Nothing is ever fatal to the chain.
  - **Storage and API.** Migration `0007_platform_publications.sql` adds the cursors. `GET /api/platform/status` shows them.
  - **Topology.** The topology test pins the binding to the production AI Worker.
  - **Platform-side auditor.** The Indicator Auditor of RFC §6 lives in the platform (owner decision 5); no platform change is needed.

- fix(scoring): scoring v3 (owner approval 2026-11-05). WAIT is missed only when a 2:1 trade would have won: `1 × ATR` on one side before `0.5 × ATR` on the other. In production, v2 still marked H1 WAIT right on only 1 of 35 candles, because over 4 H1 candles some side almost always reaches `0.5 × ATR` first. BUY and SELL scoring is unchanged. Rows are rescored within the lookback.
- feat(15m,war-room): owner decisions A and B.
  - **A: calibration status once.**
    - Each agent's evidence status is stored once in the War Room brief and shown once on Nexus. Agents never repeat calibration disclaimers; 12 of 17 openings at 16:00 had led with "not yet calibrated".
    - Uncalibrated HUD agents are capped at confidence 0.4.
    - The bus and the moderator weigh verified evidence above uncalibrated readings.
    - Agent ids written with spaces are accepted. The 16:00 opening of `interstellar_eyes` had failed as `agent_mismatch`.
    - Agents are named, not referred to by id, in debate text. The 16:00 Risk Manager rebuttal had failed the natural-language check over `kinetic_engine`.
  - **B: verified 15m lane.**
    - `m15-local-producer.mjs` and `publishM15LocalComputation` publish 15m records (`M15_PARITY_PUBLISH`).
    - New identities `prophet_m15` and `gmi_m15` have their own provenance; the H1 and 5m hashes are unchanged.
    - The parity sweep covers the last two closed 15m candles.
- fix(scoring): scoring v2 (owner approval 2026-11-05).
  - WAIT is a missed opportunity only when a BUY or a SELL on the same candle would have been a hit. A whipsaw counts as a hit for WAIT.
  - Under v1, a 0.5 × ATR touch within 4 candles marked nearly every WAIT as missed: the chain was right on 1 of 35 candles and the room on 0 of 56.
  - Older rows are rescored within the lookback, and scorecards read only v2. Migration `0006_outcome_runs_version.sql`.
- feat(war-room): Live War Room 15m (owner decision).
  - **Room.** At every 15m close, all agents open on a communication bus with one role each: H1 Desk, News, Technical, Risk and 13 HUD agents. The minority and the Risk Manager then debate for at most 3 minutes, and the moderator concludes for the Nexus main panel. The H1 decision stays the constraint.
  - **Rollout.** Behind `WAR_ROOM_ENABLED` (on), with `WAR_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR = 112`. It replaces the legacy live room and the per-H1 HUD agents.
  - **APIs.** `GET /api/war-room/latest` and `GET /api/tldr`.
  - **No confirmation, no entry** (owner rule): the Risk Manager enforces it from the H1 and VERIFIED 5m Prophet R38 confirmations, and a BUY or SELL conclusion without the matching confirmation is rejected.
  - **Nexus.** The main panel is the conclusion, the War Room shows the latest discussion only, and the right panel holds only the Threads 1H and News Thread TLDR. The left panel lists the War Room agents.
- feat(scoring): RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P1 (owner approval of the scoring definition and the stuck-state thresholds).
  - Deterministic outcome scoring of the H1 chain, bus, HUD-agent, indicator and live-room stances: horizon 4 candles, threshold `0.5 × ATR(14)`.
  - A stuck-state detector: a field unchanged for 8 H1 candles while the range exceeds `3 × ATR`.
  - New migration `0005_outcome_scores.sql`, run in the parity sweep cron, and new read APIs `GET /api/scorecards` and `GET /api/stuck-states`.
- feat(m5): B2. The 5m execution context is the bar's 5m parity record, reshaped (`m5-context.mjs`), sent once per closed 5m bar; `buildM5ProphetContext` is retired. The H1 chain and the bus label it VERIFIED 5m while `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` are approved, otherwise UNVERIFIED 5m. It still never changes the H1 decision or prices.
- fix(agents): a retried thread step (HUD agents, live room, news threads) replaces its stored failure instead of being ignored. Stored ok messages stay final, and the row keeps its id. Before, `INSERT OR IGNORE` kept the first `invalid_output` forever while each retry still spent a model call (preview-e2e on PR #95).
- fix(agents): prompts for answers without a `refs` field (moderators, HUD agents) no longer ask the model to back claims with refs; they ask it to name sources in plain words. The HUD moderator had written the evidence paths into its summary and failed the natural-language check (preview-e2e on PR #95).
- fix(fusion): a local record's freshness is judged from its candle's close (computed within 30 minutes of it), not from when the analysis runs. Retries and backfills after a Workers AI timeout no longer degrade the candle with `calculation_stale` (the 11:00 and 12:00 UTC candles of 2026-11-05). Ingest still rejects records that are old now.
- fix(bus): the fallback model (a reasoning model whose hidden trace shares `max_tokens`) gets twice the token budget, and an answer cut off by the limit is recorded as `finish_reason_length` instead of malformed output. Found by preview-e2e on PR #95: the primary timed out and the fallback's round-2 answer stopped mid-JSON at 3072 tokens.
- feat(hud): HUD agents on in production (`HUD_AGENTS_ENABLED`, owner approval 2026-11-05). Docs record owner decision "pilihan 1" (the 5m alerts may be deleted once `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5` are approved) and the backlog (B4, Grid Bot Protocol).
- test(worker): `test/worker-scheduled.test.mjs` bundles `src/index.ts` with esbuild and runs the real `scheduled()` handlers. The parity sweep cron approves a candle's local records and the backfill runs its analysis; the news cron fails into a logged error.
- feat(live-room): Phase C6. Four HUD agents (Trade Management, Kinetic Engine, Fabio AMT, Aegis Footprint) speak in the live 15m room from the 15m HUD snapshot, behind `HUD_AGENTS_ENABLED`. `LIVE_ROOM_MAX_CALLS_PER_HOUR` goes from 36 to 48.
  - Preview only: the hourly model-call caps count rows in the preview D1 that every preview-e2e run shares, so several runs in one hour exhausted them (all bus messages `budget_exhausted`). Preview caps are now 150 (bus and live room) and 60 (HUD agents); production caps are unchanged.
- feat(hud): Phase C5/C7. HUD agents in Threads 1H, behind `HUD_AGENTS_ENABLED` (off in production, on in preview).
  - One agent per computed HUD posts after each H1 synthesis, then a moderator. Agents run on the primary model only, within 30 calls per hour, and each sees only its own verified or not-yet-calibrated reading.
  - Nexus shows them by HUD name with a parity badge.
  - pi's scaffolds are replaced.
- `8caa888` (PR #92) feat(hud): Phase C2/C3 HUD snapshots and readings.
- feat(hud): Phase C2/C3. HUD snapshots per closed H1 and 15m bar from the local engines, covering 13 of the 14 HUDs (Grid Bot not computed).

- feat(chain): local-primary architecture (owner decision). Parity-approved local records start and drive the H1 chain; TradingView webhooks are calibration only and never start the chain or count as decision evidence.
  - `INDICATOR_SOURCE` (`local`, or `webhook` for rollback) and `CALIBRATION_INTERVAL_DAYS` (30).

GMI 5m uses `gmi-core-tf.mjs`, a timeframe-parameterised copy proven equal to `computeGmi` on H1. There are new provenance entries `prophet_m5` and `gmi_m5`. The H1 hashes are unchanged.

The sweep also reconciles the last six closed 5m candles. Reconcile tasks start the AI chain only on `ANALYSIS_TIMEFRAMES`, so a 5m local record can never run the H1 chain.

## 2026-11-04
